Explanatory Remarks:

1. Structural elements are enclosed in <>.

Text:

<1>
|| śrīḥ ||
|| śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ ||
|| viṣṇusaṃhitā ||
|| atha prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

śrīśailaśikhare siddhamāsīnaṃ tantrapāragam|
praṇamyātyujjvalākāraṃ pṛṣṭavānaupamanyavaḥ|| 1.1 ||
bhagavan! durnirīkṣyo'rkaṃ jitvevāsi sthitastviṣā|
nedṛśaṃ jātvahaṃ rūpaṃ dṛṣṭavānasmi kasyacit|| 1.2 ||
kiṃ devānāṃ bhavāneko munīnāmathavā nṛṇām|
tvāmahaṃ praṣṭumicchāmi tava vismāpitastviṣā|| 1.3 ||
tejaso'tyujjvalasyāsya ślāghanīyaḥ samudbhavaḥ|
kuto'trāgāḥ kimarthaṃ vā katame janma vā kule|| 1.4 ||
kṛtakṛtyo'spṛho vā tvamatulyamahimā dhruvam|
tvadgotranāmakarmādi yāthātathyena sarvaśaḥ|| 1.5 ||
mahat kautūhalaṃ śrotumavijñeyaṃ nacenmayā|
iti pṛṣṭo'bravīt siddho hariṃ dhyātvā kṛtāñjaliḥ|| 1.6 ||
harṣagadgadayā vācā saromāñcāśruvikriyaḥ|
śrūyatāmakhilaṃ vakṣye māyaivaiṣā hi vaiṣṇavī|| 1.7 ||
buddhyādyakṣārthanānātvairyā mohayati dehinaḥ|
sumatirnāma baido'hamṛṣeridhmavataḥ sutaḥ|| 1.8 ||
sāṅgeṣu triṣu vedeṣu tantreṣu ca kṛtaśramaḥ|
bālye'haṃ brahmacaryeṇa śaile'smin vimalodake|| 1.9 ||
raibhyasya japato'bhyāśe ciraṃ śuśrūṣako'vasam|
ciradṛṣṭaṃ didṛkṣustamimaṃ pūrvopakāriṇam|| 1.10 ||
ihāgato'smi bhadraṃ te viṣṇulokād vihāyasā|
ko na mantrastvayā labdhaḥ pureha japato guroḥ|| 1.11 ||
kimanyadvā yadabhyāsāt pariṇāmo'yamīdṛśaḥ|
tasminnanupadiśyaiva mantrasiddhyā tirohite|| 1.12 ||
rudan kā gatirityuccairaśrauṣaṃ khe'sphuṭāṃ giram|
gatistāranamonāmetyanayā durgamārthayā|| 1.13 ||
śivamantraṃ smṛtaṃ japtvā tatsiddhyā'drākṣamīśvaram|
tenokto brahmamantraṃ tvaṃ japetyenaṃ tato'japam|| 1.14 ||
tataścāvirabhūd brahmā varado lokabhāvanaḥ|
tenāpyukto'smī namro'haṃ viṣṇumantrastu japyatām|| 1.15 ||
tato jaṭākṣamālāditapoliṅgadharāvubhau|
manvānastāvaparyāptau paryāptaṃ viṣṇumeva ca|| 1.16 ||
ṣaḍakṣaraṃ japan mantramaharniśamanākulam|
viśvarūpamarūpaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭavānasmi rūpiṇam|| 1.17 ||
śaṅkhacakragadāpadmaśārṅgāsiśarakheṭakaiḥ|
śrīvatsakaustubhāpītakauśeyairvanamālayā|| 1.18 ||
kirīṭahārakeyūrakuṇḍalādivibhūṣaṇaiḥ|
alaṅkṛtamudārāṅgamaprameyaṃ surottamam|| 1.19 ||
divyarūpaṃ khagārūḍhaṃ divyaganghānulepanam|
sahasradityasaṃkāśaṃ durnirīkṣaṃ surāsuraiḥ|| 1.20 ||
ṛgyajussāmajairmantraiḥ stotraiśca vividhairapi|
prasādyainaṃ tato bhaktyā vyajñāpayamahaṃ punaḥ|| 1.21 ||
bhagavan! devadeveśa! vāsudeva! jagatpate!|
tajjñānaṃ me'nugṛhṇīṣva yena syāt kṛtakṛtyatā|| 1.22 ||
ityukto bhagavān prītaḥ prāha gambhīrayā girā|
diṣṭyā te vatsa! dhīḥ śuddhā sumatyākhyā tavāmṛṣā|| 1.23 ||
parituṣṭo'smi bhadraṃ te tasmād vakṣyāmi te hitam|
śṛṇu tvaṃ dīkṣito bhūtvā pūrvamīśvarasaṃhitām|| 1.24 ||
īśvarād brahmaṇaḥ paścāt tathaiva brahmasaṃhitām|
mad viṣṇusaṃhitāṃ śrutvā tato jñānamavāpsyasi|| 1.25 ||
trayo mantrāstvayā japtāḥ śivasya brahmaṇo mama|
tasmādanugraheṇāpi tribhyo bhāvyaṃ tava kramāt|| 1.26 ||
ityuktvā'ntarhite deve smṛtaḥ prādurabhūcchivaḥ|
sa nataṃ prāha māṃ prīto viṣṇūktaṃ kriyatāmiti|| 1.27 ||
tato dīkṣāvidhiṃ kṛtvā tasmādīśvarasaṃhitām|
avāpya dvādaśādhyāyāṃ nirbījārcāvidhāyinīm|| 1.28 ||
tataḥ smṛtāgatād devād brahmaṇo brahmasaṃhitām|
śrutvā dvātriṃśadadhyāyāṃ bījābījārcanāśrayām|| 1.29 ||
tataḥ smṛtāgataṃ devamaṣṭabāhumadhokṣajam|
pūrvavat praṇipatyāhaṃ vyajñāpayamidaṃ punaḥ|| 1.30 ||
devade! namaste'stu vāsudeva! surottama|
īśvarād brahmaṇaścāhaṃ śrutavānasmi tatkṛte|| 1.31 ||
saṃhite sarvalokeśa! tvanniyogād yathāvidhi|
atastvamanugṛhṇīṣva yathāvad viṣṇusaṃhitām|| 1.32 ||
iti vijñāpito bhaktyā bhagavān viṣṇuravyayaḥ|
ācaṣṭākhilasiddhyarthāṃ vistīrṇāṃ viṣṇusaṃhitām|| 1.33 ||
aṣṭottaraśatādhyāyāmaṣṭaiśvaryādisiddhidām|
jñānacaryākriyāyogaśubhapādacatuṣṭayām|| 1.34 ||
bījābījasabījārcāvidhānādisamanvitām|
tato naṣṭaḥ kṣaṇānmohaḥ kāmeṣūparatā spṛhā|| 1.35 ||
chinnā me saṃśayāḥ sarve prasannā cābhavanmatiḥ|
prakāśaṃ cābhavat sākṣāt tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam|| 1.36 ||
divyatūryaravo jajñe puṣpavṛṣṭiśca śobhanā|
samantāt sukhasaṃsparśaḥ sugandhiśca samīraṇaḥ|| 1.37 ||
tataścāntarhite tasmin bhagavatyakhileśvare|
japārcādiparo bhūtvā kañcit kālamihāvasam|| 1.38 ||
japato me kadācittu puṣpavṛṣṭyādi pūrvavat|
sunimittānvitaṃ jajñe sarvāścukṣubhire diśaḥ|| 1.39 ||
tato drutamupāgamya tārkṣyo māṃ hariśāsanāt|
ādāya divyayā gatyā viṣṇulokamupānayat|| 1.40 ||
tatra sākṣānmahāviṣṇuṃ brahmādibhirabhiṣṭutam|
divyarūpaṃ punardṛṣṭvā praṇamyātiṣṭhamagrataḥ|| 1.41 ||
sa prīto matsamīpe tvaṃ vaseti varado'bravīt|
susukhaṃ tatsamīpe'hamuṣitvā bahuvatsaram|| 1.42 ||
prāpyāṣṭaguṇamaiśvaryaṃ tatprasādādanatyayam|
dyutiṃ cemāmiha prāptaścaram̐llokān yadṛcchayā|| 1.43 ||
tvaṃ tu bhakto vinītaśca śrotavyaṃ caiva te'khilam|
aśakyā'lpena kālena sā śrotuṃ viṣṇusaṃhitā|| 1.44 ||
vakṣyāmyuddhṛtya sāraṃ te yathā na syādihāśrutam|
mayā pṛṣṭo yathā bhūyo bhagavān saṅgrahecchayā|| 1.45 ||
sārabhūtāṃ mamāvocat saṃkṣiptāṃ viṣṇusaṃhitām|
tantroddeśo'tha tadvyākhyā vaibhavaṃ kṣetranirṇayaḥ|| 1.46 ||
mantroddhāro'rcanā mudrā saṃskāro'gneśca maṇḍalam|
dīkṣābhiṣecanaṃ bhūmiḥ prāsādo bimbalakṣaṇam|| 1.47 ||
pratiṣṭhāpañcakaṃ caiva bimbaśuddhikramastathā|
adhivāsaḥ pratiṣṭhā ca pratiṣṭhānantarakriyā|| 1.48 ||
utsavaśca tathā yātrā balirviśvārcanaṃ tathā|
jīrṇoddhāro mahotpātaśamanaṃ snapanaṃ tathā|| 1.49 ||
prokṣaṇaṃ karmaśeṣaśca samayācārasaṅgrahaḥ|
yogo bhāgavataścaivamadhyāyāstriṃśadeva tu|| 1.50 ||
yā'ṣṭottaraśatādhyāyā mahatī viṣṇusaṃhitā|
tatroktānāṃ tu sarveṣāmarthānāmiha saṅgrahaḥ|| 1.51 ||
seyaṃ guhyatamā puṇyā spaṣṭārthā viṣṇusaṃhitā|
bhuktimuktipradā jñeyā viṣṇunā svayamīritā|| 1.52 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<2>
|| atha dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ ||


atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt tantravyākhyānamāditaḥ|
yena samyakkṛteneha siddhirbhavati śāśvatī|| 2.1 ||
dīkṣābhiṣekavān bhakto brāhmaṇaḥ samayasthitaḥ|
parānugrahavān vāggmī siddhaḥ siddhāntapāragaḥ|| 2.2 ||
vādajalpavitaṇḍābhiḥ paratantravighātakṛt|
sarvalakṣaṇasampannaḥ satyavādī dṛḍhavrataḥ|| 2.3 ||
nirdoṣo yogavid dāntaḥ karmajñaḥ kālavittamaḥ|
vyācakṣītopasannebhyaḥ svaśiṣyebhyo viśeṣataḥ|| 2.4 ||
dīkṣitebhyo vinītebhyo bhaktebhyaścāpi yatnataḥ|
dvijebhyo'dhītavedebhyaḥ śraddadhānebhya eva ca|| 2.5 ||
abhivādya guruṃ śiṣyaḥ sukhāsīnaḥ prayatnavān|
paṭhet praṇavapūrvaṃ tu śṛṇuyācca pūnarguroḥ|| 2.6 ||
abhyasecca dvijaireva tādṛśairdīkṣitaiḥ saha|
jñātvevamabhiṣiktastu parebhyaḥ pratipādayet|| 2.7 ||
dīkṣayecca tataḥ śiṣyān kuryāc sthāpanādikam|
dīkṣito'pi na śūdrastu paṭhecca śṛṇuyāt tathā|| 2.8 ||
gurudattaṃ japenmantramarcayet sthaṇḍile ca saḥ|
vyākhyāsthāpitadevārcāhomeṣvasya na kartṛtā|| 2.9 ||
dīkṣitasyāpi kintveṣa kāmaṃ vipraistu kārayet|
sarve'rthā yena tanyante trāyante ca bhayājjanāḥ|| 2.10 ||
iti tantrasya tantratvaṃ tantrajñāḥ paricakṣate|
vedamūlatayā tantramāptamūlatayā'thavā|| 2.11 ||
purāṇavat pramāṇaṃ syāt tathā manvādivākyavat|
dṛṣṭānumopamāśābdairarthāpattyā ca pañcadhā|| 2.12 ||
sarve'pyarthāḥ pratīyante yadi nābhāsatā bhavet|
dṛṣṭamakṣodbhavaṃ jñānaṃ śabdādiviṣayā matiḥ|| 2.13 ||
pramāṇaṃ viṣayākṣādidoṣairābhāsatā smṛtā|
tataḥ sāmānyato dṛṣṭaṃ pūrvavaccheṣavat tridhā|| 2.14 ||
anumānaṃ bhaved bhāvibhūtārthajñānasādhanam|
upamā yatra sādṛśyādupameyārthadarśanam|| 2.15 ||
yā'rthādāpadyate seyamarthāpattiriheṣyate|
vedatantrādibhedena śābdaṃ tu bahudhā smṛtam|| 2.16 ||
svayaṃprakāśa ekeṣāmātmā'nyairanumīyate|
prakṛtiśca vikṛtyobhāvāgamairvidhairapi|| 2.17 ||
niṣkalaḥ sakalairbhāvaiḥ sṛṣṭaiḥ sraṣṭā'numīyate|
jñātvaivaṃ sṛjyate sarvamiti sarvajña eva saḥ|| 2.18 ||
āptaproktatayā tantraṃ pramāṇamiti ye viduḥ|
vedaprāmāṇyamapyāhurāptamūlatayaiva te|| 2.19 ||
tacchaivavaiṣṇavabrāhmasaurakaumārabhedataḥ|
pañcadhā bhidyate tantraṃ vaktṝṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ|| 2.20 ||
viṣṇordharmapravaktṛtvāt tatproktaṃ manuvākyavat|
brahmaviṣṇū hi dharmāṇāṃ pravaktārau budhaiḥ smutau|| 2.21 ||
yathā tu vedavṛkṣasya śākhābhedā hmanekaśaḥ|
tathā bhedāḥ samākhyātāḥ pañcarātrasya sūribhiḥ|| 2.22 ||
kriyāpāṭhaviśeṣaistu bhidyante te pṛthak pṛthak|
tanmūlāni ca tantrāṇi munibhirdevamānuṣaiḥ|| 2.23 ||
bahudhā sampraṇītāni tenedaṃ bahudhā smṛtam|
tathā bahuvidhe'pyasmin pañcarātre'tivistare|| 2.24 ||
abhedena sthitaṃ tattvamekameva tu nānyathā|
gocaraṃ kulamityuktamanuṣṭhānaviśeṣataḥ|| 2.25 ||
tadeva jñāpakaṃ teṣāṃ deśikatve na saṃśayaḥ|
vaikhānasāḥ sāttvatāśca vikhyekāntikamūlakāḥ|| 2.26 ||
gocarāstu samākhyātā vāsudevādyajāśrayāḥ|
bhidyante pañcadhaite tu gocarāḥ pāramārthikāḥ|| 2.27 ||
arcanaṃ sarvakālaṃ tu devadevasya nityaśaḥ|
ayācitopapannena kuṭumbasya ca poṣaṇam|| 2.28 ||
vṛttibhedaḥ kriyā ceti yatra vaikhānasaṃ kulam|
ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā viṣṇorārādhanaṃ sphuṭam|| 2.29 ||
kṣatravṛttyopapannena kuṭumbasya ca poṣaṇam|
nāvartanaṃ ca saṅgrāme sarvaśāstreṣu kauśalam|| 2.30 ||
etaistu lakṣaṇairyuktāḥ sāttvatāste prakīrtitāḥ|
kṛṣivāṇijyagorakṣā dvikālaṃ viṣṇupūjanam|| 2.31 ||
etacca dṛśyate yatra śikhinaste prakīrtitāḥ|
śuśrūṣaṇaṃ dvijātīnāṃ bhaikṣavṛttyā ca vartanam|| 2.32 ||
sakṛccārādhanaṃ yeṣāṃ te tataikāntikāḥ smṛtāḥ|
ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā trikālamathavā punaḥ|| 2.33 ||
arcanaṃ devadevasya manovākkāyakarmabhiḥ|
ayācitopapannena vartanaṃ yeṣu dṛśyate|| 2.34 ||
svaśiṣyād vartanaṃ vā'pi te jñeyā mūlasaṃjñakāḥ|
pratyekaṃ pañcabhirbhedaistānyāsan pañcaviṃśatiḥ|| 2.35 ||
tantrāṇi punareteṣāmaparyantaḥ pravistaraḥ|
bhaktāśca bhagavadbhaktā dāsāḥ pāriṣadāstathā|| 2.36 ||
iti bhāgavatānto'nyo vibhāgaśceha kīrtitaḥ|
bhaktā varṇaratāḥ śāntā vaiṣṇavā ye tvadīkṣitāḥ|| 2.37 ||
dīkṣitā bhagavadbhaktā dāsā vai neṣṭhikāḥ smṛtāḥ|
pārṣadāstatkriyaikasthāstanniveditavṛttayaḥ|| 2.38 ||
nityamātodyavādyaistu pūjakā brahmacāriṇaḥ|
viprā bhāgavatā jñeyāḥ sarvotkṛṣṭatamāstu te|| 2.39 ||
samayī putrakaścaiva sādhako deśiko guruḥ|
iti dīkṣitabhedo'nyo vihitaśceha viṣṇunā|| 2.40 ||
maṇḍalaṃ vidhinā''likhya sāṅgamūrtiṃ yathāvidhi|
darśayet yamihācāryaḥ samayī nāma sa smṛtaḥ|| 2.41 ||
maṇḍale devamārādhya sāṅgamūrtiṃ yathāvidhi|
darśayet svayamācāryaḥ putrako nāma sa smṛtaḥ|| 2.42 ||
pradarśya maṇḍalaṃ hutvā mantrānagnau yathāvidhi|
dīkṣayed yaṃ guruḥ śiṣyaṃ sādhakaḥ sa tu mantrabhāk|| 2.43 ||
maṇḍalārāghanāhomavidhānairakhilaiḥ kramāt|
dīkṣayed yaṃ guruḥ śiṣyaṃ sa tantrajñastu deśikaḥ|| 2.44 ||
dīkṣitaḥ samāyācāraśīlavān sarvatantravit|
abhiṣiktastu vijñeyo gururdhyānārcanādikṛt|| 2.45 ||
varṇeṣu viprā vipreṣu vaiṣṇavāsteṣu dīkṣitāḥ|
teṣu mantravidasteṣu tantrajñāsteṣu pūjakāḥ|| 2.46 ||
teṣu ca granthavanto'tra pāṭhakāsteṣu tadvidaḥ|
teṣu siddhāśca teṣvevamakṣiṣikto viśiṣyate|| 2.47 ||
tato'nugrahakartā'smād vyākhyātā'smāt svayaṃ hariḥ|
saṅgrahādevamākhyātā mayā te tantragocarāḥ|| 2.48 ||
sevyāstat prepsubhirnityaṃ viṣṇoryat paramaṃ padam|
rātrayo gocarāḥ pañca śabdādiviṣayātmikāḥ|| 2.49 ||
mahābhūtātmakā vā'tra pañcarātramidaṃ tataḥ|
avāpya tu paraṃ tejo yatraitāḥ pañca rātrayaḥ|| 2.50 ||
naśyanti pañcarātraṃ tat sarvājñānavināśanam|
adhyetavyamataḥ śravyamanuṣṭheyārthamādarāt|| 2.51 ||
pramāṇapravaraṃ tantraṃ pañcarātrākhyamīdṛśam|
etābhyaḥ pañcarātribhyo vyatiriktaṃ nirañjanam|| 2.52 ||
yadā paśyet paraṃ tatvaṃ tadā muktaḥ sa nānyathā|
sa eva deśiko jñeyaḥ saṃsārarṇavatārakaḥ|| 2.53 ||
sa eva bhagavān viṣṇurnāphalastadanugrahaḥ|

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<3>
|| atha tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ ||


atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepād viṣṇorvaibhavamuttamam|
yena jñātena śuddhātmā vaiṣṇavaṃ padamāpnuyāt|| 3.1 ||
devateha paraṃ jyotireka eva paraḥ pumān|
sa eva bahudhā loke māyayā bhidyate svayā|| 3.2 ||
puruṣākhyaḥ svayaṃ māyāṃ prakṛtiṃ vyajya sa dvidhā|
sthitastridhā ca sattvādiguṇabhedāt pratīyate|| 3.3 ||
viṣṇubrahmaśivākhyo'sau sthityutpattyantakṛnmataḥ|
mūrtayo vāsudevādyā dharmajñānādibhedataḥ|| 3.4 ||
catasrastasya vijñeyā vedavarṇayugāśrayāḥ|
parameṣṭhī pumān viśvo nivṛttiḥ sarva ityasau|| 3.5 ||
pañcadhopaniṣadbhedānmahābhūtatvamāgataḥ|
manaḥ śrotrādibhiḥ ṣaṅbhiraṅgaiśca hṛdayādibhiḥ|| 3.6 ||
ṣaḍakṣarātmako nityamṛtubhiścaiṣa bhidyate|
saptavyāhṛtibhirlokaiśchandobhiḥ kratubhistathā|| 3.7 ||
saptadhā bhidyamāno'sau vijñātavyo vicakṣaṇaiḥ|
aṣṭaprakṛtibhiścāsāvaṣṭamūrtibhireva ca|| 3.8 ||
aṣṭākṣaramayo nityamaṣṭadhā caiṣa bhidyate|
nārāyaṇo nṛsiṃhaśca varāho vāmanastathā|| 3.9 ||
rāmabrahmendrasūryāśca candrastairnavadhā sthitaḥ|
indro'gniśca yamaścaiva nirṛtirvaruṇastathā|| 3.10 ||
vāyuśca soma īśāno brahmā'nantaśca te daśa|
ekādaśendriyairbhinnastathā dvādaśamāsapaiḥ|| 3.11 ||
sa trayodaśadhā caiva viśvedevādibhiḥ smṛtaḥ|
sa caturdaśadhā bhinno manubhiścākṣuṣādibhiḥ|| 3.12 ||
tithibhiścaiva vijñeyaḥ sa pañcadaśadhā sthitaḥ|
svaraiḥ ṣoḍaśadhā bhinno dikkoṇāvāntaraistathā|| 3.13 ||
mūrtyantaraiśca vijñeyo bahudhā tasya vistaraḥ|
ekadvitricatuṣpañcaṣaḍādyā viśvatomukhāḥ|| 3.14 ||
mukhabhedāḥ samākhyātāstasya viśvātmano hareḥ|
dvyādayo viśvataḥ pāṇerbhujabhedāstathā smṛtāḥ|| 3.15 ||
vividhābharaṇā dīrghā vividhāyudhadhāriṇaḥ|
mūrdhānaścaiva tasyoktā lasanmakuṭakuṇḍalāḥ|| 3.16 ||
sahasraṃ pauruṣe sūkte pādāścākṣīṇyanekaśaḥ|
hiraṇyagarbho'nekātmā vimalaḥ śyāma eva ca|| 3.17 ||
nīlaḥ pītaśca raktaśca nānāvarṇaśca kīrtitaḥ|
candrādityau smṛtau tasya vāmadakṣiṇalocane|| 3.18 ||
brahmāṇamāhurmurdhānaṃ keśāṃścāsya vanaspatīn|
bhruvermadhyaṃ tathā rudraṃ somaṃ ca manasi sthitam|| 3.19 ||
ekādaśāsya vijñeyā rudrāḥ kaṇṭhaṃ samāśritāḥ|
nakṣatragrahatārāśca darśanāstasya kīrtitāḥ|| 3.20 ||
dharmādharmau tathordhvādharoṣṭhasampuṭamāśritau|
idrāgnī tāluke tasya jihvā caiva sarasvatī|| 3.21 ||
diśaśca vidiśaścaiva śrotrayoḥ saṃvyavasthitāḥ|
vāyuḥ prāṇeṣu vijñeyo maruto'ṅgulayaḥ smṛtāḥ|| 3.22 ||
ṛṣayo romakūpasthāḥ samudrā bastigocarāḥ|
nadyaśca vasudhā cāsya nāgāśca nalake sthitāḥ|| 3.23 ||
jātusthāvaśvinau devau parvatāścorusaṃsthitāḥ|
guhye'sya guhyakā jñeyā vasavaścorasi sthitāḥ|| 3.24 ||
nakhāgreṣu ca vijñeyā divyā oṣadhayaḥ sthitāḥ|
nāsikāyāḥ puṭau jñeyāvayane dakṣiṇottare|| 3.25 ||
ṛtavo bāhumūlasthā māsāstasya kareṣu ca|
lalāṭāgre sthitāḥ siddhā bhruvormeghāḥ savidyutaḥ|| 3.26 ||
yakṣakinnaragandharvā daiteyā dānavāstathā|
rākṣasāścāraṇāścasya jaṭharaṃ tu samāśritāḥ|| 3.27 ||
pitaraḥ pretakūśmāṇḍavetālapramathāstathā|
pātālagocarāścāsya pādayugme vyavasthitāḥ|| 3.28 ||
pārśvayostasya vijñeyā yajñā vedikatāntrikāḥ|
agnihotrādikarmāṇi varṇāśramagatāni ca|| 3.29 ||
svāhāsvadhāvaṣaṭkārāḥ sarve'sya hṛdaye sthitāḥ|
ye vai sahasranāmāno viṣṇavaḥ parikīrtitāḥ|| 3.30 ||
sahasramūrtayaste'tra yathāyogamavasthitāḥ|
yataḥ sahasrasaṃkhyā'pi bahusaṃkhyā prakīrtitā|| 3.31 ||
mūrtayaścāsya sarvāstāḥ saṃkhyātītā hyanekaśaḥ|
devādīnāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ mūrtayo'traiva kīrtitāḥ|| 3.32 ||
tasmāt sahasramūrtiḥ san viṣṇuḥ sarvātmako mataḥ|
darpaṇānāṃ bahutve tu dṛśyate naikatā yathā|| 3.33 ||
tadvad bahutvaṃ manyante viṣṇostasyālpacetasaḥ|
yathā'mbhasīndubimbāni pratiśabdāśca naikadhā|| 3.34 ||
eko'pyātmā bahuṣvevamityāhustattvadarśinaḥ|
paramārthamajānanto mūḍhāstvajñānamohitāḥ|| 3.35 ||
kṣetrajñasya bahutvaṃ hi vadantīha ramanti ca|
brāhmaṇā yasya mukhataḥ kṣatriyā yasya bāhutaḥ|| 3.36 ||
vaiśyā yasyoruto jātāstadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam|
īdṛśaṃ taṃ mahāviṣṇumaprameyamanāmayam|| 3.37 ||
tatprasādādṛte vaktuṃ jñātuṃ vā naiva śakyate|
sarvadevāśrayo viṣṇuḥ sarve devāstadātmakāḥ|| 3.38 ||
aśeṣaṃ vāṅmayaṃ cedaṃ lokālokaṃ carācaram|
vyāptaṃ viṣṇuśarīreṇa vāyunevāmbaraṃ sadā|| 3.39 ||
sarve viṣṇuparā devāḥ sarvaśāstreṣu kīrtitāḥ|
yato jātā'khilā sṛṣṭinte tallayabhāginī|| 3.40 ||
tato'nyaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣāt ko viśvaṃ vyāpya tiṣṭhati|
ādhārādheyabhāvena dvidhāvastho janārdanaḥ|| 3.41 ||
sarvabhūtahitāyāsau sthitaḥ sakalaniṣkalaḥ|
evaṃ cobhayarūpo'sau jñeyo viṣṇuḥ parāt paraḥ|| 3.42 ||
sthūlasūkṣmaparatvena tridhā ca bhagavān sthitaḥ|
prabhaviṣṇurmahāviṣṇuḥ sadāviṣṇuśca sa smṛtaḥ|| 3.43 ||
sa hyātmā cāntarātmā ca paramātmā ca sa smṛtaḥ|
vairājaṃ laiṅgikaṃ caiśaṃ vahirantaśca sarvaśaḥ|| 3.44 ||
śabdādiścinmayaṃ rūpaṃ jāgratsvapnasuṣuptigam|
mantrānusvāranādeṣu trayamanveṣayed budhaḥ|| 3.45 ||
vede sāṅkhye ca yoge ca pañcarātre ca kevale|
dharmaśāstre purāṇe ca munibhirdevamānuṣaiḥ|| 3.46 ||
paṭhyate nikhilairnityaṃ viśvaṃ viṣṇumayaṃ jagat|
atītānāgataṃ caiva vartamānaṃ ca kiñcana|| 3.47 ||
indriyāṇīndriyārthāśca bhūtāntaḥkaraṇāni ca|
avyaktaṃ triguṇā māyā vidyā dharmādayastathā|| 3.48 ||
niyatiśca kalā kālaḥ sarvamanyacca tanmayam|
viṣṇureva paro devaḥ sarvabhūteṣvavasthitaḥ|| 3.49 ||
sarvabhūtāni caivāsau na tadastīha yanna saḥ|
devāsurādayo martyāḥ paśavaśca sarīsṛpāḥ|| 3.50 ||
taruvallītṛṇauṣadhyo mahābhrāśanividyutaḥ|
śailābdhisaridārāmanagarāṇi sarāṃsi ca|| 3.51 ||
lokāścānantakālāgnipretāvāsoragālayāḥ|
sapta bhūrādayo brāhmaśaivavaiṣṇavasaṃjñitāḥ|| 3.52 ||
sarve ca viṣṇunaikena vyāptā ityavadhāraya|
varāho bhārgavaḥ siṃho rāmaśrīdharavāmanāḥ|| 3.53 ||
aśvakṛṣṇau ca dikṣveṣāṃ lokairaṇḍaṃ sahākhilam|
yaccānuktamaśeṣeṇa viṣṇoretā vibhūtayaḥ|| 3.54 ||
viśvavyāpitayaivaiṣa viṣṇutvaṃ prāptavān prabhuḥ|
vasanāt sarvabhūteṣu vāsudevatvameva ca|| 3.55 ||
ādimūrteḥ samākṛṣṭa iti saṅkarṣaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ|
pradyumno dyumnapuṣṭatvādaniruddho'nirodhanāt|| 3.56 ||
acyuto'cyavanād yogāt tridhāmā dhāmabhistribhiḥ|
vilomendriyagamyatvājjñeyo'ntaryāmyadhokṣajaḥ|| 3.57 ||
vaikuṇṭhāmalavarṇatvād vaikuṇṭhaścāyamucyate|
keśau sargāntayorasya sta ityevaiṣa keśavaḥ|| 3.58 ||
naranārīprakartṛtvānnarāṇāṃ cāyanādayam|
nārāyaṇo narotthānāmayanatvādapāṃ ca saḥ|| 3.59 ||
mādhavo madhuṣūtpattyā dhavatvād vā śriyaḥ smṛtaḥ|
gāṃ vindatīti govindo duḥkhānāṃ haraṇaddhariḥ|| 3.60 ||
madhvākhyāsuraghātitvāducyate madhusūdanaḥ|
tribhiḥ svairvikramairvyāpta iti jñeyastrivikramaḥ|| 3.61 ||
vāmano hrasvatāyogācchrīdharo vahanācchriyaḥ|
hṛṣīkākhyendriyeśatvāddhṛṣīkeśo'yamīritaḥ|| 3.62 ||
padmaṃ nābherabhūd yasya padmanābhastataḥ smṛtaḥ|
udarālambi dāmāsyetyukto dāmodaraśca saḥ|| 3.63 ||
sa rudro rodānājjāto brahmā bṛṃhaṇakarmaṇā|
indraśca paramaiśvaryād vahanād vahnirucyate|| 3.64 ||
yamaḥ saṃyamanāt pusāṃ varaṇād varumastathā|
vāyurvānāt savāt soma īśaśceṣṭo janeṣvataḥ|| 3.65 ||
ādityo'ditiputratvāccandraścandayatīti saḥ|
ityevaṃ guṇagṛttyohyaiḥ śabdaireko'pyanekadhā|| 3.66 ||
pratīyate'mbhasīvendurbahutvaṃ nāsya tāvatā|
yathā sarvagato vāyuratisūkṣmo na dṛśyate|| 3.67 ||
tathā sarvagato viṣṇurajñaistajjñaistu duśyate|
icchājñānakriyābhedāt tisro vai tasya śaktayaḥ|| 3.68 ||
yābhirdvādaśadhā bhinnāścatasrastasya mūrtayaḥ|
sā tu śaktiḥ parā sūkṣmā yecchākhyā kāmarūpiṇī|| 3.69 ||
otaṃ protaṃ yayā sarvaṃ dṛśyate sacarācaram|
dvidhā vibhajya sā''tmānaṃ kriyājñānapravartanam|| 3.70 ||
prakaroti jagat kṛtsnaṃ svatantramiva tadvaśāt|
kriyāśaktyāparo viṣṇuḥ jñānaśaktyā khageśvaraḥ|| 3.71 ||
taṃ yajñapuruṣaṃ prāhustapaśchandomayaṃ khagam|
icchāśaktyā tu vijñeyaḥ puruṣoḥ yaḥ paro'vyayaḥ|| 3.72 ||
jñānapūrvaṃ pravarteta kriyā kartā tataḥ punaḥ|
jñānādhārā kriyā jñeyā na caikā saṃpravartate|| 3.73 ||
kriyājñānaprabhedena śaktirekā parasya nuḥ|
dvidhā vyāpya jagat kṛtsnaṃ carācaramavasthitā|| 3.74 ||
śaktiśaktimatoryasmānna bhedo'sti parasparam|
abhinnaṃ tena boddhavyaṃ kriyājñānadvayaṃ budhaiḥ|| 3.75 ||
eka eva tridhārūpo bhedenānena saṃsthitaḥ|
kriyā jñāna tathecchā ca tritayaṃ caikameva hi|| 3.76 ||
upacāraḥ smṛto bheda ekasyaiva mahātmanaḥ|
khagopendraśivāścaivamabhinnāstena kirtitāḥ|| 3.77 ||
indriyāṇīndriyārthāśca buddhereva vibhūtayaḥ|
ahaṅkāravikārāśca jalabudbudavanmatāḥ|| 3.78 ||
goṣvapyanekavarṇāsu yathā kṣīraikavarṇatā|
tathā''śrayeṣu bhinneṣvapyekarūpamavekṣyatām|| 3.79 ||
kṣaṇabhaṅgi jagat sarvaṃ viddhyetat sacarācaram|
tadabhaṅgyekameveha yad viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam|| 3.80 ||
tasyeśvarasya caiśvaryāt sarvametat pravartate|
seśvaraṃ hi jagat kṛtsnaṃ nānīśvaramidaṃ bhavet|| 3.81 ||
kṣetrajñasya bahutvaṃ ca vadantyajñānamohitāḥ|
na kṣetrajñabahutvaṃ tu tattvajñānāṃ tu sammatam|| 3.82 ||
upādhibhedād ye tvāhurbahutvaṃ paramātmanaḥ|
saṃsarantyeva te mūḍhā nāpnuvanti paraṃ padam|| 3.83 ||
yeṣāṃ viśvasṛjo'pyasya sarvajñatvamanīpsitam|
taiḥ pramāṇaprameyānāṃ sambandho no'vadhāritaḥ|| 3.84 ||
saṃsthānināṃ samutpattyā jagato janma yanmatam|
tadīśakṛtamanyo'laṃ prakṛterna hi codane|| 3.85 ||
tasmādīśakṛtaṃ viśvaṃ tadūḍhaṃ tanmayaṃ tathā|
tatpālitaṃ tadarthaṃ ca tadīyaṃ ceti gṛhyatām|| 3.86 ||
tasyedṛśasya devasya prabhāvo nanvadurgamaḥ|
svayameva sa taṃ veda yadi vā na tathā śruteḥ|| 3.87 ||
yato jātā'khilā sṛṣṭiriyaṃ ko veda tat param|
arvāgdevā hi tatsṛṣṭā brahmādyā api mūrtayaḥ|| 3.88 ||
tathā'pi mahimoddeśastasyokto'cintyarūpiṇaḥ|
vakti yanmahimānantyaṃ na te viṣṇāviti śrutiḥ|| 3.89 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<4>
|| atha caturthaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt kṣetrakṣetrajñanirṇayam|
sṛṣṭisaṃhārayogaṃ ca bhoktṛbhojyatvasaṃyutam|| 4.1 ||
kṣetrākhyā prakṛtirjñeyā tadvit kṣetrajña īśvaraḥ|
ubhayaṃ cedamatyantamabhinnamiva tiṣṭhati|| 4.2 ||
avyaktaṃ buddhyahaṅkārau tanmātrāṇīha pañca ca|
ekādaśendriyairbhūtaiḥ kṣetramevaṃ prakīrtitam|| 4.3 ||
sthūlasūkṣmavibhāgena dvidhā kṣetramavasthitam|
kāryaṣoḍaśakaṃ sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ buddhyādimātrajam|| 4.4 ||
tadanityaṃ ca nityaṃ ca śarīraṃ sarvadehinām|
puryasyāmuṣito bhoktā puruṣaścaiṣa kathyate|| 4.5 ||
cinmātraḥ puruṣo vyāpī vyāpyā pūstriguṇā jaḍā|
tanniyogāt svatantreva sūte bhāvān haratyapi|| 4.6 ||
parārthā'cetanāsūkṣmā triguṇā nityavikriyā|
prakṛtirguṇabhedāt tu buddhyādivikṛtistridhā|| 4.7 ||
sattvaṃ sukhaṃ rajo duḥkhaṃ tamo mohaśca te guṇāḥ|
te'nyonyābhibhavavyaktamithunāśrayavṛttayaḥ|| 4.8 ||
laghuprakāśakaṃ sattvaṃ kriyānirbandhakṛd rajaḥ|
tamo guru nirodhyete sitaraktāsitāḥ smṛtāḥ|| 4.9 ||
tribhireva guṇairetaiḥ prakṛtisthaiḥ samanvitāḥ|
sarve bhāvā bhavantyete śreṣṭhamadhyamakanyasāḥ|| 4.10 ||
prakṛterguṇasaṃyogabhedaireko'pyanekadhā|
nirguṇo'pi pumān bhāti sphaṭiko'nyairivānvitaḥ|| 4.11 ||
sāttvikairarcayenmuktyai bhāvairbhogāya rājasaiḥ|
tāmasaiḥ parapīḍādau miśritairmiśrasiddhaye|| 4.12 ||
tasmāt taṃ pratyupāsīnaḥ sarvān bhāvānatandritaḥ|
kuryācca sāttvikāneva bhāvānanyopamardanāt|| 4.13 ||
mahadākhyā'ṣṭadhā buddhirjātā'vyaktād guṇaistribhiḥ|
caturekatribhedā sā buddhiraṣṭavidhā smṛtā|| 4.14 ||
dharmo jñānaṃ ca vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ sattvabuddhayaḥ|
avairāgyaṃ rajobuddhiradharmādyāstamodhikāḥ|| 4.15 ||
yamaśca niyamo dharmo manovākkāyajakriyaḥ|
devaprasādanāyāsau kartavyo mokṣakāṅakṣibhiḥ|| 4.16 ||
śraddhā dhṛtiḥ prasādākhyo mānaso dharma uttamaḥ|
satyapriyahitālāpo vāgdharma iha kīrtitaḥ|| 4.17 ||
śārīraḥ paricaryāśuddhyanugrahamayastridhā|
ete dharmāstu kartavyā varṇāśramagatāśca ye|| 4.18 ||
vaidikāstāntrikā vā'sya siddhirbhavati nānyathā|
jñānaṃ tu mokṣadharmārthakāmāṅgatvāccaturvidham|| 4.19 ||
dvidhā vivekanirvedakṛtaṃ vairāgyamiṣyate|
ādyamābhyantaraṃ muktyai bāhyamanyattu bandhanam|| 4.20 ||
jāto buddherahaṅkārastrividhastriguṇātmakaḥ|
yena janturanātmānamātmānamiva manyate|| 4.21 ||
vaikṛtaḥ sāttviko garvo rājasastaijasāhvayaḥ|
bhūtādistāmaso nāmnā garvabhedā ime smṛtāḥ|| 4.22 ||
vaikārikādahaṅkārāt pañca buddhīndriyāṇi ca|
samutpannāni yairjñānaṃ narasyārtheṣu jāyate|| 4.23 ||
karmendriyāṇi pañcaiva karmārthāni ca taijasāt|
mano jātaṃ dvirūpaṃ ca tataḥ saṅkalpasādhanam|| 4.24 ||
manaḥ śrotrādiṣu jñeyaṃ cittaṃ vāgādiṣu smṛtam|
bhūtādeḥ pañca jātāni tanmātrāṇi yathākramam|| 4.25 ||
śabdaḥ sparśaśca rūpaṃ ca raso gandhaśca pañcamaḥ|
parameṣṭhyādayo jñeyā viṣṇostāḥ pañca śaktayaḥ|| 4.26 ||
yābhireṣa paro devaḥ parame vyomni tiṣṭhati|
cicchaktiḥ sarvakāryādikūṭasthā parameṣṭhyasau|| 4.27 ||
dvitīyā tasya yā śaktiḥ puruṣākyādivikriyā|
viśvākhyā vividhābhāsā tṛtīyā karaṇātmikā|| 4.28 ||
caturthī viṣayaṃ prāpya nivṛttyākhyā sthitā punaḥ|
pūrṇajñānakriyāśaktitaḥ sarvākhyā tasya pañcamī|| 4.29 ||
etābhiḥ śaktibhiryuktaḥ paramātmā nirañjanaḥ|
utpattisthitisaṃhārahetuḥ syādakriyo'pi san|| 4.30 ||
nigrahānugrahau cāpi karoti jagataḥ prabhuḥ|
ekaikotkarṣabhedena pañcadhā pañcaśaktibhiḥ|| 4.31 ||
pañcātmānaḥ smṛtā hyete sūkṣmarūpā vyavasthitāḥ|
saṃsāraviṣayātītā yogagamyāḥ sanātanāḥ|| 4.32 ||
etat sūkṣmaśarīraṃ tu kathitaṃ mūrttikāraṇam|
tanmātrebhyastu bhūtāni kramājjātāni pañca vai|| 4.33 ||
vyoma vāyuśca tejaśca jalaṃ pṛthvī ca pañcamī|
etat sthūlaśarīraṃ tu sarvādhāraṃ prajāyate|| 4.34 ||
śrotraṃ tvakcakṣuṣī jihvā ghrāṇaṃ buddhīndriyāṇi tu|
karmendriyāṇi vākpāṇiguhyapāyvaṅghrayaḥ smṛtāḥ|| 4.35 ||
evaṃ sṛṣṭiḥ śarīrāntā sthūlasūkṣmavibhāgataḥ|
kīrtitā te mayā sargaviparīto layakramaḥ|| 4.36 ||
yathaikasūkṣmadeho'pi dehī janmani janmani|
dehairanekatāmeti tatā devo'pi mūrtibhiḥ|| 4.37 ||
parasya puruṣasyaiva śaktayaḥ koṭiśo'parāḥ|
jātināmasvarūpāṇāṃ bhedād bhinnā iva sthitāḥ|| 4.38 ||
śarīraṃ bhautikaṃ prāpya karmabhiḥ svairupārjitaiḥ|
dīrghaṃ bhramanti saṃsāre te vai saṃsāriṇo janāḥ|| 4.39 ||
śubhena karmaṇā yānti gatimūrdhvamukhīṃ śubhām|
aśubhenāśubhaṃ caiva tathā gatimadhomukhīm|| 4.40 ||
pumāṃso vyākṛte magnāścidrūpā api te svataḥ|
aśaktāḥ svaṃ paraṃ jñātumāśrayadhvāntamohitāḥ|| 4.41 ||
rāgadveṣādayasteṣāṃ doṣāḥ prakṛtisambhavāḥ|
saṃsāravāsanāmeva kurvantyasya pṛthagvidhām|| 4.42 ||
teṣāṃ sattvaguṇodreke gatirdevatvamiṣyate|
mānuṣyaṃ rajasā proktaṃ tiryaktvaṃ tamasā tathā|| 4.43 ||
evaṃ śarīriṇaḥ sarve gacchanti vividhāṃ gatim|
kālacakraṃ samāruhya bhramantyajñānamāyayā|| 4.44 ||
yatra vyastāḥ samastā vā dharmādyāḥ sāttvikā guṇāḥ|
sa priyo'syāpriyaścānyo yatrādharmādayaḥ sthitāḥ|| 4.45 ||
pralayānte tu samprāpte nirvyāpāraḥ paraḥ pumān|
vijñānaghanarūpo'sau parame vyomni tiṣṭhati|| 4.46 ||
yata evamato labdhvā śarīraṃ sarvasādhanam|
śubhaṃ karmaiva kartavyaṃ tatprasādāya mānavaiḥ|| 4.47 ||
prasādite'smin sarveṣāṃ svavarṇāśramakarmabhiḥ|
sarve hastagatāḥ kāmā muktiścānte na durlabhā|| 4.48 ||
kāryārthā mūrtayastasya lokānugrahahetavaḥ|
ataḥ sākāramiṣṭvemaṃ bhaktyāpyāḥ sarvasiddhayaḥ|| 4.49 ||
bhaktyaiva parayā tuṣṭo devadevaḥ sa yoginām|
pūjādyanugrahāyādau rūpaṃ bheje caturbhujam|| 4.50 ||
tasmāt tenaiva rūpeṇa dhyeyo'rcyaśca sadā budhaiḥ|
ākāre'smin kṛtā pūjā stutirvā dhyānameva vā|| 4.51 ||
vidhinā śāstradṛṣṭena deva eva kṛtā bhavet|
udayāyāparaḥ pūjyo nirvāṇāya paraḥ pumān|| 4.52 ||
pūrvāhṇe cāparāhṇe ca pūjanaṃ śasyate tayoḥ|
śyāmamaṣṭabhujaṃ devaṃ kalpayitvā savāhanam|| 4.53 ||
sāyudhaṃ saparīvāramudayārthī prapūjayet|
śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam|| 4.54 ||
caturbhujamanantasthaṃ yajenmokṣāya bhaktitaḥ|
sṛṣṭibījaṃ viduḥ padmaṃ cakraṃ sthitinibandhanam|| 4.55 ||
gadaśaṅkhau ca saṃhāramuktyarthau sarvadā smṛtau|
sarvatattvayutasyaiva pūjā saṃhāramārgataḥ|| 4.56 ||
dhyānaṃ phalānapekṣitvamiti mokṣārthināṃ kramaḥ|
uttarottarabhūyiṣṭhaṃ karmavāgbuddhyupāsanam|| 4.57 ||
tāmasaṃ rājasaṃ caiva sāttvikaṃ ca tathā bhavet|
samayo'gre samācāraḥ svādhyāyo dravyasaṅgrahaḥ|| 4.58 ||
buddhiryāgaḥ stitirdhyānamityevaṃ vidhiraṣṭadhā|
dvividhaḥ puruṣo bhakto dīkṣito'dīkṣitastathā|| 4.59 ||
dīkṣitaḥ sarvakartā'nyaḥ samayārcanamātrabhāk|
kriyārcā samayo bhaktaviśvāsaḥ pūjanādaraḥ|| 4.60 ||
naḥ kathāśrutiḥ śāntiranājīvo vratāṣṭakam|
svārāmotpāditaiḥ puṣpairvanyaiḥ krītaistu vā'rcayet|| 4.61 ||
pratigrāhyaṃ na pūjārthaṃ dātureva phalaṃ yataḥ|
alābhe gandhapuṣpābhyām puṣpairapi samarcayet|| 4.62 ||
puṣpālābhe tu toyena toyālābhe tu cetasā|| 4.63 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ caturthaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<5>
|| atha pañcamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣopānmantroddhāravidhiṃ param|
yasmin samyakkṛte mantrā vīryavantaḥ syuriṣṭadāḥ|| 5.1 ||
vivikte susame deśe śucau snigdhe manorame|
puṣpaprakarasaṃkīrṇe mantroddhāraṃ samācaret|| 5.2 ||
srātvā''cāntaḥ kṛtanyāso gandhapuṣpādyalaṅkṛtaḥ|
sānugaḥ prayato mantrī sūtrapātaṃ prakalpayet|| 5.3 ||
catuṣṣaṣṭipadaṃ kṛtvā candanena sugandhinā|
ādihāntāṃ samālikhya mātṛkāmaṣṭavirgiṇīm|| 5.4 ||
ādipaṅkau dviśo vargaṃ vargaśo'nyatra cāntataḥ|
nādāt sarvagatādaṣṭavargotpattimanusmaret|| 5.5 ||
nādo'kāraḥ svayaṃ jāto vāsudevātmakastataḥ|
saṃkarṣaṇākhyayā''kāra ikāraśca śivākhyayā|| 5.6 ||
īkāraśca tato jātastau ca saṃkarṣaṇātmako|
brahmokāro'ta ūkāraḥ pradyumanākhyau ca tau smṛtau|| 5.7 ||
haṃsākhyo'smādṛkāro'ta ṛkāraścāniruddhakaḥ|
lṛkāro mahadākhyo'ja lṝkāraśca tato'jani|| 5.8 ||
tato rudrākhya ekāra aikāraścāpyanantaraḥ|
prajāpatyākhya okāra aukāraśca tadātmakaḥ|| 5.9 ||
bindustasmāddharīśātmā visargo'smācca te svarāḥ|
tasmāddharīśād brāhmyādimātṛbhedena saptadhā|| 5.10 ||
kādivargā yaśādī ca nyastavyāste'nyapaṅktiṣu|
evaṃ sarasvatīṃ devīmaṣṭavargātmikāṃ subhām|| 5.11 ||
gandhapūṣpairyajed varṇadevatānāmabhiḥ kramāt|
tato gāruḍamaśvibhyāmuddhṛtaṃ varṇahārakam|| 5.12 ||
yamākhyamodvayaṃ ceṣṭvā tena mantrān samuddharet|
praṇavāṣṭākṣaroddhāre vāsudevaḥ svayaṃ bhavet|| 5.13 ||
ṣaṭke saṃkarṣaṇo brahmā gāyatryāṃ cākrike haraḥ|
pradyumnastvajitoddhāre nārasiṃhe'niruddhakaḥ|| 5.14 ||
vāsudevādibhiḥ sve sve bījamantrāśca hetayaḥ|
ādityenaiva tūddhāryāḥ pañcopaniṣadādayaḥ|| 5.15 ||
ādyādimuddharet pūrvaṃ punastatraiva pañcamam|
ṣaṣṭhāntaṃ cādivargonaṃ trayamekatra yojayet|| 5.16 ||
etat tat paramaṃ tattvamakṣaraṃ paramaṃ padam|
anenaivārcanaṃ viṣṇorjapo homaśca mokṣadaḥ|| 5.17 ||
tridevaguṇavedāgnisandhyālokadaśāsvayam|
vedādirakṣaravyāptyā sarvamantrāditāṃ gataḥ|| 5.18 ||
pañcamāntaṃ paraṃ tārāt ṣaṣṭhāntaṃ satrayodaśam|
devanāma caturthyantamityetanmantralakṣaṇam|| 5.19 ||
evaṃ ṣaḍakṣaro mantro nirbījo'ṣṭākṣarastathā|
dviṣaṭke bhagavacchabdo namaskārāt paro bhavet|| 5.20 ||
bījaṃ tārādinatyantaṃ sarvatrākhyā na cet parā|
yatrā'khyā tu caturthyantā namaskārastadā'ntataḥ|| 5.21 ||
caturthamaṣṭame bījamādiṣaṣṭhāntabindukam|
hṛdayaṃ tadabījasya viṣṇubījaṃ ca yad viduḥ|| 5.22 ||
dvitīyādyaṃ śiraścāsya bindvantamupakalpayet|
aṣṭamādyaṃ śikhābījaṃ tṛtīyasvarabheditam|| 5.23 ||
caturthaṃ kavacaṃ cāntye pañcamasvarabindumat|
tadevāstraṃ bhavet tasya ṣoḍaśasvarasaṃyutam|| 5.24 ||
etat pañcāṅgamuddiṣṭaṃ nirbījasya ṣaḍātmanaḥ|
yadākhyājātibhiryuktaṃ sarvakāmapradaṃ viduḥ|| 5.25 ||
hṛdbījaṃ mūlamaṅgāni dvicatuṣṣaṣṭhayogataḥ|
netrāntāni ṣaḍapyāhurdvādaśāntyacaturdaśaiḥ|| 5.26 ||
namontāni nirākhyāni sākhyāni tu sabījake|
praṇavānantaraṃ bījamanante cennamonvitam|| 5.27 ||
sabījasyedamuddiṣṭaṃ mūlamantrasya lakṣaṇam|
tārāt sabindukairvarṇaiḥ svarabheditabījakaiḥ|| 5.28 ||
namontākhyaiḥ ṣaḍaṅgāni netrāntaṃ tat prakalpayet|
hṛdayaṃ kundasaṅkāśaṃ śiro bhinnāñjanaprabham|| 5.29 ||
śikhā tu piṅgalā jñeyā kavacaṃ hemasannibham|
vidyudākāramastraṃ syānnetraṃ jvalanasannibham|| 5.30 ||
jñānasantoṣanityatvasvātantryāvighnavaibhavaiḥ|
hṛdayādiguṇairyuktaḥ sakalo'nugrahādikṛt|| 5.31 ||
svāhākāravaṣaṭkārau home śāntikapauṣṭike|
namaskāro japārcādāvaiśvarye praṇavo'ntataḥ|| 5.32 ||
ādau tāranamomadhye bindvantāḥ ṣoyarāvalāḥ|
parāya bhagavatsthāne pañcopaniṣadādiṣu|| 5.33 ||
prathamaḥ parameṣṭhyātmā puruṣātmā tathāparaḥ|
viśvātmā'tha nivṛttyātmā sarvātmā pañca te smṛtāḥ|| 5.34 ||
āvāhane visarge ca yojyāḥ pañca kramotkramāt|
prokṣaṇe śāntihome ca dravyaśuddhau ca kīrtitāḥ|| 5.35 ||
puruṣaḥ prakṛtiścobhau jīvaprāṇasamāhvayau|
buddhyahaṃkārasaṅkalpāstanmātrāṇīndriyāṇi ca|| 5.36 ||
bhūtāni ceti bindvantairmādikāntairanukramāt|
pañcaviṃśatitattvāni vijñātavyāni deśikaiḥ|| 5.37 ||
aṣṭamāntyaṃ svarāntyābhyāṃ kāladiṅmantrayoḥ kramāt|
khakāro vainateyasya vighneśasya tataḥ param|| 5.38 ||
kālabījamanantasya dīrghairaṅgāni tasya ṣaṭ|
akṣabījaṃ tu varṇāntyaṃ cakramaṇḍalakalpane|| 5.39 ||
padmasya praṇavo'syāṃśāḥ patrakesarakarṇikāḥ|
hrasvākṣarāṇi bījāni nābhisthe savanatraye|| 5.40 ||
asaṃvṛtasvaraiḥ kālamaramāseṣu yojayet|
avisargaiḥ svarairādyairvāmadakṣiṇapārśvayoḥ|| 5.41 ||
rāśīnāṃ tairvisargādyairapyūhyāstithayo dvidhā|
aśvayukprabhṛtīnyeṣu nakṣatrāṇi yathākramam|| 5.42 ||
bindvantairādikāntaistu hādimāntairnava grahāḥ|
arkādayaḥ svarāśisthā rāhuketū ca yadgatau|| 5.43 ||
sakāraśca ṣakāraśca nemyorantarbahiṣṭhayoḥ|
prakṛtyādīni tattvāni yojyānīha pṛthak kramāt|| 5.44 ||
dvādaśākṣarabījaistu yojyā viṣṇvādayo'ragāḥ|
ṛtavo mūlabījaistu vasantādya yathākramam|| 5.45 ||
cakramaṇḍalayāge tu viśeṣo'yamihoditaḥ|
bhadrakasthaṇḍilārcāsu na mantrāḥ kālasaṃśrayāḥ|| 5.46 ||
nāmāṣṭakadviṣaṭkasthaṃ vidmahedhīmahenvitam|
krameṇa kalpayet tanno viṣṇurante pracodayāt|| 5.47 ||
gāyatrī vaiṣṇavī jñeyā sarvapāpaharā tviyam|
yad dattamanayā sarvaṃ pratigṛhṇāti devarāṭ|| 5.48 ||
upacāre ca yoktavyā pakve pūrṇāhutau ca sā|
sahasrāreti saṃkīrtya huṃkāraṃ phaṭ ca yojayet|| 5.49 ||
ṣaḍakṣaramidaṃ jñeyaṃ sudarśanamakhaṇḍitam|
kṣudrabhūtapiśācādivāraṇaṃ roganāśanam|| 5.50 ||
sacaturdaśabindvantyaṃ nārasiṃhaṃ ca tādṛśam|
vilomāṣṭamavargārṇaistārapūrvairabindubhiḥ|| 5.51 ||
vāsudevādayo yojyā namontā dikṣu mūrtayaḥ|
dīrghaistaireva koṇeṣu śāntiḥ śrīśca sarasvatī|| 5.52 ||
ratiśceti kramād yojyāścatasro mūrtiśaktayaḥ|
aṣṭamasyādibījaṃ tu dvisaptāntena saṃyutam|| 5.53 ||
saptamasya dvitīyaṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ ca tathā bhavet|
dvitīyasya tṛtīyaṃ ca tṛtīyāvaraṇe kramāt|| 5.54 ||
dīrgho'ntyādistu bindvanto dvitīyasya dvitīyakaḥ|
bindvekādaśasaṃyuktaḥ so'ntyādiśca sabindukaḥ|| 5.55 ||
dikkoṇeṣu kramādaṣṭau bījāni kathitāni ca|
vargādibhiḥ kramād bījairindrādyāḥ paritaḥ smṛtāḥ|| 5.56 ||
vajrādayo'stramantrāśca tadvitīyaiḥ sabindubhiḥ|
viṣvakseno'ntavauṃkāro namontaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|| 5.57 ||
sarvāsāṃ devatānāṃ tu guṇaśaktisamanvayāt|
bījāni kalpayed vidvān pañcarātraviśāradaḥ|| 5.58 ||
akṣarāṇi tu vāk proktā na sā prakṣīyate kila|
tasmād varṇe gṛhīte'pi na śūnyaṃ puṭamucyate|| 5.59 ||
vyañjanāni svarānatra gṛhṇīyācca pṛthak pṛthak|
tad gṛhītaṃ gṛhītaṃ ca sandhayitvānyaduddharet|| 5.60 ||
sakalīkaraṇaṃ nāma mantrasaṃskāra uttamaḥ|
tasmāduddharaṇaṃ kāryamiṣṭvā koṣṭheṣu mātṛkām|| 5.61 ||
anakṣareṣu koṣṭheṣu pūjayenmantramuddhṛtam|
evaṃ samuddhṛtā mantraḥ praṇavoddīpitākṣarāḥ|| 5.62 ||
vīryavanto bhaviṣyanti bhuktimuktiphalapradāḥ|
gatvaikānte śucau deśe srātācānto jitendriyaḥ|| 5.63 ||
maṇḍale devamārādhya sāṅgaṃ sāvaraṇaṃ kramāt|
japet ṣaḍviṃśatiṃ lakṣān vratastho'nanyamānasaḥ|| 5.64 ||
tilānāṃ ghṛtamiśrāṇāṃ sahasraṃ caiva homayet|
tatastu sidhyate mantro varadaścāpi jāyate|| 5.65 ||
saptalokyaṃ yadiṣṭaṃ syādātmanaḥ sukhasādhanam|
tadbhogyatvamanenāśu yāti dhyānaprayogataḥ|| 5.66 ||
devaśaktiramoghaiṣā na kvacit pratihanyate|
devadānavayakṣeṣu kimpunarmānuṣe jane|| 5.67 ||
yakṣiṇī kinnarī cātha divyayoṣā ca mānuṣī|
tasya ṣaṭpañcasaptaikamāsataḥ kiṃkarī bhavet|| 5.68 ||
nāma saṃkīrtya sādhyāyā bījānte vaśametviti|
bījamante ca yoktavyaṃ jvaladvahnisphuliṅgamuk|| 5.69 ||
kruddhaṃ plutaprayogeṇa sādhayedacirād balāt|
evamevābhicāreṣu phaṭkārāntaṃ prayojayet|| 5.70 ||
nāmānte nāśamāyātu viśeṣo'yaṃ ca vidyate|
nāmānte nāma ityuktvā tato bījaṃ vicintayet|| 5.71 ||
ākarṣe ca vaśīkāre viśeṣo'yamihoditaḥ|
visaṃjñaṃ vihvalaṃ sādhyamāyāntaṃ cintayedalam|| 5.72 ||
kalaśo hemaratnāḍhyastaddhṛto'viratāṃ vahan|
dhyeyaḥ svapurato dhārāṃ dhanasyāparimāṇataḥ|| 5.73 ||
bhūtikāmasya yogo'yaṃ nāmnā vaiśravaṇasya hi|
prayacchatviti nāmānte vasunāma sanāmakam|| 5.74 ||
vaśīkāre parāṃ kanyāmāpnoti ciramabhyasan|
labhyatāmiti nāmānte tasyāḥ saṃparikīrtayet|| 5.75 ||
uccāṭe gacchatu jñeyamāyātvānayane'bhyaset|
prītāvāvahatu prītiṃ vidveṣe dveṣamṛcchatu|| 5.76 ||
yat sisādhayiṣurvidvānidaṃ siddhaṃ punarjapet|
tasya sidhyatitat kṣipraṃ kintu śraddhābiyogataḥ|| 5.77 ||
hṛdbījaṃ tu plutaṃ cintyaṃ jvaladaṣṭatanau sthitam|
anenaivātmanaḥ kṛtvā sakalīkaraṇaṃ tanau|| 5.78 ||
yena yena svareṇaitad yuktaṃ yatra niyujyate|
tadetat sādhayatyāśu na moghamiti dāraṇā|| 5.79 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ pañcamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<6>
|| atha ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt snānācāmādi pūjanam|
yena samyakkṛteneha sādhakaḥ siddhimāpnuyāt|| 6.1 ||
tīrthe nadyāṃ taṭāke vā sudhīrgatvā kuśādimān|
mṛdamastreṇa saṅgṛhya sarvadoṣavivarjitām|| 6.2 ||
astreṇa kṣālite tīrthe saṃsthāpya vibhajet tridhā|
astreṇa ca hṛdā prokṣya bhāgāṃstān kavacena ca|| 6.3 ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ parikramya namaskṛtvā'tha tajjalam|
pūrvāṃśena malasnānaṃ kṛtvā majjet triromiti|| 6.4 ||
dvitīyenālabhet paścādācamya vidhipūrvakam|
tṛtīyaṃ dikṣu sarvāsu kṣiped vighnopaśāntaye|| 6.5 ||
pratisrotaḥ punarmajjet tīrthaṃ dhyātvā tu vaiṣṇavam|
tīrthaṃ punaḥ samāsādya vidhinā''camya vāgyataḥ|| 6.6 ||
mantranyāsaṃ kramāt kṛtvā prokṣya mūlādibhistathā|
tataścācamya kurvīta prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kramāt|| 6.7 ||
mūlamantreṇa sāṅgena vyastena susamāhitaḥ|
nābhyāśliṣṭena toyena tatastenādhamarṣaṇam|| 6.8 ||
samastena samuddiṣṭaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ tathā|
kṛtvā'bhiṣekamuttīrya paridhāya sitāmbaram|| 6.9 ||
ācānto nyasya samprokṣya kuśāgraiḥ pūrvavat kramāt|
upaviśya śucau deśe prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ caret|| 6.10 ||
paratattvena kṛtvaivaṃ namaskṛtya ca mārjayet|
upasthānaṃ tataḥ kuryāt paratattvena deśikaḥ|| 6.11 ||
dhyātvā jyotirmayaṃ viṣṇuṃ maṇḍalasthaṃ jalārghyadaḥ|
sthitvā'ṣṭottarajaptena mūlamantraśatena tu|| 6.12 ||
gāyatryā caiva vaiṣṇavyā praṇavādyantaruddhayā|
upaviśya śucau deśe tatastarpaṇamārabhet|| 6.13 ||
viṣṇvādidevatāścarṣīn manuṣyāṃ pitṝṃstathā|
tarpayet tānaśeṣeṇa paścāt tīrthaṃ tu saṃharet|| 6.14 ||
mūlamantraṃ japan gacched vāgyataścintayan harim|
ājānu pādau prakṣālya hastāvākorparaṃ punaḥ|| 6.15 ||
triḥ pibedaṃ brahmatīrthena dvirūnmṛjya mukhaṃ punaḥ|
vāmahastaṃ ca pādau ca prokṣyāṅgāni punaḥ spṛśet|| 6.16 ||
vedāstrayastribhiḥ pānaiḥ pitaraśca pitāmahāḥ|
kramaśastṛptimāyānti tathaiva prapitāmahāḥ|| 6.17 ||
mārjane prathame brahmā dvitīye dakṣa eva ca|
tatrātharvetihāsāśca tṛptimāyānti saṃsmṛtāḥ|| 6.18 ||
cakṣuṣoḥ spṛṣṭayostṛptiṃ candrādityau prayāsyataḥ|
aṅguṣṭhe'nāmikāṃ kṛtvā tarjanīṃ pavano nasoḥ|| 6.19 ||
kaniṣṭhāṃ karṇayordasrau bāhvoraṅguṣṭhakena ca|
śakraśailāśca tṛpyanti nābhau viṣṇuśca madhyayā|| 6.20 ||
mahāviṣṇuḥ sadā viṣṇuścaturbhirhṛdi ke'khilaiḥ|
etadācamanaṃ proktaṃ dīkṣitasya viseṣataḥ|| 6.21 ||
yāgasthānaṃ samāsādya devaṃ saṃsmṛtya vāgyataḥ|
astrema gṛhamālokya vediṃ sthaṇḍilameva ca|| 6.22 ||
tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya namaskṛtya samāhitaḥ|
kṛtvopavītavadvastramuttaraṃ prāṅmukhaḥ śuciḥ|| 6.23 ||
nārasiṃhena digbandhaṃ kṛtvā padmāsanasthitaḥ|
paratattvaṃ samuccārya kṛtvā brahmāñjaliṃ śubham|| 6.24 ||
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryād dhāraṇātrayasaṃyutam|
nābhimaṇḍalamadhye yaṃ dhūmraṃ caṇḍānilātmakam|| 6.25 ||
viśleṣayadaghaṃ dehāt sarvagātropaśoṣakam|
dhārayeddhṛdaye cāgniṃ tāreṇārkādupāhṛtam|| 6.26 ||
jvālābhistiryagūrdhvādho dahantaṃ kāṣṭhavat tanum|
śikhānte jīvamāyojya bījena susamāhitaḥ|| 6.27 ||
gatvā suṣumnārandhreṇa tato'rkasyoparisthitāt|
candrādamṛtamānīya svatanuṃ bhasmasātkṛtām|| 6.28 ||
plāvayitvā navaṃ dehaṃ pārthivaiḥ paramāṇubhiḥ|
jalabudbudamadhyasthaṃ prājāpatyaṃ tu kalpayet|| 6.29 ||
ātmānaṃ yojayitvā'tra sarvajñaṃ gatakalmaṣam|
astraśodhitayormūlaṃ karayorvyāpakaṃ nyaset|| 6.30 ||
dakṣiṇe'ṅguṣṭhamārabhya bījāni kramaśo nyaset|
tārādyantāni vāme'pi talāntamubhayatra tu|| 6.31 ||
aṅgāni ca tayornyasya sarvāṅge vyāpakaṃ nyaset|
prathamaṃ mūrndhi vinyasya lalāṭe ca dvitīyakam|| 6.32 ||
tṛtīyaṃ hṛdaye caiva caturthaṃ nābhimaṇḍale|
pañcamaṃ tu nyasedūrvoḥ pādāvasthaṃ tu ṣaṣṭhakam|| 6.33 ||
sṛṣṭinyāso'yamāsīne kartavyaḥ kāmyasiddhaye|
sthite kuryāt sthitinyāsaṃ nābhyādi hṛdayāntakam|| 6.34 ||
śayite saṃhṛtinyāsaṃ pādādi pratilomataḥ|
sthitinyāso gṛhasthasya sṛṣṭyākhyo brahmacāriṇaḥ|| 6.35 ||
mumukṣoḥ saṃhṛtinyāsa evaṃ nyāsasridhā smṛtaḥ|
evaṃ nyāsavidhiṃ kṛtvā tārādyantaiḥ ṣaḍakṣaraiḥ|| 6.36 ||
hṛdayādīni cāṅgāni svasthāneṣu niyojayet|
japtvā mantraṃ tato'streṇa saṃvidhya śakalaṃ tyajet|| 6.37 ||
puṣpamastreṇa ca kṣiptvā sambhārānupakalpayet|
astrakṣālitapātrasthe gandhapuṣpānvite jale|| 6.38 ||
mantraṃ nyasyāṣṭakṛtvastu praṇavenābhimantrayet|
tatastu prokṣaṇaṃ kuryādastramantreṇa sarvataḥ|| 6.39 ||
phaṭkārāntena medhāvī tataḥ pīṭhaṃ prakalpayet|
maṇḍale sthaṇḍile'rcāyāṃ pūjayeddhṛdi vā budhaḥ|| 6.40 ||
sthaṇḍilādyarcanāt siddho hṛtpadme manasā'rcayet|
dharmādharmādipādeṣamanantamayamāsanabh|| 6.41 ||
triguṇākalitaṃ māyāvidyāsitasitāstaram|
vipulaṃ sthaṇḍilebhyarcya gandhapuṣpairyathākramam|| 6.42 ||
tasyopari tathā padmaṃ praṇavena samāhitaḥ|
tadaṃśaiḥ sūryasomāgnimaṇḍalātmatrayaṃ punaḥ|| 6.43 ||
uparyupari saṅkalpya patrakesarakarṇikāḥ|
vimalotkarṣiṇī jñānā kriyā yogā yathākramam|| 6.44 ||
prahvī satyā tatheśā ca kesareṣvaṣṭa śaktayaḥ|
madhyato'nugrahā cānte pūjyāḥ sarvārthasiddhaye|| 6.45 ||
hṛdyevaṃ yogapīṭhasthaṃ dhyātvā viṣṇumatandritaḥ|
kṣālite taijase pātre gandhapuṣpayutodake|| 6.46 ||
praṇavena smared viṣṇumavyaktasyoparisthitam|
viṣṇumūrtiṃ tato dhyātvā sarvatejomayaṃ vibhum|| 6.47 ||
karṇikopari sañacintya nirudhya sakalīkṛtam|
pūjayet sāṅgamavyagre bhaktyā'rghyādibhiracyutam|| 6.48 ||
pūrveṇa hṛdayaṃ nyasya dakṣiṇena śirastathā|
paścimena śikhāṃ caiva kavacaṃ cottareṇa tu|| 6.49 ||
āgneyādiṣu kāṇeṣu pūjayedastramuttamam|
vāsudevādikān nyasya dikṣu śaktīśca koṇataḥ|| 6.50 ||
tadbahiḥ śaṅkhacakrābjagadā dikṣu niveśayet|
koṇeṣu ca tataḥ śārṅgakheṭakhaṅgaśarān nyaset|| 6.51 ||
indramagniṃ yamaṃ caiva nirṛtiṃ varuṇaṃ tathā|
vāyuṃ somaṃ tatheśānaṃ svāsu dikṣu kramānnyaset|| 6.52 ||
vajraṃ śaktiṃ ca daṇḍaṃ ca khaḍgaṃ pāśaṃ dhvajaṃ tathā|
gadāṃ triśūlaṃ ca bahisteṣāmastrāṇi kalpayet|| 6.53 ||
agrato vainateyaṃ tu tṛtīyāvaraṇād bahiḥ|
viṣvaksenaṃ tathaiśānyāṃ dvitīyāvaraṇād bahiḥ|| 6.54 ||
sarveṣāmapi caiteṣāṃ pratyekama padmamāsanam|
nāmabhiḥ kalpayed vidvānardhyaṃ devāya bhaktitaḥ|| 6.55 ||
pādyamācamanīyaṃ ca srānamālepanaṃ tathā|
vastrālaṅkārapuṣpāṇi dhūpadīpanivedyakam|| 6.56 ||
tatsarvaṃ viṣṇugāyatryā dātavyaṃ tu yathākramam|
dattvā devāya bhūṣāntaṃ snānādyaṅgeṣu kalpayet|| 6.57 ||
gāyatryā ca tataḥ puṣpaṃ devāyānyebhya eva ca|
vyastena mūlamantreṇa samastena ca taiḥ saha|| 6.58 ||
yāgo'yaṃ tasya vijñeyaḥ sadā'nye'rcyāḥ svanāmabhiḥ|
paratattvena vyāpyānte pūjayet taṃ sanātanam|| 6.59 ||
dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ nivedyaṃ ca dattvā'smai vidhipūrvakam|
yathāsambhavato'nyeṣāṃ nivedyamupakalpayet|| 6.60 ||
agnau ca kalpayet sāṅgaṃ yogapīṭhasthamujjvalam|
paristaraṇaparyukṣāpavitrakaraṇādi yat|| 6.61 ||
praṇavenaiva sarvaṃ tat kṛtvā'thājyena homayet|
daśāṃśamaṅgamūrttīnāmanyeṣāmekaśaḥ kramāt|| 6.62 ||
namaḥ pariṣadebhyaścetyaṣṭadikṣu baliṃ haret|
ācamya sakalīkṛtya japtvā stutvā praṇamya ca|| 6.63 ||
arghyaṃ dattvā kṣamasveti saṃhṛtya hṛdi yojayet|
dhyāyejjyotirmayaṃ devaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam|| 6.64 ||
pītavastramudārāṅgaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam|
śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ vāsudevaṃ caturbhujam|| 6.65 ||
śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇiṃ dhṛtaśrīvatsakaustubham|
tathā saṅkarṣaṇaṃ dhyāyejjātarūpasamaprabham|| 6.66 ||
dūrvāmarakataprakhyaṃ pradyumnaṃ ca tathā smaret|
nīlāmbudapratīkāśamaniruddhaṃ tathaiva ca|| 6.67 ||
śāntasphaṭikasaṅkāśāṃ śriyaṃ padmarajaḥprabhām|
sarasvatīṃ tathārūpāṃ ratiṃ śyāmāṃ tu cintayet|| 6.68 ||
śuklavarṇaṃ tathā śaṅkhaṃ cakramagnisamaprabham|
gadāṃ caiva haridrābhāṃ suvarṇābhaṃ tu paṅkajam|| 6.69 ||
gadāṃ strīlakṣaṇairyuktāṃ sarvasaṃhārakāriṇīm|
khaḍgamākāśasaṃkāśaṃ dhyāyeccakrasamākṛtim|| 6.70 ||
śārṅgaṃ ca tatsamaṃ pītamadhobhāge gadāsamam|
śaraṃ ca dhanurākāraṃ kheṭakaṃ khaṅgasannibham|| 6.71 ||
cakramugraṃ prabhūtākṣaṃ nābhyaraprathinemikam|
lelihānaṃ durālokaṃ bhramadvahnisphuliṅgamuk|| 6.72 ||
caturbāhuṃ dvibāhuṃ vā daṃṣṭriṇaṃ piṅgamūrdhajam|
lambodaraṃ tathā śaṅkhaṃ hrasvakuñcitamūrdhajam|| 6.73 ||
indraḥ śyāmo yamaḥ pīto varuṇaḥ śyāma eva ca|
śaṅkhāmalaruciḥ somo vahniḥ śoṇastu kīrtitaḥ|| 6.74 ||
sadaṃṣṭro nirṛtiḥ śyāmo vāyurdhūmraḥ praśasyate|
īśānastu bhaved rakta evaṃ dhyāyet kramādimān|| 6.75 ||
indrakāraṃ bhaved vajraṃ citraśāstroditaṃ tu vā|
evamastrāṇi dikpānāmanyeṣāṃ ca prakalpayet|| 6.76 ||
agnivarmaścaturbāhustārkṣyo lūnāgranāsikaḥ|
grasanniva dṛśā dhyeyo mahāpakṣo mahābalaḥ|| 6.77 ||
viṣvakseno'bhravarṇastu śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ|
pañcamūrdhā caturbāhuranantaḥ pṛṣṭhato hareḥ|| 6.78 ||
dhyeyo'nye pārṣadāstasya jñātavyāścāgamāntare|
yo'nena vidhinā viṣṇuṃ pūjayed bhaktimān naraḥ|| 6.79 ||
tasya hastagatāḥ kāmā ihāmutra ca yoginaḥ|| 6.80 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<7>
|| atha saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepānmudrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ param|
nyāsādiṣu prayujyante yatā mudrāḥ sugopitāḥ|| 7.1 ||
añjaliḥ prathamā mudrā kṣipraṃ devaprasādanī|
vandanī hṛdayāsaktā kiñcid dakṣiṇato natā|| 7.2 ||
ūrdhvāṅguṣṭho vāmamuṣṭirdakṣiṇo'ṅguṣṭhabandhanaḥ|
vāmasya tasya cāṅguṣṭho bhavatyūrdhvamukhaḥ sthitaḥ|| 7.3 ||
tisraḥ sādhāraṇā mudrā mūrtīnāmiha kirtitāḥ|
padmāsanagato mūrdhni kuryādañjalibandhanam|| 7.4 ||
mudrā brahmāñjalirnāma devasānnidhyakāriṇī|
anayā baddhayā caiva nityaṃ tuṣyanti devatāḥ|| 7.5 ||
tasmāt tu sarvadevānāṃ mudrāmenāṃ pradarśayet|
sammukhau prasṛtau kṛtvā kiñcidākuñcitāṅgulī|| 7.6 ||
aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ likhedantarmuṃdraiṣā''vāhinī smṛtā|
kṛtvā natāṅgulī hastāvūrdhvaṅguṣṭhau tu cālayet|| 7.7 ||
sānnidyamudrikā jñeyā nyastavat sthāpanī bhavet|
ubhāvantargatāṅguṣṭhai muṣṭī kuryānnirodhinī|| 7.8 ||
saṃhatau tu karau kṛtvā madhye'ṅguṣṭhau nipīḍayet|
prasārya cāṅgulīḥ sarvā mudreyaṃ parameṣṭhinaḥ|| 7.9 ||
ekaikasya kramādanyā aṅgalīśca niyojayet|
puruṣādikrameṇaiva sarvamudrāḥ prakalpayet|| 7.10 ||
saptajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yacca pātakapañcakam|
mudrāpañcakavijñānānnaśyatyeva na saṃśayaḥ|| 7.11 ||
vāmahastena muṣṭiṃ tu kṛtvā'ṅguṣṭhaṃ samucchritam|
hṛdi saṃyojayedetāṃ gṛhītvetaramuṣṭinā|| 7.12 ||
yonimudrā tviyaṃ proktā vaiṣṇavī bhuktimuktidā|
darśayed devadevasya baddhvā padmāsanaṃ puraḥ|| 7.13 ||
dṛṣṭvaivaṃ darśitāṃ sadyaḥ praṇāmaṃ yānti devatāḥ|
yakṣavidyādharādyāśca bhramanti bhayamohitāḥ|| 7.14 ||
vāmahastamathottānaṃ kṛtvottānaṃ ca dakṣiṇam|
saṃśliṣya bhrāmayet paścād grathitvā cāṅgulīḥ kramāt|| 7.15 ||
koṭimudrā tviyaṃ proktā devadevasya ca priyā|
anāmāmadhyayormadhye niveśyāṅguṣṭhamāyatam|| 7.16 ||
dakṣiṇasya tu hastasya mudreyaṃ hṛdaye smṛtā|
nirvartya muṣṭimāhanyādaṅguṣṭhāgreṇa tarjanīm|| 7.17 ||
sthāpayet tu lalāṭānte mudreyaṃ śirasi smṛtā|
vartayitvā dṛḍhāṃ muṣṭiṃ kuryādaṅguṣṭhamūrdhvagam|| 7.18 ||
sthāpayet tāṃ śikhādeśe śikhāmudrā prakīrtitā|
sampraveśyāntaraṅguṣṭhau veṣṭayitvā samantataḥ|| 7.19 ||
saṃharet tatpradeśinyau mudreyaṃ kavace smṛtā|
agrahastaṃ parāvṛtya baddhvā nārācamuṣṭivat|| 7.20 ||
visṛjet tiryagāyamya mudrāstrasyeyamīritā|
bhrūmadhye sthāpayed baddhvā śikhāmudrāmadhomukhīm|| 7.21 ||
netramudreyamākhyātā ṣaḍetāstvaṅgamudrikāḥ|
ātmarakṣaṇametābhiḥ kartavyaṃ sādhakaiḥ sadā|| 7.22 ||
kṛtvā'ṅguṣṭhau samāvūrdhvaṃ badhvā hastau parasparam|
yogasampuṭamudreyaṃ dhyāne sarvatra yujyate|| 7.23 ||
tathaivācchidravat kṛtvā kuryānmūrdhanyadhomukhīm|
abhiṣeke prayoktavyā mudreyaṃ pāvanī parā|| 7.24 ||
maṇibandhasamau hastāvūrdhvaṃ śākhāḥ prasāritāḥ|
antaḥ praveśitāṅguṣṭhau padmamudrāsane smṛtā|| 7.25 ||
maṇibandhasamau hastau tiryak sambhrāmya cakravat|
paryāyeṇa prayoktavyā cakramudrā mahodayā|| 7.26 ||
muṣṭiṃ kṛtvā tu hastābhyāmaṅguṣṭhau vinatāvubhau|
yuktvā sambhrāmya nirmuktau gadākhyā vighnanāśinī|| 7.27 ||
nibadhya dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhaṃ vāmahastasthamuṣṭigam|
kṛtvā cāṅguṣṭhatarjanyau saṃyukte prasṛte same|| 7.28 ||
tisrastu dakṣiṇasyānyā badhnīyurmuṣṭimūrdhvataḥ|
śaṅkhamudreyamuddiṣṭā dravyāṇāṃ sthāpane parā|| 7.29 ||
dhanuṣastu dhanurmuṣṭiḥ śaramuṣṭiḥ śarasya ca|
khaṅgasya kośaviśleṣo mudrā kheṭasya maṇḍalam|| 7.30 ||
ubhe kaniṣṭhike baddhvā pradeśinyāvubhe tathā|
hastāvubhau parāvṛttāvaṅguṣṭhau sahitāvadhaḥ|| 7.31 ||
madhyamānāmike yukte cālayet pakṣayugmavat|
baddhā garuḍamudreyaṃ vāhanādau balāvahā|| 7.32 ||
viralordhvāṅguliṃ hastaṃ kṛtvā''yāmena darśayet|
mudrānantasya vijñeyā śayanādau prayujyate|| 7.33 ||
kṛtvottānāvubhau hastau sampuṭāgrau prasāritau|
kurvīta prārthanāmudrāṃ sānnidhyādiṣu sarvadā|| 7.34 ||
śrīvatsakaustubhārthaḥ syāduraḥsparśo dvipārśvagaḥ|
ūrdhvamudrāṃ prayujyādāvuttānīkṛtya tāṃ punaḥ|| 7.35 ||
muñced vāmakaniṣṭhādikramānmūrticatuṣṭaye|
kiñcidākuñcitāgrāstu yojayitvā'ṅgulīḥ śanaiḥ|| 7.36 ||
tarjanyau madhyamāpṛṣṭhe yojayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ|
aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ likhedantaḥ śaktīnāṃ yonisaṃjñitā|| 7.37 ||
vāmahastena saṅgṛhya karaṃ tiryakprasāritam|
saṃhataṃ cālayedeṣā dhvajamudrā prakīrtitā|| 7.38 ||
madhyānāmāntare'ṅguṣṭho nirgato madhyamānatā|
dakṣiṇasya tu muṣṭau syānmudrā vaināyakī smṛtā|| 7.39 ||
ūrdhvāṅguṣṭho bhavenmuṣṭirliṅgamudrā śive smṛtā|
tarjayed vāmatarjanyā nāsāgrāsannayā jagat|| 7.40 ||
viṣvaksenasya sā mudrā jīrṇoddhārādiṣu smṛtā|
mānaso rūpasaṅkalpo mudrā mokṣārthināṃ smṛtā|| 7.41 ||
itareṣāṃ tu hastābhyāṃ prayogaḥ śasyate budhaiḥ|
nānyasandarśane mudrā nānimittaṃ ca bandhayet|| 7.42 ||
guhyametaddhi tantreṣu tasmād rahasi yojayet|
mudaṃ kurvanti devānāṃ rākṣasān drāvayanti ca|| 7.43 ||
ityevaṃ sarvamudrāṇāṃ mudrātvaṃ tāntrikā viduḥ|
paṭānte bandhayenmudrā nacāpi kathayed budhaḥ|| 7.44 ||
kathanājjāyate sraṃsastasmād gopyāḥ sadā budhaiḥ|
ānantyādakhilā mudrā vaktuṃ brahman! na śakyate|| 7.45 ||
devatānāṃ bahutvācca mudroddeśo mayeritaḥ|
praṇāmaprārthanādyāstu mudrāḥ sarvadivaukasām|| 7.46 ||
acyutasyāpi pūjāyāmajñānāṃ kalpayedimāḥ|
alābhe sarvamudrāṇāmañjalirhidi mūrdhni vā|| 7.47 ||
sāmānyamudrā vijñeyā sarveṣāṃ ca divaukasām|
mantraśca praṇavo yojyaḥ siddhirevaṃ bhaviṣyati|| 7.48 ||
pūrvaṃ kṛtvā karanyāsaṃ mudrābandhaḥ śubho bhavet|
anyathā viphalāḥ sarvā bhaviṣyanti na saṃśayaḥ|| 7.49 ||
vṛthā'nyadarśane vā'pi prayuktā viphalāstathā|
kupyanti devatāścāsya siddhimāśu haranti ca|| 7.50 ||
guptaṃ mudrāgaṇaṃ yastu yathākālaṃ pradarśayet|
kāmāḥ sarve'sya sidhyanti prīyante cāsya devatāḥ|| 7.51 ||
sāmānyākhyā viśeṣākhyāstathāṅgākhyāśca kīrtitāḥ|
tathaiva parivārākhyā mudrā hyetāścaturvidhāḥ|| 7.52 ||
madhyamānāmike vidvānaṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ tu pīḍayet|
kaniṣṭhā tarjanīyojyā śrīvatsasya pradarśayet|| 7.53 ||
anāmā pṛṣṭhasaṃlagnā dakṣiṇasya kaniṣṭhikā|
kaniṣṭhayā tu saṅgṛhya tarjanyā'nāmikāṃ tathā|| 7.54 ||
gṛhītvā'nāmikāṃ madhyāmaṅguṣṭhaṃ cochritaṃ kuru|
tasminnaṅguṣṭhake śliṣṭā vāmahastasya tarjanī|| 7.55 ||
madhyame madhyamāṃ kṛtvā cāṅuṣṭhaṃ maṇibandhane|
kaustubhasya tviyaṃ mudrā darśanīyā ca sādhakaiḥ|| 7.56 ||
sammukhāvucchritau hastau vyomamadhye vimocayet|
tarjanyaṅguṣṭhakau śliṣya mālāmudrāṃ pradarśayet|| 7.57 ||
evaṃ caturvidhā mudrā gopyā yatnena sarvadā|
prayojyāśca yathākālaṃ sādhakaiḥ siddhikāṅkṣibhiḥ|| 7.58 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<8>
|| atha aṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepādagnisaṃskāramuttamam|
prācyāṃ kuṇḍamudīcyāṃ vā sthaṇḍilaṃ vā'pyasambhave|| 8.1 ||
dvihastamekahastaṃ vā caturaśraṃ trimekhalam|
dvimekhalaṃ vā kuṇḍaṃ syādekamekhalameva vā|| 8.2 ||
ekakuṇḍasya mānārdhaṃ khātamanyatra tatsamam|
dvādaśāṣṭacaturbhiḥ syādaṅgulairmekhalonnatiḥ|| 8.3 ||
sāttavikī rājasī cātra tāmasī mekhalāḥ smṛtāḥ|
caturaṅgulavistārāḥ sarvāstā hastamātrake|| 8.4 ||
dviguṇe dviguṇā jñeyā yoniḥ paścimato bhavet|
caturdaśāṅulā paścāt tannālaṃ caturaṅgulam|| 8.5 ||
aṣṭāṅgulaṃ bhaved vṛttaṃ nirvāhastu ṣaḍaṅgulaḥ|
gajoṣṭhasadṛśo'śvatthapatrāgrākāra eva vā|| 8.6 ||
prājāpatyā gajoṣṭhāgrā yoniranyā tu vaiṣṇavī|
kuṇḍasthā vaiṣṇavī śaktiḥ prakṛtistriguṇātmikā|| 8.7 ||
sā yoniḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ siddhīnāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ|
prācyāṃ śiraḥ samākhyātaṃ bāhū dakṣiṇasaumyayoḥ|| 8.8 ||
udaraṃ kuṇḍamityuktaṃ yoniḥ pādau ca paścime|
padānyekonapañcāśat kuṇḍe saptavibhājite|| 8.9 ||
ādihāntāṃ kramāt teṣu padeṣu parikalpayet|
praṇavaṃ pūrvamuddiśya mekhalātritayeṃ'śakān|| 8.10 ||
vyāpinaṃ puruṣaṃ nādaṃ smared vyāptāṃ ca mātṛkām|
visargaṃ yonimadhye ca dhyātvā'gnīṣomabindukam|| 8.11 ||
garbharūpā tu sā yonirmātṛkeyaṃ tadudbhavā|
pitṛmartyasurāṃśasthāṃ prādakṣiṇyena pūjayet|| 8.12 ||
hakāraṃ madhyakoṣṭhe'ntyaṃ viṣṇuṃ jīvātmakaṃ tathā|
garttākārodarā seyaṃ prakṛtirgarbhabhāginī|| 8.13 ||
jñātavyā sādhakenaivaṃ parāṃ siddhimabhīpsatā|
gāyatrī kuṇḍarūpeyaṃ caturviṃśatidhā sthitā|| 8.14 ||
tārātmakaḥ pumān viṣṇuradhiṣṭhātā'tra kīrtitaḥ|
nābhiguhyāntaraṃ vidyādagnisthānaṃ tu dehinām|| 8.15 ||
tatraivādhīyate garbhastatra homaśca siddhaye|
caturviṃśāṅgulaṃ kuṇḍaṃ gāyatrīsaṃkhyayā smṛtam|| 8.16 ||
caturviṃśatitattvāni dvihaste dvyaṅgulāni tu|
svarāḥ pañcadaśādhastād yoniḥ pañcadaśāṅgulā|| 8.17 ||
ṣoḍaśaḥ savisargo'tra nābhyante saṃvyavasthitaḥ|
saiva śaktiḥ parā viṣṇoḥ paramāmṛtavāhinī|| 8.18 ||
agnīṣomātmikā divyā sarvaṃ jñeyaṃ ca tanmayam|
sāgnirūpeṇa saṃhāraṃ somarūpeṇa codayam|| 8.19 ||
karoti binduyugmasthā nityameṣā madicchayā|
brahmalokaṃ sthitā bhittvā gatā mūrdhnā mamālayam|| 8.20 ||
pitṛyānaṃ ca vāme syād devayānaṃ ca dakṣiṇe|
madhyame brahmaṇo mārgo muktaye saivamīritā|| 8.21 ||
sā'gnirūpā vrajatyūrdhvaṃ somarūpā vrajatyadhaḥ|
vāmadakṣiṇato hitvā sthitā vyāpya jagattrayam|| 8.22 ||
iḍā ca piṅgalā nāḍyau vāmadakṣiṇapārśvayoḥ|
suṣumnordhvagatā nāḍī mokṣamārgastu sa smṛtaḥ|| 8.23 ||
tatra cittaṃ samādhāya dharmadharmavivarjitam|
sarvarogavinirmukto layamāyātyasaṃśayam|| 8.24 ||
evaṃ kuṇḍaśarīraṃ tu jñātavyaṃ mātṛkāmayam|
līyate viśvamatreti kuṇḍaṃ tadviśvakuṇḍanāt|| 8.25 ||
tasmāt kuṇḍe'tra hotavyaṃ vidhidṛṣṭena vartmanā|
susaṃskṛte yathā kṣetre phalaṃ prāpnoti karṣakaḥ|| 8.26 ||
hutabhuk ca tathā jñeyaḥ saṃskṛtaḥ phalado nṛṇām|
yathaivājyasya saṃskāraḥ kartavyo'gnestathaiva ca|| 8.27 ||
hotavyaṃ saṃskṛte tasmādagnau nāsaṃskṛte budhaiḥ|
śiṣyasyaiva tu saṃskāro vahnerājyasya vā'tra yaḥ|| 8.28 ||
nāgnyarthastattvatastasmādiṣṭo yāgeṣu sarvathā|
mathitaṃ maṇijaṃ vā'tha śrotriyāgārajaṃ tu vā|| 8.29 ||
śarāveṇāgnimānīya tamastreṇa tu śodhayet|
saṃhāramudrayā tasya jīvamātmani yojayet|| 8.30 ||
śayyāṃ tu cintayitvā''dau dharmādharmādisaṃyutām|
varāstaraṇasaṃchannāṃ dhūpāmodādhivāsitām|| 8.31 ||
sugandhakusumākīrṇāṃ nānādravyaiśca śobhitām|
dhyāyellakṣmīṃ ca vāgrūpāṃ bhūṣitāṃ navayauvanām|| 8.32 ||
sābhilāṣāmṛtusnātāṃ phullapaṅgajadhāriṇīm|
evaṃ ca cintayet saumyaṃ sadā prahasitānanam|| 8.33 ||
ratyarthinaṃ samāsaktadṛṣṭiṃ devīmukhāmbuje|
evaṃrūpāvubhau dhyātvā gandhapuṣpaiḥ prapūjayet|| 8.34 ||
devasya suklamadhyasthamagnibījaṃ tu cintayet|
garbhāśayapraviṣṭaṃ tu tadraktena samanvitam|| 8.35 ||
devaṃ visarjayet paścāt garbhādhāne kṛte tathā|
kuṇḍabhūtāṃ tato devīṃ vahnibhūtaṃ ca taṃ smaret|| 8.36 ||
tatpuṃsavanasīmantajātanāmāśanakriyāḥ|
hṛdayādibhiraṃṣṭāṣṭa hutvā''jyaṃ kalpayet kramāt|| 8.37 ||
vaiṣaḍantena mantreṇa plutāntena yathāvidhi|
pṛthak pūrṇāhutiṃ dadyād gandhapuṣpaiśca pūjayet|| 8.38 ||
praṇavastu sruvo jñeyo mātṛkā srugiti dvayam|
puruṣaprakṛtitvena vijñātavyaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ|| 8.39 ||
ratnimātraṃ sruvaṃ kuryād vartulaṃ dvyaṅgulaṃ mukham|
goṣpadaṃ tu yathā magnamalpapaṅke tathā bhavet|| 8.40 ||
vartulānantaragranthiviṣkambheṇāṅgulo bhavet|
daṇḍastu vartulākāro granthivakrādivarjitaḥ|| 8.41 ||
kramāt tanūkṛtāgro'dho nāḍī syāccaturaṅgulā|
svayaṃ nipatitaḥ śuṣko dagdho bhagnaśca dantinā|| 8.42 ||
duṣṭadeśabhavo vakraḥ sphuṭitaśca sakoṭaraḥ|
sakṣataḥ kṛmijuṣṭo'nyavṛkṣajaścānyatomukhaḥ|| 8.43 ||
sruvaḥ syādabhicārāya srugvā tasmānna kārayet|
kṣīravṛkṣodbhavaḥ śāntyai khādiraḥ sarvakāmadaḥ|| 8.44 ||
brahmavarcasakṛd brāhmo juhūrbrāhmī sadā bhavet|
ṛgvedādyāstrayo vedā brahmādyāścaiva devatāḥ|| 8.45 ||
bhūrādayo'gnayo'vasthāḥ savanānyakṣarāṇi ca|
praṇavāṃśakramāddaṇḍagranthivṛtteṣu kalpayet|| 8.46 ||
bāhumātrāṃ srucaṃ saptaṣaṭpañcāṅgulavistarām|
kārayenmadhyato vṛttaṃ tribhāgena ca khātayet|| 8.47 ||
tiryagūrdhvaṃ samaṃ tasya bahirardhaṃ tu śodhayet|
aṅgulasya caturthāśaṃ śaiṣārdhe'rdhaṃ tathā'ntataḥ|| 8.48 ||
khātasya mekhalāṃ ramyāṃ śeṣārdhena tu kārayet|
kalādhyardhāṅgulāyāmaṃ kaṇṭhaṃ tryaṃśaikavistṛtam|| 8.49 ||
caturaṅgulavistāraṃ mukhaṃ pañcāṅgulaṃ tathā|
samāyāmaṃ tu kartavyaṃ śubhaṃ haṃsamukhākṛti|| 8.50 ||
dhārākulyā ca tanmadhye vṛttagartapraveśinī|
kaṇṭhe kanīyasī nāhaṃ suṣiraṃ tasya kalpayet|| 8.51 ||
dhārā syāttadadhobhāge daṇḍamūle ca yuktitaḥ|
śeṣaṃ daṇḍaṃ suvṛttaṃ ca krameṇāgraṃ tanūkṛtam|| 8.52 ||
prādakṣiṇyād bilasyāntarnyastavyāḥ ṣoḍaśa svarāḥ|
daṇḍe'nye koṇato'ntaḥsthā bilapārśvāgragāḥ pare|| 8.53 ||
tayā pūrṇā'tra dātavyā sruvasampuṭaguptayā|
sruveṇa kevalenāpi homastatsahitena ca|| 8.54 ||
srucā no sruvayā? tāraḥ kevalo'pi hi siddhidaḥ|
sṛṣṭyartho'yaṃ sruvastasmāt tenāsīnastu homayet|| 8.55 ||
sthityarthaṃ sruk tayā pūrṇāṃ tiṣṭhanneva tu dāpayet|
gāyatrī tu juhūrbrāhmī syāccaturviśadṅgulā|| 8.56 ||
daṇḍāgre'syāṃ mukhāgre'ntargaṇḍayorājyavartmani|
hṛdayādīni netrāntānyaṅgāni parikalpayet|| 8.57 ||
tāropastīrṇayā nityaṃ tayā pakvaṃ tu homayet|
prāṇāpānasvarūpau ca sruksruvau kathitāvubhau|| 8.58 ||
kartavyau nābhisaṃlagnau prāṇasthānaṃ tu tadviduḥ|
dhūmastu nāsikā jñeyā pārśvayoḥ karṇacakṣuṣī|| 8.59 ||
pūrvāparaṃ lalāṭāsyaṃ vahnisthānamidaṃ bhavet|
kṛtvollekhādi gāyatryā paridhāyārcite'nale|| 8.60 ||
pūrvavat paṅkajaṃ dhyātvā sarvatattvānyanukramāt|
madhye janārdanaṃ dhyāyet sūryakoṭisamaprabham|| 8.61 ||
āhutīnāṃ sahasreṇa tarpayet taṃ śatena vā|
daśa pūrṇāhutīstatra mūlamantreṇa homayet|| 8.62 ||
daśāṃśenetarāṃścaiva tarpayeccarūṇaiva vā|
naityake tu vidhirhyeṣa kartavyo dīkṣitairdvijaiḥ|| 8.63 ||
śoṣadāhāmṛtaplāvakāṭhinyaparikalpanaiḥ|
tattadbījakṛtaiḥ śodhyaṃ vahnerāyatanaṃ sadā|| 8.64 ||
yatparistīryate darbhairyatpraṇītā nidhīyate|
yadbrahmā kalpyate cātra sarvaṃ tadvighnaśāntaye|| 8.65 ||
snigdhe pradakṣiṇavarte susamiddhe hutāśane|
vidhūme lelihāne ca hotavyaṃ karmasiddhaye|| 8.66 ||
alpatejāśca rūkṣaśca śuklaḥ kṛṣṇaśca yo'nalaḥ|
bhūlehī viṣphuliṅgī ca durgandhaśca na śasyate|| 8.67 ||
rūpādibhedataḥ kālī karālī ca manojavā|
lohitā cārdhadhūmrā ca jvālā cātha sphuliṅaginī|| 8.68 ||
viśvarūpeti saptaitā jihvā jñeyā havirbhujaḥ|
tābhirasya mukhaṃ pūrṇaṃ vidīrṇa saptajihvakam|| 8.69 ||
lelihyamānamujjvālaṃ sadā dhyeyaṃ juhūṣatā|
karṣārdhaṃ juhuyādājyamannaṃ grāsārdhamātrakam|| 8.70 ||
śuktimātrāhutīrdadhnaḥ kṣīrasya madhunastathā|
yavataṇḍulaśālīnāṃ prasṛtiḥ pṛthukasya ca|| 8.71 ||
lājānāṃ muṣṭimātraṃ tu phalānāṃ svapramāmataḥ|
bhakṣyāṇāmabhayāmātraṃ mūlānāṃ khaṇḍanatrayam|| 8.72 ||
dūrvāṇāṃ ca trayaṃ sāgraṃ saktūnāṃ karṣamātrakam|
tilānāṃ sarṣapāṇāṃ ca mudgānāṃ caiva pañcakam|| 8.73 ||
ekaikaṃ caiva hotavyaṃ puṣpāṇāṃ samidhāṃ tathā|
caturgṛhītamāpūrya sruveṇājyaṃ mahāsrucā|| 8.74 ||
purṇāhutistu dātavyā nityamacchinnadhārayā|
aratnimātramidhmākhyaṃ samitpūgaṃ ghṛtāplutam|| 8.75 ||
pālāśaṃ yugapannityaṃ hotavyamavikampitam|
sruveṇājyamupastīrya juhvāṃ piṇḍadvayaṃ caroḥ|| 8.76 ||
ghṛtābhighāritaṃ pakvaṃ yat sādyantasruvāhuti|
evamekaikapiṇḍaṃ tu punardvirabhighāritam|| 8.77 ||
pradhānāhutihomānte hotavyaṃ sviṣṭakṛd bhavet|
anyat sarvaṃ ghṛtābhyaktaṃ dakṣiṇasya karasya tu|| 8.78 ||
juhuyāddevatīrthena kaniṣṭhāmūlavartinā|
sruveṇaiva dravadravyaṃ dadhikṣīrādi sarvadā|| 8.79 ||
hotavyaṃ vidhinā sarvamanyathā doṣamāpnuyāt|
pūrvaṃ triḥ kṣālitaiḥ śālitaṇḍulairamalaiścarum|| 8.80 ||
yantreṇāgnāvadiśritya śrapayed vyajanānilaiḥ|
saṃskṛtya vidhinā cājyaṃ darbhaiḥ sammṛjya ca sruvam|| 8.81 ||
nidhāya paridhīnadbhiḥ pariṣicyāgnimarcayet|
idhmāghārājyabhāgeṣu huteṣvāhūya devatām|| 8.82 ||
pakvamanyacca hotavyaṃ samidādi yathākramam|
āghāravat kramo hyeṣa kartavyaḥ siddhimīpsatā|| 8.83 ||
ādhāyāgniṃ paristīrya saṃskṛtyābhyarcya ca kramāt|
hotavyamakhilairdravyairayamāpūrviko vidhiḥ|| 8.84 ||
homaśeṣaṃ samāpyātha vidhinodvāsya devatām|
svaśaktyā dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādevaṃ siddhyanti siddhayaḥ|| 8.85 ||
sāmānyena samākhyātamagnikāryamidaṃ mayā|
viseṣatastu kartavyaṃ tatra tatraiva vakṣyate|| 8.86 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāmagnikāryo'ṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<9>
|| atha navamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepānmaṇḍalaṃ sārvakāmikam|
yad jñātvā munayaḥ samyak parāṃ siddhimito gatāḥ|| 9.1 ||
yāgabhūmiṃ parīkṣyādau sarvadoṣavivarjitām|
śalyairvimocitāṃ yatnāt samāṃ pūrvottaraplavām|| 9.2 ||
śodhayitvā yathānyāyaṃ maṇḍapaṃ tatra kārayet|
caturaśraṃ caturdvāramekadvāramathāpi vā|| 9.3 ||
toraṇadhvajasaṃyuktaṃ vitānādivibhūṣitam|
samaṅgalāṅkurādarśalājakumbhāṣṭamaṅgalam|| 9.4 ||
agnyāyatanasaṃyuktamādarśasamavedikam|
saṃskṛtya mārjanālepagavyagandhākṣatādibhiḥ|| 9.5 ||
sūtrapātaṃ tataḥ kuryāt digvibhāgānurūpataḥ|
prākpaścimāyate sūtre madhyaṅkobhayapāśvagau|| 9.6 ||
kṛtvā'ṅkau lāñchayet tābhyāṃ matsyau dakṣiṇasaumyayoḥ|
tayośca sūtramāsphālya lāñchayed dikṣi mānataḥ|| 9.7 ||
dvābhyāṃ dvābhyāmathāṅkābhyāṃ lāñchayet koṇamatsyakān|
bāhusūtrāṇi cāsphālya caturaśraṃ prasādhayet|| 9.8 ||
samaṃ ṣoḍaśadhā bhaṅktvā sūtrāṇyāsphālya bhadrakam|
ālikhenmaṇḍalaṃ tatra caturaśraṃ suśobhanam|| 9.9 ||
pañcavarṇaiścaturdvāraṃ sitābjaṃ tat sapīṭhakam|
caturbhirnavakaiḥ padmaṃ pīṭhaṃ paṅkyaikayā bahiḥ|| 9.10 ||
dvābhyāṃ vīthīṃ tathā dvārāṇyabjasthānaṃ tu vartayet|
tadardhe dvādaśāṃśone caturdhāvartiteṃ'śakaiḥ|| 9.11 ||
pṛthivyādīni tattvāni karṇikādiṣu kalpayet|
karṇikāṃ kesarān sandhīn dalānyagrāṇi ca kramāt|| 9.12 ||
koṇasūtraṃ samāsphālya kuryādaṣṭadalaṃ yathā|
kṣipteṣu sandhisūtreṣu dalasandhyantareṇa tu|| 9.13 ||
dalānyagrāṇi mānena pārśvasthena ca vartayet|
pīṭhe tribhiścaturbhiśca pādagātrāṇi kalpayet|| 9.14 ||
dve'ntarbahiśca catvāri dvāre dvāre tu mārjayet|
trīṇyekaṃ caiva śobhārthamupaśobhārthamanyathā|| 9.15 ||
ṣaḍbhiḥ koṇaṃ latāpatravitānairvīthirujjvalā|
ekahastaṃ dvihastaṃ ca caturhastaṃ ca bhadrakam|| 9.16 ||
tato'dhikaṃ tu cakrābjamaṣṭahastāntamiṣyate|
dvādaśāratnikā vedirdaśāṣṭāratnireva vā|| 9.17 ||
tasyā dvādaśabhāgena madhyato'kṣaṃ prakalpayet|
nābhiṃ ca tāvatīṃ kṣetramarāṇāṃ dviguṇaṃ bahiḥ|| 9.18 ||
nābhivannemivīthyośca kramāt kṣetrāṇi kalpayet|
akṣabhimiṃ tridhā kṛtvā karṇikādi yathākramam|| 9.19 ||
vartayennābhisaṃsthāni dalānyagrāṇi kalpayet|
trinābhi dvādaśāraṃ taccakrābjaṃ śubhavīthikam|| 9.20 ||
kalpayed vidiśo'rasthā diśo'rāntaragocarāḥ|
tulyamevobhayormānaṃ sarvatra parikalpayet|| 9.21 ||
araṃ dvādaśadhā kṛtvā sūtrāṇyūrdhvagatāni ca|
mūlāgrayorarāḥ sthūlā vṛttarūpāśca madhyataḥ|| 9.22 ||
pipīlikodarāḥ kāryā viparītā yavodarāḥ|
nemikṣetraṃ dvidhā kṛtvā prathayaścādharottarāḥ|| 9.23 ||
catirviṃśatidhā kāryaste cārāgrānyasandhayaḥ|
bahirāvaraṇaṃ cātra nemimānena kalpayet|| 9.24 ||
tatra sthānāni kalpyāni dikpālānāṃ yathākramam|
caturaśrāṇi sarvāṇi tālamātrāṇi kalpayet|| 9.25 ||
bahirdvārādi kartavyaṃ caturaśraṃ yathoditam|
evaṃ maṇḍalāmālikhya rajobhiḥ pūrayet kramāt|| 9.26 ||
sitaraktāsitāpītaśyāmairevātra pañcabhiḥ|
sarvatra karṇikā pītā sitānyabjadalāni ca|| 9.27 ||
pṛthulāruṇamadhyāni pītānyeva yathākramam|
kesarāṇyacchabindvagrāṇyujjvalāni prakalpayet|| 9.28 ||
dalāntarāṇi śyāmāni vyomabāhyo'śrayo'ruṇāḥ|
sitā dharmādayaḥ śyāmāstathā'dharmādayaḥ smṛtāḥ|| 9.29 ||
dvārāṇi tu sitānyeva caturdikṣu prakalpayet|
uttarādīni sarvāṇi viprādīnāṃ yathākramam|| 9.30 ||
śobhā raktopaśobhā tu pītā nīlaṃ ca koṇakam|
bhadrake'nyadyathāśobhaṃ sarvavarṇaistu kalpayet|| 9.31 ||
cakrābje'pi tathā padmaṃ tridhā nābhimathālikhet|
sitaraktāsitaiḥ pītāḥ sarve'rāḥ sitasandhayaḥ|| 9.32 ||
prathayaḥ śuklakṛṣṇāḥ syurvyatyayāt pītasandhayaḥ|
nānāvarṇāni kāryāṇi dikpānāṃ svastikāni tu|| 9.33 ||
pītācchapāṭalaśyāmakṛṣṇarekhāstu tadbahiḥ|
sitaraktāsitāstisro bhadrake vihitāḥ kramāt|| 9.34 ||
dvārādi bhadrake yattat cakrābje'pi bahistathā|
kalpanīyaṃ prayatnena dīkṣāyāṃ deśikottamaiḥ|| 9.35 ||
yena rūpeṇa yallekhyaṃ vidhivaccakramaṇḍalam|
tadvarṇenaiva rajasā tattatpūraṇamatra tu|| 9.36 ||
ratnairoṣadhibhirlohargandhairbījaiśca dhātubhiḥ|
patraiśca yājñikairvṛkṣai rajāṃsyetāni kalpayet|| 9.37 ||
muktyai nyāso'pasavyena devatīrthena tu śriyai|
sarvakāmasamṛddhyarthaṃ kartavyo'ṅgulibhistu saḥ|| 9.38 ||
granthivakrakṛśasthūlanimnonnatavimiśraṇaiḥ|
chidraiśca bahavo doṣā rekhācchedo na sarvathā|| 9.39 ||
pramādādyatra śiṣyāṇāṃ maṇḍalasya tu lekhane|
granthyādi syādatandrī tu samaye kuśalo guruḥ|| 9.40 ||
karṇikākesarādīni lalitāni prayojayet|
sakṛdarpaṇamātreṇa na punaḥ parivartanaiḥ|| 9.41 ||
karṇikāmucchritāṃ kuryāt patrarekhāśca sarvataḥ|
kaniṣṭhāmadhyamāṅguṣṭhamitā rekhāstridhocchritāḥ|| 9.42 ||
mukhye madhye'dhame'bjādikramādūhyaśca tatkṣayaḥ|
tanmadhyeṣu rajonyāsastattatpādocchrito bhavet|| 9.43 ||
granthyādiparihāreṇa prayatnād deśikottamaḥ|
sarvatra samarūpāṇi rajāṃsi vikiret kramāt|| 9.44 ||
kuḍye paṭe ca tactakraṃ likhitaṃ mānayogataḥ|
pūjanīyaṃ gṛhe nityaṃ kṣudrabhūtādiśāntaye|| 9.45 ||
kāle svayaṃ tu sampaśyenna tat sādhāraṇo janaḥ|
tasyānudhyānamātreṇa sarvameno viśīryate|| 9.46 ||
tasyāntike na tiṣṭhanti vetālā brahmarākṣasāḥ|
idaṃ cakramupārūḍho viṣṇuḥ sarvajagatprabhuḥ|| 9.47 ||
pūjyo bhaktaiḥ prayatnena sadā sarvārthasiddhaye|
asambhave dvihastādimānenāpyarcanādiṣu|| 9.48 ||
cakrābjaṃ vartayedvidvānalpaṃ nānalpasambhave|
asambhave'pi sthānāni nemyantāni na hāpayet|| 9.49 ||
bahirāvaraṇaṃ tvalpe kalpanīyaṃ yathepsitam|
varṇāḥ sitāruṇāḥ pītāḥ pṛṣṇāḥ śāntyādidevatāḥ|| 9.50 ||
yojyā yugeṣu vedeṣu varṇeṣu ca yathākramam|
yugāntarānulomāṅkarūpaṃ śyāmaṃ tu pañcamam|| 9.51 ||
pañcavarṇamayaṃ viśvaṃ tanmātrā mūrtibhedataḥ|
viṣayendriyabhedācca pañcadhedamavasthitam|| 9.52 ||
nādarūpeṇa vijñeyaṃ vyāptamādyakṣareṇa tu|
varṇādikalpanātītaṃ tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam|| 9.53 ||
vyāpinī tu parā śaktirarūpā sarvatomukhī|
avimuktastayā devo draṣṭavyaḥ sādhakaiḥ sadā|| 9.54 ||
avarmā sā samākhyātā sarvavarṇā ca sādhakaiḥ|
tadbījaṃ sā parā māyā prakṛtiḥ sā guṇātmikā|| 9.55 ||
ekaiva sā parā sūkṣmā guṇabhedāt tridhā bhavet|
tadbhedāddevabhedo'pi brahmopendraharātmakaḥ|| 9.56 ||
pañcadhā sā bhavet paścād yathā pūrvaṃ mayoditam|
tadbhedānuvidhāyitvaṃ devasya śrutidarśanāt|| 9.57 ||
kalpitaṃ munibhirbhedāstvasaṃkhyeyā guṇodbhavāḥ|
puruṣaṃ maṇḍalaṃ vidyāt padmaṃ hṛdayamasya tu|| 9.58 ||
bāhvantaragataṃ madhyaṃ nābhimūle'bjanālakam|
pade dve paścimadvāre jñātavye dakṣiṇottare|| 9.59 ||
padmanālaṃ navacchidraṃ vimalādikalāśrayam|
tasya mūlaṃ bhavet sūkṣmo bījarūpo janārdanaḥ|| 9.60 ||
ādhārabhūtaṃ sarvasya mahimni sve vyavasthitam|
padmamūlaṃ vijānīyādyato'kārādayo'bhavan|| 9.61 ||
marutaḥ kaṇṭakā nālaṃ granthirguṇamayo mahān|
buddhibhedā dalānyaṣṭau dharmādharmādayastataḥ|| 9.62 ||
nālānte'haṃkṛto'nantastanmātrendriyabhūtabhṛt|
padmo'vyaktamakārādibhedo'rkendvagnimaṇḍalam|| 9.63 ||
buddhigarvamanomātrā dakṣiṇādidalāṣṭakam|
ityanye vimalādyaṣṭaśaktijaṃ cāpare viduḥ|| 9.64 ||
aṇḍamadhyagataṃ padmaṃ piśvasyāyatanaṃ mahat|
māyāṇḍādhaḥkapālasthā vidyā cordhvaṃ vyavasthitā|| 9.65 ||
purītataṃ hṛdabjasya kalpayet prativāraṇam|
tadbāhyataḥ purākāro vairājo deva ucyate|| 9.66 ||
pītācchāruṇakṛṣṇeṣu śyāme ca kṣmādayaḥ sthitāḥ|
vasavo'bjadalāgrasthā rudrādityāśca kesarāḥ|| 9.67 ||
maruto dalasandhisthā reṇavo grahatārakāḥ|
dalāntarāgrarekhāsu saritaḥ sāgarāstathā|| 9.68 ||
merustu karṇikāntastho bījeṣu munayaḥ sthitāḥ|
sūryendvagnigato viṣṇustānatītya sthitastviṣā|| 9.69 ||
tastvarūpaṃ paraṃ nityaṃ yaḥ paśyati sa paśyati|
ātmānamantarātmānaṃ paramātmānamityapi|| 9.70 ||
tasya turyaṃ paraṃ rūpaṃ yaḥ paśyati sa eva saḥ|
yakṣādyāḥ kaṇṭakā nāle romāṇyapsarasaḥ smṛtāḥ|| 9.71 ||
sūtraṃ prajāpatiḥ patramūle prāṇādivāyavaḥ|
cakramaṇḍalamadhyastha ātmā sarveśvaro hariḥ|| 9.72 ||
niṣkalastu samākhyātaḥ parame vyomni saṃsthitaḥ|
evaṃ yo vetti devaṃ taṃ maṇḍalāntargataṃ tathā|| 9.73 ||
sa hitvā vaiṣṇavīṃ māyāmāpnuyāt paramaṃ padam|
avidhāne'pi yo devaṃ maṇḍalāntaḥ sthitaṃ yajet|| 9.74 ||
paśyed vā tena deveśaḥ sākṣād dṛṣṭo bhaved dhruvam|
maṇḍale'tra bhavennityaṃ sānnidhyaṃ sarvarūpiṇaḥ|| 9.75 ||
tasmāt tīrthādipuṇyebhyo viśiṣṭaṃ maṇḍale'rcanam|

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ navamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<10>
|| atha daśamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepād dīkṣāpūjādyanugraham|
śiṣyādhikārasiddhyarthaṃ nirvāṇārthaṃ ca yoginām|| 10.1 ||
dīyante siddhayaḥ sarvāḥ kṣīyante granthayo'pyataḥ|
dīkṣātvamevaṃ dīkṣāyā dharmādharmahṛdātmanaḥ|| 10.2 ||
vasantagrīṣmayordīkṣā kāryā vā kārttikādiṣu|
śaradāṣāḍhayoścokto varṇajānāmanugrahaḥ|| 10.3 ||
jātiśaucaguṇān pūrvaṃ parīkṣya svayamādarāt|
śuddhān gururvijānīyāt srīśūdrāṃstu viśeṣataḥ|| 10.4 ||
te tu bhaktiṃ tato jñātvā kartavyā parameśvare|
devavad bhaktimanto'sya kuryuḥ śuśrūṣaṇaṃ ciram|| 10.5 ||
bhagavaṃstvatprasādena saṃsārārṇavatāraṇam|
icchāmaścaihikīṃ siddhiṃ viśeṣeṇa tapodhana!|| 10.6 ||
iti vijñāpya santoṣya dānānugamasāntvanaiḥ|
tadicchayoktasambhārānāhareyuryathābalam|| 10.7 ||
dravyāṇyānāyya saṃśodhya pūrvodyuradhivāsya ca|
dvādaśyāṃ dīkṣayecchiṣyāniṣṭvā devavidhānataḥ|| 10.8 ||
devālayanadītīrthagoṣṭhārāmādisannidhau|
brāhmaṇādivibhāgena yāgasthānaṃ prakalpayet|| 10.9 ||
dīkṣārthinastu yāvantaḥ sambhavantyatra vaiṣṇavāḥ|
te sarve samamevātra yaterannātmaśaktitaḥ|| 10.10 ||
yatno'rtho vā'dhiko yasya phalaṃ tasyādhikaṃ tathā|
vittaśāṭhyaṃ tu yaḥ kuryānnarake sa pated dhruvam|| 10.11 ||
vaiṣṇavairabhyanujñāto dīkṣitaiḥ kuśalaiḥ saha|
snātvā''camya śucirbhūtvā kṛtinityaḥ svalaṃkṛtaḥ|| 10.12 ||
yāgārthaṃ kalpitāṃ bhūmimācāryaḥ svayamāviśet|
sañcintya manasā devaṃ sakalaṃ maṇḍalātmakam|| 10.13 ||
saṃskṛtya vidhinā vediṃ kalaśanyāsamārabhet|
nṛsiṃhajaptān siddhārthān phaḍantena vikīrya tu|| 10.14 ||
mūlena vikirān japtvā pañcagavyaṃ tu sādhayet|
gomūtraṃ praṇavenaiva visargeṇa tu gomayam|| 10.15 ||
bindvantena payo dadyāddadhi dīrgheṇa viṣṇunā|
ghṛtamādyena śuddhena viṣṇubījena pūjayet|| 10.16 ||
gomūtrād gomayaṃ tvardhaṃ kṣīramaṣṭaguṇaṃ tataḥ|
dadhi saptaguṇaṃ dadyādājyaṃ caiva caturguṇam|| 10.17 ||
tribījena kuśaiḥ prokṣya mūlena vikirān kṣipet|
trireva prāṅmukhastiṣṭhaṃstaṃ dhyātvottānapāṇinā|| 10.18 ||
pīṭhanyāsakramādīśe kalaśaṃ karkarīyutam|
saratnaṃ nirvraṇaṃ ramyaṃ lohajaṃ sūtraveṣṭitam|| 10.19 ||
pūrayitvā'rcayed vidvān gandhapuṣpairyathākramam|
dharmādharmādipīṭhasthaṃ paṅkajaṃ kalaśe nyaset|| 10.20 ||
kṣādimūrtiśiphānālasaraḥ kaṇṭakarandhrakam|
dharmādyantaḥsthayuggranthisādharmyādinapuṃsakam|| 10.21 ||
kādyaisribhisribhiḥ patraiḥ karṇikāntaiḥ samanvitam||
visargabindudīptāgrapūrvādisvarakesaram|| 10.22 ||
tārāṃśatritayopetamātmavidyāparakramāt|
nādaśaktiyutaṃ madhye tatrāvāhyārcayet prabhum|| 10.23 ||
vardhanyāṃ cāsane siddhe tasmādāvāhanaṃ bhavet|
ājye'rghye ca praṇītāyāṃ sruksruveṣu viśeṣataḥ|| 10.24 ||
pañcagavye carau kuṇḍe maṇḍale viṣṇuhastake|
pūjayitvā tathā sāṅgaṃ kalaśasthaṃ tu keśavam|| 10.25 ||
agrato'streṇa vardhanyā maṇḍapasya samantataḥ|
acchinnāṃ pātayed dhārāmīśādyantaṃ pradakṣiṇam|| 10.26 ||
paścāt tenaiva mārgeṇa sādhakaḥ kalaśaṃ nayet|
saṃhṛtya vikirān sarvān vikīrṇān pātragaiḥ saha|| 10.27 ||
sarvadhānyasthitaṃ kumbhaṃ pūjayed vardhanīṃ tathā|
navenāṃśukayugmena veṣṭayet taṃ sitena tu|| 10.28 ||
ghaṭikāṃ ca tathaikena rakṣāṃ cāstreṇa kalpayet|
vikiredastrajaptaiśca sarvadhānyaiḥ samantataḥ|| 10.29 ||
sammṛjya mūrtibhiścāgnau saṃskṛte śrapayeccarum|
mūlena mūrtibhiścāgnau saṃskṛte śrapayeccarum|| 10.30 ||
kṣālitājyāktasakṣīrānukhāmāropya taṇḍulān|
kṣālitān samupastīrṇān nyasyāloḍyāvaghaṭya ca|| 10.31 ||
upastīryodagudvāsya kṣālitaṃ prokṣya pūjayet|
kuryād bhasmordhvapuṇḍraṃ ca tatpārśve'ṣṭākṣareṇa tu|| 10.32 ||
upastīrya śubhe pātre kṣiptaṃ pratyabhighārya ca|
abhighārya kṣataṃ cādyaṃ bhāgaṃ devāya kalpayet|| 10.33 ||
dvitīyaṃ kalaśasthāya vahnisthāya tṛtīyakam|
ghṛtāplutaṃ tu mūlena juhuyādāhutitrayam|| 10.34 ||
taccheṣeṇa baliṃ pītvā pañcagavyaṃ caturthakam|
praṇavena purā pītvā pañcagavyaṃ caturthakam|| 10.35 ||
bhāgaṃ śiṣyaiḥ sahāśnīyād guruḥ kāyaviśodhanam|
saptābhimantritaṃ prācyāṃ nikhātaṃ dvādaśāṅgulam|| 10.36 ||
bhakṣyaṃ tairdantakāṣṭhaṃ ca kṣīravṛkṣajamavraṇam|
śubhaṃ parīkṣayet tena śāntihomo'śubhe smṛtaḥ|| 10.37 ||
ācāntaḥ sakalo bhūtvā prārthayed gururacyutam|
pradakṣiṇamupāvṛtya namaskṛtya prasādya ca|| 10.38 ||
saṃsārārṇavamagnānāṃ paśūnāṃ pāśamuktaye|
tvameva śaraṇaṃ deva! sadā tvaṃ bhaktavatsala|| 10.39 ||
devadevānujānīhi prākṛtaiḥ pāśabandhanaiḥ|
pāśitān mokṣayiṣyāmi tvatprasādāt paśūnimān|| 10.40 ||
labdhānujñastu saṃśodhya dhāraṇābhirupoṣitān|
astreṇa prokṣya liṅgasthān paśūn svātmani yojayet|| 10.41 ||
samprokṣya taṃ punardehaṃ vātāgnidhṛtamagrataḥ|
bhasmakūṭamiva dhyātvā plāvayed vāriṇā'khilam|| 10.42 ||
udakaikanidhiṃ samyag dhyātvā saṃsāramaṇḍalam|
kalpayet pārthivīṃ śaktiṃ brahmāṇḍaikasvarūpiṇīm|| 10.43 ||
tanmātrāsambhṛtāṃ śubhrāṃ vṛttāṃ budbudasannibhām|
liṅge saṃkrāmite dhyāyet puruṣaṃ praṇavātmakam|| 10.44 ||
vibhaktendriyasaṃsthānaṃ buddhyā'bdenāṇḍabhedanam|
śakale rodasī madhye prajāpatimimaṃ smaret|| 10.45 ||
praṇavena punaḥ prokṣya sakalīkṛtya deśikaḥ|
svahaste sakalaṃ dhyāyenmaṇḍalādikamādarāt|| 10.46 ||
caturdvāraṃ puraṃ tacca brahmatīrthādyanukramāt|
madhye padmaṃ yathāproktaṃ patrāṇyaṅgulayaḥ smṛtāḥ|| 10.47 ||
karṇikā talamadhyaṃ tu parvāṇyasya tu kesarāḥ|
agnisthānaṃ ca madhye'tra tanmadhye keśavaḥ sthitaḥ|| 10.48 ||
maṇḍalatritayasyāntaḥ sūryakoṭisamaprabham|
taṃ dhyātvā pūjayenmadhye gandhapuṣpairyathākramam|| 10.49 ||
taṃ hastaṃ dāpayenmūrdhni śiṣyasya susamāhitaḥ|
haste viṣṇuḥ sthito yasmin viṣṇuhastastu sa smṛtaḥ|| 10.50 ||
janmāntarasahasraistu yat pāpaṃ sañcitaṃ purā|
sarvaṃ tat sparśamātreṇa vilayaṃ yātyasaṃśayam|| 10.51 ||
evamekaṃ bahūn vāpi janayed dhyānayogataḥ|
evaṃ saṃśuddhadehaṃ taṃ gandhapuṣpaiḥ prapūjayet|| 10.52 ||
karau sammṛjya mūlena netre baddhvā suvāsasā|
netramantreṇa medhāvī sadaśenāhatena tu|| 10.53 ||
śiṣyaṃ puṣpāñjalidharaṃ prāṅmukhaṃ tu praveśayet|
brāhmaṇādikrameṇaiva bahavaśced vivikṣavaḥ|| 10.54 ||
dvāraṃ tu dakṣiṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ maṇḍapasya praveśane|
uttarādīni catvāri viprādīnāṃ viśeṣataḥ|| 10.55 ||
purapraveśanaṃ kāryaṃ śiṣyakāmānurūpataḥ|
prajābalārthasaubhāgyaṃ ratnānnavijayastriyaḥ|| 10.56 ||
dhṛtimokṣāyurārogyaṃ paśuputrasukhādi ca|
pūrvādiṣu praveśe syāt karaṃ dattvā praveśayet|| 10.57 ||
kṛtapradakṣiṇaṃ śiṣyaṃ kramād vanditadikpatim|
devābhimukhamānīya prāḍmukhaṃ sthāpayet tataḥ|| 10.58 ||
tūṣṇīṃ sa guruṇoddiṣṭo yatavākkāyamānasaḥ|
jānudvayena bhūmiṃ tu viṣṭabhya natipūrvakam|| 10.59 ||
dhyātvā devaṃ hariṃ bhaktyā sūryakoṭisamaprabham|
lakṣmyā samanvitaṃ saumyamaṣṭabāhumalaṅkṛtam|| 10.60 ||
samāhitaḥ kṣipenmālyaṃ gurvadeśapratīkṣayā|
tannetrāvaraṇaṃ muktvā puṣpapātaṃ vilokayet|| 10.61 ||
puṣpapātānūrūpeṇa mūrtināmnā tamāhvayet|
amantramarcanaṃ tatra kārayed devamūrtiṣu|| 10.62 ||
devāntaṃ brāhmaṇasyoktaṃ varmāntaṃ kṣatriyasya tu|
guptāntaṃ nāma vaiśyasya śūdre dāsāntamuddiśet|| 10.63 ||
strīṇāṃ devīti nāma syāt sā mūrtistasya kāmadā|
dṛṣṭamātre praṇaśyanti maṇḍale pāpasañcayāḥ|| 10.64 ||
dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ kramād dattvā nivedyaṃ cātra kalpayet|
prakṛtyādipṛthivyantaṃ sādhibhūtādhidaivatam|| 10.65 ||
adhyātmasaṃhṛtaṃ sarvaṃ kramācchiṣyatanau nyaset|
dakṣiṇenopaveśyainaṃ darbhapāṇiṃ tu vāgyatam|| 10.66 ||
anvārabdhena hotavyamācāryeṇa vijānatā|
agnau sampātavidhinā tattvānāṃ tu śataṃ śatam|| 10.67 ||
dattvā pūrṇāhutiṃ sarvaṃ kumbheśāya nivedayet|
karaṇīṃ kartarīṃ cātha rajāṃsi ghaṭikāṃ tathā|| 10.68 ||
yaccānyadupayogi syāt sarvaṃ tad vāyugocare|
sāṅgena mūlamantreṇa parāmṛśyādhivāsayet|| 10.69 ||
tataḥ śayīta taiḥ śiṣyaiḥ kuśānāstīrya taṃ smaran|
duḥsvapnādiṣu śāntiḥ syānnānyathā siddhiraihikī|| 10.70 ||
prātaḥ snātvā visṛjyeśaṃ maṇḍale punararcayet|
pravālamuktābhāraṇahemapuṣpairmanoharaiḥ|| 10.71 ||
nivedyairvividhaiścāgniṃ santarpyāhūya tān kramāt|
pūrvavat kṛtadevārcānagre padmāsanasthitān|| 10.72 ||
samprokṣya sakalīkṛtya viṣṇuhastena ke spṛśet|
prakṛtyādipṛthivyantāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ dhyātvā'khilāṃ kramāt|| 10.73 ||
tanmātrabhūtāṃ taddehe jīvena samatāṃ gatām|
tāreṇātmani saṃyojya dhyātvā samarasīkṛtam|| 10.74 ||
jīvaṃ taddehakālāgniśaktiyonisamarpaṇāt|
garbhaṃ janmādhikāraṃ ca bhogaṃ nāśaṃ ca pañcabhiḥ|| 10.75 ||
aṣṭāṣṭāhutirbhirhutvā narake ca tathā punaḥ|
pātāle bhuvi śaṃśodhya sthāvaratvādijanmabhiḥ|| 10.76 ||
vilomaṃ śūdraviṭkṣatrabrāhmaṇatveṣu yojayet|
kriyāḥ ṣoḍaśa mūlena pākayajñādibhiḥ kramāt|| 10.77 ||
śodhayitvā'śvamedhāntaiḥ kalpayedantyamāśramam|
kṛtvā'ntyeṣṭiṃ piśācādiyonyaṣṭakavibhāgataḥ|| 10.78 ||
trisurāntaṃ kramānnītvā nāśayet karmabandhanam|
tataḥ samprokṣya taddehamāhutīnāṃ śatena tam|| 10.79 ||
sṛṣṭīśvaramudāsīnaṃ pūrṇāhutyā ca kalpayet|
mūlajaptena śuklena rajasā hṛdi tāḍayet|| 10.80 ||
viyuṅkṣvahuṃphaḍantena nāmnā tattvākṣarādinā|
viśleṣyāyāhihāmantabījādyāmantritena ca|| 10.81 ||
ādāya samatāṃ nītvā yuṅkṣvahuṃphaḍiti kramāt|
prakṛtyākṛtya tenaiva saṃhārāntena homayet|| 10.82 ||
vahnāvakhilatattvānāmālaye'vyākṛte harau|
līyamānaṃ kramāt sarvaṃ tattvādhvānaṃ smared budhaḥ|| 10.83 ||
śuddhaṃ pareṇa tattvena pūrṇāhutyā ca sandhayet|
evaṃ tattvāni saṃśodhya prakṛtiṃ paramātmani|| 10.84 ||
vilāpya jñānayogena dadyāt pūrṇāhutiṃ budhaḥ|
nirvāme prakṛtiṃ dagdhvā sandadhyāt pūrṇayā pare|| 10.85 ||
adhikāre samādadhyānnaraṃ prākṛtamīśvare|
iṣṭvā devamaśaktau tu sarvopakaraṇānvitam|| 10.86 ||
sadyo'dhivāsya dvādaśyāṃ dīkṣayet sthaṇḍile'pi vā|
aṣṭāṣṭāhutibhiḥ pūrvaṃ kramāt tattvāni śodhayet|| 10.87 ||
manaso vāsanā yeyaṃ sā mukteḥ paripanthinī|
tacchuddhyā śudhyati kṣetrī duṣṭayā dūṣyateca saḥ|| 10.88 ||
manaso vāsanocchedaḥ karmabandhavimocanāt|
nirbījakaraṇaṃ tasya śodhanaṃ samudāhṛtam|| 10.89 ||
guruṇā yojito nityaṃ yogī yogaṃ samabhyaset|
na sidhyati vinā tena yogo nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ|| 10.90 ||
dagdhāṅgaṃ visphuliṅgābhamākṛṣya sa

<11>
|| athaikādaśaḥ paṭala ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepād dīkṣitasyābhiṣecanam|
putrako deśiko vā'pi yenācāryatvamṛcchati|| 11.1 ||
prathamaṃ samaye dīkṣā tantrajñāne tato'rcane|
tisro hyetāḥ prayoktavyā dīkṣā dharmavivṛddhaye|| 11.2 ||
ālikhya maṇḍalaṃ pūrvaṃ parivārasamāyutam|
darśayecchiṣyamācāryo yamasau samayī smṛtaḥ|| 11.3 ||
maṇḍale devamārādhya puṣpāñjalipurassaram|
darśayed vidhinā yaṃ tu sa bhavet tantradīkṣitaḥ|| 11.4 ||
agnau hutvā'khilān mantrān pūjayitvā ca taṃ prabhum|
darśayed vidhinā yaṃ tu sa jñeyo deśiko'rcakaḥ|| 11.5 ||
putrako'dhītya tantrāṇi dvitīyaṃ mantramāpnuyāt|
tṛtīyaṃ pūjayed devamityevaṃ trividhaḥ kramaḥ|| 11.6 ||
ekenaiva prayogeṇa tisro dīkṣā hyanugrahe|
samayagranthapūjārthāḥ kartavyastattvavedinā|| 11.7 ||
pūrvaṃ kṛtvā tu saṃskāraṃ garbhādhānādikaṃ kramāt|
vratabandhāntamācāryastattvena parameṇa vai|| 11.8 ||
ekaikaṃ daśahomena tatastattvāni saṃharet|
vekārādīni tattvāni pañcabhūtātmakāni vai|| 11.9 ||
dhyātvā śiṣyaśarīre tu ṣaṣṭhaṃ kṣetrajñalakṣaṇam|
homena śodhayitvā'nte pūrṇayā yojayet pare|| 11.10 ||
pañcabhūtasvarūpeṇa tatra māyā vyavasthitā|
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu paramaṃ tatvamabaddhaṃ paramārthataḥ|| 11.11 ||
na svabhāvasya vicchittirbhavedagnyauṣṇyavad yataḥ|
tasmād ghaṭādivad baddho vyomavat paramaṃ padam|| 11.12 ||
evaṃ jñātvā tu medhāvī vyatiriktaṃ nirañjanam|
ghaṭavad yaḥ smṛto bandhastaṃ homena vināśayet|| 11.13 ||
pṛthivyādi samārabhya vekārādyaistu pañcabhiḥ|
ekaikaṃ śatahomena kramāt tattvāni śodhayet|| 11.14 ||
pañca pūrṇāhutīrhutvā bandhacchedaṃ ca bhāvayet|
kṣetrajñaṃ śatahomānte māyābandhavivarjitam|| 11.15 ||
dhyāyan pūrṇāhutiṃ dadyānmaktidīkṣeyamuttamā|
samayādivibhāgena karma yasya yadīritam|| 11.16 ||
tadevāsya punaḥ kāryaṃ prāyaścittavidhāvapi|
abde'bde'pi ca kartavyā dīkṣā śiṣyeṣvanugrahāt|| 11.17 ||
yāgairdvādaśabhiryastu dīkṣito'sau hariḥ svayam|
yaṃ tu śiṣyaṃ guṇopetamācāryaṃ kartumicchati|| 11.18 ||
tasyābhiṣecanaṃ kuryād vidhinā'nena mantravit|
rājño'bhiṣecanaṃ kuryād vidhinā'nena mantravit|| 11.19 ||
sādhakasyānapatyasya daridrasya ca rogiṇaḥ|
pūrvavanmaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā sarvalakṣamalakṣitam|| 11.20 ||
samabhyarcyātra deveśaṃ hutvā'gnau ca yathāvidhi|
kalaśān sthāpayedagre nirdoṣān sūtraveṣṭitān|| 11.21 ||
pañcaratnasamāyuktān sahiraṇyān sapallavān|
veṣṭitān vastrayugmaistu caturo dikṣvanukramāt|| 11.22 ||
madhyataḥ pañcamaṃ caiva sakūrcaṃ sāpidhānakam|
praṇavaṃ kalaśe pūrve sarvamantrātmakaṃ nyaset|| 11.23 ||
akāraṃ dakṣiṇe caiva sarvatantrātmakaṃ budhaḥ|
paścime viṣṇugāyatrīmadhikāramayīṃ tathā|| 11.24 ||
uttare'pi nyased viṣṇuṃ sarvagaṃ jñānavigraham|
madhyame vaiṣṇavaṃ bījaṃ śabdabrahma sanātanam|| 11.25 ||
tatastānarcayet sarvān gandhapuṣpākṣataiḥ kramāt|
yogādhikāratantrārthasarvavidyāsamanvitān|| 11.26 ||
yathoktamātṛkābjasthatārādijyotiṣo'khilān|
mantratantrādirūpeṇa dhyātvā devaṃ pṛthak kramāt|| 11.27 ||
sahasrāvartitaṃ kuryācchatāvartitameva vā|
ekaikaṃ kalaśaṃ tvevamācāryaḥ susamāhitaḥ|| 11.28 ||
tato homaṃ ca kurvīta yathāvat sādhakeśvaraḥ|
upasaṃhāramārgeṇa nirvāṇākhyaṃ yathoditam|| 11.29 ||
evaṃ yogavidhānena kṛtvā tatrādhivāsanam|
abhiṣekaṃ tataḥ samyak kuryād vai sādhakasya tu|| 11.30 ||
maṇḍapasyottare pārśve pūrvaṃ kṛtvā suśobhanām|
ekahastāṃ dvihastāṃ vā caturhastāṃ tu vedikām|| 11.31 ||
tālocchritāṃ catuḥstambhe maṇḍape chadanānvite|
vitānavastrasañchanne sragdāmādivibhūṣite|| 11.32 ||
alaṃkṛte yathāśobhaṃ lājakumbhaiścaturdiśam|
likhitvā svastikaṃ madhye caturaśraṃ suśobhanam|| 11.33 ||
sitena rajasā tatra śrīparṇīdrumasambhavam|
bhadrapīṭhaṃ tu saṃsthāpya caturaśraṃ navaṃ śubham|| 11.34 ||
anantaṃ sāmbujaṃ tasmin dharmādimayamarcayet|
tato'gre prāṅmukhaḥ sthitvā dhyātvā pīṭhagataṃ prabhum|| 11.35 ||
manasā pūjayitvā tu kāryamenaṃ nivedayet|
bhagavan! dīkṣitasyoktamācāryatve'bhiṣecanam|| 11.36 ||
tat kariṣyāmyanujñātastvayā sarvārthasiddhaye|
