labdhānujñastataḥ kuryādabhiṣekaṃ yathāvidhi|| 11.37 ||
sṛṣṭikrameṇa vai dhyātvā tattvādhvānaṃ subhāvitaḥ|
praveśayed guruḥ śiṣyaṃ prāṅmukhaṃ śuklavāsasam|| 11.38 ||
dhyātvā'nantāsanasthaṃ tamarcitaṃ viṣṇumavyayam|
ānīya kalaśānatra svāsu dikṣu niveśayet|| 11.39 ||
strībhiḥ susvarakaṇṭhābhirgīyamāne'tha maṅgale|
śaṅkhatūryaninādena nīrājanapurassaram|| 11.40 ||
tato'bhiṣecayet sārdhaṃ brahmaghoṣeṇa deśikaḥ|
pūrvoktaireva mantraistu dhyātvā mantrātmakaṃ jalam|| 11.41 ||
pratikumbhaṃ tathā'bhyarcya dhūpayitvā yathākramam|
caturbhiḥ pañcamenātha paraṃ jñānātmakena ca|| 11.42 ||
nīrājanaṃ punaḥ kṛtvā pūjayed gandhapuṣpakaiḥ|
paridhāpya nave vastre śobhane tamupāgatam|| 11.43 ||
ācāntamupaveśyāgre bṛsīṃ dattvā svayaṃ guruḥ|
so'pi praṇamya deveśaṃ tasyāmāsīta vāgyataḥ|| 11.44 ||
sakalīkṛtya mantraistaṃ pūjayet pūrvavat punaḥ|
yogapaṭṭakamuṣṇīṣaṃ cakraṃ caivāṅgulīyakam|| 11.45 ||
adhikāraṃ svakīyaṃ ca tattvaṃ cāsmai nivedayet|
dadyācca vaiṣṇavaṃ jñānaṃ saṃsāracchedakāraṇam|| 11.46 ||
śrāvayet samayāṃścaitāṃstantroktān deśikottamaḥ|
anugrāhyāstvayā śiṣyā vaiṣṇavāḥ śubhacāriṇaḥ|| 11.47 ||
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrā yoṣita eva vā|
anyadevaratā ye tu cāturvarṇyācca ye bahiḥ|| 11.48 ||
ḍāmbikāḥ pāpakarmāṇo devabrāhmaṇanindakāḥ|
teṣāmanugraho naiva kartavyo vaiṣṇava! tvayā|| 11.49 ||
samīpe tu gururyatra na kartavyā svatantratā|
bahuyojanamātre tu ācāryo yatra tiṣṭhati|| 11.50 ||
tatra dīkṣā na kartavyā anuktena tvayā'nagha!|
gurūpūjā ca kartavyā yathā viṣṇostathā tvayā|| 11.51 ||
vyākhyānādiṣu kāleṣu smartavyaśca sadā guruḥ|
na kadācit tvayā kāryamapramāṇaṃ gurorvacaḥ|| 11.52 ||
kṛte pramāde kartavyaṃ prāyaścittaṃ tadājñayā|
na kadācicca kartavyo lokodvegastvayānagha!|| 11.53 ||
vaiṣṇavānāṃ ca vaibhāṣyaṃ yacca loke'pi garhitam|
pratipattiśca vṛddhānāṃ kartavyā śāsratastvayā|| 11.54 ||
abhimāno na kartavyaḥ svapneṣvapi kadācana|
samayāśca tvayā vatsa! ācārāśca mayoditāḥ|| 11.55 ||
pālitavyāḥ prayatnena yāvajjīvaṃ mamājñayā|
ācāryo vaiṣṇave tantre tvaṃ bhava prāṇināṃ priyaḥ|| 11.56 ||
śiṣyāstvāmupasarpantu teṣu vartasva śāstravat|
nārthalobhena vartethā nāśayā na bhayena vā|| 11.57 ||
pañcarātre viśeṣeṇa devaṃ ca satataṃ smara|
ayanādiṣu kāleṣu yaṣṭavyo viṣṇuravyayaḥ|| 11.58 ||
guravaḥ pūjanīyāśca manovākkāyakarmabhiḥ|
vṛthaṣā hiṃsā na kartavyā bhartavyāśca svaśaktitaḥ|| 11.59 ||
dīnānāthāstathāndhāśca pūjyā viprāśca sarvadā|
udyuktaḥ sarvadā tiṣṭhennityakarmaṇyatandritaḥ|| 11.60 ||
svalpamahnā japaṃ kuryāt svalpamagnau ca homayet|
svalpaṃ vai dhyānamātiṣṭhet satrametat sadā guroḥ|| 11.61 ||
evamuktvā guruḥ śiṣyaṃ devāgrasthaṃ sucetasam|
tantraṃ caivākhilaṃ dadyāt sādhake mantrameva tu|| 11.62 ||
devasya purato gandhapuṣpākṣatayutairjalaiḥ|
culukaṃ pūrayitvā tu mantramuccārya tatkare|| 11.63 ||
pradadyāt sidhyatāṃ śīghraṃ taveṣṭaṃ mantrajaṃ phalam|
ityuktaḥ svayamutthāya kaॉtvā'cāryaṃ pradakṣiṇam|| 11.64 ||
āsane sthāpayitvā'sya pādau prakṣālya bhaktitaḥ|
toṣayed dhanadhānyena śaktibhaktivaśād gurum|| 11.65 ||
paritoṣya tu pāṇibhyāmupasaṅgṛhya pādayoḥ|
prārthya pūjaya devaṃ tvamityanujñāpayet gurum|| 11.66 ||
labdhānujñaḥ sa śiṣyo'pi tataḥ prabhṛti bhūjayet|
evaṃ tenānanujñāto yaḥ pūjāṃ kurute naraḥ|| 11.67 ||
na tasya pūjayā siddhiriti śāstrasya niścayaḥ|
tasmānmaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ devamācāryapūjitam|| 11.68 ||
dṛṣṭvā'nujñānaṃ guroḥ prāpya pūjayet puruṣottamam|
evaṃ yo vaiṣṇavīṃ dīkṣāṃ kuryāt kārayitā ca yaḥ|| 11.69 ||
tāvubhau gacchataḥ svargaṃ narakaṃ ca viparyaye|
viṣvaksenaṃ ca yāgānte diśyaiśānyāṃ prapūjayet|| 11.70 ||
piṅgaśmaśruṃ vivṛttākṣaṃ gadāpāṇiṃ caturbhujam|
śaṅkhacakradharaṃ devaṃ jagatsaṃrakṣaṇe sthitam|| 11.71 ||
tarjayan vāmatarjanyā nāsāgrāsannayā jagat|
vāruṇe maṇḍale sāṅgaṃ śiṣṭadravyaistu pūjayet|| 11.72 ||
visṛjyoktena vidhinā maṇḍalasthamadhokṣajam|
nirmālyaṃ sakalaṃ tasmai samāhṛtya nivedayet|| 11.73 ||
tasya viṃ vaṃ namaḥ ṣvāṃ vīṃ svāheti ca yathākramam|
kseṃ vaṣaṭ tathā viṣvaṃ vaiṃ humityapi kalpayet|| 11.74 ||
knena vaḥ phaḍitīmāni pañcāṅgāni tadarcane|
praṇītasthaṃ jalaṃ siktvā prastaropari tena tu|| 11.75 ||
ātmānaṃ saha śiṣyaistu prokṣyāgniṃ ca visarjayet|
viṣvaksenaṃ visṛjyāsmai nirmālyaṃ sakalaṃ tataḥ|| 11.76 ||
pūrvadattaṃ jale'gādhe dhṛtamanyairnidhāpayet|
vaiṣṇavān pūjayecchaktyā brāhmaṇāṃstu viśeṣataḥ|| 11.77 ||
anivāritamannādyaṃ sārvajanyaṃ ca kārayet|
evamācāryatāṃ prāptau dīkṣito dharmavatsalaḥ|| 11.78 ||
varṇāśramarato bhakto vinītaḥ samayānvitaḥ|
ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā trikālaṃ vā'rcayeddharim|| 11.79 ||
śiṣyānathānuguhṇīyād dīkṣāpūjābhiṣecanaiḥ|
sthāpanādikriyāyāṃ ca tathā''cāryatvamarhati|| 11.80 ||
dīkṣānte cābhiṣekānte śaktyā sampūjya vaiṣṇavān|
prabhāte'vabhūthaṃ gacched yatra tīrthaṃ paraṃ bhavet|| 11.81 ||
tatra snātvā'rcayitvā ca brāhmaṇaiḥ svasti vācayet|| 11.82 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāmabhiṣeko nāmaikādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<12>
|| atha dvādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepād yāgabhūmestu lakṣaṇam|
yatreṣṭvā vidhivad devaṃ labhyante sarvasiddhayaḥ|| 12.1 ||
tridhottamādhamā madhyā caturdhā jātibhedataḥ|
supadmā bhadrikā pūrṇā dhūmretyapi tathaiva bhūḥ|| 12.2 ||
bahupāṃsuraśuṣkāmbuḥ kṣipraṃ vrīhyādirohaṇā|
pradakṣiṇajalā śreṣṭhā samā śītoṣṇakālayoḥ|| 12.3 ||
nadī pūrvavahā yatra kṣīrapuṣpaphaladrumāḥ|
ramante yatra gomartyāḥ samā snigdhā ca sottamā|| 12.4 ||
śaṅkhābhrādisvarā ceṣṭā savarṇā sarvavarṇinām|
alābhe kṛtrimaivaṃ syādadhamā syādato'nyathā|| 12.5 ||
sarvaplavā ca madhyoktā lakṣaṇaiśca śubhāśubhaiḥ|
caturaśrāṣṭaṣaḍbhāgāpādādhikasamāyatā|| 12.6 ||
viprādīnāṃ smṛtā bhūmiḥ sarveṣāṃ vottamasya yā|
sitā raktā ca pītā ca kṛṣṇā viprādibhūstathā|| 12.7 ||
madhurā ra kaṣāyā ca tiktā ca kaṭukā kramāt|
ghṛtāsṛkcaruviḍgandhā bahuvarṇā tu varjitā|| 12.8 ||
dhānyapūrṇāmakumbhasthavardhamānājyavarttibhiḥ|
sitādibhirniśi jñeyā prajvālyāvṛtya darśanāt|| 12.9 ||
khāte rātryuṣitaiḥ puṣpaistathā jñeyā sitādibhiḥ|
valmīkaśarkarārandratuṣāṅgārāsthibhasmabhiḥ|| 12.10 ||
keśakardamakīṭaiśca saṃyuktāṃ varjayenmahīm|
dhūmratāraukṣyadaurgandhyakāṭhinyaiścāśubhā mahī|| 12.11 ||
adiksthā jalahīnā ca kuṭilā dviguṇāyatā|
vṛttā pañcatriṣaṭkoṇā cārdhacandrā ca śūrpikā|| 12.12 ||
pīluśelvakṣaveṇvarkasnukpiśācakaṭudrumaiḥ|
śyenagṛdhrarkṣagomāyusūkaradhvāṅkṣavānaraiḥ|| 12.13 ||
rakṣoyamāgnivātāpyanimnatvena ca ninditā|
madhyanimnā sagarbhā ca pratilomā ca neṣyate|| 12.14 ||
kuśaiḥ śaraiśca dūrvābhiḥ kāśaiśca brāhmaṇādibhūḥ|
puṣpairdīpaiśca vijñeyā natvā vijñāpya taṃ prabhum|| 12.15 ||
nimittaṃ brūhi me deva kalproktaṃ dvijādikam|
varṇāryaṃ tu prayukte tu tato varticatuṣṭaye|| 12.16 ||
muhūrte gamite yasya dīpo jvalati tasya bhūḥ|
vāstu saṃkīrṇamiṣṭaṃ ca sarveṣā sarvabhāsanāt|| 12.17 ||
nyagrodhodumbarāśvatthaplakṣāḥ pūrvādayaḥ subhāḥ|
ete vyastāḥ samastā vā śastā vāmāstvakāmadāḥ|| 12.18 ||
nādeyaṃ tu jalaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ dikṣu sarvāsvavasthitam|
kūpamaindraiśasaumyeṣu na caikasalilaṃ śubham|| 12.19 ||
jalādrivṛkṣagulmāśca pūrvādiṣu śubhāḥ sthitāḥ|
śigrunimbādayaśchedyāstatra hastaśatāntare|| 12.20 ||
bahiḥsthitā na doṣāya madhye kāryāstu vā śubhāḥ|
dviguṇaṃ gṛhavakṣoccaṃ tyaktvā doṣāya na sthitāḥ|| 12.21 ||
gṛhoccadviguṇād bāhye phalapuṣpadrumāstathā|
bakulāśokapunnāganāgacandanacampakāḥ|| 12.22 ||
bilvāmrapanasādyāśca palāśādyāśca śobhanāḥ|
antassāraṃ bahissāraṃ nissāraṃ sarvasārakam|| 12.23 ||
caturdhā sthāvaraṃ teṣāmasāraṃ na gṛhāgrataḥ|
kadalyādi bahirdhāryaṃ gṛhapṛṣṭhāntapakṣataḥ|| 12.24 ||
sarvasāraṃ tu sarvatra ciñcādi parikīrttitam|
antassāraṃ tathā vidyād bahissāramasāravat|| 12.25 ||
api sauvarṇakaṃ vṛkṣamupagehaṃ na dhārayet|
gulmavallyoṣadhīnāṃ tu naikānto vidhirīdṛśaḥ|| 12.26 ||
candanāgarukarpūrakadambatilakārjunaiḥ|
kramukairnālikeraiśca kuśairdarbhaiḥ samāvṛtā|| 12.27 ||
padmotpalavanākīrṇā toyāḍhyaindroraplavā|
ketakīkṣusamākīrṇā supadmā śāntidā mahī|| 12.28 ||
nadīsāgaraparyantatīrthāyatanamāśritā|
kṣīravṛkṣasamākīrṇā phalavṛkṣasamākulā|| 12.29 ||
udyānopavanopetā phullagulmalatākulā|
paścime yājñikairvṛkṣairvrīhikṣetreṇa dakṣiṇe|| 12.30 ||
bhadrikā bhūmirākhyātā sukhadā tatra yajvanām|
kiṃśukāśokabakulairaṅkolaiścaiva nimbakaiḥ|| 12.31 ||
khadirai rohitaiḥ plakṣairmādhavīcampakāsanaiḥ|
kulasthatilaniṣpāvakodravaiśca samākulā|| 12.32 ||
giripārśvāśritā caiva gireḥ śikharamāśritā|
aprabhūtodakā bhūmiḥ sā pūrṇeti ca puṣṭidā|| 12.33 ||
pīluveṇuvanākīrṇā snuhiśleṣmātakānvitā|
vibhītakārkasaṅkīrṇā kaṭhinā śarkarānvitā|| 12.34 ||
śyenagṛdhrarkṣagomāyusūkaradhvāṅkṣasaṅkulā|
soṣarā garbhayuktā ca kṣayadā dhūmrikā bhavet|| 12.35 ||
evaṃ parīkṣya yatnena gandhavarṇarasādibhiḥ|
gulmādi pariśodhyātra karṣayellāṅgalaiḥ śubhaiḥ|| 12.36 ||
pañcadhā daśadhā vā'pi sarvadhānyāni vāpayet|
karṣayitvā'tha bhūyo'pi kuryāt puṣphalānvitām|| 12.37 ||
atyantopahatā yā tu tasyāḥ śuddhirathocyate|
śataśaḥ karṣayitvā tu gokulaṃ tatra vāsayet|| 12.38 ||
tato varṣoṣite kṛṣṭvā tilādīnyatra vāpayet|
yadā sasyeṣu puṣpāṇi phalāni ca bhavanti hi|| 12.39 ||
tadā kṛṣṭā punarbhūmiḥ śuddhimṛcchatyasaṃśayam|
bhūtakūrabaliṃ tatra pūrvaṃ dattvā vicakṣaṇaḥ|| 12.40 ||
yāvat prākāraparyantaṃ tāvat kuryāt parigraham|
bhūtāni rākṣasā vā'pi ye'tra tiṣṭhanti kecana|| 12.41 ||
te sarve hyapagacchantu sthānaṃ kuryāmahaṃ hareḥ|
ityanujñāpya bhūtādīṃstatastaṃ vidhimācaret|| 12.42 ||
indrādīśāvasānaṃ tu śaṅkudviguṇaveṣṭanāt|
nimittaiḥ śobhanairmantrī kuryāt sūtraprasāraṇam|| 12.43 ||
punnāmaikatarūdbhūtā nirghṛṇāḥ śaṅkavo dṛḍhāḥ|
prāṅmukhenāṣṭadhā tāḍyāḥ pūjitā lohamudgaraiḥ|| 12.44 ||
khātvā puruṣamātraṃ tu jalāśmāntamathāpi vā|
tato nyasya ghaṭaṃ madhye vidhinā pūrṇamarcitam|| 12.45 ||
saratnaṃ dhānyarāśisthaṃ muhūrtte maṅgalānvite|
sumṛdbhiḥ khātamāpūrya hastapūraṃ tato'śmabhiḥ|| 12.46 ||
pādonaṃ plāvayitvā'dbhiḥ pālāśāśvatthamudgaraiḥ|
ākoṭya gharṣayitvā ca sudṛḍhaṃ kārayet talam|| 12.47 ||
dvāradeśe'rcayed devaṃ sthaṇḍile'gniṃ ca tarpayet|
caturaśraṃ samaṃ kṛtvā tatra vāstuṃ ca tarpayet|| 12.48 ||
sūtrapātaṃ punaḥ kuryādekāśītipadaṃ yathā|
susamaḥ sukhado vāsturviṣamo na sukhāvahaḥ|| 12.49 ||
dadhyakṣatājyapuṣpāmbuhemaratnaiḥ pradakṣiṇam|
maṅgalārthaṃ likhet pūrvaṃ nāṅgulyādyaiḥ kadācana|| 12.50 ||
īśamūrdhni nirṛtyaṅghrau tatrottānaśaye sthitān|
kuśapuṣpākṣatānnādbhirgandhapuṣpaiḥ prapūjayet|| 12.51 ||
brahmā navasu madhye syād dikṣu ṣaṭke dvike'nyataḥ|
dvātriṃśad bahirīśādikramādarcyāstu devatāḥ|| 12.52 ||
brahmā madhyapade pūjyaḥ tatprācyāmāryakastataḥ|
sāvitraḥ savitā cāgnau tato dvipadagau smṛtau|| 12.53 ||
vivasvān dakṣiṇe ṣaṭke jayantendrau dvikadvaye|
ṣaṭke tu paścime mitro vāyavyadvikayoḥ punaḥ|| 12.54 ||
rājayakṣmā ca rudraśca ṣaṭke kṣitibhṛduttare|
āpavatsastathāpaśca dvikayostadbahiḥ śivaḥ|| 12.55 ||
parjanyaśca jayantendrasūryasatyabhṛśāmbarāḥ|
prācyāmagnyādikān vakṣye pūṣā ca vitathastathā|| 12.56 ||
gṛhakṣato dharmarājo gandharvo bhṛṅgarājakaḥ|
mṛgaśca nairṛte koṇe pūjayecca pitṝṃstataḥ|| 12.57 ||
dauvārikaṃ ca sugrīvaṃ puṣpadantaṃ jalādhipam|
asuraṃ taiva śoṣaṃ ca pāpayakṣmāṇameva ca|| 12.58 ||
vāyuṃ nāgaṃ tathā mukhyaṃ bhallāṭaṃ somameva ca|
aditiṃ ditiṃ kuberaṃ ca bahirīśādiṣu kramāt|| 12.59 ||
carakīṃ ca vidārīṃ ca pūtanāṃ pāparākṣasīm|
śarvaskandaṃ cāryamaṇaṃ jambhakaṃ pilipiñchakam|| 12.60 ||
dikṣu teṣāṃ baliṃ dattvā madhyādārabhya sarvataḥ|
bhūtebhyo'pi baliṃ dadyādaṣṭadikṣu yatākramam|| 12.61 ||
pūjitā vāstudehasthāstripañcāśat tu devatāḥ|
siddhaye syuranarthāya gṛhabhājāmapūjitāḥ|| 12.62 ||
saṅkalpitopavāsastu susnātaḥ susahāyavān|
suliptāyāṃ samāsīnaḥ kṣitau rātrāvudaṅmukhaḥ|| 12.63 ||
jpet svapnapradaṃ mantraṃ yāvannidrāvaśaṃgataḥ|
tataḥ paśyatyasau svapnaṃ tadbhūmiphalasūcakam|| 12.64 ||
susvapne'tra gṛhaṃ kuryāt sugṛharkṣadinodaye|
sumṛdā sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā caturhastaṃ suśobhanam|| 12.65 ||
pralipta padmamadhyasthamudakumbhaṃ niveśayet|
sarvauṣadiyutaṃ bhadraṃ ratnagarbhaṃ sapallavam|| 12.66 ||
sitacandanadigdhāṅgaṃ padmamālāvibhūṣitam|
sitavastrayugacchannamavyagraṃ svastikānvitam|| 12.67 ||
tanmukhe praṇavenādau dhyātvā kamalamujjvalam|
sāṅgulīyakaro mantrī grahāṃstatra prakalpayet|| 12.68 ||
baddhapadmāsanān hṛṣṭān pūjayecca svanāmabhiḥ|
sūryaṃ raktaṃ sitaṃ somaṃ raktamaṅgārakaṃ punaḥ|| 12.69 ||
budhaṃ cāmīkaraprakhyaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ śanaiścaram|
pītaṃ bṛhaspatiṃ śukraṃ śuklameva tu kalpayet|| 12.70 ||
rāhuṃ cāsitapītābhaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ketuṃ yatākramam|
svavarṇalakṣitaiḥ puṣpaiḥ sarve'rcyāstu vidhānataḥ|| 12.71 ||
kuryāddhomaṃ baliṃ cānte gītavāditrasaṃyutam|
stutiṃ kṛtvā grahāṇāṃ tu pavitraṃ stotramīrayet|| 12.72 ||
śubhāśubhamahārthāni nimittāni grahādhipa!|
divyāntarikṣabhaumāni pṛcchato me prakāśaya|| 12.73 ||
ityuktvoddhṛtya taṃ kumbhaṃ śākunaṃ sūktamuccaran|
parito bhrāmayed yatnājjayaśabdādisaṃyutam|| 12.74 ||
aniṣṭadarśane'pyevaṃ bhūmau sthāpitapūjitam|
uddhṛtya bhrāmayed bhūyastadā khe dṛśyate'dbhutam|| 12.75 ||
siddhānāṃ darśanaṃ śabdaḥ sātapā vṛṣṭireva vā|
ratnapuṣpaphalānnānāṃ dhānyamadhvājyavāsasām|| 12.76 ||
dadhipāṭhīnabhekānāṃ mūtrasya piśitasya ca|
kūrmasya vā mahīpṛṣṭhe prapāto nabhasaḥ śubhaḥ|| 12.77 ||
nṛpabrāhmaṇakanyānāṃ pūrvakumbhasya darśanam|
hastyaśvaśikhihaṃsābhraśaṅkhaveṇusamāḥ svarāḥ|| 12.78 ||
ityevamādayo jñeyāḥ karmakāle vicakṣaṇaiḥ|
spaṣṭe ghrāte śrute dṛṣṭe'pyaniṣṭe varjayet kṣitim|| 12.79 ||
dinamanyat samāsādya prāguktavidhivartmanā|
sunimittaṃ ca vijñāya kartā vai karma kārayet|| 12.80 ||
tatheṣṭvā'gniṃ kṣametyuktvā brāhmaṇaiḥ svasti vācya ca|
baliṃ dattvā khanet prāgvat tāṃ dharāṃ pūrayet punaḥ|| 12.81 ||
gṛhamadhye tu medhāvī śubhāṃ pūrvaprakalpitām|
nyaset kūrmaśilāṃ caiva yasyāṃ kūrmo niveśyate|| 12.82 ||
rājataṃ vinyaset kūrmaṃ sauvarṇaṃ tadvadeva tu|
pūrvavat kalpayet padmaṃ sāṣṭavargāṃ ca mātṛkām|| 12.83 ||
ādhāraśaktimatreṣṭvā natvā vijñāpayet tataḥ|
tvameva paramā śaktistvamevāsanadhārikā|| 12.84 ||
santṛptayā tvayā devi! sthātavyamiha sarvadā|
kūrmarājamakūpāraṃ tathā viśvasya dhārakam|| 12.85 ||
dhyātvā pūrvoktapadmasthaṃ svanāmnaiva tu pūjayet|
namaste kūrmarūpāya viṣṇave viśvadhāriṇe|| 12.86 ||
kariṣyamāṇaṃ prāsādamiha tvaṃ dhartumarhasi|
iti vijñāpya taṃ bhaktyā gaṇeśaṃ cātra pūjayet|| 12.87 ||
kūrmasya dakṣiṇe pārśve padmaṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvavat|
nāmayuktena bījena gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ kramāt|| 12.88 ||
kuryād bījasya pañcāṅgaṃ tridīrghaṃ dvādaśāntagaiḥ|
nivedya laḍḍukāpūpapṛthukādi viśeṣataḥ|| 12.89 ||
dhyāyed gajānanaṃ raktaṃ bṛhatkukṣiṃ caturbhujam|
pāśāṅkuśadharaṃ bhakṣyaṃ dadhānaṃ dantameva ca|| 12.90 ||
evaṃ sampūjyate nityaṃ gaṇeśo yatra bhaktitaḥ|
vighnāstatra na jāyante vardhante cātra sampadaḥ|| 12.91 ||
maṇḍale sthaṇḍile cainaṃ yāge yāge ca pūjayet|
gaṇeśaṃ vidhinā ya

<13>
|| atha trayodaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt prāsādavidhimuttamam|
yatkriyāvyavasāyo'pi sadyaḥ sarvāghanāśanaḥ|| 13.1 ||
aṣṭeṣṭakānidhāne'pi phalaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate|
anumeyaṃ hi tenaiva phalaṃ prāsādavistare|| 13.2 ||
sarvayajñatapodānatīrthavedeṣu yat phalam|
tat sarvaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ yajamānasya jāyate|| 13.3 ||
dravyasaṃsthānabhedācca phalaṃ bhūyo'sya vardhate|
hitaṃ nātaḥ paraṃ tasmācchaktaiḥ kāryaṃ tu sarvathā|| 13.4 ||
vṛṣābhyāṃ kapilābhyāṃ tu samābhyāṃ prāṅmukho guruḥ|
kṛṣṭvā'sanamayaireva yaṣṭīyugahalairbhuvam|| 13.5 ||
dvādaśākṣaramantreṇa navavastrādyalaṃkṛtaḥ|
śamyāsamīkṛte gobhiḥ sasyamutpādya khādayet|| 13.6 ||
punaḥ kṛṣṭveṣṭakādhānaṃ kuryād dvāre tu kalpite|
dvārasya dakṣiṇe bhāge kartavyā prathameṣṭakā|| 13.7 ||
ullekhādyājyabhāgāntaṃ kṛtvodaksthaṇḍile kramāt|
pakvaṃ ca viṣṇugāyatryā hutvā'thājyāhutīḥ kramāt|| 13.8 ||
vyastauraṣṭākṣarairaṣṭau vyāhṛtīndrādibhistataḥ|
agnaye caiva somāya grahebhyo'pi yathākramam|| 13.9 ||
nakṣatrebhyaśca bhūtebhyo nāgebhya iti caiva hi|
devatābhyaśca sarvābhyo viśvebhyaśceti deśikaḥ|| 13.10 ||
viṣṇave ceti hutvā'nte vyāhṛtīrjuhuyāt tataḥ|
samidājyacarūn hutvā gāyatryā'ṣṭottaraṃ śatam|| 13.11 ||
tataḥ sviṣṭakṛtaṃ hutvā homaśeṣaṃ samāpya ca|
iṣṭakāsu ca mūlena sampātājyaṃ samarpayet|| 13.12 ||
aśvatthaśaṅkunā rekhāḥ pūrvāgrāścottarāgrakāḥ|
tāreṇāṣṭau samālikhya prāṅmukhaḥ susamāhitaḥ|| 13.13 ||
caturaśre catuṣkoṣṭhe nyasyāgnyanilakoṣṭhayoḥ|
pūrvāgrasūtre saumyāgre tathaiva nirṛtīśayoḥ|| 13.14 ||
sthitvā kṛtāñjaliḥ paścād dhyātvā devamadhokṣajam|
mūlamaṣṭaśataṃ japtvā dvādaśākṣarameva ca|| 13.15 ||
tataḥ svākṣarasaṅkhayāto japedaṣṭākṣaraṃ budhaḥ|
prabhuṃ taṃ praṇato dhyātvā koṣṭheṣvaṣṭabhirakṣaraiḥ|| 13.16 ||
agnyādīndrāntamaṣṭau tā iṣṭakā vinyaset kramāt|
upānaḍvat prayuñjīta varjayennimnamunnatam|| 13.17 ||
iṣṭakāstu nidhāyaivaṃ śvabhraṃ sampūrya vāribhiḥ|
sampātaratnagandhāḍhyairvimalaistu sugandhibhiḥ|| 13.18 ||
kalaśaiḥ pūritaiḥ pūrvamarcitairmantrasaṃskṛtaiḥ|
śuklapuṣpākṣatān nyasya tāreṇāvartamīkṣayet|| 13.19 ||
śubhāśubhāvihāvartau jñātavyau dakṣiṇottarau|
śālyādikṣetramṛtsābhirgartaṃ pracchādayet tataḥ|| 13.20 ||
visṛjya śilpino'trāpi kalpayet svapnadarśanam|
śubhe lābho'śubhe mokṣaḥ śāntiḥ kāryā tvadarśane|| 13.21 ||
garbhādhānaṃ tataḥ kuryānnāgarbhe dhāmni sampadaḥ|
mṛttikā daśa saṅgrāhyā mūlāni jalajāni ca|| 13.22 ||
dhātavo ratnabījāni lohādi ca yadiṣyate|
aṣṭāṅgulaṃ tadardhoccaṃ pādahīnottaracchadam|| 13.23 ||
sāndraṃ tāmramayaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ garbhapātraṃ ca śobhanam|
suguptaṃ tadyathā bhittau bhittimānena vā bhavet|| 13.24 ||
pādukopari viprasya bhuvi rājño'nyayoradhaḥ|
dvārasya dakṣiṇe bhāge tṛtīyāṃśe dviśeṣite|| 13.25 ||
gomūtreṇāplute nyasyed dvāramūlasya pārśvataḥ|
kṣālite pañcagavyena pātre'smin pariśodhite|| 13.26 ||
dhāraṇābhiryathānyāyaṃ dhyātvā pīṭhe'kṣarāmbujam|
bhūmaṇḍalamanantasthaṃ saśailāmbhodhidiggajam|| 13.27 ||
prādakṣiṇyena kṛtvā'ntaḥ pūrvaṃ sāgaramṛdvṛtim|
digvidikṣu ca madhye ca gāyatryā vinyasenmṛdaḥ|| 13.28 ||
giritīrthanadībhyaśca hradāt karkaṭakālayāt|
valmīkakhaladantyukṣaśṛṅgebhyaścāhṛtāḥ kramāt|| 13.29 ||
paṅkajotpalayormūle kumudasya ca deśikaḥ|
dikṣu lohitakasyāpi catvāryevaṃ kramānnyaset|| 13.30 ||
manaśśilāṃ harītālamañjanaṃ śyāmasīsake|
saurāṣṭrīṃ rocanāṃ caiva gaurikaṃ pārataṃ tathā|| 13.31 ||
vajramauktiktikavaiḍūryaśaṅkhaspaṭikapuṣyakān|
candrakāntaṃ mahānīlaṃ padmarāgaṃ tathā punaḥ|| 13.32 ||
śālinīvārakau caiva kaṅkumāṣakulusthakān|
niṣpāvatilamudgāṃśca pākapūrṇān sasarṣapān|| 13.33 ||
dikṣu kāñcanarūpyāyastrapuṇyatha hiraṇmayam|
suparṇaṃ kūrmarūpaṃ ca śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ ca koṇataḥ|| 13.34 ||
dhanurgadāmbujaṃ madhye pratyekaṃ ca hiraṇmayam|
etat sarvaṃ samānīya nyastavyaṃ garbhabhājane|| 13.35 ||
kṛtvaivaṃ pañcagavyena prokṣya dvādaśavidyayā|
agnerdakṣiṇataḥ kṛtvā juhuyāt pūrvavat kramāt|| 13.36 ||
ghṛtaṃ dvādaśabhirvarṇairdikpairnārāyaṇena ca|
tārādinā hutāntena vakṣyamāṇaiśca nāmabhiḥ|| 13.37 ||
bījāni sarvadhātūni sarvalokāśca parvatāḥ|
samudrāḥ sarvatīrthāni nadyaścātha hradāstathā|| 13.38 ||
pātālāścātha nāgāśca diggajā vṛṣabhāstathā|
vyāhṛtyantaṃ tato hutvā sampātavidhinaiva tu|| 13.39 ||
ājyaṃ samarpya tat pātraṃ vidhāyāgrakarasthitam|
japtvā mantraṃ bhuvaṃ dhyātvā prāṅmukho niśi deśikaḥ|| 13.40 ||
sumuhūrtte dvijānujñāṃ prāpya tūryādinisvanaiḥ|
śaktiṃ dhyātvā'rcayitvā'syāṃ garbhabhūtamidaṃ smaran|| 13.41 ||
brahmāṇḍaṃ pārthivaṃ śubhraṃ gṛhamūrttiprasūtaye|
dhyātvā'smin pūrvavaccorvī śanairmantramudīrayet|| 13.42 ||
sarvabhūtadhare! kānte! parvatastanamaṇḍite|
samudraparidhāne! tvaṃ devi! garbhaṃ samāśraya|| 13.43 ||
evaṃ nyaste tu garbhe'smin sarvatra ca sukhaṃ bhavet|
hiraṇyaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā prāsādaṃ kārayet tataḥ|| 13.44 ||
dvārsthāpanādadhaḥ prāgvadiṣṭvā vāstuṃ yathākramam|
kṛtvā homaṃ ca saṃspṛśya śaktyā ratnādi vinyaset|| 13.45 ||
dvāramācchādya vastrābhyāṃ gavyādikṣālitaṃ śubham|
gandhādibhiḥ samabhyarcya śāntihomādisaṃyutam|| 13.46 ||
muhūrte sthāpayed vidvān madhyānmadhyaṃ vimucya tu|
ātmatattvamadho nyasya vidyātattvaṃ ca śākhayoḥ|| 13.47 ||
paratattvaṃ ca madhye tat suśirassaṃvyavasthitam|
sthāpayet praṇavenātha pūjayecca punaḥ kramāt|| 13.48 ||
sthirāprameyavyāpitvabodhanityāvināśitāḥ|
tṛptiśca sapta hotavyāḥ kramādāhutisaptakaiḥ|| 13.49 ||
pratīhārādayo devā ye tasmin saṃvyavasthitāḥ|
nāmabhiste'tra hotavyāstato deyaśca tadbaliḥ|| 13.50 ||
bhittistambhādivinyāse sirāmarmādipīḍanam|
sarvathā parihartavyaṃ vāstukopo'nyathā bhavet|| 13.51 ||
kiñciduttarato nītvā pūrvato vā sirādivit|
stambhādīn vinyasedevaṃ vedhadoṣo na jāyate|| 13.52 ||
prāgdvāramṛṣabhasthaṃ syād dhvajasthaṃ paścimāmukham|
anyathā sarvanāśaḥ syādāyadoṣānna saṃśayaḥ|| 13.53 ||
prāgdvāraṃ grāmamadhye syāt paścime cottare tathā|
dakṣiṇendrāgniṣu prāyaḥ paścimadvāramiṣyate|| 13.54 ||
triṃśadantaṃ trihastādi saptāntaikādibhūmikam|
vṛttaṃ vā caturaśraṃ vā darśanīyaṃ tu kārayet|| 13.55 ||
bhūvṛddhyā vāyuvahnīndravaruṇārkenduviṣṇavaḥ|
gṛhāṇāṃ devatā jñeyā bhūmīnāṃ mūrtayo'parāḥ|| 13.56 ||
śayānāsīnatiṣṭhanto gacchan pūrve ca yoginaḥ|
bhūmīnāṃ devatā jñeyā dikkoṇasthāstato'parāḥ|| 13.57 ||
varāho nārasiṃhaśca śrīdharaśva hayānanaḥ|
jāmadagnyaśca rāmaśca vāmanaḥ kṛṣṇa eva ca|| 13.58 ||
tata ūrdhvaṃ caturdikṣu vainateyaṃ prakalpayet|
madhye brahmā śivo'nte syāt kalaśe tu svayaṃ hariḥ|| 13.59 ||
kalaśānte mahāviṣṇuḥ sadāviṣṇustadagrataḥ|
jñātavyā gṛhagarbhārcāḥ sthūlasūkṣmaparātmikāḥ|| 13.60 ||
anantaṃ pādukaṃ vidyādātmatattveśvaraṃ prabhum|
vidyātattvamadhiṣṭhānamita ūrdhvaṃ paraṃ tathā|| 13.61 ||
vedyāmindrādayo diksthā viṣvaksenaḥ praṇālake|
pādukādyaṅghridaṅghoru paṭṭikāntaṃ pratiḥ kaṭiḥ|| 13.62 ||
mekhalā raśanā kukṣirgarbhaḥ stambhāśca bāhavaḥ|
madhyaṃ nābhiśca hṛt pīṭhamapānaṃ jalanirgamaḥ|| 13.63 ||
pādādhārastvahaṃkāraḥ piṇḍikā buddhirucyate|
tadante prakṛtiḥ padmaṃ pratimā puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ|| 13.64 ||
ghaṇṭā jihvā mano dīpo dāru snāyuḥ śilā'sthi ca|
tvak sudhā lepanaṃ māṃsaṃ rudhiraṃ tatra yo rasaḥ|| 13.65 ||
cakṣuḥ śikharapārśve tu dhvajāgraṃ ca śikhā bhavet|
talakumbho bhavet pāṇirdvāraṃ prajananaṃ smṛtam|| 13.66 ||
śukanāsaiva nāsoktā gavākṣaṃ śravaṇaṃ viduḥ|
kapotāliṃ tathā skandhaṃ kaṇṭhaṃ cāmalasārakam|| 13.67 ||
ghaṭaṃ śiro ghṛtaṃ majjā vāṅ mantraḥ secana payaḥ|
nāmaśaityādivarṇānnadhūpeṣu viṣayāḥ sthitāḥ|| 13.68 ||
randhre vātāyane dhāmni lepe sthairya ca khādayaḥ|
parvāṇi sandhayo jñeyā lohabandhāstathā nakhāḥ|| 13.69 ||
keśaromāṇi caivāsya vijñeyā dugdhakūrcakāḥ|
prāsādapādamātroccaḥ prākāraḥ parito bhavet|| 13.70 ||
gopuraṃ pādahīnoccaṃ tatsamaṃ maṇḍapaṃ viduḥ|
pīṭhadviguṇavistāro garuḍasyaṭa ca maṇḍapaḥ|| 13.71 ||
tāvanto lokapālānāṃ viṣvaksenasya cālayāḥ|
pṛṣṭhe'nantasya cāgneyyāṃ haviśśālā ca tāvatī|| 13.72 ||
mātṝṇāmālayāḥ kāryā dakṣiṇe cottare tathā|
indrāgnīśagatāḥ kāryāḥ sarve pratyaṅmukhā gṛhāḥ|| 13.73 ||
rakṣovaruṇavāyavyagatāśca prāṅmukhāstathā|
dakṣiṇottarayoḥ kāryāḥ prāsādābhimukhā yathā|| 13.74 ||
tuṣahīnairanuttānairaṣṭabhirmadhyamairyavaiḥ|
uttamaṃ saptabhirmadhyamadhamaṃ ṣaḍbhiraṅgulam|| 13.75 ||
gāyatryakṣarasaṃkhyātairaṅgulaireva sādhitaḥ|
yo hastastena mātavyaṃ sarvaṃ trividhamiṣyate|| 13.76 ||
śilābhiriṣṭakābhirvā kāṣṭhairvā mṛdbhireva vā|
kuryāt prāsādavistāraṃ sadoṣaṃ tu vivarjayet|| 13.77 ||
vṛddhāṃ bālāṃ ca caṇḍālīṃ garbhiṇīṃ duṣṭabhūmijām|
śilāṃ tyaktvā śubhāmanyāṃ gṛhṇīyād varṇayogataḥ|| 13.78 ||
pulliṅge pratimā proktā srīliṅge pīṭhikā smṛtā|
ratnanyāsādadhastāttu napuṃsakaśileṣyate|| 13.79 ||
kāryā gṛhānurūpā'rcā gṛhaṃ vā'rcānurūpataḥ|
nyāyo'yaṃ pīṭhikādīnāmapi jñeyo vicakṣaṇaiḥ|| 13.80 ||
na jīrṇairupayuktairvā śilādyairvaiṣṇavaṃ gṛham|
kārayet pīṭhikāṃ vā'rcāmābhicāro'nyathā bhavet|| 13.81 ||
purātanyo vivarṇāḥ syurapakvāścātra neṣṭakāḥ|
garbhavantaḥ svayaṃ śuṣkāḥ patitā vā'gnidūṣitāḥ|| 13.82 ||
asārā duṣṭadeśasthā dūṣitāścāpi na drumāḥ|
kaṭukaṇṭakipaiśācakṣudracaityādayastathā|| 13.83 ||
śūlasūcidhvajastambhacakrayaṣṭikṛtāni ca|
na yuñjyācchāstravid gehe nottamaṃ yojayedadhaḥ|| 13.84 ||
ghaṭīyantrādidārūṇi na kapitthākṣajāni ca|
vāhayenmūlamagreṇa nottamaṃ sandhayennave|| 13.85 ||
nādhomukhaṃ niyuñjīta na tathā dakṣiṇāmukham|
anuyāge nāvanyeva dravyāṇi śubhadāni tu|| 13.86 ||
prāsāde'dhaḥ śilādīni śubhānyanyāni vecchayā|
yadārcotpāṭitā sthāpyā tadā pūrvaiva pīṭhikā|| 13.87 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ trayodaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<14>
|| atha caturdaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt pratimālakṣaṇaṃ param|
yonisaṃsthānabhedaṃ ca sādhakānāṃ hitāya vai|| 14.1 ||
ratnalohaśilādhātumṛtkāṣṭhālekhyabhedataḥ|
pratimā saptadhā jñeyā nirdoṣā ratnalohajā|| 14.2 ||
bahudoṣā śilā sā tu puṇyaśailodbhaveṣyate|
vāruṇyaindrī tathā''gneyī vāyavī bhūścaturvidhā|| 14.3 ||
ādyayoryā śilā saiṣā nirdoṣā'pi tu nānyayoḥ|
nimagnā bhuvi yā ramyā snigdhā'rkakiraṇojjhitā|| 14.4 ||
ekavarṇā suvarṇā ca savarṇā cātiśobhanā|
śvetabjābhā mṛge grāhyā saumyā viprasya sā śilā|| 14.5 ||
aindrī raktā kulasthābhā jyeṣṭhāyāṃ kṣatriyasya tu|
phalgunyāṃ bhagadevatyā pītā vā haritā viśaḥ|| 14.6 ||
kṛṣṇā mudganibhā'śvinyāṃ śūdrasyaivāśvidevatā|
viśālā nātibahalā chidravanmadhurasvarā|| 14.7 ||
rambhādalākṛtiḥ strī syācchītalā ca manoramā|
gambhīraninadā gurvī sadṛḍhā visphuliṅginī|| 14.8 ||
aśvatthavaṭapatrābhā viśālā bahalā pumān|
napuṃsakaṃ dvayākārā taddhīnā yā saparpaṭā|| 14.9 ||
nibiḍāvayavā snigdhā dhīraśabdā suśītalā|
sugandharasūrupāḍhyā yuvatiḥ sā suśobhanā|| 14.10 ||
bālā śastrāsahā mṛdvī picchilā pallavāsthirā|
caṇḍālī śulkinī vā syād yasyāṃ na ramate manaḥ|| 14.11 ||
sā hi maṇḍūkakhaṇḍā ca vṛddhā rūkṣā'tijharjharā|
bahuvarṇā'tinīlā ca dhūmrā ca sthūlaromikā|| 14.12 ||
hīnasvanā ca nindyāṅgī soṣṇā caṇḍālikā smṛtā|
valmīkavṛkṣasaṃlagnā vahnivātātapāhatā|| 14.13 ||
citicaityasamīpasthā bāhyālīḍhā'ntyasevitā|
grahajuṣṭā vivarṇā ca catvarāntarjaloṣitā|| 14.14 ||
kalpitā'nyatra śiṣṭā ca saṃskṛtā yena kenacit|
sagarbhamaṇḍalacchidrasandhikīlakajālakā|| 14.15 ||
sphoṭāvartasirāgranthibhedayuktā ca varjitā|
hīnarekhottamā hīnā hīnā rekhottamottamā|| 14.16 ||
śilāsavarmā rekhāḥ syuḥ suvarṇāścātiśobhanāḥ|
indrāyudhapatākormikalaśābhā manoramā|| 14.17 ||
sadā vṛddhikarī jñeyā gobrāhmaṇahitāvahā|
śrīvatsaśaṅkhacakrābjanandyāvartapradakṣiṇā|| 14.18 ||
toraṇacchatravastrokṣakanyāsrakkusumopamā|
prāsādasvastikāśvebhavardhanī śapharopamā|| 14.19 ||
rathekṣaṇāṅgulīkūrmacāmarāṅkuśakesarī|
ratnābharaṇagovṛkṣapatracandrārkatārakāḥ|| 14.20 ||
pavitrakuṇḍikādarśamṛgahaṃsādayaḥ śubhāḥ|
ekadvibahuvicchede putreśagṛhiṇīkṣayaḥ|| 14.21 ||
vakrasthūlakṛśā rūkṣāśchinnāgrāśca na śobhanāḥ|
saṅkīrṇā bahuvarmā ca yasyāṃ neṣṭā ca sā śilā|| 14.22 ||
a vṛddhikṛdi śatrughnamu saṃvṛtamaśobhanam|
sandhyakṣareṣu vṛddhiḥ syāt kādimānteṣu sampadaḥ|| 14.23 ||
yā yogavāheṣūccāṭo rādau bhīpuṣṭiśāntayaḥ|
siddhirūṣmasu mokṣo'ntye tiryagrekhāstvaśobhanāḥ|| 14.24 ||
sagarbhoddhāṭyamānā yā bhidyate śithiladhvaniḥ|
maṇḍalādiyutoṣṇā vā vivarṇā vā'pi tāṃ tyajet|| 14.25 ||
śilāyāṃ kṣālitāyāṃ tu yadi kiñcinna lakṣyate|
madhyamā sā śilā jñeyā hīnā yuktā śubhāśubhaiḥ|| 14.26 ||
dhvanirghaṇṭānibho yatra sphuliṅgāśchedane sadā|
dṛśyante tacchirastasyāḥ pṛṣṭhamūrdhvamadhomukham|| 14.27 ||
dvijo vā śilpinā kanyā yad brūyādadhivāsitā|
tacchiraḥ syāt tato vakraṃ kalpayedavikalpitam|| 14.28 ||
prākpaścimaśirā grāhyā prākpratyagdvāraveśmanoḥ|
alābhe yāmyasaumyāgrā koṇāgrā tu na śasyate|| 14.29 ||
sthaṇḍile devamiṣṭvā''dau brāhmaṇaiḥ svasti vācayet|
sudine prāśya dadhyannaṃ rakṣāmaṅgalapūrvakam|| 14.30 ||
śastrāṇyastreṇa sampūjya śobhanānyabhidhāya ca|
śastrāṇyastreṇa sampūjya prāṅmukho vā'pyudaṅmukhaḥ|| 14.31 ||
maṅgalāni ca sandṛśya nimittāni parīkṣya ca|
pratimārthaṃ tu nirgacchedācāryaḥ śilpibhiḥ saha|| 14.32 ||
vidyārājñīṃ sadā dhyāyannagrato'rghyakaraḥ sthitaḥ|
nimittaiḥ śobhanairgacchet kṛtvaivaṃ punaranyathā|| 14.33 ||
nirgatastu śilāṃ paśyet tadāprabhṛti nityaśaḥ|
pratimāṃ kārayet kartā tatkarmāntaṃ haviṣyabhuk|| 14.34 ||
someśendreṣu na syāccet tādṛśī śobhanā śilā|
siddhaṃ śilākaraṃ gacchet kṛtvā digyajanaṃ punaḥ|| 14.35 ||
diśo vilokya cāstreṇa rakṣāmaṅgalapūrvakam|
tatrādhivāsanaṃ kuryādācāryaḥ prayato niśi|| 14.36 ||
śilāyā maṇḍapaṃ kṛtvā pariśritya samantataḥ|
caturdikṣu patākābhiścaturvarṇābhiranvitam|| 14.37 ||
iṣṭvā hariṃ ca digdevān bhūtakrūrabaliṃ haret|
devāgre darbhaśayyāyāmupoṣya niyatendriyaḥ|| 14.38 ||
ācāryaḥ susthito viṣṇuṃ dhyātvā mantramudīrayet|
namaḥ sakalalokāya viṣṇave prabhaviṣṇave|| 14.39 ||
viśvāya viśvarūpāya svapnādhipataye namaḥ|
tenāṣṭaśatajaptena tataḥ savpnaṃ sa paśyati|| 14.40 ||
śobhanaṃ syācchubhe svapne kartavyā śāntiranyathā|
śobhanāṃ tu śilāṃ jñātvā varṇaṃ liṅgaṃ śirastathā|| 14.41 ||
udbudhyasveti mantreṇa lāñchayet tatra tatra tu|
ācāryo'streṇa kṛtvaivaṃ śilpibhirgrāhayet tataḥ|| 14.42 ||
devamiṣṭvā baliṃ dattvā kṛtvā'tha svastivācanam|
śilāṃ rathe samāropya prayatnādānayet tataḥ|| 14.43 ||
rathaśilpivikārādau śatamastreṇa homayet|
dhvajādighoṣaiḥ paryetya śālāyāmuttare nyaset|| 14.44 ||
saṃkalpya pratimāmekāṃ śilāyāṃ vṛkṣa eva vā|
nānyāṃ tu pratimāṃ kuryāt kṛtā bhavati niṣphalā|| 14.45 ||
svayamutpāditā mukhyā krītā dravyaistu madhyamā|
viditāvayavaiḥ śuddhā yā hradādiṣu sā'dhamā|| 14.46 ||
śilāyāmiva saṃskāraḥ kartavyo dārukarmaṇi|
dārucandanaśamyākamadhūkāsanabilvakāḥ|| 14.47 ||
saralaḥ spandanaḥ sālaḥ stabakaḥ stambhakastimiḥ|
varaṇastindukāśokaparṇīpanasaśiṃśapāḥ|| 14.48 ||
anye'pi yājñikā vṛkṣāḥ sāravantaśca śobhanāḥ|
ṛjuḥ snigdhaḥ samo'śākhaḥ śubhadeśodbhavo ghanaḥ|| 14.49 ||
agarbhavraṇarandhrādirdṛḍhamūlaḥ śubho mataḥ|
dakṣiṇapravaṇaḥ śuṣko dagdhaḥ pakṣigrahāśrayaḥ|| 14.50 ||
patitaścaityavalmīkacitādisthaśca neṣyate|
adhobhāgaṃ tu saṃśodhya darbhānāstīrya sarvataḥ|| 14.51 ||
gāyatrīparipūtena sarvataḥ prokṣya vāriṇā|
hutvā'nenaiva mantreṇa kṣīravṛkṣasamicchatam|| 14.52 ||
yatparaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ tattvaṃ yacca bhāgavataṃ punaḥ|
tatsarvamekasaṃlīnamasmin dehe vibudhyatām|| 14.53 ||
dharmaṃ puṇyaṃ tathā satyaṃ sarvamekatra vartatām|
vṛkṣa! lokasya śāntyarthaṃ gaccha devālayaṃ śubham|| 14.54 ||
devatvaṃ yāsyase tatra dāhacchedavivarjitam|
jalapuṣpapradānena sadhūpairbalibhistathā|| 14.55 ||
lokāstvāṃ pūjayiṣyanti tato yāsyasi nirvṛtim|
gandhapuṣpaiśca saṃpūjya baliṃ dattvā ca pūrvavat|| 14.56 ||
svapnaṃ dṛṣṭvā dvijāyoktvā śobhanānyabhidhāya ca|
pūrvottaramukho mantrī japtvedaṃviṣṇurityṛcam|| 14.57 ||
madhvājyāktakuṭhāreṇa smṛtvā'straṃ chettumārabhet|
divā vārebhiretyevaṃ pātaṃ tasya samuddharet|| 14.58 ||
somendreśeṣu tatpātaḥ subho'nyatra na śasyate|
bhagne'smin kārayecchāntiṃ mahāvyāhṛtihomataḥ|| 14.59 ||
agnaāyāhimantreṇa patitaṃ sodhayed drumam|
caturaśraṃ tataḥ kṛtvā rathenāśmavadānayet|| 14.60 ||
mṛttikā'pyevamāneyā savarṇā doṣavarjitā|
puṇyādāyatanād ramyāduddhṛtāsīti cāhṛtā|| 14.61 ||
apakvaṃ pārthivaṃ śastaṃ pakvamatra vigarhitam|
kapilājyātasītailakaṣāyaiścūrṇasaṃyutām|| 14.62 ||
śrīveṣṭakādisaṃyuktāṃ peṣayitvā punaḥ punaḥ|
māsapakṣoṣitāṃ kṛtvā kārayet pratimāṃ tataḥ|| 14.63 ||
saṃskṛtaṃ pratimāśūlamadhivāsyāgramaṇḍape|
ratnanyāsaṃ purā kṛtvā muhūrte stāpayed guruḥ|| 14.64 ||
lohe sikthamayīmarcāṃ kārayitvā mṛdāvṛtām|
suvarṇādīni saṃśodhya vidrāvyāṅgāravat punaḥ|| 14.65 ||
kuśalaiḥ kārayed yatnāt sampūrṇāṃ sarvatoghanām|
puṣṭidā'rcā tu sauvarmī rājatī kirttidā smṛtā|| 14.66 ||
tāmrajā dhanaputrārthā maṇijā sukhadā bhavet|
dārujā śrīkarī jñeyā mṛnmayī sarvakāmadā|| 14.67 ||
ekaberaṃ śilāloharatnadhātumayaṃ bhavet|
bahuberaṃ tu mṛddārukṛtamālekhyameva ca|| 14.68 ||
prāsāda eva tu sthāpyā yā śilādārumṛnmayī|
pratimā'nyā gṛhe pūjyā prāsāde ca yathāvidhi|| 14.69 ||
aṅguṣṭhādivitastyantaṃ gṛhe saṃsthāpya pūjayet|
prāsāde cordhvamāhastāt paraṃ prāsāda eva tu|| 14.70 ||
jaṅgamaṃ pūjayennityamāvāhanavisargataḥ|
nāvāhanavisargābhyāmekaberaṃ pratiṣṭhitam|| 14.71 ||
karmārcāṃ lohajāṃ kṛtvā pādamātrāṃ śubhānanām|
mūlānurūpasaṃsthānāṃ śodhayitvā yathāvidhi|| 14.72 ||
tasyāmāvāhya sampūjya lepyādau visṛjet punaḥ|
dvārocce'ṣṭāṃśahīne syāt tribhakte'rcā dvibhāgikā|| 14.73 ||
navāṃśone samaste vā pādonā vā'pi tatsamā|
uttamaṃ pārthive mānaṃ dārave madhyamaṃ bhavet|| 14.74 ||
maṇije cādhamaṃ jñeyamicchayā'nyatra kalpayet|
uttamā daśatālārcā navatālā tu madhyamā|| 14.75 ||
aṣṭatālādhamā jñeyā devyaḥ syuścāṣṭatālikāḥ|
daśadhā navadhā ceṣṭaṃ mānaṃ kṛttvaikamaṃśakam|| 14.76 ||
bhaṅkttvā dvādaśadā jñeyaṃ svāṅgulaṃ yena mīyate|
grīvāhṛnnābhimeḍhrāntaṃ mukhamānena kalpayet|| 14.77 ||
tad bhaṅkttvā mūrdhni kaṇṭhāṅghrijānudeśe samaṃ nyaset|
jaṅghorū dviguṇau kāryau bāhū cājānulambinau|| 14.78 ||
kiñcidūnāḥ pramāṇena jñeyāścaivopabāhavaḥ|
kirīṭamadhikaṃ kuryāt padmaṃ nālaṃ ca mānataḥ|| 14.79 ||
tiryak ṣoḍaśasūtrāṇi saptordhvāni ca kalpayet|
tiryagūrdhvagataiḥ sūtrairmukhe dvādaśadhā kṛte|| 14.80 ||
keśapañcamasūtrasthaṃ madyaikāntaritaṃ dvayoḥ|
padayorakṣiṇī kārye padamānena vistṛte|| 14.81 ||
tayormadhye śubhā nāsā kāryākṣidviguṇāyatā|
tāre sṛkvāntasūtrasthe kaṃbīrau puṭasaṃmitau|| 14.82 ||
kuryād bhrūsūtragau karṇau netre netrāntarāyate|
tārake tatrtribhāgasthe jyotiṣī tatrtribhāgataḥ|| 14.83 ||
netravannāsikāgroccaṃ nimnaṃ mūlaṃ tadardhataḥ|
niṣpāvābhau nimnapuṭau bhrūmadyaṃ netramadhyavat|| 14.84 ||
adharastatsamo'nyoṣṭhagojikāmānamardhataḥ|
karṇāyāmastu nāsāvad vistāraśca tadagravat|| 14.85 ||
tuṭukau netrasūtrasthau tadvistāreṇa sammitau|
lalāṭakaṇṭhavistāro nāsikādviguṇo mataḥ|| 14.86 ||
cibuko karṇamūlāt tu nāsikā dviguṇāyatā|
stanāntaraṃ mukhaṃ vidyād dviguṇaṃ caiva kakṣayoḥ|| 14.87 ||
kaṇṭhād dviguṇa eveṣṭo nābhideśe tu vistaraḥ|
kaṭau netrādhikstryaṃśaṃ stanakakṣāntaraṃ viduḥ|| 14.88 ||
tāvat kakṣāṃsamadhyaṃ ca kārayet skandhamunnatam|
kaṭivat sandhikakṣāntaṃ prakoṣṭhaṃ nāsikādikam|| 14.89 ||
tatra madhyamayormānaṃ nāsikādharayogataḥ|
kalpayedardhaparvone tathā tarjanyanāmike|| 14.90 ||
tayorapyardhaparvone tathāṅguṣṭhakaniṣṭhike|
tale netrādhikāyāme madyamāyāmavistare|| 14.91 ||
tatrāṅguṣṭhapradeśinyornetrasammitamantaram|
antaraṃ madhyamātulyaṃ kaniṣṭhāmaṇibandhayoḥ|| 14.92 ||
maṇibandhastu nāsāvat tato'ṅguṣṭhāntaraṃ tathā|
dviguṇo bāhuvistāro netronaṃ caiva korpare|| 14.93 ||
sarvatra triguṇo'nāho bāhū gopucchavacchubhau|
madyamāparvaṇī pūrve same śeṣaṃ tadardhakam|| 14.94 ||
aṣṭāṃśonāni parvāṇi tato deśinyanāmayoḥ|
pādonāni kanīyasya yoktavyānyanurūpataḥ|| 14.95 ||
tarjanīmadhyamāparvasame cāṅguṣṭhaparvaṇī|
sarvāṅgulīṣu kāryāḥ syuragraparvārdhato nakhāḥ|| 14.96 ||
nāho'ṅguṣṭhasya nāsāvadaṣṭāṃśonastato'nyayoḥ|
ttataścaivaṃ kaniṣṭhāyā vṛtte bhujasame sphijau|| 14.97 ||
ūrū mukhavadakṣyūnaṃ jānu jaṅghā mukhārdhataḥ|
mukhāyatatalau pādau purastājjaṅghayā samau|| 14.98 ||
kūrmonnatau kramāduccau tatrāṅguṣṭhau tadardhakau|
pradeśinyau ca tattulye hīnāḥ śeṣā nakhānnakham|| 14.99 ||
anaṅguṣṭhāsriparvāṇo nakhā raktārdhacandravat|
nāho'ṅguṣṭhe'nyamadhyāvat tarjanyaṅguṣṭhadairghyavat|| 14.100 ||
aṣṭāṃśonāḥ kramādanyāḥ kārayedanurūpataḥ|
karṇapāśau ca keśāśca bhūṣāvastrāyudhāni ca|| 14.101 ||
sanniveśāśca yoktavyā yathāśebhaṃ vijānatā|
viśāladhavalātāmre pakṣmale caiva locane|| 14.102 ||
sādhāraṇīva dṛṣṭiḥ syāt paśyatāṃ nādhaūrdhvagā|
paṭe vā lekhayitvaivaṃ taṃ paṭaṃ sādhakaḥ svayam|| 14.103 ||
ekānte tu pratiṣṭhāpya pūjayennityamādarāt|
bimboccārdhaṃ tu pīṭhoccamāyāmo bimbamānataḥ|| 14.104 ||
keśāntamukhamāno vā pdamatulyo jalāśrayaḥ|
nādhikaṃ pīṭhamiṣṭaṃ tu jaṅgamasya ṣaḍaṅgulāt|| 14.105 ||
aṅgulābhyāṃ naca nyūnaṃ lohajasyaikayonikam|
śailamanyatra vijñeyaṃ ratnajānāṃ tu lohajam|| 14.106 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ caturdaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<15>
|| atha pañcadaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt pratiṣṭhāpañkaṃ kramāt|
parameṣṭhyādibhirmantraiḥ kartavyaṃ tu viśeṣataḥ|| 15.1 ||
sthāpanāstāpanā caiva tathā saṃsthāpanā punaḥ|
prasthāpanā ca pañcoktāḥ pratiṣṭhāpanayā saha|| 15.2 ||
sthitāsīnaśayānānāṃ yānagasya calasya ca|
yā kriyā pañcadhā proktā sā pratiṣṭheti kīrtitā|| 15.3 ||
sthāne saumyā bhavenmūrtiḥ saumyāgnaiyī tathāsane|
āgne'yī śayane'nyatra yathākāmaṃ tu kalpayet|| 15.4 ||
sāttvikī rājasī cāpi tāmasī ca yathākramāt|
sitā raktā'sitā mūrtiḥ śyāmā sarvatra vā bhavet|| 15.5 ||
sthitā tu daivikī mūrttiḥ parasya paramātmanaḥ|
kiñcinmānuṣasaṃyuktā tvāsīnā'nyā samocyate|| 15.6 ||
sthāne tu vyāpako viṣṇurvyāpyavyāpaka āsane|
vyāpyastu śayane jñeyaḥ savanatrayadevatāḥ|| 15.7 ||
pārśvayorbrahmarudrābhyāmarkendubhyāṃ tadantike|
pṛṣṭhe śriyā sarasvatyā śāntyā ratyā ca pārśvayoḥ|| 15.8 ||
varāhahayaśīrṣābhyāṃ tatpṛṣṭhe daśabhiḥ sthiḥ|
pārśve'jabhṛgudakṣaiśca rudrāgnimanubhistathā|| 15.9 ||
sanatkumāraskandābhyāṃ śrīsthānasthitasiddhibhiḥ|
āsīnaścāmakhyagrakarābhirdvādaśāvṛtaḥ|| 15.10 ||
pradyumnenāniruddhena nābhipadmasthitena ca|
pañcabhiḥ śayito'grasthamārkaṇḍeyamahīyutaḥ|| 15.11 ||
tridhoktānāṃ tu sarveṣāmeṣāmanyatamān kramāt|
yānagasya parīvārān yathākāmaṃ prakalpayet|| 15.12 ||
tathā dhanurgadācakraśaṅkhāsiśaralāṅgalaiḥ|
kheṭakena ca padmena vṛtaḥ sādhāraṇairbhate|| 15.13 ||
caturbhujaḥ sthito deva āsīno'ṣṭabhujo bhavet|
śayānasyecchayā yojyā bhujāḥ sarvatra vecchayā|| 15.14 ||
pañcopaniṣadastvetāḥ susūkṣmā devaśaktayaḥ|
pañcatanmātraśabdena kīrttitā vedavādibhiḥ|| 15.15 ||
pañcaitāḥ saṃhatā yasmācchaktayaḥ sarvahetavaḥ|
pañcabhiryukta evābhiḥ sa sargādau pravartate|| 15.16 ||
nigrahānugrahau cāpi karotīśaḥ svato'kriyaḥ|
sthāpanādiṣu sarvatra pūrṇaiśvaryastu nānyathā|| 15.17 ||
guṇapradhānato bhedāt saṃhatāsveva śaktiṣu|
vibhāgaḥ pañcadhā jñeyo naikaśaḥ pṛthagāsthiteḥ|| 15.18 ||
vicitrakāryakaraṇasambandho bhogakāraṇam|
ityanye śaktayaścāsya sakāryakaraṇāstathā|| 15.19 ||
ataḥ pratiṣṭhānipuṇairācāryaistantrapāragaiḥ|
saṃhatāstāḥ prayoktavyā bimbaiśvaryaprasiddhaye|| 15.20 ||
sthāpanādivibhāgaḥ syād bimbavinyāsajo'pyataḥ|
prādhānyakhyāpanārthaiva bhedoktiriti tadvidaḥ|| 15.21 ||
pañcaśaktimaye sūkṣme dehe sādhāraṇe punaḥ|
mūrttayastasya mūlāṅgaviśeṣād bahudhā smṛtāḥ|| 15.22 ||
vṛttāyate ca prāsāde caturaśrāyate tu vā|
sthāpyaṃ śayanamanyatra sthitāsīnayiyāsavaḥ|| 15.23 ||
yatra vā tatra vā kāle pratiṣṭhā muktaye kṛtā|
uttare tvayane kāryā śuklapakṣe ca muktaye|| 15.24 ||
punarvasvaśvinīśroṇapuṣyahastottaratraye|
revatīrohiṇīsvātībharaṇīṣu ca śasyate|| 15.25 ||
pratipacca dvitīyā ca pañcamī ca trayodaśī|
daśamī paurṇamāsī ca dvādaśī tithayastvimāḥ|| 15.26 ||
somo bṛhaspatiścaiva bhārgavo'tha budhastathā|
ete saumyagrahā yojyā vārodayanirīkṣaṇaiḥ|| 15.27 ||
saptamānnavamād vā'pi prāgeva syācchubhe'hani|
pañcamād vā'ṅkurāropastrikālokṣārcanānvitaḥ|| 15.28 ||
trividhaṃ pātramuddiṣṭaṃ maṅgalāṅkuraropaṇe|
pālikā ghaṭikā caiva śarāvaśceti bhedataḥ|| 15.29 ||
viṣṇubrahmaśivādhīśā nirdoṣāḥ sarvakarmasu|
pratyekaṃ dvādaśa grāhyāḥ pālikāḥ ṣoḍaśaiva vā|| 15.30 ||
pañcaviṃśāṅguloccāstāstadvaktraṃ ṣoḍaśāṅgulam|
ṣaḍaṅgulaṃ catuṣkoccaṃ pīṭhaṃ tatsandhiraṅgulam|| 15.31 ||
ghaṭikāmānamaṅgulyaḥ ṣoḍaśa dvādaśaiva vā|
pañca vaktrāṇi dikṣūrdhvaṃ mitāni caturaṅgulaiḥ|| 15.32 ||
pālikāpīṭhavat pīṭhaṃ nālaṃ tu caturaṅgulam|
tālāsyoccaṃ śarāvaṃ tu caturaṅgulapīṭhakam|| 15.33 ||
triṣaṅkttīkṛtya pātrāṇi sugupte sthāna uttare|
agnyādīśānaparyantaṃ sthāpayitvā prayatnataḥ|| 15.34 ||
mṛdvālukākarīṣaistu pūritānyuttarottaram|
dūrvādibhiralaṃkṛtya dhānyakūrcasthitāni ca|| 15.35 ||
yavamudgatilavrīhimāṣaśimbakulusthakān|
priyaṅgusarṣapaśyāmarājamāṣāḍhakīyutān|| 15.36 ||
dvādaśākṣaramantreṇa kṣālayecca śubhānimān|
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā''dau brāhmaṇaiḥ svasti vāṃcya ca|| 15.37 ||
aṣṭākṣareṇa bījāni kramāt teṣu vinikṣipet|
prādakṣiṇyena pūrvoktakramādavahito guruḥ|| 15.38 ||
jitaṃ te puṇḍarīkākṣa! namaste viśvabhāvana!|
subrahmaṇya! namaste'stu namaḥ puruṣa! pūrvaja!|| 15.39 ||
tritārakanamo'ntena tenaivaṃ savanatraye|
kūrcapāṇiḥ kramāt siñced gandhahāridravāriṇā|| 15.40 ||
devatānāmabhistāni gandhapuṣpaiśca pūjayet|
pātrāṇāṃ devatāḥ proktā bījānāmadhunā śṛṇu|| 15.41 ||
yavasarṣamudgeṣu brahmā rudro hariḥ kramāt|
vāyuḥ pūjyastu niṣpāve skandaścaiva priyaṅguke|| 15.42 ||
māṣeṣvindraḥ kulusthe'gniḥ śāliṣvarko yamastile|
varuṇo rājamāṣe śrīrāḍhakyāṃ śyāmagaḥ śaśī|| 15.43 ||
maṇḍaleṣu sakūrceṣu pūjayitvā'ṣṭadikkramāt|
rātrīśebhyo baliṃ dadyāt pārṣadāntaṃ jalānvitam|| 15.44 ||
palalaṃ rajanīcūrṇaṃ salājaṃ dadhisaktukam|
bhūtakrūrabaliṃ dadyāt pūrvaṃ bhūtapraharṣaṇam|| 15.45 ||
dvitīyaṃ paitṛkaṃ caiva dadyāt satilataṇḍulam|
dhānoḍumbikalājaistu tato yakṣabaliṃ haret|| 15.46 ||
nālikerapayassaktuśālipiṣṭavimiśritam|
caturthaṃ niśi vai dadyāt tato nāgabaliṃ budhaḥ|| 15.47 ||
pañcamaṃ tu baliṃ dadyāt brāhmaṃ padmākṣatairyutam|
carumā'pūpayuktena ṣaṣṭhaṃ śaivabaliṃ haret|| 15.48 ||
gulānnaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ caiva saptamaṃ tadanantaram|
pāyasaṃ kṛsaraṃ dadyādaṣṭamaṃ navamaṃ tathā|| 15.49 ||
śuddhavāsāḥ śucirbhūtvā niyatātmā haviṣyabhuk|
pratirātraṃ baliṃ dadyāt tataḥ snānaṃ samācaret|| 15.50 ||
prātaḥ karmadine dattvā paścāt karmaṇi yojayet|
praviśet gururevātra nānyaḥ ko'pi kathañcana|| 15.51 ||
ucchiṣṭādyaśubhaṃ sarvaṃ varjayecca prayatnataḥ|
adattvā tu baliṃ tatra na kuryāt pālikākriyām|| 15.52 ||
balyadāne tu naiṣphalyāt sarvathā'ṅkuraropaṇam|
balinā saha kartavyaṃ prayatnād diśikottamaiḥ|| 15.53 ||
maṇḍape stambhamūleṣu dhānyayukteṣu vinyaset|
karmārambheṣu pātrāṇi samyagrūḍhāṅkurāṇi tu|| 15.54 ||
śyāmeṣu dravyanāśaḥ syād rakteṣu kalaho dhruvam|
tiryaggateṣu rogaḥ syādaprarūḍhe mṛtirbhavet|| 15.55 ||
śubhaṃ pīteṣu śukleṣu sameṣūrdhveṣvṛjuṣvapi||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena sadā rakṣyāṇyatandritaiḥ|| 15.56 ||
yadā'ṅkurāṇi rohanti tadāprabhṛti nityaśaḥ|
mārjanaṃ grāmavīthīnāṃ kṛtvā''sicya samantataḥ|| 15.57 ||
nāstikonmattapāṣaṇḍipatitādīkṣitāśucīn|
tato nirvāsya yatnena yāgadravyāṇi saṃharet|| 15.58 ||
prāsādasyāgrataḥ kṛtvā maṇḍapaṃ caturaśrakam|
caturdvāraṃ vitānādiśobhitaṃ suparicchadam|| 15.59 ||
toraṇānyatha catvāri pañcahastocchritāni ca|
dhvajānaṣṭau patākāśca navamaṃ tārkṣyalakṣaṇam|| 15.60 ||
prāsādavistarotsedhān daśadvārāyataṃ dhvajam|
ghaṭā haimādayo grāhyā mārttikā vā navāḥ śubhāḥ|| 15.61 ||
lohajaṃ ca mahat pātraṃ kumbhaṃ droṇavahaṃ tathā|
prasthodvahāni catvāri carusthālīśca śobhanāḥ|| 15.62 ||
ājyasthālīṃ praṇītāṃ ca samiddarbhakuśāṃstathā|
sauvarmarājate cānye pātre dve prasṛtodvahe|| 15.63 ||
arghyādyarthāni cānyāni lauhānyabjanibhāni ca|
āyudhāni tathānyāni jātihemamayāni ca|| 15.64 ||
kūrmaṃ garuḍamabjaṃ tu ratnāni vividhāni ca|
lohān dhātūṃśca bījāni darvīsruvajuhūsrucaḥ|| 15.65 ||
mṛttikāghaṭikāḥ sūtrarajāṃsi kusumāni ca|
gandhaguggulutailāni dadhikṣīragulaṃ madhu|| 15.66 ||
gavyaṃ ghṛtaṃ ca homārthaṃ gomayaṃ mūtrameva ca|
kambalājinakhaṭvāśca śayanaṃ ca navaṃ śubham|| 15.67 ||
upadhānadvayopetaṃ sakapolasthaladvayam|
śuklāstaraṇasaṃyuktaṃ vastrāṇi vividhāni ca|| 15.68 ||
savatsāṃ kapilāṃ caukāṃ hemaśṛṅgīṃ payasvinīm|
surabhīśca catasro'nyā dohanāni śubhāni ca|| 15.69 ||
kaṣāyārthastvacaścaiva homadravyāṇi sarvaśaḥ|
bhūṣaṇāni ca ramyāṇī cakrikāpallavāṃstathā|| 15.70 ||
dūrvādyoṣadhijātāni phalāni madhurāṇi ca|
mūlānyudvarttanādīni haimaṃ yajñopavītakam|| 15.71 ||
maṅgalāni śubhānyaṣṭau kuṇḍikāṃ vardhanīṃ tathā|
śuṣkendhanāni dīpāṃśca sūtraṃ dhānyāni taṇḍulān|| 15.72 ||
caturvidhaṃ tathā vādyaṃ gāyakān narttakāṃstathā|
viprān vedavidaḥ śāntān vaiṣṇavāṃstu viśeṣataḥ|| 15.73 ||
śabdanyāyanimittajñān prayogjñāṃśca vaidikān|
muhūrttajñāṃśca kālajñān vratino mantrapāṭhakān|| 15.74 ||
yatīṃśca vimalajñānāṃstakṣakāṃśca viśeṣataḥ|
uktānuktaṃ ca yat sarvaṃ kārye dṛṣṭvā tadāharet|| 15.75 ||
yāgopakaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ sthānādi ca viśeṣataḥ|
sarvaṃ bhogena duṣṭaṃ syāt tasmāt sarvaṃ navaṃ śubham|| 15.76 ||
malakṣālanamātreṇa śuddhirbhavati laukikī|
devapūjāsu śāstroktaṃ snānaṃ syāt kāyaśodhanam|| 15.77 ||
lokasaṃvyavahāreṣu dharmaśāsrāntareṣu ca|
vihitāḥ śuddhayo grāhyā viṣṇubhaktena dhīmatā|| 15.78 ||
khananādīni duṣṭāyāḥ śodhanāni vidurbhuvaḥ|
śuddhāmapi tu yāgārthaṃ gomayenopalepayet|| 15.79 ||
tad dhenujaṃ navaṃ bhūsthaṃ kṣīrādyapi sugālitam|
ghṛtaṃ vilīnamutpūtaṃ sugandhīṣṭaṃ viśeṣataḥ|| 15.80 ||
gāṃ liptvā'streṇa saṃvidhya hastaṃ prakṣālya sammṛśet|
prokṣyākṣataiḥ sasiddhārthaiḥ kiret tat sthānaśodhanam|| 15.81 ||
toyena sāmlakalkena tāmraṃ haimaṃ tu vāriṇā|
rājataṃ gṛhadhūmena palālāṅgārakeṇa vā|| 15.82 ||
lojataṃ bhasmanā śudhyecchaṅkhādi lavaṇena ca|
mṛdā'dbhiḥ phalapātrāṇi dāravāṇi tu takṣaṇāt|| 15.83 ||
pārthivāni tu dāhena navānyevādadīta vā|
lepagandhāpanodena śuddhiḥ sarvasya kīrttivā|| 15.84 ||
sakūrce gandhapuṣpāḍhye jale tārābhimantrite|
dhyātvā mantramayaṃ jyotiḥ prokṣaṇaṃ dravyaśodhanam|| 15.85 ||
akṣārākaluṣaṃ śuddhaṃ bhūri nadyādisambhavam|
surasaṃ durjanāspṛṣṭamambhaḥ syāt sarvakarmasu|| 15.86 ||
lauhī pātravadarcā'pi śodhyā cūrṇaistu śailajā|
śodhanaṃ dāravasyaivaṃ citrasya malahānataḥ|| 15.87 ||
mārjanaṃ maṇijānāṃ tu dhātujānāṃ tu śailavat|
evaṃ saṃśodhya saṃhṛtya sṛṣṭvā'ṅgaiḥ sakalīkṛtam|| 15.88 ||
śudhyatyasthāvaraṃ beraṃ sthāvaraṃ snapanādibhiḥ|
mārjanādeva śudhyanti kṣālanād vā kuśādayaḥ|| 15.89 ||
sadarbhāsana āsīnaḥ pūjāsthānādadhaḥ sudhīḥ|
darbhapāṇiḥ sakūrcena vāriṇā mantratejasā|| 15.90 ||
prokṣaṇācchodhayet sarvamātmānaṃ dāraṇādibhiḥ|
nyāsaśca praṇavo darbhāḥ praśastā mantraśodhane|| 15.91 ||
āvṛttigaṇanaṃ mudrā dhyānaṃ ca parikīrtitam|
satyapriyahitairvāco jñānaṃ tuṣṭiśca cetasaḥ|| 15.92 ||
sarveṣāmapi vijñeyā bhaktirekā viśodinī|
sthalajaṃ karavīrādi jalajaṃ kamalādi ca|| 15.93 ||
avarjitaṃ navaṃ puṣpaṃ sugandhi vinivedayet|
yatkiñcit pītavarṇaṃ tu sitaṃ vā śucinā''hṛdatam|| 15.94 ||
avastrāhṛtamamlānaṃ puṣpaṃ syād grāhyamāpādi|
sacchidraṃ mukulaṃ jīrṇaṃ patitaṃ pātravarjitam|| 15.95 ||
bhuktaseṣamagandhaṃ ca keśakīṭādimiśritam|
puṣpamāpadyapi tyājyamarkapatragataṃ tathā|| 15.96 ||
pratiṣiddhāni puṣpāṇi susnāto'pi yadi spṛśet|
snāyāt punaraśaktaścedupaspṛśyārcayet prabhum|| 15.97 ||
svayaṃ patnyathavā putraḥ śiṣyo vā puṣpamāharet|
śuddhapāṇiḥ supātrasthaṃ pidhāyānātape nyaset|| 15.98 ||
rātripuṣpairyajed rātrau divāpuṣpairdivā tathā|
āpatkāle yathāyogaṃ prāptakālaṃ tu śasyate|| 15.99 ||
nirgandhamapi mandārapītakoraṇḍakādi yat|
praśastaṃ tadapi grāhyaṃ girikarṇyādivarjitam|| 15.100 ||
dūrvāpāmārgabhadrāṇāṃ tṛlasīgandhapūrṇayoḥ|
tamālāśvatthabilvānāṃ śamyāḥ kuśapalāśayoḥ|| 15.101 ||
patrāṇi dhātakībrāhmībhṛṅgāṇāmapi puṣpavat|
yavapriyaṅguniṣpāvamudgamāṣacaṇāṅkurān|| 15.102 ||
susaṃbhṛtānalūnāgrān puṣpālābhe prakalpayet|
puṣpaṃ svārāmajaṃ mukhyaṃ madhyamaṃ vanyamāhṛtam|| 15.103 ||
adhamaṃ krītamarcāyāṃ phalaṃ dātuḥ pratigrahe|
pṛthagvā saha vā piṣṭaiḥ sitāsrendurugāyasaiḥ|| 15.104 ||
kṛtrimairvā'rcayad gandhaiḥ prāṇyaṅgarahitaiḥ śubhaiḥ|
sālasarjapurāṇāṃ vā dārusevyendvayorujām|| 15.105 ||
dhūpamājyādisaṃyuktaṃ śubhe pātre nivedayet|
tailenājyena vā dīpaṃ dārusrehena vā''padi|| 15.106 ||
sūtravarttiyutaṃ tadyādamukhāniladīpitam|
triḥ kṣālitairanutsvinnairamalaiḥ śālitaṇḍulaiḥ|| 15.107 ||
śṛtaṃ ghṛtopadaṃśādiyutamannaṃ nivedayet|
kālapakvānyaduṣṭāni phalāni madhurāṇi ca|| 15.108 ||
alābhe kevalaiḥ puṣpairjalairvā manasā'rcayet|| 15.109 ||

||iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ pañcadaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<16>
|| atha ṣoḍaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepād bimbaśuddhiṃ yathākramam|
ācāryaṃ varayet pūrvaṃ tataḥ karmāṇi kārayet|| 16.1 ||
devastu dvividho jñeyaḥ sthāvaro jaṅgamastathā|
pratimā sthāvaro deva ācāryo jaṅgamastathā|| 16.2 ||
bhaktānukampī bhagavān vidhimantrapuraskṛtām|
pūjāṃ tayoḥ pragṛhṇāti yājyo nahyanyatā hariḥ|| 16.3 ||
ācāryānumatādeva kriyā kāryā vijānatā|
tatpūjayaiva devaśca varado nānyatā bhavet|| 16.4 ||
dīkṣādisaṃskṛtaḥ śānto vidvānakrodhano yuvā|
samayācārakṛd vāgmī varṇāśramaguṇānvitaḥ|| 16.5 ||
ācāryaḥ sarvathā grāhyaḥ sthāpanādiṣu yajvanā|
sarvarogopaśāntena bhadrākāreṇa dhīmatā|| 16.6 ||
śrotriyeṇa gṛhasthena sthāpyo devastu siddhaye|
kulīnāḥ śucayo dakṣā dīkṣitāstantrapāragāḥ|| 16.7 ||
bhaktā dakṣā vinītāśca viprā mūrtidharāḥ smṛtāḥ|
devāgāre gṛhe nādyāstīre goṣṭhe tarusthale|| 16.8 ||
anyatra vā śubhe sthāne durjanālokavarjite|
gomayena samālipte pañcagavyādiśodhite|| 16.9 ||
āsane vastrasaṃvīte sadarbhe prārthitaṃ gurum|
caturbhirmūrtipaiḥ sārdhamaṣṭadvādaśabhistu vā|| 16.10 ||
upaveśya yathānyāyaṃ pādyādibhirathārcayet|
pūrvottaradiśābhāge prāgagreṣu kuśeṣu ca|| 16.11 ||
yajamānaḥ śucirbhutvā pūjādravyāṇi sādayet|
praṇavena sakūrcāni pātrāṇi tu śubhāni ca|| 16.12 ||
vastrakuṇḍalayugmāni bhūṣaṇāni śubhāni ca|
kūrcasaṃskṛtatoyena prokṣyottānāni vai kramāt|| 16.13 ||
kūrceṣu praṇavenaiva vinyasyārdhyādi yojayet|
kuśāgrayavasiddhārthatilamāṣaphalākṣataiḥ|| 16.14 ||
sakṣīragandhapuṣpaiśca yojayitvā'rghyamarcayet|
pādyamācamanīyaṃ ca pātrayoranyayostathā|| 16.15 ||
mantrayet punaretāni mūlāṅgairmūrttibhistu vā|
dattvā kūrcānyathaiteṣāmāsanārthaṃ tu vāgyataḥ|| 16.16 ||
sapavitrairjalaiḥ pādau kṣalayeddhṛdayena tu|
śirasā''camanaṃ dadyācchikhayā cārghyameva ca|| 16.17 ||
vastrayugmāni bhūṣāśca śeṣābhyāṃ tu nivedayet|
pāyasaṃ bhojayet sājyaṃ praṇamya vidhinā'rcya ca|| 16.18 ||
soṣṇīṣaṃ gurumācāntamabhivādya prasādya ca|
yathālābhaṃ suvarṇādi dadyād devamanusmaran|| 16.19 ||
ṛtvigbhyo'pi tathā dattvā varayet praṇipatya tān|
kāryamuktvā vidhānena pūjayecca punaḥ pṛthak|| 16.20 ||
santoṣya priyavādaiśca praṇamya prārthayet tataḥ|
mayā sampūjitairatra dakṣiṇābhiśca toṣitaiḥ|| 16.21 ||
kriyatāṃ viṣṇuyāgo me prārthayāmi prasīdatu|
ityuktvā dakṣiṇe pāṇāvālambya varayet kramāt|| 16.22 ||
tatroṃ tathā kariṣyāmītyācāryastu samīrayet|
evaṃ vai sarvayāgeṣu guruṃ sampūjya yatnataḥ|| 16.23 ||
praṇipatyartvijastebhyo dattvā'rghyaṃ varayed budhaḥ|
punarevaṃ tu tantrajñān dīkṣitān hominastathā|| 16.24 ||
vedādhyayanakartṝṃśca śuddhān vai mantrapāṭhakān|
kalpayed yajamānastānācāryavacane sthitān|| 16.25 ||
vāstusaṅgrahamārabhya tīrthasnānānāvasānikam|
kārayet sarvametena bhaktyā harimivārcayet|| 16.26 ||
śayane sanniveśyādau bimbaṃ tu suparīkṣitam|
gṛhītaṃ dhanadānena pūjayitvā'tra śilpinaḥ|| 16.27 ||
nayanonmīlanaṃ tuṣṭaiḥ kārayecca yathāvidhi|
praṇavena tu kartavyaḥ pratimālokasaṅgrahaḥ|| 16.28 ||
plutaṃ praṇavamuccārya pādayostāṃ tadā spṛśet|
prakṣālya vāribhiḥ pūrvaṃ darbhaiḥ saṃghṛṣya sarvataḥ|| 16.29 ||
spṛṣṭvā govālakaiścaināṃ ghṛtāktāṃ kṣālayet tataḥ|
śamīpallavasaṃyuktānakṣatān saṃskṛte'nale|| 16.30 ||
bhūrādisatyaparyantaiḥ pratyekaṃ juhuyācchatam|
sthūladeho yato viṣṇurvairājaśrutidarśanāt|| 16.31 ||
tasmādevaṃ hute viṣṇoḥ sampūrṇā pratimā bhavet|
brāhmaṇaiḥ svasti vācyātha śākunaṃ sūktamuccaran|| 16.32 ||
dattvā dūrvākṣatān mūrdhni praṇavena samāhitaḥ|
nīrājanaṃ tataḥ kuryānmaṅgalāni ca darśayet|| 16.33 ||
aparāhṇe tu samprāpte mūrttipaiḥ sāhito guruḥ|
gatvā nadyāṃ taṭāke vā devakhāte manorame|| 16.34 ||
toyapūrṇe'thavā pātre sampūjya varuṇaṃ punaḥ|
sapatākaṃ taṭaṃ kṛtvā śodhitaṃ sarvato vṛtam|| 16.35 ||
saṃhṛtyāgnimayaṃ dhyātvā bimbaṃ saṃhāramārgataḥ|
darbhairvastraiśca saṃchādya phalakāyāṃ niveśya ca|| 16.36 ||
śaṅkhadundubhinirghoṣairjayaśabdaiśca saṃyutam|
yogapīṭhaṃ smarañ jyotirbimbamapsu niveśayet|| 16.37 ||
lokapālān bahirdhyātvā pañcamantraiśca rakṣayet|
prākśiraḥ śāyayitvaivaṃ bimbaṃ toye'dhivāsayet|| 16.38 ||
trirātramaśmano lohamekarātraṃ maṇiṃ niśām|
snātvā''camya śucirbhūtvā vastrālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ|| 16.39 ||
mūrttipaiḥ sārdhamāgamya prāsādaṃ sapradakṣiṇam|
nyāsādikaṃ kramāt kṛtvā dakṣaiṇe prāṅmukhaḥ śuciḥ|| 16.40 ||
prāsādaṃ śodhayet samyak muktamātraṃ tu takṣakaiḥ|
sūtreṇa darbhadāmnā ca veṣṭayitvā samantataḥ|| 16.41 ||
astradṛṣṭyā samālokya garbhāgāraṃ punastathā|
kuśāstramārjitaṃ toyaiḥ prokṣya gandhaiśca lepayet|| 16.42 ||
sarṣapākṣatadūrvābhiḥ samantād vikiret punaḥ|
tataścāstreṇa samprokṣya kuryāt tālatrayaṃ tathā|| 16.43 ||
dūrvākṣatakuśāgraistu vikiret pañcaśaktibhiḥ|
sarjaśraveṣṭalākṣābjaśvetalodhraphalādibhiḥ|| 16.44 ||
aṣṭākṣareṇa sarvatra dhūpaṃ dattvā sadīpakam|
pūrṇakumbhaṃ ca dhānyaṃ ca nyasya puṣpāstramocanam|| 16.45 ||
kṛtvā rākṣoghnasūktena pañcagavyena sarvataḥ|
cakramantreṇa ca prokṣya gomayenopalepite|| 16.46 ||
āgneyyāṃ sthaṇḍile homamārabheta yathāvidhi|
rakṣohaṇādimantrābhyāṃ pakvamājyaṃ ca homayet|| 16.47 ||
kṛṇuṣvāṣṭādaśairbhūyo yedevā iti pañcabhiḥ|
jātavedādibhiścaiva kramād vyāhṛtibhistathā|| 16.48 ||
apāmārgasamidbhistu śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tataḥ|
cakramantreṇa vai hutvā havirājyaṃ samāpayet|| 16.49 ||
vastreṇānīya pātre tu homabhasmasamāyutam|
ājyaśeṣamapāṃ śeṣamapāmārgarasairyutam|| 16.50 ||
mantrayeccakramantreṇa śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tataḥ|
palāśāśvatthaśākhabhiḥ prokṣya sarvatra mandire|| 16.51 ||
kṛṇuṣvetyādibhiḥ paścāt vikired yavasarṣapaiḥ|
tālatrayaṃ punaḥ kuryādagniprākārameva ca|| 16.52 ||
punaraṣṭaśataṃ japtvā cakraṃ śvabhraṃ ca pūjayet|
evaṃ kṛtvā'tha rākṣoghnaṃ niśi viprāṃśca bhojayet|| 16.53 ||
tamevaiśe'gnimādhāya sthaṇḍile vrīhitaṇḍulaiḥ|
tatpūrve sthaṇḍile kalṛpte vasrāstīrṇe ghaṭaṃ nyaset|| 16.54 ||
kuśadūrvāśamīparṇakūrcasthaṃ jalapūritam|
jātihema nyaset tasmin vidhinā śāntimarcayet|| 16.55 ||
pādyādibhiḥ kramād dravyaiḥ śannodevītyṛcā tataḥ|
vāstoṣpatetimantrābhyāṃ pakvaṃ hutvā ghṛtāhutīḥ|| 16.56 ||
vāstoṣpatedhruvetyādyaiḥ ṣaṅbhirjātādipañcabhiḥ|
śannavargeṇa juhuyāt punarvyāhṛtibhistathā|| 16.57 ||
śamīsamidghṛtānnājyaṃ tayaivarcā tu homayet|
dūrvābhiścātha mūlena sagavyābhiḥ samāpayet|| 16.58 ||
hutaśeṣaṃ tato dadyādagreṇāgniṃ kuśeṣu ca|
namorudreti toyājyaistvaṃ viprayajuṣokṣaṇam|| 16.59 ||
śāntitoyasamāyuktaṃ kuryācca svastivācanam|
puṇyāhaṃ mūrtipaiḥ sārdhaṃ śivaṃ vāstviti vācayet|| 16.60 ||
brāhmaṇān bhojayet paścāt pāyasaṃ dvādaśottamān|
maṇḍape śodhite sthāpya antastambheṣvṛgādayaḥ|| 16.61 ||
dvāreṣu ca samālāni toraṇāni yathākramam|
suśobhanākhyamāśvatthaṃ subhadrākhyamudumbaram|| 16.62 ||
naiyagrodhaṃ sukarmākhyaṃ suhotraṃ plākṣameva ca|
syonāpṛthivīmantreṇa prakṣipet sākṣatāṃstilān|| 16.63 ||
ucchrayasveti saṃsthāpya prādakṣiṇyena pūjayet|
dikṣu pīto'ruṇaḥ śyāmaḥ kṛṣṇo'cchaḥ kṛṣṇapāṇḍaraḥ|| 16.64 ||
padmavarṇaśca citro'ṣṭau dhvajāḥ sthāpyā yathākramam|
kumudaḥ kumudākṣaśca puṇḍarīko'tha vāmanaḥ|| 16.65 ||
śaṅkukarṇaḥ sarvanetraḥ sumukhaḥ supratiṣṭhitaḥ|
ityete pūjanīyāśca dhvajānāmadhidevatāḥ|| 16.66 ||
anekakoṭisaṃghaiśca bhūtaiḥ pratyekaśo vṛtāḥ|
pūrṇakumbhān pratidvāraṃ dvau dvau vinyasya pūjayet|| 16.67 ||
padmarāgapravālāpsu prāk pūjyau pūrṇapuṣkarau|
vaiḍūryapuṣyatoyasthau tataivānandanandanau|| 16.68 ||
vīrasenasuṣeṇau ca nīle maratake tathā|
sambhavaprabhavau caiva muktāsphaṭikatoyayoḥ|| 16.69 ||
vāstupūjāṃ purā kṛtvā toraṇadhvajapūjanam|
bhūmeḥ parigraho'gneśca saṃskāro'nantaraḥ smṛtaḥ|| 16.70 ||
trātāraṃ yadulūko'tha imaṃ me santa? ityṛcaḥ|
dikpālakalaśārcāyāṃ tanmantrā iha kīrtitāḥ|| 16.71 ||
bahvṛco'dhvaryavaścaiva chandogātharvaṇā dvijāḥ|
dharmaśāsrārthatattvajñā dikṣu pārāyaṇe smṛtāḥ|| 16.72 ||
aitihāsikavākyārthatattvajñā brahmāvittamāḥ|
koṇeṣu mūrtipā evaṃ kalpyā homeṣvanukramāt|| 16.73 ||
svāṃ svāṃ vidyāṃ paṭhantaste taccittā niśi jāgṛyuḥ|
svastike mūlavinyastadhānye'ṣṭakakuśānvite|| 16.74 ||
dvādaśākṣaramantreṇa śayanaṃ tatra kalpayet|
kalaśān sthāpayedaṣṭau pīṭhasya paritaḥ kramāt|| 16.75 ||
adbhiḥ sampūritān sarvān dhānyarāśigatān śubhān|
sapallavamukhān sūtraveṣṭitān vastraveṣṭitān|| 16.76 ||
vyastairaṣṭākṣarairetān gandhapuṣpaiśca pūjayet|
yogapīṭhaṃ prakalpyātra śaktiparyantamarcayet|| 16.77 ||
sarvadhānyaiśca pūṣpaiśca nirvapet pīṭhaparyayāt|
vidyādhipatayaścātra pūjanīyāḥ samantataḥ|| 16.78 ||
viṣṇumaindre tatastatra dakṣiṇe madhusūdanam|
trivikramaṃ tu vāruṇyāṃ vāmanaṃ tu tathottare|| 16.79 ||
śrīdharaṃ tu tathāgnyyāṃ hṛṣīkeśaṃ tu nairṛte|
padmanābhaṃ ca vāyavyāṃ dāmodaramathāpare|| 16.80 ||
aiśānyāṃ tasya pīṭhasya sthāpayejjalabhājanam|
soṣṇīṣaḥ sottarīyaśca vāgyato vāri gālayet|| 16.81 ||
navenāromavastreṇa prokṣitena ca mantrataḥ|
pūrayet praṇavenaiva pātraṃ kusumavāriṇā|| 16.82 ||
pūjayet pūrayitvā tu dvādaśākṣaravidyayā|
rakṣitavyaṃ vitānādyaiḥ samyagācchādya sarvataḥ|| 16.83 ||
maṅgalāṅkurapātrāṇi maṅgalāni ca vinyaset|
śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ patākāṃ ca śrīvatsaṃ darpaṇaṃ vṛṣam|| 16.84 ||
matsyayugmaṃ ca kumbhaṃ ca maṅgalāni pracakṣate|
kuryānmaṇḍapamaiśānyāṃ caturaśraṃ samantataḥ|| 16.85 ||
caturhastaṃ catuḥ stambhaṃ madhye pīṭhasamanvitam|
snānadravyaiśca saṃyuktaṃ kramāt kumbheṣu pūritaiḥ|| 16.86 ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā ghṛtamaṣṭākṣareṇa tu|
sampātājyena saṃspṛśya kalaśānabhimantrayet|| 16.87 ||
hiraṇyavarṇāṃ hariṇīmiti sarvān yathākramam|
tatrārcāṃ tu samānīya snāpayedekaberake|| 16.88 ||
ratnanyāsaṃ purā kṛtvā lepyārcā kriyate yadā|
pratimā'nyā tu kartavyā lohajā karmakāraṇāt|| 16.89 ||
pratiṣṭhānyāsamudrādi mūlārcāyāṃ prakalpayet|
snānādiśeṣakarmāṇi karmārcāyāmiti sthitiḥ|| 16.90 ||
mūlārcāṃ vā'pi karmārcāṃ tathā toye'dhivāsitām|
uddhṛtya vidhṛtāṃ yatnāccaturbhirmūrttidhārakaiḥ|| 16.91 ||
darbhapāṇiḥ sthito vidvānupātiṣṭheta saṃsthitām|
namaste'rce! sureśāni! pratīke viśvakarmaṇaḥ|| 16.92 ||
prabhāvitāśeṣajagaddhātri! tubhyaṃ namo namaḥ|
tvayi sampūjayāmīśaṃ nārāyaṇamanāmayam|| 16.93 ||
rahitā yonidoṣaistvamṛddhiyuktā sadā bhava|
vijñāpyaivaṃ dukūlāṃśusiddhārthakayutaṃ śubham|| 16.94 ||
urṇāsūtreṇa badhnīyāt kautukaṃ dakṣiṇe kare|
aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ tu sārayitvā jitādinā|| 16.95 ||
aṣṭākṣareṇa saṃjaptaṃ saptadhā vighnaśāntaye|
tato'rcāṃ tu suvastrāḍhyāṃ brahmayānena tāṃ nayet|| 16.96 ||
śākunenaiva sūktena vitānādyupaśobhitām|
trīṇītyāsthāpya pīṭhe tāṃ bhadraṃ karṇeti kīrtayet|| 16.97 ||
tejo'sīti samālipya gavyenājyena sādhakaḥ|
cūrṇaistoyaiśca saṃkṣālya snāpayet tairyathākramam|| 16.98 ||
mṛtkaṣāyauṣadhītīrthairgavyairvyastaiḥ samastakaiḥ|
nadyāstaṭābhyāṃ saṅgṛhya mṛdaṃ valmīkajāṃ tathā|| 16.99 ||
kulīravāsataḥ kṣetrād viṣāṇāgrācca veditaḥ|
śamīpalāśakhadirabilvāśvatthavikaṅkatāḥ|| 16.100 ||
udumbarāśca nyagrodhāḥ kaṣāyāṅgāni caiva hi|
śaṅkhapuṣpīṃ priyaṅguṃ ca viṣṇukrāntīṃ śatāvarīm|| 16.101 ||
dūrvāṃ caivāmṛtāṃ caiva gṛhṇīyād rajanīṃ vacām|
hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ samudrajyeṣṭheti ca kramāt|| 16.102 ||
idamāpa imaṃ me ca tīrthaṃ taṃ viniyojayet|
gāyatrtryā caiva gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam|| 16.103 ||
āpyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇeti vai dadhi|
tejo'si śukramityājyaṃ devasyatvā kuśodakam|| 16.104 ||
pañcagavyaṃ payasvatyā yāsāṃ gandhodakaṃ tathā|
āpohiṣṭheti vai dadyāt svacchatoyaṃ tataḥ param|| 16.105 ||
parameśvaramantreṇa snāpayet sakalena ca|
madhuvāteti saṃyojya taijase madhusarpiṣī|| 16.106 ||
uddhāṭya nayane cāsya tato hemaśalākayā|
citraṃ devatyṛcā caikāpyāyasvetyṛcā param|| 16.107 ||
yajamānaśca gāṃ dadyād govindaḥ prīyatāmiti|
snāpayet kalaśaiḥ paścāccaturbhiḥ koṇagairapi|| 16.108 ||
brahmaśrīcampakakṣīranīpāmrabalulārjunaiḥ|
sāśokaiḥ pallavairmantrī haṃsaḥ śuciṣadityṛcā|| 16.109 ||
padmakaṃ rocanāṃ dūrvāṃ darbhastambācchasarṣapān|
candane jātirāvarttaṃ kundaṃ viṣṇorarāṭayā|| 16.110 ||
yavavrīhitilā hema rajataṃ kūlamṛddvayam|
gomayaṃ bhūmyasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ somaṃ rājānamityṛcā|| 16.111 ||
samīvaryamṛtākrāntisahābhṛṅgamahauṣadhīḥ|
syāmākaṃ viśvataścakṣuriti taiḥ snānamācaret|| 16.112 ||
āvāhayettato devaṃ pītāmbaradharaṃ harim|
ehyehi bhagavan! viṣṇo! lokānugrahakāraka!|| 16.113 ||
yajñabhāgaṃ gṛhāṇemaṃ vāsudeva! namo namaḥ|
pādyamācamanaṃ cārdhyaṃ dadyānmūlena vai tataḥ|| 16.114 ||
ahate vāsasī dadyāt pavitraṃ cāṣṭakena tu|
sauvarṇaṃ dāpayet sūtraṃ viṣṇugāyatriyā punaḥ|| 16.115 ||
bhūṣaṇāni tato dadyād dvādaśākṣaravidyayā|
nīrājanaṃ tataḥ kuryād gandhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ|| 16.116 ||
sragbhiśca dhūpadīpaiśca phalagorocanāñjanaiḥ|
pādukāvyajanacchatramadhuparkaiśca pūjayet|| 16.117 ||
vāridhārāṃ ca mūlena pātayed devapādayoḥ|
ācāmaṃ ca punardhūpaṃ mukhavāsaṃ ca darpaṇam|| 16.118 ||
aṅgarāgaṃ ca mālāṃ ca dadyāt puṣpāñjaliṃ tataḥ|
sāmabhiśca tataḥ stutvā pavitraṃ stotramīrayet|| 16.119 ||
puṇyāhaṃ cātra kurvīta vaiṣṇavaiḥ saha mantravit|
tilāśca dakṣiṇā deyā vaiṣṇavebhyo yathāvidhi|| 16.120 ||
samāhitaḥ smaret paścād bhaktyā tu puruṣottamam|
evaṃ kṛtvā tu tāmarcāmānayecchayanaṃ prati|| 16.121 ||
pauruṣeṇa tu sūktena śaṅkhabheryādinisvanaiḥ|
dvādaśākṣaramantreṇa śayane sannaveśayet|| 16.122 ||
ayaṃ sa iti coktvā'nte sarvayogeśvaromiti|
japedaṣṭākṣaraṃ mantraṃ maṅgalāni ca vinyaset|| 16.123 ||
hemapātre ghṛtaṃ pūrṇaṃ śiraḥ sthāne'sya vinyaset|
rājataṃ madhupūrṇaṃ ca pādasthāne vicakṣaṇaḥ|| 16.124 ||
sakṣīraṃ tāmrapātraṃ ca dakṣiṇena prakalpayet|
lohapātragataṃ vāme śāntitoyaṃ ca vinyaset|| 16.125 ||
mṛtpātre pañcagavyaṃ ca pañcamaṃ tu tadagrataḥ|
teṣāmālabhanaṃ kuryāt parameṣṭhyādibhiḥ kramāt|| 16.126 ||
caturaśraṃ dhanurvṛttaṃ trikoṇaṃ ca yathākramam|
kārayed dikṣu catvāri kuṇḍāni kuśalo guruḥ|| 16.127 ||
aratnimātre pīṭhasya samantāddhastamānataḥ|
trimekhalāni sarvāṇi kāryāṇyardhāvaṭāni ca|| 16.128 ||
ācāryakuṇḍāduddhṛtya paścādanyeṣu nikṣipet|
kuṇḍeṣu saṃskṛtaṃ vahniṃ kṛtvollokhādikaṃ kramāt|| 16.129 ||
jananādivihīnaṃ tu prakṛtinyāsapūrvakam|
svasākhāgnimukhānte'nnaṃ gāyatryā juhuyuḥ kramāt|| 16.130 ||
hṛdayaṃ pūrvakuṇḍe tu dakṣiṇe tu śirastathā|
paścime tu śikhāṃ kuryāduttare kavacaṃ tathā|| 16.131 ||
netrāstrayorna kāryo'tra homaḥ syānmūrtibhistu vā|
samidājyacarun lājān sarṣapāṃśca yavāstilān|| 16.132 ||
vrīhīnājyaṃ punaścaivaṃ sarvakuṇḍeṣu homayet|
mūlenāṣṭasahasraṃ tu tadācāryastu homayet|| 16.133 ||
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ cātra pūrṇā dadyāt śate śate|| 16.134 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ ṣoḍaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<17>
|| atha saptadaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepādadhivāsanamuttamam|
yadyogājjāyate'rcāyāṃ sadā sannihito hariḥ|| 17.1 ||
astradagdho karau jātāvanyau tattvamayau smaret|
praṇavāṅguṣṭhakau kāryau tau svarāṅguliparvakau|| 17.2 ||
sabindukatalau sargapuṣṭhagau vāmadakṣiṇau|
vyañjanāni pṛthaṅ nyasya nādamaṅguṣṭhayornyaset|| 17.3 ||
recayitvā tato vāyuṃ praviśet tatra pūrake|
vidyākośaṃ tataḥ paścādānupūrvyeṇa mantravit|| 17.4 ||
mahābhūtamayaṃ dehaṃ dharmādharmasamanvitam|
śuṣkaṃ dagdhaṃ kramād dhyātvā plāvitaṃ ca svabījakaiḥ|| 17.5 ||
bindurūpayutaṃ dhyāyedātmānaṃ vyomnyavasthitam|
yathā siktād bhaved bījādaṅkurastadvadātmanaḥ|| 17.6 ||
prabhavaṃ cintayed divyaṃ praṇavatvamupāgatam|
triguṇāmaṣṭadhīyuktaṃ prakṛtiṃ tāṃ vidurbudhāḥ|| 17.7 ||
ādyāvakārau buddhyākhyau ikārau cāpyahaṅkṛtiḥ|
mano dvau tatparau vyomapañcatattvaṃ svarāntikam|| 17.8 ||
ātmajīvodaraśrotrajihvāghrākṣitvacaḥ kramāt|
ūṣmāntasthāḥ śiraḥkūṭaṃ bāhujaṅghe ca vargaśaḥ|| 17.9 ||
kavargo'ṅgulayaḥ sarvāḥ sthūladeho'yamīdṛśaḥ|
sūryāmbujasthaṃ vāgīśaṃ vāgīśvaryā samanvitam|| 17.10 ||
sarvaśaktiyutaṃ mantramaṣṭabāhuṃ hariṃ smaret|
divākarasahasrābhaṃ durnirīkṣyaṃ surāsuraiḥ|| 17.11 ||
brahmarandhreṇa dehāntaḥ praviśantaṃ vicintayet|
vyāpakaṃ vinyasenmūlamaṅgāni ca yathākramam|| 17.12 ||
ātmānaṃ sarvagaṃ dhyātvā sarvajñaṃ viṣṇumavyayam|
kuryād bhāvānvito viṣyaṇoradhivāsanamuttamam|| 17.13 ||
bimbābimāninaṃ jīvaṃ tārakalpitamāhṛtam|
dhyānenātmaikatāṃ nītvā pṛthivyādīni saṃharet|| 17.14 ||
śuṣkadagdhāplutorvyapsu tā vahnau so'nile sa khe|
pañcatattvamayaṃ viśvaṃ vilīnaṃ vyomni bhāvayet|| 17.15 ||
tanmanogarvadhīmāyākramāt tāreśvaraṃ nayet|
tāṃ svāmavyākṛtiṃ māyāmadhiṣṭhāya sisṛkṣayā|| 17.16 ||
nādātmā parameṣṭhyādipañcakaṃ sakalaṃ sṛjet|
praṇavātmā svayaṃ viṣṇurnakārādiṣu mūrtayaḥ|| 17.17 ||
vāsudevādayaḥ kāntāḥ khādi kṣmāntaṃ ca pañcakam|
sthūladeho'yamākhyātastasya viṣṇoḥ ṣaḍātmanaḥ|| 17.18 ||
so'niruddhābjasambhūtaḥ pṛthivyātmā prajāpatiḥ|
sargabhinnādato viṣṇorjāta ityaja ucyate|| 17.19 ||
sa sṛṣṭvā pūrvamaptattvaṃ sasarjāṇḍaṃ ca pārthivam|
evaṃ sṛṣṭvā jātā caturdaśajaganmayī|| 17.20 ||
tadaṇḍaṃ pañcabhūtātmabhūtaṃ śubhraṃ hiraṇmayam|
bimbaṃ dhyātvā svayaṃ viṣṇurjīvaṃ saṃkrāmayeddhṛtam|| 17.21 ||
sa viṣṇustārajīvātmā nādibhiḥ pañcaśaktibhiḥ|
lipimūlāṅgavinyāsaiḥ sakalo'ṣṭabhujo bhavet|| 17.22 ||
śayane sthāpayitvā'rcāṃ tato hutvā ghṛtaṃ kramāt|
kuryāt ṣoḍaśakaṃ nyāsaṃ nāhutvā nyāsa iṣyate|| 17.23 ||
tāravyāhṛtilipyṛkṣakālavarṇaja (lā?) laṃ śrutiḥ|
vairājadevatāyajñaguṇāstrāṇi yathākramam|| 17.24 ||
taiḥ śaktimantrajīvāntairnyāsaḥ ṣoḍaśadā smṛtaḥ|
homayet praṇavaṃ pūrvaṃ mātrātrayavibheditam|| 17.25 ||
brahmāṇaṃ keśavaṃ rudraṃ pādahṛnmastake nyaset|
āhutyaṣṭaśataṃ cātra hotavyaṃ tairanukramāt|| 17.26 ||
vyāhṛtitritayaṃ caiva trailokyaprīṇanāya vai|
akārādikṣakārāntaṃ mātṛkāṃ homayet tataḥ|| 17.27 ||
tālvāsyavīkṣaṇaśrotradantoṣṭheṣu kajihvayoḥ|
asaṃvṛtā nasorgaṇḍadvitaye caiva saṃvṛtāḥ|| 17.28 ||
nābhihṛcchroṇijaṭharaśrotradantāgranāsikāḥ|
dṛṣṭistvagiti kūṭādya dorjaṅghāṅgulayastathā|| 17.29 ||
nakṣatragrahatārāśca hutvā'ṅgeṣu niveśayet|
dṛśorurodhīvāgretolalāṭaṅghrikace grahāḥ|| 17.30 ||
rohiṇyādikramādevaṃ nakṣatrāṇi ca homayet|
hṛtkakeśalalāṭāsyanāsikādaśaneṣvatha|| 17.31 ||
śrutyorbāhvoḥ karadvandve stanayoḥ kukṣipārśvayoḥ|
kaṭyāṃ cakṣuṣi liṅge'ṇḍe pāyāvūrvośca jaṅghayoḥ|| 17.32 ||
pādayośca kramādevaṃ kṛttikāntoḍukalpanā|
dhruvo nābhyāṃ sthitastasya kaṇṭhe saptarṣayastathā|| 17.33 ||
kaṭideśe sthitaṃ tasya vijñeyaṃ mātṛmaṇḍalam|
padāni trīṇi pāde'sya vanamālā tu vīthikā|| 17.34 ||
romāṇi tārakāsaṃghā agastyaḥ kaustubho maṇiḥ|
kālarūpaṃ yajet pūrvaṃ saṃvatsaratanuṃ harim|| 17.35 ||
kāsyahṛddvistanakroḍakaṭidvyūrunalāṅghriṣu|
caitrādayaḥ sthitā māsā dvādaśaivaṃ saphālgunāḥ|| 17.36 ||
utpūrvaścānupūrvaśca paripūrvaśca vatsarāḥ|
sampūrvaśceti vijñeyāḥ catvārastasya bāhavaḥ|| 17.37 ||
parvāṇyasyāṅgaparvāṇi ṛtavaścāṅgasaṃjñakāḥ|
ahorātrāṇi cāsthīni romaṇyalpāḥ kṣaṇādayaḥ|| 17.38 ||
kṛtādi mukhahṛcchroṇipāde yugacatuṣṭayam|
manvantarāṇi bāhvośca vibhāgena prakalpayet|| 17.39 ||
parārdhadvitayaṃ jaṅghe mahākalpaṃ śiro viduḥ|
ayane dve pade caivamuktaḥ saṃvatsarākṛtiḥ|| 17.40 ||
mukhabāhūrūpādasthā varṇāśca brāhmaṇādayaḥ|
pādāṅgulīṣu vijñeyā varṇāḥ saṅkarajātayaḥ|| 17.41 ||
sarvāṅgasandhiṣu tathā utkṛṣṭāḥ saṅkarāstu ye|
gāvo muke'sya vijñeyāḥ stanayoścāpyajāvikāḥ|| 17.42 ||
padorekaśaphā ūrvorgrāmyāraṇyā vyavasthitāḥ|
keśeṣu meghā vijñeyā romāṇyabhrāṇi tasya vai|| 17.43 ||
sarvāṅgāvayavā nadyaḥ samudrāḥ kukṣisaṃsthitāḥ|
yajñāstasya śiro jñeyamṛgvedo dakṣiṇo bhujaḥ|| 17.44 ||
sāmavedaḥ smṛto vāmaḥ sarvopaniṣado hṛdi|
itihāsapurāṇāni jaṅghe yajururaḥ smṛtam|| 17.45 ||
atharvāṅgiraso nābhiḥ kalpasūtrāṇi pādayoḥ|
śīkṣāvyākaraṇaṃ vaktraṃ tarkaḥ kaṇṭhaṃ samāśritaḥ|| 17.46 ||
mīmāṃsā hṛnniruktaṃ ca chando jyotiśca cakṣuṣī|
gāruḍaṃ bhūtatantraṃ ca śrotrayugme vyavasthitam|| 17.47 ||
hastayośca dhanurveda āyurvedaśca kīrttitaḥ|
yogaśāstrāṇi hṛdyeva nītiśāstrāṇi pādayoḥ|| 17.48 ||
vāggītaṃ karayornṛttaṃ kalā vidyāśca romasu|
dyaurmūrdhendvarkanetrasya ghrāṇe prāṇātmako'nilaḥ|| 17.49 ||
nābhyāṃ vyomābdhayo bastau pāde bhūḥ sacalācalā|
hiraṇyagarbhaṃ mūrdhnyasya sarpān keśeṣu vinyaset|| 17.50 ||
rudraṃ lalāṭe bhrukuṭāvantakaṃ śravaṇe'śvinau|
mukhe vaiśvānaraṃ nyasya jihvāyāṃ ca sarasvatīm|| 17.51 ||
rudrān datsu vasūn kaṇṭhe dṛśyādityān niveśayet|
bāhvorindraṃ baliṃ caiva prahlādaṃ vāmake stane|| 17.52 ||
dakṣiṇe viśvakarmāṇaṃ nāradaṃ kukṣisaṃsthitam|
anantaṃ vāmakukṣisthaṃ varuṇaṃ bastisaṃsthitam|| 17.53 ||
mitraṃ pāyau kaṭau tārkṣyaṃ viśvān devān tathoragān|
pitṝn jānudvaye yakṣān nale gulphe tu rākṣasān|| 17.54 ||
piśācān prapade pārṣṇyornyased vidyādharāṃstathā|
grahāṃstale nakheṣveva jambhakādīn sapūtanān|| 17.55 ||
aṅgulyo'psarasaḥ kaṭyāṃ kārttikeyagaṇeśvarau|
gandharvānoṣṭhayornyasya sarvabhūtāni pṛṣṭhataḥ|| 17.56 ||
matsyaṃ ke pādayoḥ kūrmaṃ varāhaṃ jaṅghayornyaset|
narasiṃhaṃ lalāṭe tu vāmanaṃ mukhamadhyataḥ|| 17.57 ||
nābhihṛtkaṭiguhyeṣu rāmān kṛṣṇaṃ ca vinyaset|
kalkinaṃ jānudeśe tu naranārāyaṇau pade|| 17.58 ||
nyased bāhuṣu caivāsya tathā bāhusahasriṇam|
aśvamedhaḥ śirastasya naramedho lalāṭakam|| 17.59 ||
rājasūyo mukhaṃ caiva gausavaḥ kaṇṭhasaṃsthitaḥ|
dvādaśāhaśca hṛdayamahīno nābhisaṃsthitaḥ|| 17.60 ||
viśvajid viśvamedhaṃ ca kaṭideśe vyavasthitau|
agniṣṭomo'sya liṅgastho vṛṣaṇe cātirātrakaḥ|| 17.61 ||
aptoryāmastathorusthaḥ ṣoḍaśī jānugocare|
ukthyaśca vājapeyaśva nale tvagnistu pādayoḥ|| 17.62 ||
cāturmāsyāni caivāsya bāhuṣveva niyojayet|
sautrāmaṇiṃ tale nyasya paśviṣṭiṃ cāṅgulīgatām|| 17.63 ||
darśaśca paurṇamāsaśca dṛśo romāṇi ceṣṭayaḥ|
svāhākāravaṣaṭkārau stanayoḥ saṃvyavasthitau|| 17.64 ||
bāhvoraṅgulayaḥ pañcamahāyajñāḥ prakīrtitāḥ|
hṛdayaṃ dakṣiṇā tasya darbhāḥ keśāstvagambaram|| 17.65 ||
mukhamāhavanīyāgnirhṛnnābhī dakṣiṇāparau|
pravargyo bhūṣaṇaṃ tasya savanāni gatitrayam|| 17.66 ||
pākayajñā haviryajñāścaraṇāṅgulayaḥ smṛtāḥ|
ājyabhāgau dṛśau jñeyau vedī śroṇī mukhaṃ haviḥ|| 17.67 ||
homena prīṇayet pūrvaṃ tanuṃ tasya guṇātmikām|
dharmaṃ tu vinyasenmūrdhni daśadhā saṃvyavasthitam|| 17.68 ||
jñānaṃ hṛdyaṣṭadhā guhye vairāgyaṃ navadhā nyaset|
aiśvaryaṃ cāṣṭadhā pāde seyaṃ guṇamayī tanuḥ|| 17.69 ||
śaṅkhacakragadāpadmanyāsaḥ sādhāraṇaḥ smṛtaḥ|
viśeṣā ye ca tantroktā mūrttibhedena tānyapi|| 17.70 ||
hṛdi śāntirlalāṭe śrīrmukhe cāsya sarasvatī|
ratirguhye nale prītiḥ kirttistu paritā bhavet|| 17.71 ||
kukṣau tuṣṭistanau puṣṭiḥ śaktinyāso'yamīritaḥ|
mantranyāso'kṣarāṅgātmā pūrvameva samīritaḥ|| 17.72 ||
śrīvatsamurasi nyasya kaustubhaṃ nābhimaṇḍale|
vanamālāṃ gale nyasya mudrānyāso'yamīritaḥ|| 17.73 ||
nābheradhastādutthāpya karaṇaiśca samanvitam|
tanmātradehaprāṇaiśca pañcabhirvyāpya vigraham|| 17.74 ||
aiśvaryadharmajñānaiśca vairāgyeṇa ca saṃyutam|
sphuradraśmipratānena vyāptāśeṣadigantaram|| 17.75 ||
divākarasahasrābhamiḍayā brahmarandhragam|
sthitvā kiñcit lalāṭe'ntaḥ praviśantaṃ vicintayet|| 17.76 ||
śīrṣaṇyāṃstu daśa prāṇān tatrastho'pi niyojayet|
karṇikādvāramārgeṇa praviśya hṛdayāmbujam|| 17.77 ||
vṛttipañcakabhedena prāṇādīṃstatra yojayet|
pādanāsāntare prāṇamapānaṃ pṛṣṭhato nyaset|| 17.78 ||
vyānaṃ sarvagataṃ caiva udānamudarordhvagama|.
samānaṃ nābhihṛdaye nāgādīnyatha pañca vai|| 17.79 ||
puryaṣṭakaṃ hṛdi nyasya tanmadhye puruṣottamam|
anugrahakaraṃ saumyaṃ sarvajñaṃ viśvatomukham|| 17.80 ||
dhyāyedevaṃ kṛte sā'rcā sajīvā jāyate kṣaṇāt|
adhivāse na sā kāryā nyāsāḥ pañcadaśaiva tu|| 17.81 ||
sajīvakaraṇaṃ kuryāt sthāpite madhusūdane|
jīvanyāsakramaṃ yastu na jānātyupadeśataḥ|| 17.82 ||
ācāryo na sa mantavyo nigrāhyastaskaro yathā|
etasminneva kāle tu piṇḍikāṃ cādhivāsayet|| 17.83 ||
nyāsakramastu tatrāpi kathyamāno'vadhāryatām|
śodhayitvā purā darbhamṛttvaggavyāmbubhiḥ kramāt|| 17.84 ||
tatrādhivāsayed vidvān prakṛtiṃ piṇiḍikātmikām|
mātṛkāṃ vinyaset tatra śaktirūpāṃ prakalpayet|| 17.85 ||
praṇavaṃ vinyasenmadhye tadbāhye ṣoḍaśa svarān|
vyañjanāni ca tadbāhyo parjanyādi pradakṣiṇam|| 17.86 ||
tatraiva vinyaset padmaṃ padme sthāpyāśca śaktayaḥ|
anugrahaṃ yayā yukto nityaṃ sa kurute prabhuḥ|| 17.87 ||
sthitastasyāṃ sadā pūjyaḥ parātmā hariravyayaḥ|
pādyagandhādibhirdevīṃ balibhiśca prapūjayet|| 17.88 ||
ahataiścaiva vāsobhirlakṣmīrūpāṃ ca kalpayet|
homaṃ śaktyaṣṭakāntānāṃ kuryādācārya eva tu|| 17.89 ||
ayanasya ca śakteśca prāsādasya tataḥ kramāt|
tatroktastatvavinyāsaḥ pūrvamevādhunā smṛtaḥ|| 17.90 ||
tathā puruṣarūpaṃ taṃ kalpayitvā yathākramam|
prāsādaṃ pūjayet paścād gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ śubhaiḥ|| 17.91 ||
tato brahmaśilāṃ cātra śodhitāmadhivāsayet|
anantāsanavinyāso napuṃsakaśilopari|| 17.92 ||
napuṃsakaṃ paraṃ brahma sarvādhāraṃ taducyate|
ālikhenmaṇḍalaṃ cātra taṇḍulaiḥ pañcaraṅgikaiḥ|| 17.93 ||
caturaśraṃ caturdvāraṃ madhye padmasamanvitam|
pūrvaṃ tu sakalīkṛtya pratimāṃ deśikottamaḥ|| 17.94 ||
aṅgapratyaṅgabhedena svakalpoditavartmanā|
sāṅgaṃ prapūjayeddevaṃ gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ kramāt|| 17.95 ||
alaṅkārairnivedyaiśca yathāvibhavavistaram|
maṇḍale'bhyarcayedvidvān sāṅgaṃ devamadhokṣajam|| 17.96 ||
arcāyāṃ yā tu vinyastā mūrtistāṃ tatra kalpayet|
sāṅgaṃ saparivāraṃ ca tatreṣṭvā vidhinā'cyutam|| 17.97 ||
hutvā'gni ca yathānyāyaṃ pārṣadebhyo baliṃ haret|
ādyaśca karmajāścaiva ye bhūtāḥ pragdiśi sthitāḥ|| 17.98 ||
prasannāḥ parituṣṭāste pratigṛhṇantvimaṃ balimi|
dakṣiṇe paścime caiva uttare ca niyojayet|| 17.99 ||
gandhapuṣpāṇi dhūpaṃ dīpamannaṃ jalaṃ kramāt|
vidikṣu ca yathānyāyaṃ dadyāccaiva tato balim|| 17.100 ||
vṛkṣeṣu parvatāgreṣu ye vidikṣu ca saṃsthitāḥ|
bhūmau vyomni sthitā ye ca te'pi gṛhṇantvimaṃ balim|| 17.101 ||
vināyakāḥ kṣetrapālā ye cānye balikāṅkṣiṇaḥ|
pūrvādyāḥ pārṣadāścaiva pratigṛhṇantvimaṃ balim|| 17.102 ||
parito'nena vai dadyāt paribhramya pradakṣiṇam|
ācamyāntaḥ praviśyātha kṛtanyāsaḥ samāhitaḥ|| 17.103 ||
śāntiṃ vyāhṛtibhirhutvā tato devaṃ kramāt spṛśet|
ghṛtaṃ bhūriti hutvā dvau pādau devasya saṃspṛśet|| 17.104 ||
dadhnā ca bhuva ityevaṃ tato nābhyantaraṃ spaśet|
kṣīraṃ svariti hutvā'tha tato'sya hṛdayaṃ spṛśet|| 17.105 ||
bhūrbhuvaḥ svaśca hutvaivaṃ madhunā mūrdhni saṃspṛśet|
sarvaṃ mūlena hutvānte sarvagātraṃ tu saṃspṛśet|| 17.106 ||
aṣṭāṣṭāhutayaścātra nirdiṣṭāḥ śāntikarmaṇi|
sruveṇa dravyayuktena sparśanaṃ cātra kīrtitam|| 17.107 ||
dravye dravye tathā spṛṣṭe gandhapuṣpaiḥ samarcayet|
kṣālayitvā sruvaṃ paścāt kāryā dravyāntarāhutiḥ|| 17.108 ||
catasro dhenavaḥ sthāpyā dakṣiṇadvārasannidhau|
uttarābhimukhāḥ śāntā rajjubaddhāḥ savatsakāḥ|| 17.109 ||
gaṅgā ca yamunā caiva tathā godā sarasvatī|
nāmabhiḥ pūjayitvā tā gandhapuṣpairyathākramam|| 17.110 ||
duhettā viṣṇugāyattryā tayaiva śrapayeccarum|
nivedayet tayā caiva tayaivānte baliṃ haret|| 17.111 ||
bhojayed brāhmaṇāṃstatra bhaktān dvādaśa pāyasam|
maṇḍapasyottare pārśve hiraṇyaṃ dakṣiṇā bhavet|| 17.112 ||
surabhīśca gurordadyād viṣṇurme prīyatāmiti|
evaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ paścād rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ bhavet|| 17.113 ||
śaṅkhatūryādisaṃyuktaṃ nṛttagītādisaṃyutam|
setihāsapurāṇābhiḥ (ṇaiśca) katābhiśca nayenniśām|| 17.114 ||
prabhāte sudine vāre puṇyarāśyudaye tathā|
mauhūrttikopadiṣṭena lagnena sthāpayet sudhīḥ|| 17.115 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāmadhivāsapaṭalaḥ saptadaśaḥ ||

<18>
|| athāṣṭādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt pratiṣṭhāvidhimuttamam|
vāstuyāgaṃ purā kṛtvā muhūrtte sthāpanaṃ bhavet|| 18.1 ||
kṛtvā nāndīmukhaṃ śaktyā ṣaḍaṣṭau dvādaśāthavā|
bhojayed brāhmaṇān śāntān vaiṣṇavāṃstu viśeṣataḥ|| 18.2 ||
dadyādvastrāṅgulīyādi hiraṇyaṃ ca svaśaktitaḥ|
prāsādasya tu sarvasya mārjanaṃ kārayettataḥ|| 18.3 ||
tatācābhyantaraṃ darbhaiḥ saśalākaiḥ prayatnataḥ|
pañcagavyena gandhaiśca prokṣya mūlābhimantritaiḥ|| 18.4 ||
homārthaṃ vālukābhistu prāsādasya samantataḥ|
kārayeccaturaśrāṇi sthaṇḍilāni yathākramam|| 18.5 ||
dikṣu cāṣṭāsu kṛtvaivamaṣṭau ca kalaśān nyaset|
kusumodakasampūrṇān sapidhānān sapallavān|| 18.6 ||
saptasaptavibhāgena garbhāgāre vibhājite|
brahmadevamanuṣyāṇāṃ piśācānāṃ padāni tu|| 18.7 ||
madhyādārabhya yatnena kramād jñeyāni sarvataḥ|
brāhmaḥ pañcadaśāṃśaḥ syād devabhāgāstrayodaśa|| 18.8 ||
ekādaśa navāṃśaśca śiṣṭayoḥ pṛṣṭhabhāgataḥ|
gamanaṃ trividhaṃ proktaṃ pṛṣṭhasaumyesabhedataḥ|| 18.9 ||
kalpanīyaṃ yathānyāyaṃ dravyasaṃsthānayogataḥ|
sthitāsīnaśayānānāṃ sthānāni parikalpayet|| 18.10 ||
brahmāṃśe madhyamaṃ tyaktvā dvitīyaṃ cāpi pṛṣṭhataḥ|
sthitaikaberabimbasya tṛtīyaṃ sthānamiṣyate|| 18.11 ||
tribhakte dvāravistāratrisaptāṃśeṣu madhyame|
madhyārdhenottare sūtraṃ gamanārthaṃ prakalpayet|| 18.12 ||
paścimottarasaṃyoge karṇasūtraṃ prakalpya tu|
bimbāṅguliyavenaiśe gamayed dviyavena vā|| 18.13 ||
brahmabhāgaṃ parityajya sthānārcā sthāpyate yadi|
tanmaṇḍalādhipo rājā sthāpakaśca vinaśyati|| 18.14 ||
kriyā ca niṣphalaiva syāt tasmādyatnena māpayet|
kiñcinmānuṣamāśritya kuryādāsanakarma ca|| 18.15 ||
devamānuṣabhāgābhyāṃ kuryācca śayanaṃ budhaḥ|
arcanāpīṭhikā brāhme sthāpanā daivike yadā|| 18.16 ||
mānuṣe'sya parīvārāḥ paiśāce tvāyudhāni ca|
kṛtvā pādaśilāsthānaṃ vṛttaṃ vā caturaśrakam|| 18.17 ||
pādapīṭhasya cādhastāt tato brahmaśīlāṃ nyaset|
tvameva paramā śaktistvamevāsanadhārikā|| 18.18 ||
devājñayā tvayā devi! sthātavyamiha sarvadā|
iti vijñāpya tāṃ pūrvaṃ gandhapuṣpaiḥ prapūjitām|| 18.19 ||
dhyātvorvī sthāpayedgartte mantreṇānena deśikaḥ|
sthitaṃ carācaraṃ yasyāṃ ratnānāṃ nidhiravyayaḥ|| 19.20 ||
sā tvaṃ brahmaśilārūpā tiṣṭhātra dharaṇi! sthirā|
evaṃ saṃsthāpya tāṃ būyo gandhapuṣpaiḥ prapūjya ca|| 19.21 ||
ratnanyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryād vidhidṛṣṭena vartmanā|
garttāśca nimnāvaraṇāścatvāro vihitāḥ kramāt|| 19.22 ||
madhyāntamaṣṭadikṣvaṣṭau nyastavyāni yathākramam|
teṣu bījāni ratnāni dhātulohāyudhāni ca|| 19.23 ||
sarvadravyāṇi saṃyojya brahmasthāne tu vinyaset|
ye guṇāḥ prathitā viṣṇoraiśvaryāṣṭakasaṃjñayā|| 19.24 ||
tāni ratnāni diksthāni nyāsakāle vicintayet|
vāyuvyomāntarasthāni yeṣāṃ bījāni te guṇāḥ|| 19.25 ||
īśitvādikramāt samyak pūrvādyāśāsviha smṛtāḥ|
madhye tu praṇavo jñeyo guṇāṣṭakayutaḥ pumān|| 19.26 ||
śodhayet pañcagavyena garttamaṣṭākṣareṇa tu|
prokṣayet gandhatoyaiśca dvādaśākṣaravidyayā|| 19.27 ||
homakarma tataḥ kuryāt prāsādottarapārśvataḥ|
laukikaṃ cāgnimānīya śarāve'bhineva tataḥ|| 19.28 ||
vaiṣṇavīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt saṃskṛtaṃ vahnimānayet|
aṣṭākṣareṇa juhuyādājyāhutisahasrakam|| 19.29 ||
tataḥ samitsasraṃ ca dvādaśākṣaravidyayā|
hutvā''jyaṃ cātha gāyatryā ratnāni samupaspṛśet|| 19.30 ||
ājyāhutisaṃhasraṃ vā gāyattryaivātra homayet|
athavā'ṣṭaśataṃ mukhyaṃ madhyamaṃ cādhamaṃ bhavet|| 19.31 ||
tayā saṃspṛśya ratnāni gartteṣu viniveśayet|
na garttādadhikaṃ dravyaṃ na hīnaṃ ca prakalpayet|| 19.32 ||
kvaciddravyasamaṃ gartaṃ kārayet kuśalo guruḥ|
aṣṭākṣarasya varṇaistu vyastaireva yathākramam|| 19.33 ||
yavādīn dikṣu vinyasya madhye siddhārthakaṃ nyaset|
tato vajrādiratnāni padmarāgaṃ ca madhyataḥ|| 19.34 ||
manaḥ śilādidhātūṃśca pārataṃ taṃ tathā nyaset|
suvarṇādīni dikṣvaṣṭau madhye padmaṃ hiraṇmayam|| 19.35 ||
saṃspṛśed viṣṇugāyatrayā tato garttaṃ sadakṣiṇam|
bījādīnāmalābhe'pi tatsthāneṣu yathākramam|| 19.36 ||
śālimuktāharītālasuvarṇāni nyased budhaḥ|
puṇyāhaṃ vācayet pūrvaṃ vaiṣṇavaiḥ saha mantravit|| 19.37 ||
ratnanyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā piṇḍikāṃ tatra vinyaset|
praṇīya vaiṣṇavaṃ vahnimaiśānyāṃ mūlavidyayā|| 19.38 ||
juhuyāt samidho brāhmīḥ sahasraṃ śatameva vā|
aṣṭottaramathājyena gāyattryā viṣṇusaṃjñayā|| 19.39 ||
mūrtiśaktibhiraṅgaiśca ghṛtameva yatākramam|
hutvā ca śaktito vidvān śāntitoyaṃ samāharet|| 19.40 ||
tataścāgniṃ samānīya vaiṣṇavairmantravittamaiḥ|
hotavyamaṣṭadikṣvevaṃ samidbhirmūlavidyayā|| 19.41 ||
palāśakhadirāśvatthaplakṣanyagrodhajāstathā|
kāśmaryo rohitāścaiva bilvodumbarajāḥ kramāt|| 19.42 ||
dvādaśāṅgulamānāstāḥ samidhastu prakīrtitāḥ|
kaniṣṭhikāpramāṇāśca pṛthagaṣṭasahasrikāḥ|| 19.43 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā diśāhomaṃ śāntivāri samāharet|
vaiṣṇave tāmrapātre tu sarvakumbhopasaṃbhṛtam|| 19.44 ||
śatavāraṃ tu mūlena mantrayitvā'tha deśikaḥ|
aṣṭākṣarasya varṇaistu pūrayitvā pradakṣiṇam|| 19.45 ||
praṇavena tu sampūjya gandhapuṣpairyathākramam|
karaguptaṃ tadādāya sapidhānaṃ savastrakam|| 19.46 ||
dhyātvā'tra paramaṃ viṣṇuṃ tejorūpamanāmayam|
karmārcāyāstato mūrdhni siñcedomityudāharan|| 19.47 ||
siktvā tu pratimāmūrdhni sarvatīrthamayaṃ jalam|
visṛjṭaya vāsasī pūrve vastrābhyāṃ chādayet punaḥ|| 19.48 ||
gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ samyag yathāvibhavamarcayet|
uttiṣṭheti samutthāpya śaṅkhadundubhiniḥsvanaiḥ|| 19.49 ||
pratimāmānayed vidvān prāsādasya pradakṣiṇam|
ato deveti sūktena mūrtipaiḥ sahito guruḥ|| 19.50 ||
dvāradeśeṃ ca dattvā'rghyaṃ muhūrte śobhane tataḥ|
praveśayet prayatnena prāsādaṃ deśikottamaḥ|| 19.51 ||
praveśyāntaryathā kiñcidadhaścorghvaṃ na saṃspṛśet|
pīṭhaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya tataḥ sarveṇa vinyaset|| 19.52 ||
dhyāyeśca paramaṃ viṣṇuṃ niṣkalaṃ mantravittamaḥ|
sulagne sthāpayed dhyātvā sarvatattvamayaṃ sudhīḥ|| 19.53 ||
lokānugrahahetvarthaṃ sthirībhava sukhāya naḥ|
sānnidhyaṃ hi sadā deva! pratyahaṃ parivardhaya|| 19.54 ||
mā bhūt prajāvirodho'smin yajamānaḥ samṛddhayatām|
sabhūpālaṃ tathā rāṣṭraṃ sarvopadravavarjitam|| 19.55 ||
kṣemeṇa vṛddhimatulāṃ sukhamakṣayamaśnutām|
ityuktvā'gre paṭheccāsya viśvataścakṣurityṛcam|| 19.56 ||
sajīvakaraṇaṃ kṛtvā pañcopaniṣadā kramāt|
śāntiṃ hutvā yatānyāyaṃ devaṃ tamabhiṣecayet|| 19.57 ||
dhyātvā tu niṣkalaṃ viṣṇuṃ sakalaṃ bhāvayettataḥ|
ākāśaṃ tasya mūrtiḥ syāt pṛthivī tasya pīṭhikā|| 19.58 ||
vigrahaṃ kalpayet tasya tejasaḥ paramāṇubhiḥ|
tejomayaṃ tato dhyātvā śaṅkhacakragadādharam|| 19.59 ||
mantranyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryāt sargasthityantabhedataḥ|
tejomayaṃ tato dhyātvā sakalaṃ sakaleśvaram|| 19.60 ||
nirudhya praṇavenātha vibhaktendriyavigraham|
sarvaśaktisamāyuktaṃ sarvāvayavasundaram|| 19.61 ||
pītāmbarakirīṭādiprojjvalaṃ projjvalāyudham|
arcayet sakalīkṛtya sāṅgaṃ sāvaraṇaṃ tataḥ|| 19.62 ||
darśayitvā'tha mudrāṃ ca praṇamed daṇḍavat kṣitau|
brahmādiparivārāṃstu svanāmnā'strāṇi vinyaset|| 19.63 ||
arcayitvā svamudrāśca darśayet pārṣadān pṛthak|
pūjayitvā tato devaṃ praṇamyāñjalimudrayā|| 19.64 ||
japedaṣṭaśataṃ mūlamanujñāpya tamīśvaram|
nirgamyābhimukho bhūtvā puṣpatoyādisaṃyutam|| 19.65 ||
dvāri caṇḍaṃ pracaṇḍaṃ ca devadauvārikāvubhau|
namontena svanāmnā tu sthāpayitvā baliṃ haret|| 19.66 ||
vainateyamanantaṃ ca gaṇeśaṃ dakṣiṇeśvaram|
lokapālāṃśca saṃsthāpya viṣvaksenaṃ ca pūjayet|| 19.67 ||
lepye'dhivāsya karmārcāṃ praveśya suramandiram|
iyaṃ sā deva te mūrtiromityuktvā'tra vinyaset|| 19.68 ||
jīvanyāsādi sarvaṃ tu kartavyamiha pūrvavat|
snānādīni tu karmāṇi karmārcāyāṃ prayojayet|| 19.69 ||
tattvanyāsādi sarvaṃ tu mūlārcāyāmiti sthitiḥ|
viṣṇupāriṣadān sarvān balipīṭhe prakalpya tu|| 19.70 ||
upaspṛśya kṛtanyāsaḥ pravisyāntargṛhaṃ sudhīḥ|
mūlamaṣṭaśataṃ japtvā devaṃ tamabhivādayet|| 19.71 ||
