stotrairnānāvidhaiḥ stutvā mahāpuruṣapūrvaṃkaiḥ|
praṇāmamudrayā paścādaṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇipatya ca|| 19.72 ||
nṛttaṃ gītaṃ ca vādyaṃ ca vividhaṃ kārayet tataḥ|
tatrānivāritaṃ yatnādannādyaṃ cāpi kārayet|| 19.73 ||
ācāryaṃ pūjayet paścād dakṣiṇābhiḥ samūrtipam|
yāgopayogi yad dravyaṃ sarvaṃ cāsmai nivedayet|| 19.74 ||
vaiṣṇavān pūjayet paścād yathāvibhavavistaram|
lākṣā sarjarasaḥ śaṅkhaḥ sikatā kuruvindakaḥ|| 19.75 ||
puraṃ hiṅgulakaṃ bolā phalakvāthena bandhanam|
vimalādyaṣṭakaṃ peṣyaṃ dravadravyamanugrahāḥ|| 19.76 ||
navaśaktimayo bandho lakṣmīmādhavayorbhavet|
evaṃ kṛtvā'ṣṭabandhaṃ tu ghṛtādyaiḥ pañcaśaktibhiḥ|| 19.77 ||
kāsyahṛdguhyapādeṣu kuryād dūrvābhirarpaṇam|
tataḥ sampūjya saṃhṛtya darbhairvastraiśca veṣṭayet|| 19.78 ||
tithihomastu kartavyastathā tithyadhipasya ca|
ṛkṣahomo vārahomastaddevatyaśca mantriṇā|| 19.79 ||
muhūrtadevatāhomo grahahomastathāparaḥ|
sampātaṃ yojayet tatra kalaśe tu punaḥ punaḥ|| 19.80 ||
gururmūttidharaiḥ sārdhaṃ sampūjya kalaśaṃ tataḥ|
gṛhītvā snāpayed devaṃ kṛtvā puṇyāhamaṅgalam|| 19.81 ||
vāditraśaṅkhanādaiśca jayaśabdaiśca saṃyutam|
vaiṣṇavaiśca tadā sūktairjayaśabdaiśca nādayet|| 19.82 ||
gandhapuṣpaiśca vasvaiśca dhūpadīpaiśca pūjayet|
caturvidhaṃ nivedyaṃ ca tato devāya kalpayet|| 19.83 ||
baliṃ ca vikired yatnād brāhmaṇaiḥ svasti vācayet|
evaṃ kṛte'tra tithyṛkṣavārarāśigrahodbhavāḥ|| 19.84 ||
naśyanti sakalā doṣāstasmāt kāryaṃ tu sarvathā|
yajamānaṃ tataḥ paścād viṣṇukumbhajalena tu|| 19.85 ||
snāpayitvā'rcanaṃ kuryādācāryaḥ svayameva ca|
tataḥ sa guruṇā sārdhaṃ patnyā putraistathā'nujaiḥ|| 19.86 ||
sitavastradharo bhaktyā devaṃ tamabhivādayet|
tridhā kṛtvā''tmano vittaṃ bhāgaṃ devāya kalpayet|| 19.87 ||
bhāgaṃ bhāryāsutādīnāṃ bhāgamācāryadakṣiṇām|
prītidānaṃ ca dātavyaṃ pūjopakaraṇāni ca|| 19.88 ||
yo dadāti tadā kiñcit sarvaṃ buhaphalaṃ bhavet|
dhūpadīpādi saṃsthāpya devāgāraṃ tu ghaṭṭayet|| 19.89 ||
noddhāṭayet tataḥ paścāt trīṇyahāni tu kaścana|
dakṣiṇānnādyahomaistu pūjā tatra vidhīyate|| 19.90 ||
mantrasya prīṇanaṃ kuryāt sthāpite puruṣottame|
ghṛtena payasā dadhnā śuddhatoyena vā sakṛt|| 19.91 ||
snāpayitvā tu karmārcāṃ

|| athaikonaviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt pratiṣṭhānantarakriyām|
pūrvavat sthaṇḍilānyaṣṭau homārthaṃ dikṣu kalpayet|| 19.1 ||
ullekhanādikaṃ kṛtvā pūrvoktavidhinā kramāt|
praṇīta vaiṣṇavaṃ vahniṃ juhuyurdikṣu dīkṣitāḥ|| 19.2 ||
indrakratumiti prācyāmagniṃdūtaṃ hutāśane|
noktasuparṇaṃ yāmyāyāṃ nairṛtyāṃ moṣuṇaḥ parā|| 19.3 ||
yaraṃkṣantīti vāruṇyāṃ vāyavāyāhi pāvane|
somaṃrājānamityevaṃ saumye śārve triyambakam|| 19.4 ||
pañca tārān puro'nūcya pratyekaṃ pūrvataḥ kramāt|
amībhirhuhuyurmantraiḥ pakvaṃ vidhivadṛtvijaḥ|| 19.5 ||
palāśakhadirāśvatthaplakṣanyagrodhabilvajāḥ|
audumbarāśca kāśmaryaḥ samidho'ṣyaśataṃ pṛthak|| 19.6 ||
tāvadājyānnalājādīn hutvā śeṣaṃ samāpayet|
samidādi yathālābhamindrādyastraiśca homayet|| 19.7 ||
indrādīn homayitvaivaṃ svamantrairaṣṭadikṣu ca|
sampātājyayutaistoyairghaṭasthairmantrasaṃskṛtaiḥ|| 19.8 ||
saṃsnāpya pīṭhikāsvetān gandhādyaiḥ pūjayet kramāt|
skandamaindyāṃ pratiṣṭhāpya ṣaṇmukhāyeti vidyayā|| 19.9 ||
śāstāramagnau bhāṃbījaṃ bhūtādhipataye namaḥ|
brāhmī māheśvarī caiva kaumārī vaiṣṇavī tathā|| 19.10 ||
vārāhyaindrī ca cāmuṇḍī yāmye vai sapta mātaraḥ|
vīrabhadragaṇeśābhyāṃ saha prākārapārśvataḥ|| 19.11 ||
vīrabhadraḥ svarāḥ sarve saptavargāstu mātaraḥ|
kūṭo gaṇapatiśceti kramājjñeyā ca mātṛkā|| 19.12 ||
praṇavena svamantrairvā nairṛtyāṃ tu divākaram|
ghaṣotkāyeti mantro vā hutāntastadvidhau bhavet|| 19.13 ||
paścād brahmā svaṣaṭkeṇa tathānyastatsadityapi|
tato bhūriti bījena varāho vāyugocare|| 19.14 ||
durgā hrīṅkārapūrveṇa sā nāmnottaratastathā|
saśrīdharahayāsyā vā vāgīśvaryādimātaraḥ|| 19.15 ||
vāgīśvarī kriyā kīrtirlakṣmīḥ sṛṣṭiśca pañcamī|
vidyā kāntiriti jñeyāḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharāḥ|| 19.16 ||
caturthaṣaṣṭhau bindvantau dvādaśo'tha trayodaśaḥ|
ādyaścaikādaśo jñeyo dvitīyaścaindragāḥ svarāḥ|| 19.17 ||
viṣvakseno'ditau sthāpyaḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ|
tarjayan vāmatarjanyā nāsāgrāsannayā jagat|| 19.18 ||
gandhapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya śiṣṭadravyaistu yājñikaiḥ|
devopayuktaṃ puṣpādi yat prāsādāntarasthitam|| 19.19 ||
tat sarvamasmai dattvā'nte vastrābhyāṃ pariveṣṭayet|
īśānaṃ ceśadigbhāge svamantraiḥ sthāpayet tataḥ|| 19.20 ||
kumudādyāśca pīṭheṣu pūrvādidhvajadevatāḥ|
prākārāttu bahiḥ sthāpyāḥ pūjyāśca balibhiḥ kramāt|| 19.21 ||
balipīṭhe balaścāgre sagaṇo bhūtanāyakaḥ|
sthāpayitvā'rcayitvā ca sarvān vijñāpayet tataḥ|| 19.22 ||
yāvadatra jagannātho bimbamāśritya tiṣṭhati|
tāvadatraiva rakṣārthaṃ yūyaṃ tiṣṭhata sarvadā|| 19.23 ||
antarmaṇḍaladaṇḍārdhadaṇḍe syādantahārakā|
madhyahārā dvidaṇḍā ca caturmaryādabhittikā|| 19.24 ||
saptadaṇḍāyatā kāryā maryādābhittikā tataḥ|
mukhāyāmastribhāgena pādenārdhena vā punaḥ|| 19.25 ||
balipīṭhasamutsedho garbhāgāraprateḥ samaḥ|
pūjāpīṭhasamo vā'pi ṣaḍūno vā prakīrttitaḥ|| 19.26 ||
utsedhaṃ daśadhā kṛtvā saptāṃśastasya vistaraḥ|
saptaviṃśatidhā kṛtvā samameva taducchrayam|| 19.27 ||
ekāṃśaṃ pādukaṃ vidyāt caturbhirjagatīṃ punaḥ|
tribhāgaṃ kumudaṃ caiva tathaikāṃśena paṭṭikām|| 19.28 ||
daśāṃśaṃ kaṇṭhamityāhuraṃśābhyāṃ kambunidravau|
tribhiḥ kapotamityāhurekāṃśenāgrapaṭṭikām|| 19.29 ||
dvyaṃśakaṃ padmamante syādetat pīṭhasya lakṣaṇam|
uttamādhamamadhyatvaṃ bimbayogāt prakalpayet|| 19.30 ||
uttamānāṃ tu bimbānāṃ varjite madhyamādhame|
varjitā pīṭhikāścāṣṭau madhyamādhamayostathā|| 19.31 ||
pīṭhikānāṃ viparyāse kulanāśo bhaved yataḥ|
svānāṃ svānāṃ viparyāso na kāryo'taḥ sukhārthibhiḥ|| 19.32 ||
utsedhanirgamau tulyau pādukasya prakīrttitau|
jagatīkumbhayoḥ sandhau tāvadantargataṃ bhavet|| 19.33 ||
bhāgaṃ kumbhottare kṛtvā bhāgārdhaṃ paṭṭikāṃ tataḥ|
bhāgamekaṃ tribhāgaikaṃ kuryād vai kaṇṭhapādukam|| 19.34 ||
bhāgārdhāntargataṃ kaṇṭhaṃ pādamātrā tu mekhalā|
vistārādaṣṭabhāgaikaṃ pādavistāra ucyate|| 19.35 ||
catvāro dvādaśaivaṃ vā kartavyāḥ kaṇṭhapādukāḥ|
kaṇṭhāgrapaṭṭikāṃ kuryād yathāvat kumbhapaṭṭikām|| 19.36 ||
bhāgaṃ bāhyagataṃ caiva valabhyardhaṃ tu bhaṅguram|
antarbhaṅguravad bhāgaṃ salilaṃ sarvato bhavet|| 19.37 ||
ūrdhvaṃ tu paṭṭikā jñeyā padmākṛtirathāntare|
tatpīṭhaṃ madhyataḥ kṛtvā maṇḍapaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam|| 19.38 ||
bījāṅkuradhvajārohabalimaṅgalapūrvakam|
utsavānnādyapuṇyāhasthānaśuddhyādisaṃyutam|| 19.39 ||
samantāt pīṭhavistāraṃ likhitvā bhadrakāṣṭakam|
kalaśān pūrayitvā'ṣṭau ghṛtairdhānyaistu vā'mbubhiḥ|| 19.40 ||
sarvaratnasamāyuktaṃ vastrayugmaistu veṣṭayet|
lohajaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ vā'nyaṃ pīṭhasyopari kalpayet|| 19.41 ||
bhūtanāthasya cārabhya bhūtānāṃ capradakṣiṇam|
gandhādinā'rcayitvā tu phalaiḥ puṣpaiśca sarvataḥ|| 19.42 ||
bhakṣyabhojyādisaṃyuktaṃ kalaśāṃścaiva pūrvavat|
vastraiḥ saṃveṣṭayet sarvān navaiḥ keśādivarjitaiḥ|| 19.43 ||
homārthaṃ tu dvijā grāhyā aṣṭau catvāra eva vā|
svayaṃ vaikaṃ prakurvīta pūrvoktavidhinā guruḥ|| 19.44 ||
vedādhyayanasaṃyuktaṃ tathā jāgaraṇaṃ niśi|
prāsādābhyantare devamārādhya vidhipūrvakam|| 19.45 ||
balidānaṃ yathāśakti sarvaṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvavat|
sarveṣāṃ mūrtipānāṃ tu homayenmūlavidyayā|| 19.46 ||
yathā svanāmamantreṇa samidājyahavīṃṣi ca|
lājāṃśaaca sarṣapāṃścaiva tathā saktūn yavāṃstilān|| 19.47 ||
dadhi kṣīraṃ tathā kṣaudraṃ puṣpāṇi tu phalāni ca|
sarvaṃ sājyaṃ tu hotavyaṃ sarvaśāntikaraṃ śubham|| 19.48 ||
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ vā śataṃ vā homayet pṛthak|
dadyāt pūrṇāhutiṃ cātra pratidravyaṃ vidhānataḥ|| 19.49 ||
śaṅkhatūryādisaṃyuktaṃ dikṣu bhūtabaliṃ haret|
onnamaḥ prācyebhyo bhūtebhyo viśvabhūtebhyaḥ|
namo'stu viśvarūpebhyo virūpebhyaśca vo namaḥ|| 19.50 ||

namo'stu dakṣiṇebhyaśca bhūtebhyaḥ śārṅgadhanvanaḥ|
namo hāhābhyo bhūtebhyaḥ sarvebhyo vo namonamaḥ|| 19.51 ||
onnamaḥ pāścāttyebhyo bhūtebhyo bhairavebhyo viṣṇupālakebhyo viyadgrahodaravartibhyo huṃ phaṭ oṃ gacchata ṭhaṭha| viṣṇugaṇānāmato balimupāharāmi bhadraṃ no dadata prīṇayata svāhā|

susnātaḥ svastivācyātha brāhmaṇānāmanujñayā|
devasya mahatīṃ pūjāṃ kṛtvā bhaktyā samāhitaḥ|| 19.52 ||
vainateyaṃ ca sampūjya parivāraṃ ca śaktitaḥ|
bhūtanāthamathārādhya mūrtipān pūjayet kramāt|| 19.53 ||
apūpasaktubhirdhānāphalabhakṣyasamanvitaiḥ|
etatsarvaṃ kramāt kṛtvā brāhmaṇānāṃ ca tarpaṇam|| 19.54 ||
dakṣiṇā cātra dātavyā brāhmaṇebhyaḥ svaśaktitaḥ|
gandhapuṣpairathābhyarcya balipīṭhaṃ samāhitaḥ|| 19.55 ||
bhūtānāthaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya kumbhatoyena secayet|
itarān secayed dikṣu mūrttipānāṃ yathākramam|| 19.56 ||
ācāryo yajamānaśca balipīṭhaṃ pradakṣiṇam|
abhyarcya gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ praṇamed bhūtanāyakam|| 19.57 ||
pāyasaṃ kṛsaraṃ caiva gulānnaṃ ghṛtasaṃyutam|
dadhnā madhuphalairyuktaṃ pūjayed bhūtanāyakam|| 19.58 ||
ācāryo mūrtipālebhyo dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayet tataḥ|
pūrvavat snapanaṃ kuryād devadevasya śaktitaḥ|| 19.59 ||
snānaśeṣaistu kumbhasthairācāryo mūrttipaiḥ saha|
prokṣayet parivārāṃstān gandhādibhirathārcayet|| 19.60 ||

|| itiviṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ ekonaviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

|| atha viṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepādutsavasya vidhiṃ param|
sthāpito'pi vinā yena prasīdati na keśavaḥ|| 20.1 ||
abdamardhaṃ trimāsaṃ vā māsaṃ pakṣamasambhave|
navāhaṃ vā'pi saptāhaṃ pañcāhaṃ neṣyate param|| 20.2 ||
māghe vā mārgaśīrṣe vā caitre vaiśākha eva vā|
pauṣe vā phālgune kuryādutsavaṃ bahuvistaram|| 20.3 ||
pratyabdaṃ niyataṃ kuryācchaktyā sarvārthasādhanam|
puṇyāhenotsavārambhe dhvajasthāpanamācaret|| 20.4 ||
saptāhnikacatuṣkādau maṅgalaṅkuraropaṇam|
śravaṇāntaṃ tu saṃkalpya tīrthasnānāvasānikam|| 20.5 ||
kārayedutsavaṃ bhaktyā viṣṇorviṣṇuparāyaṇaḥ|
sthāpayedutsavārambhe dhvajaṃ garuḍalakṣaṇam|| 20.6 ||
dikṣu cāṣṭau yathānyāyaṃ patākādīn samujjvalān|
dhvajotthāne kṛte kiñcinna vighnairabhibhūyate|| 20.7 ||
kalaśena samaṃ kāryaṃ dhvajasyārohaṇaṃ tadā|
pūrvavanmaṇḍapaṃ kṛtvā vediṃ ca supariśritām|| 20.8 ||
agneśca jananaṃ kumbhamūrttipānāṃ ca kalpanam|
dhvajadaṇḍaḥ samaḥ kāryo dṛḍho vraṇavivarjitaḥ|| 20.9 ||
karmāntarāniyuktaśca na śuṣkaḥ patitaḥ svayam|
puṣyādiyoge saṅgrāhyaḥ sulagne śakunaiḥ śubhaiḥ|| 20.10 ||
brāhmaṇaiḥ svastivācyādau śuciḥ snātaḥ svalaṅkṛtaḥ|
tathāvidhairdvijairyukto jayaśabdādisaṃyutaḥ|| 20.11 ||
madhvājyāktakuṭhāreṇa pūrvottaramukho guruḥ|
chitvā vaṃśamupānīya kalaśaiḥ snāpayecchubhaiḥ|| 20.12 ||
mṛtkaṣāyauṣadhītīrthairgandhapuṣpaiśca pūjayet|
patākā ca śubhā kāryā sitā tadavalambinī|| 20.13 ||
syād devāgāraśikharatribhāgasamalambinī|
darbhairāveṣṭitā ghaṇṭā cāmarādarśasaṃyutā|| 20.14 ||
sammukhaṃ lekhayet tatra vinātākulanandanam|
ājānunābhikaṇṭhāgraṃ sitapītāruṇāsitam|| 20.15 ||
durnirīkṣaṃ surairdaityairjvālānvitavilocanam|
anantādyaṣṭanāgendradharaṃ nīlāgranāsikam|| 20.16 ||
sahasrādityasaṃkāśaṃ manasā''vāhya vinyaset|
pūjayed gandhapuṣpaiśca mantreṇānena mantravit|| 20.17 ||
tārādiḥ pakṣirājāya hutānto'ṣṭākṣarastu yaḥ|
sambuddhirūpa eva syādāvāhanavisargayoḥ|| 20.18 ||
candanasragbalidhyānaiḥ śayane'bhyarcya vāsayet|
homaṃ ca vidhivat kuryād vargādyaiḥ kādiśāntagaiḥ|| 20.19 ||
hutāntaiḥ praṇavādyaiśca tilān vrīhisamanvitān|
caturbhirakṣarairdikṣu cavargānantarairvinā|| 20.20 ||
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu vainateyapadānvitaiḥ|
ājyāktaṃ homayet sarvaṃ nivedyaṃ ca caturvidham|| 20.21 ||
baliṃ ca parito dadyād bhūtānāṃ dhvajavāsinām|
anekakoṭisaṃkhyānāṃ bhūtānāṃ cāpi pārṣadām|| 20.22 ||
prabhāte'bhyarcayitvaivaṃ brāhmaṇāṃścaiva tarpayet|
dhvajavāhanabhūto'sau viṣṇorviṣṇuparākramaḥ|| 20.23 ||
suparṇaḥ prīyatāṃ mahyaṃ vinatākulanandanaḥ|
ityuccārya tato gacched devatāyatanaṃ prati|| 20.24 ||
dhvajamādāya taiḥ sārdhaṃ devamūrttidharairdvijaiḥ|
vedatūryādinirghoṣaiḥ sulagne stāpayed dhvajam|| 20.25 ||
muktāgāruḍahemāni gartte vinyasya rohayet|
onnamo'ṣṭakulānāgabhūṣaṇāya nāgaśoṇitaliptāṅgāya saptapātālavāsijanavikṣobhaṇakarāya mahāśailasañcālanāyāruṇakanīyase puruṣottamavāhanāya vinatānandakarāya trailokyavikṣobhaṇāya devadānavagandharvoragamathanāyāmṛtamathanāya māturarthe nāgamohanāya hana hana vighnān nāśaya svāhā|
anena stitimantreṇa stutvā taṃ vihageśvaram|| 20.26 ||
sānnidhyaṃ kalpayet tasya pakṣirājasya sarvadā|
tataḥ prabhṛti tatsthānaṃ nopasarpanti rākṣasāḥ|| 20.27 ||
piśācā dandaśūkāśca tasmāt kāryodhvajocchrayaḥ|
snapanaṃ cātrakartavyaṃ prāsādasya tathaiva ca|| 20.28 ||
kalaśaṃ pūrayecchubhraṃ phalairnānāvidhaiḥ śubhaiḥ|
sarvadhānyaistathā śuklaiḥ kusumaiśca sugandhibhiḥ|| 20.29 ||
tattvānāṃ tu yathā nyāsastaduktaṃ prathamaṃ tava|
yajamāno'nugaiḥ sārdhaṃ dhvajamandirayoḥ punaḥ|| 20.30 ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ tridhā kṛtvā gacchedavabhṛthaṃ tataḥ|
śubhaṃ tīrthaṃ praviśyātra gaṅgāmāvāhayet punaḥ|| 20.31 ||
devi! viṣṇupade! śuddhe! sarvapāpavyapohina!|
nandinītyeva te nāma nalinī mālinīti ca|| 20.32 ||
dakṣāpatyā ca vihagī sarvabhūtapriyeti ca|
śaraṇaṃ tvāṃ prapanno'smi pāpānyamocaya mā ciram|| 20.33 ||
dhyātvā japannimaṃ mantraṃ vāri vikṣobhya pāṇinā|
majjed ye'nye'tra majjanti sarve tu syurakalmaṣāḥ|| 20.34 ||
mūlāṅgaistarpayet tatra munīn devān ṛṣīṃstathā|
pārṣadāṃśca bhavet tṛptisteṣāṃ dvādaśavārṣikī|| 20.35 ||
dīkṣānte ca pratiṣṭhānte kuryādavabhṛthaṃ tathā|
sarvatīrthasamaṃ puṇyaṃ bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ|| 2.36 ||
dhvajānanyāṃstathā sthāpya diśāpālāṅkacihnitān|
svamantraiḥ pūjayed dikṣu sarvebhyaśca baliṃ haret|| 2.37 ||
grāmavīthīśca sammṛjya prokṣya gandhodakaistataḥ|
samantād vikiret puṣpaiḥ śobhanaiśca sugandhibhiḥ|| 2.38 ||
grāmaparyantadeśe ca grāmāntaścatvareṣu ca|
caityārāmeṣu goṣṭheṣu devatāyataneṣu ca|| 2.39 ||
toraṇaiśca patākābhirmaṇḍalaiścopaśobhayet|
kadalīnālikerekṣupūgapuṣpaphaladrumaiḥ|| 2.40 ||
dīpaprāsādayantraiśca kuryācchobhāṃ samantataḥ|
savaneṣu baliṃ dadyād gandhapuṣpādisaṃyutam|| 20.41 ||
pūjayitvā yathānyāyamutsaveṣu viśeṣataḥ|
saṃsnāpya śuddhatoyaiśca payodadhyājyagandhakaiḥ|| 20.42 ||
gavyaiśca nālikerādbhirliptvā gandhaiḥ suyojitaiḥ|
hṛdyaiḥ sudhūpitaiścitraiḥ paridhāpya navaiṃśukaiḥ|| 20.43 ||
puṣpairmanoharaiḥ puṇyairvividhaiśca viśeṣataḥ|
mālāsragbhiḥ samantācca sandhūpyāyaḥ purādibhiḥ|| 20.44 ||
saratnahemabhūṣābhirbhaktyā dīpaiśca pūjayet|
svādubhiḥ sopadaṃśaiśca prabhūtairājyasaṃyutaiḥ|| 20.45 ||
caturvidhairdadhikṣīragulādyaiśca nivedyakaiḥ|
gītasaṅgītavāditrabahulaṃ bhaktaharṣaṇam|| 20.46 ||
vividhānnādyabhūyiṣṭhamutsavaṃ kārayet tataḥ|
kṛtvā nityabaliṃ dadyāt tatrotsavabaliṃ punaḥ|| 20.47 ||
viśeṣeṇa ca kartavyaṃ sarvamaṅgalasaṃyutam|
striyo dvijāśca hṛṣṭāśca bhaktāśca jayapāṭhakāḥ|| 20.48 ||
devasya parito gatvā mucyante'khilapātakaiḥ|
nṛttagītādibhiścātra viśālaṃ kuryurutsavam|| 20.49 ||
śanaiḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ gacched baliṃ dadyācca deśikaḥ|
yāvantyutsavaklṛptāni dinānyevaṃ samācaret|| 20.50 ||
gandhapuṣpākṣataiḥ sragbhiḥ phalaiśca vividhaiḥ śubhaiḥ|
kireyuḥ kautukaṃ bhaktāḥ kālacakramathāpi vā|| 20.51 ||
evaṃ tridhā paribhramya ṣaḍahāni yathāvidhi|
saptame'hvi tataḥ kuryāt puṣpayāgaṃ haripriyam|| 20.52 ||
pūrvedyurbhūśayo bhūtvā niyatātmā haviṣyabhuk|
upoṣya niśi devasya kuryāt kautukabandhanam|| 20.53 ||
karmārcāṃ tu samārādhya maṇḍape samalaṅkṛte|
prarohaghaṭikābhistu śarāvairdigvidiggataiḥ|| 20.54 ||
vaiṣṇavaiḥ kārayitvā tu ghṛtāropaṇamāditaḥ|
jayamaṅgalaghoṣaistu pradarśyādarśamaṅgalam|| 20.55 ||
jitaṃ ta iti badhnīyāt kautukaṃ dakṣiṇe kare|
ācāryasyāpi kartavyaṃ tadā kautukabandhanam|| 20.56 ||
cāmaraistālavṛntaiśca vījayenmaṅgalānvitam|
śayane sanniveśyārcāmanirvāṇapradīpakaiḥ|| 20.57 ||
rātriśeṣaṃ samāsīta sandhūpyāyaḥ purādibhiḥ|
prātaḥ snātvā'rcayitvā ca kṛtvā maṅgalavācanam|| 20.58 ||
grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ cātra piṣṭacūrṇāni kārayet|
ulūkhale nave śuddhe dūrvādibhiralaṃkṛte|| 20.59 ||
vaiṣṇavībhistu dāsībhirdvijairvā suśubhāni tu|
liptvā''jyena tu tāmarcāṃ mūlabimbamathāpi vā|| 20.60 ||
hemacūrṇādibhiḥ samyak parimṛjya samantataḥ|
haridramudgasammiśrairbhaktānāṃ mūrdhni vinyaset|| 20.61 ||
gandhacūrṇaiśca vimṛjet pratimāṃ tadvadeva tu|
snāpayed vedikāmadhye pūritaistīrthavāribhiḥ|| 20.62 ||
gandhatoyaiśca dātavyaṃ bhaktānāṃ mūrdhni tajjalam|
dattvā suvarṇacūrṇaṃ ca pūjayed vidhinā punaḥ|| 20.63 ||
snātvā''camya punarvedyāṃ śodhitāyāṃ samantataḥ|
catustoraṇayuktāyāṃ vikiret sarṣapākṣataiḥ|| 20.64 ||
kalaśairaṅkurairdīpairdarpaṇādyaiśca bhūṣayet|
dāmāni lambayet tatra muktādāmāni cābhitaḥ|| 20.65 ||
kumbheśavardhanībhyāṃ tu kṛtvā''dau bhūparigraham|
pūrvavat sūtrayitvā'tra kārayet puṣpamaṇḍalam|| 20.66 ||
pañcavarṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ puṣpairamlānaiśca sugandhibhiḥ|
mandārapītakoraṇḍaiḥ padmabilvadalairapi|| 20.67 ||
japādivarjitaiḥ śuddhairyathāvarmavibhāgataḥ|
madhye sakarṇikaṃ padammaṣṭapatraṃ suśobhanam|| 20.68 ||
ṣaṅbhirdvādaśabhirvā'pi dalairyuktaṃ prakalpayet|
pītaistu karṇikā kāryā raktavarṇaistu kesarāḥ|| 20.69 ||
haritaiḥ sandhayaścātra śuklaireva dalāni tu|
bhadrakaṃ vā likhet tatra cakrābjaṃ vā yathāruci|| 20.70 ||
bahirāvaraṇe cāṣṭau padmānyaṣṭadalāni tu|
mūrttīnāṃ cātha devīnāṃ tathā puṣpamayānyatha|| 20.71 ||
mudrāṇāṃ lokapālānāṃ tathaivāvaraṇadvaye|
ādipadmārdhamānena mūrttipadmāni kalpayet|| 20.72 ||
tadardhena tu mudrāṇāmindrādīnāṃ prakalpayet|
tāvatā vainateyasya viṣvaksenasya caiva hi|| 20.73 ||
paścimaṃ vāhayed dvāraṃ vīthīśca parito bhavet|
puṣpadantapradeśe syāt sañcāro mūrttidāriṇām|| 20.74 ||
evaṃ puṣpamayānyeva kuryāt padmāni sarvaśaḥ|
dvārādikaṃ bahiḥ sarvaṃ yathāśobaṃ prakalpayet|| 20.75 ||
yogapīṭhaṃ tu sampūjya sagātraṃ saparicchadam|
pratimāmādipadmasya karṇikāyāṃ niveśayet|| 20.76 ||
pūjayet pūrvavad devaṃ nyāsaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi|
mūrttiśaktyastralokeśāḥ pūjyāḥ padmeṣu teṣu ca|| 20.77 ||
gandhaiḥ puṣpaiśca dhūpaiśca dīpairannaiśca śobhanaiḥ|
pūjayitvā yathānyāyamagnikāryaṃ samārabhet|| 20.78 ||
catasraḥ pālikāḥ pūrvamagneḥ kṛtvā caturdiśam|
caturaśca carūn kṛtvā yathāvittānusārataḥ|| 20.79 ||
pūrvavat saṃskṛte vahnau caturdhaikaṃ vibhajya vā|
kṛtvopastaraṇādīni prathamaṃ tu nivedayet|| 20.80 ||
dvitīyaṃ juhuyādagnau ghṛtāktaṃ prāgvadeva tu|
tṛtīyaṃ parivārebhyo dattvā'nte balimeva ca|| 20.81 ||
caturthaṃ prāśayeta sājyaṃ śeṣaṃ kṛtvā tu pūrvavat|
hiraṇyaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād brāhmaṇāṃścaiva bhojayet|| 20.82 ||
nṛttagītādibhiḥ stotrairvedanādaiśca puṣkalaiḥ|
pūjanairvaiṣṇavānāṃ ca prīṇayedakhileśvaram|| 20.83 ||
dvādaśyāṃ śravaṇe'pyevaṃ kuryād yāgaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ|
saptame saptame kuryād viṣṇuyāgamatandritaḥ|| 20.84 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tathā kṛtvā viṣṇuloke mahīyate|
ayane viṣuve caiva somasūryagrahe tathā|| 20.85 ||
saṃkrame viṣṇupañcamyāṃ kṛtveṣṭaṃ prāpnuyāt phalam|
prathame divase rātrau kuryād grāmapradakṣiṇam|| 20.86 ||
ahni pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāccheṣeṣu divaseṣu ca|
baliṃ ca sarvato dadyād bhūtebhyo'ntarbahistathā|| 20.87 ||
paribhramaṇakāle ca maṇḍaleṣu baliṃ haret|
kautukasya purastāt tu gantavyaṃ balidāyibhiḥ|| 20.88 ||
gandhapuṣpādisaṃyuktaṃ jaladānasamanvitam|
balivikṣepaṇaṃ kuryād balimudrāṃ ca darśayet|| 20.89 ||
kumudādidhvajeśānāṃ pañcāśadgaṇanāyakāḥ|
bhavanti śataśasteṣāṃ tathaiva ca sahasraśaḥ|| 20.90 |

<21>
|| athaikaviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepād yātrāmasya śubhāśubhām|
tīrthayātrā śubhā jñeyā sā'śubhā yā svamandire|| 21.1 ||
pūrvedyurutsavaṃ kṛtvā niśi dadyānmahābalim|
viṣṇupāriṣadebhyaśca pramathebhyo namo namaḥ|| 21.2 ||
mātṛbhyastadgaṇebhyaśca sarvebhyo vai namonamaḥ|
ityevaṃ sarvato dattvā praviśya prārthya pūrvavat|| 21.3 ||
pīṭhe sāstaraṇe devaṃ puṇyāhasvastivācanaiḥ|
kṛtakautukamārādhya tataḥ stotrādi kīrtayet|| 21.4 ||
snātaḥ svalaṅkṛtaḥ prātaḥ kṛtvā yāgaṃ puroditam|
ṣaḍbhiḥ kanikradatpūrvairhutvā svastyādipañcakaiḥ|| 21.5 ||
mūlenāṣṭaśataṃ caiva samidājyacarūn kramāt|
śakunāni parīkṣyātha snānayātrāṃ prayojayet|| 21.6 ||
rathaṃ vā kuñjaraṃ vā'sya yātrāvāhanamānayet|
nānāvāditrakuśalān bahuṃśca paricārakān|| 21.7 ||
chatradhvajapatākāśca bhaṭān dāsāṃśca bhaktakān|
brāhmaṇāṃśca samānīya muhūrte śobhane guruḥ|| 21.8 ||
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā'tra prāptānujño dvijottamaiḥ|
devaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya praṇipatya puraḥ sthitaḥ|| 21.9 ||
pīṭhe vinyasya karmārcāṃ kāryaṃ vijñāpayecchanaiḥ|
karmārcā tīrthayātrārthaṃ taveyaṃ kalpitā hare!|| 21.10 ||
tīrthayātrāṃ kurūṣvāsyāṃ saṃkramyānugrahāya naḥ|
tataḥ saṃhṛtya tāmarcāmutpādya vidhipūrvakam|| 21.11 ||
tasyāmāvāhayed devaṃ pañcopaniṣadaiḥ kramāt|
āvāhitamanusmṛtya dattvā'rdhyaṃ pratipūjya ca|| 21.12 ||
sadaśena navenaiva vastrayugmena veṣṭayet|
soṣṇīṣaḥ sottarīyaśca kṛtanyāsaḥ samāhitaḥ|| 21.13 ||
dakṣiṇena tamādāya pāṇinā vāgyataḥ svayam|
śiṣyaiḥ parivṛtau gacchenmaṇḍapaṃ samalaṃkṛtam|| 21.14 ||
tatra maṅgalavādaṃ tu sarvamaṅgalasaṃyutam|
kuryādatra dvijāḥ sarve vadeyuśca tathāstviti|| 21.15 ||
maṇḍapadvāramāsādya dattvā'rdhvaṃ saha mūrttipaiḥ|
gehaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya tato gacched bahiḥ punaḥ|| 21.16 ||
udakumbhaṃ haviśchatraṃ yāgopakaraṇāni ca|
prarohaghaṭikāścānye dārayeyurdvijottamāḥ|| 21.17 ||
bahirnirgamya ghoṣeṇa mahatā vāgyataḥ svayam|
vāhanaṃ garuḍaṃ dhyātvā pūjayitvā'diruhya tam|| 21.18 ||
gacchet tu yojanādarvāṅ nadīṃ vā'nyajalāśayam|
diṅmantreṇa bahissālaṃ kṛtvā pūrvaṃ pradakṣiṇam|| 21.19 ||
gaccheyurvāgyatāḥ sarve sottarīyāḥ svalaṃkṛtāḥ|
pathi ghoṣaṃ janā vādyairgītanṛttaravaistathā|| 21.20 ||
vāhanairvividhaiścāpi kurvīran devatuṣṭaye|
toraṇābhyantare gatvā maṇḍapaṃ tīrthapārśvataḥ|| 21.21 ||
vediṃ pariśritāṃ kṛtvā pīṭhe devaṃ niveśayet|
dīkṣitaiḥ pācayitvā'tra pāyasaṃ vijane kvacit|| 21.22 ||
dattvā pādyādi sarveṇa tamādāya nadīṃ vrajet|
vāriṇā tīrthamāvāhya vaiṣṇavaṃ tatra pāvanam|| 21.23 ||
tīrthe tridhā nimajjyāsmin mantreṇa parameṣṭhinā|
nivesya pīṭhe dattvā'rdhyaṃ pādyamācamanaṃ tathā|| 21.24 ||
āpohiṣṭhādibhiḥ prokṣya pañcabhiśca samāhitaḥ|
tamādāya punarmajjet toye kṛtvā'ghamarṣaṇam|| 21.25 ||
tatra snānaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ viduḥ|
pīṭhe niveśya vastre dve paridhāya nave svayam|| 21.26 ||
ācamya tu kṛtanyāsaḥ pūjayed vidhinā punaḥ|
ājyuktaṃ nivedyaṃ ca dattvā'smai vidhipūrvakam|| 21.27 ||
tīrthe'smin vaiṣṇave puṇye snāto'si puruṣottama!|
atastvamanugṛhṇīṣva svasthānopāśrayeṇa naḥ|| 21.28 ||
iti prārthya tamādāya pūrvavad vāhanaṃ nayet|
tamāruhya yathāpūrvaṃ prāsādamaśanairnayet|| 21.29 ||
yātrāhnyeva gṛhaṃ prāpya bimbe saṃkrāmayet tataḥ|
doṣo'nyathā mahānatra kartṝṇāṃ bhavati dhruvam|| 21.30 ||
gatvā garbhagṛhaṃ pīṭhe niveśya susamāhitaḥ|
toyena gandhapuṣpaiśca buddhyā''vāhya samantrakam|| 21.31 ||
mūlabimbe yathānyāyāṃ sarveṇa pratiropayet|
yātrādāvantataścaivamāvāhanavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ|| 21.32 ||
sakalīkṛtamārādhya dattvā'rdhyaṃ praṇipatya ca|
nirgacched dvāramāvṛtya brāhmaṇāṃścātra bhojayet|| 21.33 ||
niśi dadyād baliṃ caivaṃ śubhayātrā mayoditā|
aśubhā vāstusiddhyarthā kathyamānā'vadhāryatām|| 21.34 ||
prāsāde śithile jīrṇe mṛdbimbe citra eva vā|
niṣkrāmayennave gehe vidhinā'nena devatām|| 21.35 ||
parjanye vā jayante vā dityadityośca sambhavāt|
prāgadvāre paścimadvāre mṛgadauvārikasthitam|| 21.36 ||
prāsādābhimukhadvāramalpagehaṃ navaṃ śubham|
ekabhūmikamagrīvaṃ mṛdbhiḥ kāṣṭhairathāpi vā|| 21.37 ||
pīṭhaṃ kāṣṭhamayaṃ ramyaṃ saśvabhraṃ kārayet tataḥ|
kuryāllohamayaṃ bimbaṃ dāravaṃ vā'stramāpadi|| 21.38 ||
kṛte kālāvadhau samyak saṃkrāmaṇamiheṣyate|
māsādi dvādaśābdāntaṃ kālamatra pracakṣate|| 21.39 ||
navaṃ gehaṃ tu saṃśodhya sarvaṃ kṛtvā'tha pūrvavat|
rākṣoghnamāditaḥ kṛtvā sarvakarmāṇyataścaret|| 21.40 ||
sthāpanoktavidhānena maṇḍapaṃ kārayet punaḥ|
dhvajatoraṇasaṃyuktaṃ maṇḍayedaṅkurādibhiḥ|| 21.41 ||
gavyairastreṇa samprokṣya madhye svastikamālikhet|
vrīhibhiḥ sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā tasmin vāsobhirāstaret|| 21.42 ||
taṇḍulān nikṣipet tasmin kambalādibhirāstaret|
kuśairvastraiśca puṣpaiśca gandhādibhirathārcayet|| 21.43 ||
jale'divāsya karmārcāṃ prakṣālya salilaiḥ śubhaiḥ|
mṛttoyaiḥ patratoyaiśca kaṣāyairgavyapañcakaiḥ|| 21.44 ||
gandhapuṣpairalaṃkṛtya vastrairācchādya śāyayet|
śaṅkhabheryādinādena gandhapuṣpādisaṃyutam|| 21.45 ||
ā saptarātrāt sandhyāsu devatābhyo baliṃ haret|
śṛṇvantu devatāḥ sarvāḥ prāsādamimamāśritāḥ|| 21.46 ||
yaducyamānamasmābhiranugṛhṇantu tena naḥ|
pūrvaiḥ pūrvaṃ kṛtamidaṃ devālayamasāravat|| 21.47 ||
prāptaṃ kālavaśād bhūyo vayaṃ tat kartumudyatāḥ|
kālenaitāvatā bhūyaḥ prāsāde'smin punarnave|| 21.48 ||
prāpayāmo vayaṃ satyamityuktvā ghoṣayed budhaḥ|
māsādarvāk tu kālaścet tatra saṃkoca iṣyate|| 21.49 ||
prāsādadehasaṃsthāni sarvatattvānyanukramāt|
devatāśca tathā hṛtvā parivārasamāyutam|| 21.50 ||
dhyātvā saṃhāramārgeṇa yathāvad deśikottamaḥ|
tejorūpaṃ tu tat sarvaṃ mūlabimbe niyojayet|| 21.51 ||
tadūrdhvaṃ ca tathā kṛtvā saṃkrāmayitumārabhet|
praviśya garbhagehaṃ tu mūrtipaiḥ sahito guruḥ|| 21.52 ||
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā'tha brāhmaṇaiḥ svasti vācayet|
devasya mahatīṃ pūjāṃ kṛtvā bhaktyā yathāvidhi|| 21.53 ||
praṇipatya puraḥ sthitvā kāryaṃ vijñāpayecchanaiḥ|
bhagavan! vāstvabhinavaṃ bimbaṃ ca (vā) tava śobhanam|| 21.54 ||
kārayiṣyanti te bhaktāstadanujñātumarhasi|
kleśavāsastvayā deva! rocanīyo'lpake gṛhe|| 21.55 ||
yāvannavaṃ śubhaṃ kṛtvā punaḥ saṃsthāpayāmahe|
tato lohamayaṃ kumbhaṃ śodhītaṃ sūtraveṣṭitam|| 21.56 ||
devasya purato nyasya sapavitrākṣatādikam|
tato nirgamya taiḥ sārdhaṃ bimbasaṃskāramārabhet|| 21.57 ||
saṃhārasṛṣṭiyogena dhyātvā tattvānyanukramāt|
jananādikramāddhutvā tattvānyagnau yathāvidhi|| 21.58 ||
nirgatya pṛcchedācāryaḥ kartṝn kālāvadhiṃ punaḥ|
kiyantaṃ kālamatreśaḥ pravāsavasatiṃ vaset|| 21.59 ||
māsādūrdhvaṃ dvādaśābdārvāk kālāvadhiḥ smṛtaḥ|
nārvāṅ nordhvaṃ kleśavāsaṃ vāsudevo'numanyate|| 21.60 ||
iti pṛṣṭāstu te brūyuḥ kālāvadhimasaṃśayam|
viśrāvya kālaniyamaṃ devatā nīyate bahiḥ|| 21.61 ||
vijñāpayet tato devaṃ praviśya susamāhitaḥ|
kālaṃ kartṛbhiruddiṣṭaṃ tataḥ kuryādimaṃ vidhim|| 21.62 ||
yātrāhomādisaṃyuktaṃ muhurte śobhane guruḥ|
hutvā vāstoṣpatiṃ sadyo devaṃ niṣkrāmayed bahiḥ|| 21.63 ||
kalaśaṃ pūjayitvā'gre pūritaṃ vastraveṣṭitam|
kumbhe tasmin vidhānena yogapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet|| 21.64 ||
sahemaratnagandhāmbhojāmbūnadamayāmbujam|
āvāhya vidhinā tasmin devadevaṃ sanātanam|| 21.65 ||
pañcopaniṣadairmantrairmantramūrtisamanvitam|
dattvā'rvyaṃ pūjayitvā'tra gandhapūṣpaiḥ praṇamya ca|| 21.66 ||
vastrādiveṣṭitaṃ bimbaṃ prayatnāt parikalpya ca|
kalaśaṃ dhārayan mūrdhnā nirgacched vāgyato gurūḥ|| 21.67 ||
śaṅkhadundubhinirghoṣairjayaśabdaiśca puṣkalaiḥ|
gehaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya brahmaghoṣasamanvitam|| 21.68 ||
śanaiḥ śanairvrajed yatnād vāstrālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ|
śākunasvastisūktābyāmatodevādina tathā|| 21.69 ||
antarmaṇḍapamānīya sthāpayecchayane harim|
dvādaśākṣaramantreṇa nyāsaṃ mūlena kalpayet|| 21.70 ||
sūktena ca stutiṃ kṛtvā pauruṣeṇa vicakṣaṇaḥ|
viṣṇusūktena mūlena dviṣaṭkenāṣṭakena ca|| 21.71 ||
nāmabhiḥkeśavādyaiśca dadyāt puṣpāṇi bhaktitaḥ|
śālitaṇḍulasampūrṇāñ jalapūrṇānathāpi vā|| 21.72 ||
ghaṭān prāgādi vinyasya keśavādīn prapūjayet|
praṇavenaiva tān sarvān devādāhṛtya vinyaset|| 21.73 ||
mūrtidevyo bahiḥ pūjyā mudrā dikpāśca tadbahiḥ|
stutvā'nujñāpya taṃ devaṃ prathamāṃ śāntimabhyaset|| 21.74 ||
dikkuṇḍeṣvagnimādhāya pūrvavanmūrtidhārakāḥ|
ādhivāsanikān homānārabheran yathāpuram|| 21.75 ||
hutvā devamanujñāpya kuryācchāntiṃ ca deśikaḥ|
spṛṣṭvā mantraistato japtvā kṣālayed gavyapañcakaiḥ|| 21.76 ||
vyastaiścaiva samastaiśca kaṣāyāmalākāmbubhiḥ|
hemamiśrajalaiḥ paścāt snāpayecchuddhavāribhiḥ|| 21.77 ||
sūktaiḥ stutvā'rcayed devaṃ gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ kramāt|
kalaśān sthāpayet paścād dvāradeśeṣu sarvataḥ|| 21.78 ||
dravyāṇi pūjanārthāni homārthāni ca saṃharet|
tūryavāditrahastāṃśca kalpayet kuśalān bahūn|| 21.79 ||
etat sarvamupānīya nimittānyupalakṣya ca|
sthānāni devatānāṃ ca kalpayitvā yathākramam|| 21.80 ||
duḥsvapnādiṣu jāteṣu prāyaścittāni kārayet|
mūlena juhuyādājyaṃ sahasraṃ śatameva vā|| 21.81 ||
puṇyāhajayaghoṣaiśca muhūrte śobhane guruḥ|
devaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā labdhānujñaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ|| 21.82 ||
sampūjya vidhinā devaṃ puṇyāhajayamaṅgalaiḥ|
praṇavena tamutthāpya śākunena tu deśikaḥ|| 21.83 ||
svastisūktena cānīya praviśet sapradakṣiṇam|
praviśya taiḥ samādiṣṭaṃ kālaṃ vijñāpya viṣṇave|| 21.84 ||
sthāpayet pratimāṃ tatra mantrametamudīrayet|
bhagavannalpagehe'smin vāsaḥ kleśo'pi te hare!|| 21.85 ||
vastavyo bhagavaṃstāvad yāvad gehaṃ samāpyate|
kārakasya parāmṛddhimanujānan mahodayām|| 21.86 ||
bhaktānāmanukampārthamiha tvaṃ sthātumarhasi|
ityuktvā kiñcidānamya siñcet tadvāri mūrdhani|| 21.87 ||
pañcopaniṣadān mantrān ghyātvā sarveṇa deśikaḥ|
sṛṣṭinyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryāt pauruṣaṃ sūktamuccaret|| 21.88 ||
tato mūrtyā tu saṃyojya pūjayet kalpavartmanā|
kalpayet parivārāṃśca diśāhomān prakalpayet|| 21.89 ||
dakṣiṇāṃ ca tato dadyādannādyaṃ cātra kārayet|
yannoktaṃ tacca vai kuryāt pratiṣṭhāvidhinā sudhīḥ|| 21.90 ||
niṣkrāmādipraveśāntaṃ kartā syānniyato va

<22>
|| atha dvāviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepād balikarma yathākramam|
trisandhyamucitaṃ nityamutsavādau viśeṣataḥ|| 22.1 ||
ārādhya vidhinā devaṃ gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ kramāt|
saṃskṛtaṃ saṃskṛte vahnau devāyānnaṃ nivedayet|| 22.2 ||
taccheṣeṇaiva karmārcāpīṭhaṃ pātre prakalpayet|
vinā mantreṇa lokāgnau balyarthamapi pācayet|| 22.3 ||
sthālyāṃ tu taṇḍulāñ śuddhān nirūptān mūlavidyayā|
prokṣya tridhā parikṣālya prajvālyāgniṃ ca tejasā|| 22.4 ||
paced vyajanavātena havirna skandyate yathā|
abhighāryāvatāryātha vinyaset pātraghaṭṭitam|| 22.5 ||
pātre'nyasminnupastīrya kṣiptaṃ pratyabhighārya ca|
sājyaṃ nivedayed bhāgaṃ devāyānyaṃ ca homayet|| 22.6 ||
gomayenopalipyaiśe pātraṃ darbheṣu sādayet|
haimaṃ vā rājataṃ tāmraṃ ratnimātraṃ tu maṇḍalam|| 22.7 ||
aṣṭākṣareṇa samprokṣya samyak prottānitaṃ jalaiḥ|
abhighārya haviśśeṣaṃ hṛdā tasmin vinikṣipet|| 22.8 ||
pīṭhaṃ prakalpayet tena karmārcā yatra neṣyate|
anyadā masṛṇīkṛtya dadhyājyakleditaṃ śubham|| 22.9 ||
śivakaṃ kārayet tena caturaṅgulavistaram|
dvādaśāṣṭāṅgulotsedhaṃ ṣaḍaṅgulamathāpi vā|| 22.10 ||
vinyasya kāñcanaṃ mūrdhni śaktitamāvāhya pūjayet|
parameṣṭhyātmikīṃ madhye puruṣādisamāvṛtām|| 22.11 ||
tasmin saṃsthāpayedarcāṃ sarveṇāvāhya pañcabhiḥ|
mūlenāvāhya vā hemni varṇamūrtivṛtaṃ harim|| 22.12 ||
gandhapuṣpaistamabhyarcya dhyātvā savanabheditam|
tatpātraṃ sthāpayenmūrdhni dīkṣitasya dvijanmanaḥ|| 22.13 ||
uṣṇīṣādiyuto maunī gṛhitvā vyāhṛtiṃ japet|
devasya purataḥ sthitvā praṇamya tu bahirvrajet|| 22.14 ||
śaṅkhadundubhinirghoṣairjayaśabdaiśca puṣkalaiḥ|
aṣṭamaṅgalasaṃyuktaṃ vitānādyupaśobhitam|| 22.15 ||
devanāgāpsaroyakṣamunigandharvakinnarāḥ|
sarve cānugamiṣyanto dhyeyāḥ svaguṇasaṃvṛtāḥ|| 22.16 ||
gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ jalaṃ dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ chatraṃ ca cāmaram|
maṅgalāni havirdhaṇṭāṃ gṛhṇīyustatra dīkṣitāḥ|| 22.17 ||
nirgamya śamitairghauṣairbrāhmaṇānāmanujñayā|
maṅgalāni gururbrūyād dvijāścānye tathāstviti|| 22.18 ||
jagannirmāṇasaṃhārasthityanugrahahetavaḥ|
devānāmīśvarā devā maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|| 22.19 ||
viṣṇurbrahmā ca rudraśca pracaṇḍaścaṇḍa eva ca|
garutmān ṛṣabhaścaite maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|| 22.20 ||
indraścāgniryamaścaiva nirṛtirvaruṇastathā|
vāyurvaiśravaṇeśānau maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|| 22.21 ||
varāhanarasiṃhau ca rāmaśrīdharavāmanāḥ|
hayāsyavāsudevau ca maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|| 22.22 ||
pūruśca nirṛtiścaiva siṃho nārāyaṇācyutau|
aniruddho hariḥ kṛṣṇo maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|| 22.23 ||
kumudaḥ kumudākṣaśca puṇḍarīko'tha vāmanaḥ|
śaṅkukarṇaḥ sarvanetraḥ sumukhaḥ supratiṣṭhitaḥ|| 22.24 ||
devadauvārikāścaite maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu viṣṇupāriṣadāśca ye|| 22.25 ||
anupāriṣadāścānye maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
bhūrlokabhuvarlokādyāḥ sapta lokāḥ prakīrtitāḥ|| 22.26 ||
sāgarāśca tathā sarve maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
tathā pātālalokāśca rudralokāḥ sukhoṣitāḥ|| 22.27 ||
dikṣu sarvāsu tiṣṭhanto maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
vedāścāpyuvedāśca setihāsāḥ kathā api|| 22.28 ||
ṣaḍaṅgāni ca śāstrāṇi maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
gaṅgā ca yamunā caiva tathā godā sarasvatī|| 22.29 ||
narmadā caiva kāverī maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
sūryādayo grahāścātha meṣādyā rāśayastathā|| 22.30 ||
māsāścāpi saviṣṇvādyā maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
sarve vidyeśvarāścāpi ye divyā ye ca mānavāḥ|| 22.31 ||
divyantarikṣabhūmisthā maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
dharā dhruvaśca somaśca āpaścaivānalo'nilaḥ|| 22.32 ||
pratyūṣaśca prabhāsaśca maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
yajñāśca pañcayajñāśca pākayajñāstathaiva ca|| 22.33 ||
sarvayajñamahāyajñā maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
kumāryaśca kumārāśca gaṇeśāśca vināyakāḥ|| 22.34 ||
sarpāśca kinnarāścāpi maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
mṛgavyādhaśca śarvaśca nirṛtiścāja ekapāt|| 22.35 ||
ahirbudhniḥ pinākī ca bhavano'theśvarastathā|
sthāṇurbhavaśca pāpārirmaṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|| 22.36 ||
dhātā'ryamā ca mitraśca varuṇoṃ'śo bhagastathā|
indro vivasvān pūṣā ca parjanyatvaṣṭṭaviṣṇavaḥ|| 22.37 ||
ete vai dvādaśādityā maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
yavakrītaśca krītaśca arvāvasuvibhāvasū|| 22.38 ||
kakṣīvān nāradaḥ kaṇvo maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
unmuciḥ pramuciścaiva ātreyo'gastya eva ca|| 22.39 ||
dṛḍhāyuścedhmavāhaśca maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
dṛṣadguhyamahadguhyaparivrājāstathaiva ca|| 22.40 ||
ekadvitrisamākhyātā maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
kaśyapo'trirvasiṣṭhaśca bhāradvājo'ṅgirāstathā|| 22.41 ||
kauśiko bhagavāṃścaite maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
āvaho vivahaścaiva udvahaḥ suvahastathā|| 22.42 ||
parīvaho vivahaścaite maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|
śvetaketuśca vyāsaśca vālmīkiḥ suyaśāstathā|| 22.43 ||
aurvaśeyaśca śaktiśca tathā caiva parāśaraḥ|
saptaite divyamunayo maṅgalaṃ pradiśantu naḥ|| 22.44 ||
evaṃ maṅgalavādāt tāḥ siddhiṃ yacchanti devatāḥ|
sarvakarma samārambhe kartavyaṃ ca sumaṅgalam|| 22.45 ||
maṇḍapadvāramāsādya śaṅkhatūryādinisvanaiḥ|
dvāḥsthādibhyo baliṃ dadyād gandhapuṣpādisaṃyutam|| 22.46 ||
ebhirmantrairupasthāya svamantrairabhipūjya tān|
tatra tatra baliṃ dadyād devadṛṣṭyā'valokitam|| 22.47 ||
namaste dvārapālāya caṇḍāyāmitatejase|
narasiṃhatanūjāya cakreṇāṅkitamaulaye|| 22.48 ||
namaste dvārapālāya pracaṇḍāyāmitaujase|
narasiṃhatanūjāya śaṅkhenāṅkitamaulaye|| 22.49 ||
vāhanāya mahāviṣṇostārkṣyāyāmitatejase|
garuḍāya namaste'stu sarvasarpendramṛtyave|| 22.50 ||
daityadarpavināśāya sahasrākṣāya dhīmate|
kuliśavyagrahastāya namaste'stu śatakrato!|| 22.51 ||
mukhaṃ yaḥ sarvadevānāṃ yena havyaṃ ca nīyate|
yena pravartate sarvaṃ namastasmai havirbhuje|| 22.52 ||
yena saṃhriyate sarvaṃ yena dharmaśca rakṣyate|
yasmād bibheti loko'yaṃ pretanātha! namo'stute|| 22.53 ||
rākṣasā yātudhānāśca piśācāścāśrayanti yam|
tasmai nirṛtirūpāya rakṣasāṃ pataye namaḥ|| 22.54 ||
yena saṃrakṣyate sarvaṃ yasmin sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam|
amoghapāśahastāya tasmaipāṃpataye namaḥ|| 22.55 ||
pūryante ca hriyante ca yena prāṇādayaḥ kramāt|
bhūtānāṃ pataye nityaṃ namastasmai marutpate!|| 22.56 ||
yatprasādājjagat kṛtsraṃ dhanena paripūryate|
yakṣaguhyakanāthāya dhananātha! namo'stu te|| 22.57 ||
yena kālāgninā loko yugānteṣvavagṛhyate|
tasmai te brahmaṇaḥ putra! virupākṣa! namo'stu te|| 22.58 ||
vīrabhadra! namaste'stu namo vighnesvarāya ca|
brāhmyādibhyaśca saptabhyo mātṛbhyo vai namo namaḥ|| 22.59 ||
śrīdharāya namaste'stu namaste'svamukhāya ca|
vāgīśvaryādimātṛbhyaḥ sarvābhyo vai namo namaḥ|| 22.60 ||
viṣvaksena! namaste'stu devanirmālyadhāriṇe|
śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇe! mahāpāriṣadeśvara!|| 22.61 ||
ye samastaṃ jagad vyāpya tiṣṭhati balikāṅkṣiṇaḥ|
viṣṇupāriṣadāḥ sarve gṛhṇantu balimuttamam|| 22.62 ||
ityāghoṣya bahirdikṣu gaṇairaṣṭavidhaiḥ saha|
kumudādibaliṃ dattvā balipīṭhe baliṃ haret|| 22.63 ||
ye bhūtā iti dikṣūrdhvaṃ bhūtanāthāya madhyataḥ|
muṣṭinā muṣṭinā sarvān prīṇayed viṣṇupārṣadān|| 22.64 ||
gṛhṇantu balimetaṃ tu bāhyasthā vihagānanāḥ|
ye krūro ye'lpasatvāśca bhūtapretaniśācarāḥ|| 22.65 ||
vetālāścaiva mātaṅgā hīnāhīnāśca ye gaṇāḥ|
sarveṣāmeva teṣāṃ ca harāmi balimuttamam|| 22.66 ||
sarvebhyaścaiva bhūtebhyaḥ sagaṇebhyo namo namaḥ|
triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛttya balinā toṣayed gaṇān|| 22.67 ||
savane savane caiva nṛttagītādisaṃyutam|
gandhapuṣpādi dattvā'nte baliśeṣaṃ jalāplutam|| 22.68 ||
nirharedoṃ namo viṣṇupārṣadebhya iti bruvan|
ācamya prayato viṣṇuṃ smarannetya samāhitaḥ|| 22.69 ||
sarveṇāropya tāmarcāṃ punarmūle niyojayet|
viṣvaksenāya tat sarvaṃ samālyaṃ dāpayeddhaviḥ|| 22.70 ||
piṇḍe tu balipātre vā patite dūṣite'pi vā|
tatra prakṣālya tat snātvā kṛtvā nyāsaṃ yathākramam|| 22.71 ||
pariśrityāśu tāṃ bhūmiṃ gomayenopalepayet|
pāyasaṃ śrapayitvā'tra ghṛtamiśraṃ suśobhanam|| 22.72 ||
likhitvā strastikaṃ tatra cūrṇaiḥ śāliniśāmayaiḥ|
vrīhibhiḥ sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā tasminnāstīrya cāmbaram|| 22.73 ||
taṇḍulaiśca kuśaiḥ puṣpaiḥ prokṣyāstreṇa samāhitaḥ|
mūlena vinyaset pātraṃ pravaṇena spṛśet tataḥ|| 22.74 ||
punaścāstreṇa samprokṣya dakṣiṇe śāntimarcayet|
ullekhanādikaṃ kṛtvā saṃskṛtyāgniṃ yathāvidhi|| 22.75 ||
hutvā'nnaṃ mūlamantreṇa praṇavenājyameva ca|
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vā'pi saṃhasraṃ vā yathākramam|| 22.76 ||
haviśśeṣaṃ tataḥ paścād balipātre tu nikṣipet|
tat kṛtvā pūrvavat prokṣya mūlenāvāhanaṃ bhavet|| 22.77 ||
pādmamardhyaṃ tato dattvā gandhapuṣpairathārcayet|
namaskṛtya tataḥ stutvā stotrairevaṃ nivedayet|| 22.78 ||
bhagavan! balipātasya prāyaścittavidhirmayā|
kṛto'yaṃ tvatprasādena sampūrṇaḥ śāntido'stu naḥ|| 22.79 ||
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā ca brāhmaṇaiḥ svasti vācayet|
namaskṛtya tataḥ stutvā sottarīyaḥ samāhitaḥ|| 22.80 ||
uddhṛtya vyāhṛtīrjaptvā mūrdhni tāreṇa vinyaset|
śaṅkhatūryādisaṃyuktaṃ śanairgatvā pradakṣiṇam|| 22.81 ||
antaḥ praveśayet paścādaiśānyāṃ sthāpayed budhaḥ|
udvāsyānnaṃ bahistyaktvā viṣvaksenāya kalpayet|| 22.82 ||
pātraṃ prakṣālya toyena vidhinā''camya ca svayam|
prāsāde vinyaset pātraṃ tataḥ kuryācca śāntikam|| 22.83 ||
rāśicakravidhānena keśavādīn yathākramam|
cakrābje pūjayed vidvānagnikāryasamanvitam|| 22.84 ||
balipātadigīśaṃ ca tanmāsādhipameva ca|
tithyṛkṣavārarāśīnāṃ devatāścāpi tarpayet|| 22.85 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ balidānavidhirnāma dvāviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<23>
|| atha trayoviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||


atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepād viśvārcāṃ sarvabhūtidām|
digvibhāgasamāyuktāṃ sarvalokahitāya vai|| 23.1 ||
vidhivat sthāpite deve yathānyāyaṃ prapūjayet|
kartavyā viśvadevārcā vṛddhyarthaṃ sarvadā'pi vā|| 23.2 ||
madhye sāṅgaṃ yajed viṣṇuṃ digvidikṣu tataḥ surān|
pūrvādau puruṣaṃ satyamacyutaṃ cānirūddhakam|| 23.3 ||
viśvarūpaṃ ca vaikuṇṭhaṃ sarvaṃ bhāvanameva ca|
vidyādhipatayaścaite śaṅkhacakragadādharāḥ|| 23.4 ||
viṣṇurūpāḥ sadā pūjyāḥ sagaṇāstu pṛthak pṛthak|
śaktayo'ṣṭau bhṛśādisthāḥ surūpāḥ sarvakāmadāḥ|| 23.5 ||
prajñā buddhiḥ smṛtiḥ kīrttiḥ śrutirmedhā dhṛtiḥ kṣamā|
śaṅkhaḥ sudarśanahalau gadāmusalakhaḍgakāḥ|| 23.6 ||
dhanuḥ sraguttamā cāṣṭau digvidikṣu ca tadbahiḥ|
vajradaṇḍatriśūlāni pāśo'strāṇi tathaiva ca|| 23.7 ||
tataḥ śaktiśca śastrāṇi vaiṣṇavāstraṃ bhṛśadiṣu|
astrāvaraṇamevaṃ tu śūlaṃ saparaśuṃ yajet|| 23.8 ||
indro yamaśca varuṇaḥ somo'gnirnirṛtistathā|
vāyurīśāna ityaṣṭau lokapālāstu tadbahiḥ|| 23.9 ||
ādityā viśvadevāśca maruto vighnanāyakāḥ|
skando rudrāśca vasavaścāśvinau ca bhṛśādiṣu|| 23.10 ||
bahirgrahagaṇāḥ pūjyāḥ pitṛyakṣagaṇāstathā|
gandharvāḥ sarvabhūtāni cāmbikā rākṣasāstathā|| 23.11 ||
vidyādharāśca siddhāśca pūjyāḥ sarve svanāmabhiḥ|
tato'psarolokamātṛkūṣmāṇḍāśca piśācakāḥ|| 23.12 ||
nāgāśca kinnarāḥ sarpā vyādhayaśca bhṛśādiṣu|
nandanaḥ kumudaścātha dvāḥsthau dakṣiṇavāmayoḥ|| 23.13 ||
gṛhagopuramadhye'rcyo dhvajo'śvastadbhṛśāntare|
ratho gṛhendramadhye syāt parjanyendre khageśvaraḥ|| 23.14 ||
puṃśaṅkhamadhyagaḥ pūjyaḥ suvarcāḥ kalaśādhipaḥ|
puraprāsādamadhye'rcyo balākhyo maṇḍapādhipaḥ|| 23.15 ||
bhāṇḍāgāre'kṣayaḥ pūjyo jalasthāne tathā naraḥ|
sabhāsthāne'dbhuto vṛddhikaraścātha mahānase|| 23.16 ||
unmādaḥ pacanāgāre tarpaṇo bhojanālaye|
kundaraḥ kusumāgāre nirgame vikṣarastathā|| 23.17 ||
balaścātibalaścobhau pīṭhe'rcyau balirakṣakau|
vikaṭaśca virūpākṣaḥ pūrvagopurapālakau|| 23.18 ||
dvāḥsthau dakṣiṇavāmasthau bhīmarūpau gadādharau|
sanātanaśca sanako dakṣiṇadvārapālakau|| 23.19 ||
pūjanīyau prayatnena khaṅgamudgaradhāriṇau|
daṇḍapāśadharau śāntapramodau caiva paścime|| 23.20 ||
uttare sarvadaḥ sarvadamanaścāpatūṇinau|
carako bahirīśāne vidāro'gnau tu pūtanaḥ|| 23.21 ||
nairṛte vāyubāhye tu rākṣasaḥ pāpacārakaḥ|
balipīṭhādhipaḥ prācyāṃ devo nandikaro yame|| 23.22 ||
paścād vibhīṣaṇaḥ saumye sulabhasthānapālakāḥ|
ye'nuktā mātaro'nanto viṣvakseno dhvajādhipāḥ|| 23.23 ||
revantaḥ kṣetrapālāśca te pūjyāstatra tatra vai|
sthitāsīne catuṣṣaṣṭiḥ supte te'śītirekinī|| 23.24 ||
sāmānyaṃ tu śataṃ tredhā prākārāntarvibhājanam|
antaścatuṣkaṃ prāsādaṃ dviguṇaṃ ca prakalpayet|| 23.25 ||
dakṣiṇe nandanaṃ dvāḥsthaṃ caturbāhuṃ gadādharam|
tathaiva kumudaṃ kuryād vāme paṭṭasapāśinam|| 23.26 ||
aṅgulyardhārdhayug vāme kuryādālayamāsane|
śate pādapraviṣṭāḥ syuḥ parivārālayāḥ pṛthak|| 23.27 ||
yathaiva tu catuṣṣaṣṭau saṃgamasthāḥ surāḥ pṛthak|
tathā śatapade proktā garbhādiparikalpanā|| 23.28 ||
caturthe saṅgame'ryamṇo vivasvanmitrabhūbhṛtām|
puruṣādyālayaṃ kuryāt sāvitrādyādyasaṅgame|| 23.29 ||
viśvādīnāṃ kramādevaṃ sthānāni parikalpayet|
tṛtīye saṅgame'ryamṇaḥ pañcame tu vivasvataḥ|| 23.30 ||
tṛtīye tasya mitrasya pañcame'sya tṛtīyake|
bhūbhṛtaḥ pañcame tasya tṛtīye'ryamapañme|| 23.31 ||
prajñādīnāṃ kramādevamālayāḥ parikīrtitāḥ|
śaṅkhādayo'ryamādīnāṃ caturṇāmādyasaṅgame|| 23.32 ||
musalādyālayāścaivaṃ sāvitrādyādyasaṅgame|
saṅge'ryamasavitrādidvādaśānāṃ dvayordvayoḥ|| 23.33 ||
kiñcitsūtraṃ vimucyātha vajrādyastrāṇi kalpayet|
vajradaṇḍādayo'straiḥ svaiḥ sthāpyā bhūtagaṇaiḥ saha|| 23.34 ||
bhānvindrasaṅgamādyaṣṭasthāneṣvindrādilokapāḥ|
saṅge bhṛśakhayorgehakṣatavaitathayostathā|| 23.35 ||
gandharvabhṛṅgayoścaiva dvāḥ sthasugrīvayostathā|
śvāsaśoṣaṇayornāgamukhyayordityṛkākhyayoḥ|| 23.36 ||
jayaparjanyayoścānye sampūjyā devatāgaṇāḥ|
bāhye'rkayamavārīśasomānāṃ tu grahādayaḥ|| 23.37 ||
agneḥ pitṝṇāṃ rogeśo(śād)rbahiḥ syuścaṇḍikādayaḥ|
khabāhye'psarasaḥ pūṣṇi pitṛbhadravināyakāḥ|| 23.38 ||
kūṣmāṇḍāstu mṛge dvārsthe piśācāḥ śoṣaṇe'hayaḥ|
mukhye tu kinnarāḥ sarpā vyādhayastvargale śive|| 23.39 ||
satye raṅgaṃ jalasthānaṃ parjanye kārayed budhaḥ|
carakasya vidārasya pūtanasya ca rakṣasaḥ|| 23.40 ||
īśāgnipitṛrogāṇāṃ bāhye koṇe smṛtā gṛhāḥ|
tadvāhyatastu maryādābhittirucchritagopurā|| 23.41 ||
balipīṭhaṃ tu kartavyaṃ śāstrato gopurād bahiḥ|
śakragopuramadhye tu dhvajasthānaṃ vidhīyate|| 23.42 ||
tatrāśvarathatārkṣyāṇāmālayāḥ paritaḥ kramāt|
śaṅkhagopuramadhyasthaḥ kartavyaḥ kalaśādhipaḥ|| 23.43 ||
tathā puraprasādāntaḥ sthitaḥ syānmaṇḍapādhipaḥ|
bhallāṭasaṅgame caiva bhāṇḍāgāraṃ prakalpayet|| 23.44 ||
mahendra tu sabhāsthānaṃ satye syād bhojanālayaḥ|
pūṣṇi pāko mṛge puṣpaṃ puṣpadante'mbunirgamaḥ|| 23.45 ||
satye vā sa bhavet kūpaḥ kartavyo'ditigocare|
asure tu balisthānaṃ gandho roge prakīrtitaḥ|| 23.46 ||
īśo'gniḥ pitaro roga iti devāstu koṇagāḥ|
argale kṣetrapālasya bhṛṅge vai śāsturālayaḥ|| 23.47 ||
eṣa pūrvamukhasyokto vidhirviśvārcane hareḥ|
dhvajādi viparītaṃ syāt paścimābhimukhasya tu|| 23.48 ||
asure syāt tadā raṅgaṃ satye ca balimaṇḍapaḥ|
mahendre jalamārgaḥ syādayaṃ śatapade vidhiḥ|| 23.49 ||
saṅgamasthāstu catvāra itare saṅgameśvarāḥ|
etāvāṃstu catuṣṣaṣṭau viśeṣo'tra prakīrtitaḥ|| 23.50 ||
kumudaḥ karavīraśca dvārapālau caturbhujau|
purataḥ saṅgame'ryamṇaścaturthe garuḍālayaḥ|| 23.51 ||
subhadrasya subāhośca vijayasya balasya ca|
kumudasya muneścaiva viṣvaksenasya cālayāḥ|| 23.52 ||
āgneyādiṣu kartavyā gaṇeśānāṃ prakīrtitāḥ|
sarve caturbhujāḥ kāryā gadācakrāsidhāriṇaḥ|| 23.53 ||
suvarṇaśaṅkhanīlābhrakṛṣṇapiñjaragaurakāḥ|
śyāmaśca te'ryamādisthā lokapālāśca kīrtitāḥ|| 23.54 ||
prākārasthāstu kartavyāḥ kṣetraparyantadevatāḥ|
bhānostu purataścakraṃ tato gopurapīṭhake|| 23.55 ||
tataścaturṣu koṇeṣu rakṣasāṃ sthānamucyate|
ekāśītau ca paritaḥ padaṃ tritrikasaṃkhyayā|| 23.56 ||
gaṇeśvarā divagīśāśca kartavyāḥ svapadasthitāḥ|
śeṣaṃ kuryād yathoddiṣṭaṃ viṣṇorāvaraṇakramam|| 23.57 ||
bahiḥ prākārakoṇasthāḥ kartavyā rakṣasāṃ gṛhāḥ|
gopurādi ca kartavyaṃ balipīṭhānvitaṃ tathā|| 23.58 ||
evameva vidhānena kṛtvā viṣṇorihālayam|
sarvakāmānavāpnoti gacched viṣṇupadaṃ punaḥ|| 23.59 ||
śaṅkhacakragadākhaḍgamusalaṃ halameva ca|
cāpo'ṅkuśaṃ srageteṣāmālayāścātra kīrtitāḥ|| 23.60 ||
payohemakhanīlāsṛkpadmābhrāgnitaḍitprabhāḥ|
mūrdhni svāṅkasamāyuktāḥ kartavyā harihetayaḥ|| 23.61 ||
pūrvāstvapraticālyāḥ syuritarāścāsthirāḥ śubhāḥ|
anyonyābhimukhāḥ kāryāstathānye dakṣiṇottarāḥ|| 23.62 ||
sthāneśvarasya devasya yat pūrvakṛtamāspadam|
tasya pratimukhe kāryaṃ nānyāspadamaśatrubhiḥ|| 23.63 ||
ajñānādiha yaḥ kuryānnaśyet sthānaṃ śriyā saha|
anyonyābhimukhe cobhe na kārye devatāgṛhe|| 23.64 ||
anyonyapṛṣṭhasaṃsthe ca rājarāṣṭrādināśane|
nānupṛṣṭhaṃ tathā kuryāt kṛte cānyo niṣicyate|| 23.65 ||
vyatyastamekasūtrasthaṃ na kuryādeva sarvathā|
yatomukhaḥ pradhānaḥ syād devāścānye tatomukhāḥ|| 23.66 ||
kartavyāḥ parivārāstu pradhānābhimukhāḥ sadā|
ekasūtraṃ tathākramya viparītaṃ na kārayet|| 23.67 ||
anyonyaṃ dakṣiṇākṣibhyāṃ praśastaṃ darśanaṃ dvayoḥ|
apraśastaṃ ca vijñeyaṃ tathā vāmākṣidarśanam|| 23.68 ||
parivāravidhau cāpi sūtramākramya nācaret|
aṅgulaṃ golakaṃ vā'pi parihṛtyaiva kārayet|| 23.69 ||
āsanaṃ grāmamadhye tu pūrvatomukhamiṣyate|
grāmasyābhimukhaṃ tvevaṃ sthānaṃ pūrve ca paścime|| 23.70 ||
śayanaṃ dakṣiṇasyāṃ tu kartavyaṃ paścimāmukham|
uttarasyāṃ tu śayanaṃ pūrvatomukhameva ca|| 23.71 ||
kṣetrabhāgavaśādeva kartavyaṃ tvanyathāmukham|
devāgre dakṣiṇenātha sarvato vā jalāśayāḥ|| 23.72 ||
na rudrābhimukhaṃ kuryād grāmaṃ nagarameva vā|
noccasthānagataṃ caiva durgādisthānamiṣyate|| 23.73 ||
yatra nīce sthito viṣṇuruccairanyāśca devatāḥ|
vinaśyedacirādeva tat sthānaṃ rāṣṭrameva vā|| 23.74 ||
araṇye parvate tīrthe nadītīre ca saṅgame|
kṣetre ca śraddhayā kuryād yuktyā bhūmyādisaṅgraham|| 23.75 ||
devabhāgasthitaṃ devaṃ svayaṃ tadbhāgasaṃsthitaḥ|
arcayīta prayatnena paiśācāsuravarjitam|| 23.76 ||
brahmasthānagataṃ vā'pi devadevaṃ samarcayet|
devabhāgasthitastveva dravyaistadbhāgasaṃsthitaiḥ|| 23.77 ||
vidiksūtraṃ parityajya dakṣiṇaṃ pārśvamāśritaḥ|
arcayet taṃ prayatnena yastu siddhimabhīpsati|| 23.78 ||
japaṃ sampuṭavinyastaṃ devapārśve nidhāpayet|
athavā japtamātraṃ tu dadyād bhagavate sadā|| 23.79 ||
brahmasūtraṃ pariharet pūjanājapakālayoḥ|
avighno bhavati hyeṣa japārcanavidhiḥ sadā|| 23.80 ||
evaṃ krameṇa yo viṣṇumarcayed bhaktisaṃyutaḥ|
sa bhogānīpsitān bhuktvā viṣṇoryāti salokatām|| 23.81 ||
evamiṣṭavā yathānyāyaṃ sarvadevagaṇānvitam|
tataḥ svaśaktiyogena viṣṇoḥ snānaṃ samācaret|| 23.82 ||
sarvadevārcanā homaḥ kartavyaśca svanāmabhiḥ|
sarveṣāṃ samidājyānnairhaviṣā''jyena vā pṛthak|| 23.83 ||
bhūtebhyaśca baliṃ dyācchaktyā viprāṃśca bhojayet|| 23.84 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ viśvārcanaṃ nāma trayoviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<24>
|| atha caturviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepājjīrṇoddhāravidhiṃ param|
sthāpanāt sarvathā kāryo yasmin daśaguṇo vidhiḥ|| 24.1 ||
sarvātmano harerbimbe bhagne jīrṇe cale sthite|
grāmarāṣṭrādināśaḥ syāt tasmāt kāryā taduddhṛtiḥ|| 24.2 ||
dehaṃ dehī yathā jīrṇaṃ tyaktvā dehāntaraṃ vrajet|
tyaktvā jīrṇaṃ tathā bimbaṃ devo'pi bhajate navam|| 24.3 ||
arcāṃ tyajati deveśo jīrṇaṃ jīrṇāmiva srajam|
mā vikṣan yātudhānā ityuddharedavilambitam|| 24.4 ||
viprān sadyaḥ samāhūya tantrajñān vedapāragān|
śataṃ sahasramayutamadhikaṃ vā yathābalam|| 24.5 ||
tānarcitān yathāśakti bhaktyā kṛtvā pradakṣiṇam|
namobrahmaṇyamantreṇa praṇipatya samāhitaḥ|| 24.6 ||
āśiṣo vācayed bahvīrācāryastu samūrtipaḥ|
jitaṃ ta iti natvā tān punarutthāya vāgyataḥ|| 24.7 ||
devaṃ ca manasā dhyātvā baddhāñjaliridaṃ vadet|
pūjābimbamidaṃ viṣṇoḥ sthāpitaṃ pūrvasūribhiḥ|| 24.8 ||
anena dūṣaṇenādya dūṣitaṃ ce vartate|
duṣṭabimbasya coddhāraḥ karttavya iti śāsanam|| 24.9 ||
āgamasya vayaṃ kartu vyavasāyamupāsmahe|
tatredaṃ yadi kartavyamāgamārthatayā bhavet|| 24.10 ||
bhavanto no'nujānantu bhavemājñākarā vayam|
kriyatāmiti niśśaṅkamanujñāto dvijottamaiḥ|| 24.11 ||
nivedayet punaścedaṃ tebhyo lokahitepsayā|
yathaiva devapūjāyāṃ viniyuktamaninditam|| 24.12 ||
dravyaṃ puṣpādikaṃ paścānnirmālyamiti nindyate|
evaṃ bimbamaduṣṭaṃ yat pūjāyāṃ viniyujyate|| 24.13 ||
tadeva dūṣitaṃ paścānnirmālyamiti nindyate|
tasmād duṣṭamidaṃ bimbaṃ bhavadbhirmuktasaṃśayaiḥ|| 24.14 ||
nirmālyabuddhyā tyaktavyamiti śāstrasya śāsanam|
tata āghoṣayeyuste brāhmaṇā muktasaṃśayāḥ|| 24.15 ||
śāntaye yajamānasya gurorṛtvigjanasya ca|
rājño janapadasyāsya grāmasya nagarasya ca|| 24.16 ||
tato niṣkrāmya deveśamātmasātkṛtya vā guruḥ|
āvāhya kalaśe vā'tha bimboddhāravidhiṃ caret|| 24.17 ||
prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryācchāntihomaṃ yathāvidhi|
dūrvāstrimadhurāktā vā samidhaḥ kṣīriṇāṃ tu vā|| 24.18 ||
sāvitryā tatra hotavyāḥ sājyāścaṣṭasahasraśaḥ|
bāhṛtyupaniṣadbhiśca śāntiṃ paścāt tu vācayet|| 24.19 ||
śāntirastu śivaṃ cāstu puṣṭirastvastu maṅgalam|
svasti cāstu samṛddhyastu sarveṣāṃ santu sampadaḥ|| 24.20 ||
gobrāhmaṇebhyaḥ kṣatrebhyo viṭchūdrebhyastathaiva ca|
lokebhyaḥ sarvabhūtebhyo rājñe janapadāya ca|| 24.21 ||
rāṣṭrāyāsmai viśeṣeṇa grāmāya nagarāya ca|
asmai ca yajamānāya sabhṛtyāya sabandhave|| 24.22 ||
bimboddhāravidherastu sākalyamiti ghoyet|
ācāryaṃ yajamāno'tra praṇipatya prasādya ca|| 24.23 ||
hemaratnādidānena mahatā paritoṣayet|
ātmīyaṃ ca dhanaṃ sarvamātmānaṃ ca nivedayet|| 24.24 ||
hiraṇyaṃ gāṃ mahīmaśvān ratnaṃ vāsaśca kuñjaram|
bhūṣāṇāni striyo dāsānāsanaṃ śayanaṃ tathā|| 24.25 ||
yaccānyad dayitaṃ śakyaṃ tattad dadyāditi sthitiḥ|
mūrtipebhyaśca dātavyaṃ tatpādamadhikaṃ tu vā|| 24.26 ||
dehanyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā pralayodayasaṃyutam|
ācāryaḥ sagaṇo bimbamalaṅkuryāt prayatnataḥ|| 24.27 ||
tataste kṛtapuṇyāhā baddhoṣṇīṣāḥ samāhitāḥ|
vāgyatāḥ kṛtarakṣāśca tiṣṭheyurbaddhakautukāḥ|| 24.28 ||
ācāryo yajamānasya hemajaṃ kaṅkaṇaṃ kare|
cakramantreṇa badhnīyādalābhe pītamālikām|| 24.29 ||
japtvā siddhārthakān dadyādācāryaḥ sthapaterapi|
tatastūryaravairdikṣu vedanādaiśca ghoṣayet|| 24.30 ||
ṛṣabhaṃ kuñjaraṃ vā'gre sthāpayet samalaṅkṛtam|
bālarajjvādibhirbaddhvā yantrayogaṃ ca kalpayet|| 24.31 ||
ucchvañcasva tathā nāsatsūktamā te pitastathā|
anantasūktaṃ svādiṣṭhaṃ tathānyāni śubhāni ca|| 24.32 ||
yaudhājayaṃ rauravaṃ ca bṛhatsāma rathantaram|
kayāśubhā jyoṣṭhasāma vairājaṃ yacca śobhanam|| 24.33 ||
yaduṣpavitraṃ sakalaṃ variṣṭhaṃ viṣṇudaivatam|
pauruṣaṃ pāvamānaṃ ca pañcopaniṣadastathā|| 24.34 ||
cakraśārṅgāsividyāśca rūpavidyāśca mūrtayaḥ|
guṇatrayamahaṃkāro bhūtādīnyaparāṇi ca|| 24.35 ||
āvartayadbhirṛtvigbhirācāryaḥ sahitaḥ svayam|
visṛjya pīṭhikābandhaṃ sauvarṇairlāṅgalaiḥ śubhaiḥ|| 24.36 ||
aṣṭākṣareṇa nirmālyaṃ viṣvakseno'hamuddhare|
iti kṛtvā sthirāṃ buddhiṃ bhūrbhuvaḥsuvaromiti|| 24.37 ||
gartādutthāpayed bimbaṃ yantrayogena sādhakaḥ|
vedastotrajapaistūryairjayaśabdaiśca puṣkalaiḥ|| 24.38 ||
nṛttagītāṭṭahāsaiśca tato bimbaṃ nayed bahiḥ|
ahatairvasanaiḥ ślakṣṇaiḥ samantāt pariveṣṭya tu|| 24.39 ||
kṛtvopacāraṃ gāyattryā praṇavādyantaruddhayā|
dakṣiṇe sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā saikataṃ caturaśrakam|| 24.40 ||
tilān vikīrya darbhāṃśca pratimāṃ tatra śāyayet|
kṣālayet tatra tāṃ toyairgandhapuṣpaiśca pūjayet|| 24.41 ||
trirapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā satilaistaṇḍulaiḥ kiret|
tatonyairvasanaiḥ ślakṣṇairācchādyāropya vāhanam|| 24.42 ||
chatradhvajapatākābhiścāmaraiścopaśobhitam|
mahājanaravairyuktaṃ nadīṃ sāgaragāṃ nayet|| 24.43 ||
tatrāgādhe tato dhyātvā viṣvaksenaṃ caturbhujam|
tanmantreṇa namontena bimbamapsu vinikṣipet|| 24.44 ||
pīṭhaṃ brahmaśilāṃ cāpi nirmālyamiti cintayet|
tatra srātvā tato gatvā prāsādaṃ sarva eva te|| 24.45 ||
śodhayitvā bahūn viprān bhojayitvā yathābalam|
gā nivāsya catūrātraṃ trirātraṃ vā'pi yatnataḥ|| 24.46 ||
prabhūtayavasaṃ toyaṃ tābhyo dadyācca rātriṣu|
tridinaṃ kārayitvaivamannādyaṃ svastivācanam|| 24.47 ||
rākṣoghnaṃ vāstuhomaṃ ma vedastotrādikīrtanam|
ādau kṛtvā'tra puṇyāhaṃ tato mantraiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ|| 24.48 ||
prokṣayed bahirantaśca toyairgavyaistathākṣataiḥ|
homayet pañcadurgābhirmūlopaniṣadādibhiḥ|| 24.49 ||
mantrairapi ca rākṣoghnairvadaistūryaiśca nādayet|
jale'dhivāsitaṃ bimbamāhṛtya saha mūrtipaiḥ|| 24.50 ||
kṛtvā śuddhiṃ yathānyāyaṃ maṇḍape śāyayed guruḥ|
mūrtipā juhuyurdikṣu japeyuścaiva mantriṇaḥ|| 24.51 ||
vaidikāṃstāntrikān mantrān nāmāni ca sahasraśaḥ|
oṃ kāramagrataḥ kṛtvā paṭhed vaiṣṇavamantrakān|| 24.52 ||
praṇavena ca saṃspṛśya hṛdi sañcintya mādhavam|
hṛdayaṃ devadevasya viṣṇorvikramaṇena ca|| 24.53 ||
iti mantreṇa saṃspṛśya mukhaṃ cakṣuśca nāsikām|
hiraṇyagarbhamantreṇa sarvāṅgaṃ saṃspṛśet punaḥ|| 24.54 ||
pauruṣeṇa ca sūktena tamādāyādhivāsitam|
ratnanyāsādisaṃyuktaṃ pūrvavat triḥ pradakṣiṇam|| 24.55 ||
gatvā vedajayastotranṛttagītapurassaram|
dhruvā dyauriti saṃsthāpya vaṣaṭ ta iti kīrtayet|| 24.56 ||
praṇavena tu saṃyuktaṃ parameṣṭhyādividyayā|
ekaikaṃ vinyased dhyātvā pañcopaniṣadātmakam|| 24.57 ||
kramānyamūrdhāsyahṛnnābhipādeṣvomiti sarvataḥ|
nārāyaṇena sūktena pauruṣeṇa ca sarvataḥ|| 24.58 ||
viśvataścakṣurityena sakalaṃ bhāvayet tataḥ|
kalaśasthaṃ nave bimbe saṅkrāmyaivaṃ samācaret|| 24.59 ||
pratiṣṭhāvidhinā sarvamutsavāntaṃ prayojayet|
sāvitryā pañcakṣiścātra śāntihomaṃ prakalpayet|| 24.60 ||
bāhucchede parityāgaḥ pratimāyāḥ kare tathā|
yasminnavayave bhagne vairūpyaṃ tatra tāṃ tyajet|| 24.61 ||
yadyekakaraśākhā tu bhagnā dve vā'tra na tyajet|
ataḥ paraṃ parityāgastricchede kaiścidiṣyate|| 24.62 ||
pādaśākhāparicchede'pyevamanyatra kalpayet|
sphuṭite ca parityāgo bhinne ca parikīrtitaḥ|| 24.63 ||
sauvarṇī sāṅguliḥ kāryā yā bhagnā dve ca te tathā|
lohādau cecchilābimbe tāmreṇa rajatena vā|| 24.64 ||
heticchede tu sarvatra sauvarṇaṃ tat prakalpayet|
makuṭe kuṇḍale caiva vastrādiṣu ca śasyate|| 24.65 ||
pratimāyāḥ parityāge cārāhṛtivadiṣyate|
sthāpanaṃ pūjanaṃ cā'tra na grāhyaṃ pīṭhamakṣatam|| 24.66 ||
anuyāge navānyeva dravyāṇi śubhadāni tu|
piṇḍikāpacchilādīni dvayoḥ pīṭhaṃ tu garhyate|| 24.67 ||
gahane nikhanecchailaṃ dāravaṃ vahninā dahet|
dadyād ratnaṃ ca lohaṃ ca kuryād vidrāvya vā punaḥ|| 24.68 ||
pārthivaṃ nikṣipedapsu sarvaṃ pārthivameva vā|
bhagne bimbe'pi devasya prāsādo (prasādo) yatra lakṣyate|| 24.69 ||
tatrāpi kārayet bimbaṃ sauvarṇamaparaṃ punaḥ|
lohajaṃ ratnajaṃ vā'gre sthāpitaṃ vidhinā'rcayet|| 24.70 ||
tadapyamantrakaṃ pūjyamiti cāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ|
paurāṇikeṣu bimbeṣu devādisthāpiteṣvapi|| 24.71 ||
tyāyo'yameva vijñeyaḥ svayaṃ vā yatra jāyate|
saṃśaye bahubhiḥ sārdhaṃ vaiṣṇavairvedapāragaiḥ|| 24.72 ||
devasya mahatīṃ pūjāṃ kṛtvā bhaktipurassaram|
devatūryādinirghoṣairdvādaśyāṃ śravaṇe'pi vā|| 24.73 ||
upoṣya darbhaśayyāyāṃ svapeyurdevasannidhau|
ācakṣva deva! deveśa! prapanno'smi tavāntikam|| 24.74 ||
svapne sarvāṇi kāryāṇi hṛdayasthāni yāni naḥ|
iti vijñāpya suptānāṃ svapno'trānuguṇo bhavet|| 24.75 ||
dvistrirvā'pi tathā kṛtvā kṛtyaṃ niścinuyād budhaḥ|
prāsādaṃ tu navaṃ kuryād vṛttaṃ vā caturaśrakam|| 24.76 ||
pūrvavaccaturaśre vā vṛttaṃ vṛtte tu nānyathā|
bhūmihānirna kartavyā nāṅgahāniśca tatra vai|| 24.77 ||
devatārahitaṃ sthānamāśrayantyeva nārakāḥ|
tasmāt tatparihārārthaṃ rakṣāṃ kuryād viśeṣataḥ|| 24.78 ||
govāsaṃ puradhūpaṃ ca dīpamagniṃ ca saṃskṛtam|
pañcagavyaṃ dvijān darbhān sarṣapāṃśca na hāpayet|| 24.79 ||
rākṣoghnaṃ ca japet sūktaṃ nārasiṃhaṃ sudarśanam|
stotrāṇi ca pradoṣeṣu kuryācca svastivācanam|| 24.80 ||

svastaye'stu sadā viṣṇuḥ svastaye'stu caturmukhaḥ|
svastaye'stu sadā rudraḥ svastaye'stu śatakratuḥ|| 24.81 ||
svastaye'stu sadā vahniḥ svastaye'stu yamaḥ sadā|
svastaye nirṛtiḥ khyātaḥ svasteya varuṇaḥ sadā|| 24.82 ||
svastaye'stu sadā vāyuḥ svastaye'stu dhaneśvaraḥ|
svastaye'stu sadeśānaḥ svastaye'stu marudgaṇaḥ|| 24.83 ||
svastaye santu vasavo rudrāśca svastaye sadā|
ādityāḥ svastaye santu munayaḥ svastaye sadā|| 24.84 ||
gandharvāḥ svastaye santu svastaye santu vinnarāḥ|
svastaye santu nāgāśca svastaye santu pannagāḥ|| 24.85 ||
svastaye santu vidyeśāḥ svastaye'psarasaḥ sadā|
nadyaśca svastaye santu saritaḥ svastaye sadā|| 24.86 ||
sāgarāḥ svastaye santu śailāśca svastaye sadā|
svastaye bhrātaraḥ santu pitaraḥ svastaye sadā|| 24.87 ||
devapāriṣadāḥ sarve svastaye santu naḥ sadā|
svasti bhāgavatānāṃ tu svasti cāstvagrajanmanām|| 24.88 ||
svastyastu pūjakānāṃ tu svasti cāstu gavāmapi|
svastyastu sthāvarāṇāṃ tu jaṅgamānāṃ tathaiva ca|| 24.89 ||
svasti cāstu ca mantrāṇāṃ svasti vai karmaṇāmapi|
balidānaṃ hi devasya viṣṇorasmai namo namaḥ|| 24.90 ||
yadyadvā kriyate karma tattaduktvā viśeṣataḥ|
svastivācanamācāryaḥ kuryādevaṃ śubhaṃ bhavet|| 24.91 ||
baliṃ cāṣṭavidhebhyo'tha bhūtebhyo dikṣu dāpayet|
sthāpayitvā navaṃ bimbaṃ snapanaṃ cotsavaṃ tathā|| 24.92 ||
pūjayet pūrvavannityameva siddhirbhaviṣyati|| 24.93 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ caturviśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
