|| atha pañcaviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepādutpāteṣu ca niṣkṛtim|
saṃśodhanakramaṃ caiva sthānapratimayoḥ kramāt|| 25.1 ||
utpatanti purastādityutpātā bhayaśaṃsinaḥ|
divyāntarikṣabhaumāste jñeyāstantreṣu kīrtitāḥ|| 25.2 ||
prāsādagarbhapīṭhārcāḥ śarīratvena śāṅgiṇaḥ|
kalpitāḥ sarvadā rakṣyā viprairutpātaśaṅkayā|| 25.3 ||
utpāteṣūpajāteṣu na kāryā pūjanā hareḥ|
aspṛśyāḥ pratimāpīṭhaprāsādāḥ sāṅgadevatāḥ|| 25.4 ||
akṛtāyāṃ tu niṣkṛtyāṃ spaṣṭāḥ kupyanti devatāḥ|
pūjitā vā tato yatnānniṣkṛtiṃ kārayed drutam|| 25.5 ||
vitānāgninibhaṃ viṣṇorvimbamutpātadūṣitam|
akṛtāyāṃ tu niṣkṛtyāmapūjyaṃ sarvathā budhaiḥ|| 25.6 ||
vilambane tu niṣkṛtyā vinaśyed devasannidhiḥ|
tatsthāḥ pretā bhayaṃ kuryurvyādhiśokādibhirnṛṇām|| 25.7 ||
patanaṃ calanaṃ svedo hasanaṃ caiva rodanam|
utpāṭanaṃ hṛtiścorairdāhaḥ pradhvaṃsanaṃ balāt|| 25.8 ||
pīṭhaprāsādaketvastratoraṇānāṃ ca pārṣadām|
bhaṅgaśchatrākavalmīkakṛmikīṭādisambhavaḥ|| 25.9 ||
śvacoravrātyapāṣaṇḍidevalādipraveśanam|
arcanaṃ cāpyamantrajñairanthabhaktairadīkṣitaiḥ|| 25.10 ||
kṣudrānyamantrasaṃyogaḥ kālajīrtirdigāśrayaḥ|
uṣitairnīrasairduṣṭaiḥ pratiṣiddhaiśca pūjanam|| 25.11 ||
anārādhaśca nirdoṣaḥ kālepekṣā pramādataḥ|
kalaho maraṇaṃ janma raktaśleṣmāśrupātanam|| 25.12 ||
ete cānye ca vijñeyā utpātā bahudoṣadāḥ|
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena kartavyamavilambitam|| 25.13 ||
prāyaścittaṃ yathotsāhamanyathā sarvanāśanam|
mahad bahuṣu cogreṣu nimitteṣu viśeṣataḥ|| 25.14 ||
nimitteṣvalpamalpeṣu sadyo vā yatra niṣkṛtiḥ|
bimbapīṭhagṛhālindaprāṅkaṇeṣu yathātatham|| 25.15 ||
khananādividhiṃ kuryād yathādoṣabalābalam|
khananaṃ haraṇaṃ dāhaḥ pūraṇaṃ gonivāsanam|| 25.16 ||
viprocchiṣṭaṃ ca gavyaṃ ca saptaitāḥ sthānaśuddhayaḥ|
khādirasphyena cāstreṇa khātvā hastādimānataḥ|| 25.17 ||
sadoṣānakhilān pātrairhṛtvā pāṃsūn bahiḥ kṣipet|
kuśadarbhādibhirdagdhvā tadbhasmādi punarharet|| 25.18 ||
sumṛdbhiḥ khātamāpūrya hastipādaistu yājñikaiḥ|
gomūtrasiktamākoṭya viprapādodakaistathā|| 25.19 ||
gāṃ nivāsyāśayed viprān pāyasaṃ vaiṣṇavān bahūn|
taducchiṣṭe hṛte siñced gavyaiśca vikiraiḥ kiret|| 25.20 ||
sthānaśuddhiriyaṃ proktā bimbaśodhanamucyate|
kṣālanaplāvanasnāmārjanāni yathākramam|| 25.21 ||
dhārāvagāhanaṃ paścādabhiṣaiko'tra saptamaḥ|
kṣālanaṃ darbhamṛttvagbhistūṣṇīṃ toyaiśca śodhanam|| 25.22 ||
gandhapuṣpākṣatopaitairamantraiḥ plāvanaṃ jalaiḥ|
sakūrcairdravyayuktaistairmantravat snānamambubhiḥ|| 25.23 ||
pātre yathoktamāpūrya toyaṃ mantraiḥ prapūjya ca|
kūrcāgraiḥ prokṣayed bimbaṃ mantraistadiha mārjanam|| 25.24 ||
saṃsthāpya dhānyakūrcasthamaiśānyāṃ lohasambhavam|
vāruṇaṃ pātramāpūrya sakūrcaṃ salilaiḥ śubhaiḥ|| 25.25 ||
matsyau kūrmau ca sauvarṇau rājatau cātra nikṣipet|
samṛṇālāṃ sapuṣpāṃ ca padminīṃ kṣālitāṃ tathā|| 25.26 ||
varuṇaṃ tatra sampūjya tīrthaṃ cāvāhya vaiṣṇavam|
pālāśāditripādasthaṃ dārāpātraṃ tu lohajam|| 25.27 ||
sahemacchidramāpūrya sakūrcaṃ pratimopari|
dhārā'cchidrā ca kartavyā yāmamātraṃ dine dine|| 25.28 ||
samantraṃ vāruṇāt pātrāt toyaṃ hṛtvā'tra pūrayet|
pañcopaniṣado japyā vāgyatairdarbhapāṇibhiḥ|| 25.29 ||
spṛśadbhiḥ pātramavyagrairdhyāyadbhirviṣṇumavyayam|
pauruṣaṃ pāvamānaṃ ca sūktamanyacca vaiṣṇavam|| 25.30 ||
saṃhitā vā'khilā'nyeṣāṃ kāryo'trādyantayorjapaḥ|
dhārānte pūjayed devamudvāsya varuṇaṃ punaḥ|| 25.31 ||
na cālyaṃ vāruṇaṃ pātraṃ trirātraṃ yatra japyate|
hṛtvā'mbhaḥ punarāpūrya pūrvavat sarvamācaret|| 25.32 ||
gandhatauyaiḥ payobhirvā dhārā''jyenāpi cottamā|
kāryā'trāpi ca kartavyaṃ tathā varumapūjanam|| 25.33 ||
sañcchādyārcāṃ suvāsobhirdarbhairatha samantataḥ|
saṃsthāpya yājñikaiḥ kāṣṭhaiḥ kṛtaṃ pātraṃ pramāmataḥ|| 25.34 ||
gavyairvyastaiḥ samastaiśca gandhodāntairyathākramam|
dine dine yathānyāyaṃ kartavyamavagāhanam|| 25.35 ||
pañca kumbhāṃstu saṃsthāpya sakūrcān sūtraveṣṭitān|
gandhapuṣpākṣatāmbhobhiḥ pūrṇānupaniṣatkramāt|| 25.36 ||
sapallavamukhān nyasya cakrikāśca sataṇḍulāḥ|
sampūjya vāsasā'veṣṭya taireva tvabhimantrayet|| 25.37 ||
pañcabiḥ snāpayedevamabhiṣekavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ|
ṣoḍaśa dvādaśāṣṭau vā dīkṣitā vaiṣṇavā dvijāḥ|| 25.38 ||
vaidestribhiścaturbhirvā nādayeyurdivāniśam|
navavastraparīdhānāḥ svācāntā gurumūrtipāḥ|| 25.39 ||
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā'tra yathāvidhi samāhitāḥ|
prokṣayeyuśca rākṣoghnairmantraiḥ śāntikapauṣṭikaiḥ|| 25.40 ||
vaidikaistāntrikaiścaiva sarve darbhāgrapāṇayaḥ|
tacchiṣṭādbhirgṛhaṃ prokṣya bahirantaśca sarvataḥ|| 25.41 ||
vikiret pañcabhirmantraiḥ sagavyaiḥ sarṣapākṣataiḥ|
maṇḍape śodhite vahniṃ saṃskṛtya vidhivat tataḥ|| 25.42 ||
juhuyurmūrtipāḥ sarve kuṇḍeṣvācāryasaṃyutāḥ|
kṛtvā pradhānahomāntaṃ sarvatrāgnimukhaṃ kramāt|| 25.43 ||
dhyāyanto viṣṇumavyagrā vahnisthaṃ sāṅgamavyam|
pañcabhistrisahasrājyaṃ juhuyuḥ sarvavahniṣu|| 25.44 ||
tataḥ samidghṛtavrīhitilalājasahasrakam|
kramādaṣṭākṣareṇaiva juhuyuḥ sarva eva te|| 25.45 ||
pālāśīnāṃ ghṛtāktānāṃ samidhāṃ dvādaśottaram|
śataṃ vyāhṛtibhiḥ paścāt sarve te juhuyustataḥ|| 25.46 ||
kṣīreṇāṣṭaśataṃ dadhnā madhunā''jyena ca kramāt|
sāvitryaiva tu te sarve juhuyuḥ sarvaśāntaye|| 25.47 ||
homāvasāna etebhyo yajamānaḥ svaśaktitaḥ|
hiraṇyaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt tatastaiḥ svasti vācayet|| 25.48 ||
snapanaṃ ca yathotsāhaṃ kuryādadhamavarjitam|
pādau prakṣālya sampūjya dadyādācāryadakṣiṇām|| 25.49 ||
śaktyā hiraṇyaṃ kṣetraṃ ca gāṃ ca viṣṇuṃ vicintayan|
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ viprān vaiṣṇavān samyagarcitān|| 25.50 ||
praṇamyonātiriktādiśāntyarthaṃ vācayecchivam|
catvāro mūrtipā hīne madhye'ṣṭau dvādaśottame|| 25.51 ||
savastrābharaṇā yojyā yathādoṣabalābalam|
homādhyayanakarttṝṃśca sadasyān vaiṣṇavān dvijān|| 25.52 ||
svaśaktyā pūjayet sarvānannapānādibhiḥ śubhaiḥ|
adbhuteṣvīśakoṇe'gniṃ saṃskṛtya vidhivad guruḥ|| 25.53 ||
kṛtvā pradhānahomāntaṃ kuryādājyāhutīḥ punaḥ|
pañcabhirdvādaśāṣṭārṇagāyatrījitapauruṣaiḥ|| 25.54 ||
caturviṃśatiśo hutvā śataśo vā sahasraśaḥ|
apāmārgasamicchinnā sahaindrī bhadrikāñjaliḥ|| 25.55 ||
dūrvā siddhārthakavrīhiyavāstilahavirghṛtam|
iti hutvā sasampātaṃ sagavyaṃ pañcamantritam|| 25.56 ||
toyaśeṣaṃ hṛdā siñced yatra madhvādisambhavaḥ|
snapanaṃ ca tataḥ kuryādaṣṭottaraśatādikam|| 25.57 ||
svaspṛṣṭāṃ śodhayitvā'rcāṃ bahuśe gavyaśodhitām|
śuddhādbhistvagrasairgandhaistīrthatoyaiśca śodhayet|| 25.58 ||
pañcarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā gavāṃ vāsaṃ ca kalpayet|
brāhmaṇān bhojayet paścāt puṇyāhaṃ cātra vācayet|| 25.59 ||
vedaiśca nādayediṣṭvā nivedyānnaṃ baliṃ haret|
nādayet tūryaghauṣaiśca tato homādi kārayet|| 25.60 ||
aśvatthaiḥ khadiraiḥ plakṣaiḥ palāśaiḥ pāṭalairvaṭaiḥ|
nārāyaṇena sūktena śrīsūktena tathaiva ca|| 25.61 ||
ato deveti sūktena nāsadaṃhomucā tathā|
saṃsamidbhidrapādena tathā'rāyītyanena ca|| 25.62 ||
gāyatryā pañcabhiścātra hotavyaṃ vidhinaiva tu|
samāpte snapane dadyāt pūrvavaccaiva dakṣiṇām|| 25.63 ||
animittena niṣkrāntaṃ punarāśu praveśya tu|
śāntihomaṃ ca kurvīta snapanaṃ svastivācanam|| 25.64 ||
utsavaṃ tīrthayātrāṃ ca brāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojanam|
prasūte śvasṛgālādyaiḥ śūnyaṃ kṛtvā trirātrakam|| 25.65 ||
pūrvoktaṃ kārayecchāntiṃ sthānaśuddhiṃ ca bhūyasīm|
dāhe payasvinīṃ dhenuṃ śāntināmnā samāhvayet|| 25.66 ||
dūrvādikaṃ nivedyāsyai padmapatre payo duhet|
ātapya dadhi nirmathya tat saṃskṛtyājyamāharet|| 25.67 ||
aparedyustathā dugdhvā dadhi caivaṃ prakalpayet|
tṛtīye'hni śakṛnmūtraṃ kṣīraṃ caiva tathāharet|| 25.68 ||
padmapatreṣu sampūjya kramānmūtrādipañkam|
pañcapatreṣu saṃyojya mathitvā praṇavena ca|| 25.69 ||
sampūjyāṣṭasahasraṃ tu pañcopaniṣado japet|
tena samprokṣya sarvatra snāpayet pratimāṃ tataḥ|| 25.70 ||
hṛtvā bhasmādikaṃ toyaiḥ plāvayitvā samantataḥ|
gonivāsādi kartavyaṃ prāyaścittaṃ yathākramam|| 25.71 ||
calaṃ vā niścalaṃ kuryādāvāhya kalaśe budhaḥ|
adhivāsya yathānyāyaṃ kuryādvā'tha navīkriyām|| 25.72 ||
bhagnamuddhṛtya vā bimbaṃ tathānyat sthāpayed budhaḥ|
saṅkrāntamuddhṛte bimbe nave saṃyojayet pūnaḥ|| 25.73 ||
udvāsya susthitaṃ kuryād bimbaṃ pacchilayā saha|
durjanasparśanādau tu śuddhiṃ kṛtvā'sya bhūyasīm|| 25.74 ||
homādi sakalaṃ kuryādutsavāntaṃ viśeṣataḥ|
corāhṛtistu doṣāṇāmagraṇīḥ parikīrtyate|| 25.75 ||
tatra yatnena kartavyaṃ vidhānamativistaram|
corairapahṛtaṃ bimbaṃ yadi yatnena labhyate|| 25.76 ||
śodhayitvā vidhānena sthāpyaṃ tatra tadeva tu|
alabdhe'pahṛte bhagne jīrṇe vā'bhinavaṃ punaḥ|| 25.77 ||
yonirūpaviśeṣaṃ tu nyūnamapyatra neṣyate|
prāsāde sati taṃ dhyātvā sakalaṃ bimbasaṃyutam|| 25.78 ||
saṃhṛtyāvāhayed vidvān kalaśe mantrapūrvakam|
garbhagehe'sya pīṭhe vā satyāvāhya tathaiva tu|| 25.79 ||
pīṭhaṃ garbhagṛhaṃ vā'pi yadā tasya na labhyate|
bimbasthānāt tadāvāhya yathoktaṃ vidhimācaret|| 25.80 ||
saṃkrāntasya vināśe'pi kartavyaṃ mūlanāśavat|
tadaṅgadevatānāśe tadvadeva prakīrtyate|| 25.81 ||
yatastu devatāḥ sarvā mantrātmānaḥ prakīrtitāḥ|
evameva visargaḥ syānnāśe tāsāmiti sthitiḥ|| 25.82 ||
kartavyā pañcabhiḥ śāntirapūjāyāṃ tu bhūyasī|
śatahomo'ṣṭakenātha vaiṣṇavānāṃ tu bhojanam|| 25.83 ||
daśānāṃ pāyasenātra deyā śaktyā ca dakṣiṇā|
dvādaśāntamahaḥsaṃkhyāguṇitaḥ syādayaṃ vidhiḥ|| 25.84 ||
snapanaṃ cācaturmāsānmahāsnānamataḥ param|
vatsare śāntihomena yuktaṃ viprārcanena ca|| 25.85 ||
abhimantrya haviḥ skannaṃ dakṣiṇena tu pāṇinā|
aṣṭākṣareṇa hotavyaṃ śatamājyena pañcabhiḥ|| 25.86 ||
duḥśaॉtādyapsu nikṣipya tathā hutvā paraṃ pacet|
uṣite'mbupayomiśragavyagandhādiśodhanam|| 25.87 ||
puṇyāhaṃ śāntihomaṃ ca balimannādyamācaret|| 25.88 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ pañcaviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<26>
|| atha ṣaḍviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt snapanasya vidhiṃ param|
uttamādivibhāgena kartavyaṃ tu vicakṣaṇaiḥ|| 26.1 ||
sthāpitasya caturthe'hni yātrānte grahaṇādiṣu|
upasarge'ntyasaṃsarge pradhānapuruṣakṣaye|| 26.2 ||
dīkṣābhiṣekayāgānte maṅgalye mantrasādhane|
kṣudrānyamantravinyaste ciralupte ca pūjane|| 26.3 ||
asānnidhye'bhiṣeke ca rājñāṃ vṛddhyarthameva ca|
ajñātadoṣaśāntyarthaṃ pratyabdaṃ ca yathāvidhi|| 26.4 ||
kartavyaṃ snapanaṃ bhaktyā nimittārthānusārataḥ|
dvādaśyāṃ śravaṇe puṇye vyatīpāte'yanādiṣu|| 26.5 ||
janmarkṣe yajamānasya paurṇamāsyāṃ ca kārayet|
maṇḍapaṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā toraṇadhvajaśobhitam|| 26.6 ||
muktādāmādisaṅkīrṇaṃ kusumotkaramaṇḍitam|
kartavyā vedikā madhye dvātriṃśadyavamucchritā|| 26.7 ||
yāvantaḥ kalaśā grāhyāstāvān vai vedivistaraḥ|
kuṇḍāni kārayed dikṣu maṅgalāni ca vinyaset|| 26.8 ||
aiśānyāṃ sthāpite pātre dhānyasthe navavāsasā|
nadītīrthādijaṃ toyaṃ dīkṣitotpūtamāharet|| 26.9 ||
sampūjya viṣṇugāyatryā sapavitreṇa pāṇinā|
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ vidvān sakūrcamabhimantrayet|| 26.10 ||
imā āpaḥ śivāḥ santu śubhāḥ śuddhāśca nirmalāḥ|
pāvanāḥ śītalāścaiva pūtāḥ sūryasya raśmibhiḥ|| 26.11 ||
ityuktvā śodhitāṃ vedimāpohiṣṭhādibhistribhiḥ|
prokṣya gandhākṣatairgavyairvikirecca samantataḥ|| 26.12 ||
dviṣaṭkenaindrasaumyāgrāṇyatha sūtrāṇi pātayet|
koṣṭhāni caturaśrāṇi tālamātrāṇi kalpayet|| 26.13 ||
pūrayedaṅgulotsedhā rekhāḥ sarvāḥ sitāḥ samāḥ|
vikirenmadhyataḥ kiñcit tenaiva rajasā samam|| 26.14 ||
vyatyayāt pañcavarṇairvā kartavyaṃ padapūraṇam|
catasro vīthayaḥ kāryāḥ pūrvāgrā itarāstathā|| 26.15 ||
śuddhodakānvite pakṣe dravyasnāne na vīthayaḥ|
madhyamaṃ tu padaṃ brāhmaṃ daivikaṃ tadanantaram|| 26.16 ||
parito mānuṣaṃ vārāḥ ṣoḍaśātrāṣṭa daivike|
brāhme cāṣṭadalaṃ padmaṃ kalpayet karṇikojjvalam|| 26.17 ||
sarvatra pīṭhikāḥ kāryāścaturaṅgulamucchritāḥ|
śālibhiḥ kūrcasaṃyuktairmadhye satilataṇḍulaiḥ|| 26.18 ||
gāyatryāṣṭaśataṃ hutvā pūrvavat saṃskṛte'nale|
tato'dhivāsayecchaddhān kalaśānuktalakṣaṇān|| 26.19 ||
sūtreṇa viṣṭitān sarvānaṅgulāṅgulamānataḥ|
tāreṇādhomukhān nyasya paścime darbhasaṃstare|| 26.20 ||
agnyādīśānaparyantaṃ paṅktīkṛtya vicakṣaṇaḥ|
darbhānupari vinyasya darbhamudrāṃ ca darśayet|| 26.21 ||
prokṣya gandhodakaiḥ sarvān nikiredakṣataiḥ śubhaiḥ|
uttānīkṛtya coddhṛtya pañcabhiḥ kramayogataḥ|| 26.22 ||
brāhmamānuṣadaiveṣu padeṣu viniveśayet|
aṣṭākṣareṇa kumbheṣu tataḥ kūrcāni vinyaset|| 26.23 ||
śāntike'dhomukhānyeva pauṣṭike tūnmukhāni ca|
mūlena tān punaḥ prokṣya sapavitreṇa pāṇinā|| 26.24 ||
prayataḥ prāṅmukhaḥ pūrvaṃ brāhme snānodakaṃ nyaset|
mūlena sarvamūrtyaṅgaparivāraṃ hariṃ smaran|| 26.25 ||
gandhapūṣpaistamārādhya vastrābhyāṃ veṣṭayet punaḥ|
pādyādīni kramād dravyāṇyāvāhyāvahyā dīkṣitaiḥ|| 26.26 ||
pūrayed bahirantaśca digvidikṣu samāhitaḥ|
pādyamaindre yame vā'rdhyamācāmaṃ vāruṇe nyaset|| 26.27 ||
pañcagavyaṃ tathā saumye ghṛtamāgneyagocare|
nairṛte dadhi vinyasya vāyavye kṣīrameva ca|| 26.28 ||
kṣaudramīśānadigbhāge dvādaśākṣaravidyayā|
uṣṇodakaṃ bhṛśe cātha kaṣāyaṃ vitathe tathā|| 26.29 ||
mārjanaṃ bhṛṅgarāje tu puṣpadante phalodakam|
śoṣe yavodakaṃ ratnaṃ mukhye lohaṃ tathā'ditau|| 26.30 ||
kuśodakaṃ tu parjanye yathāvad viniveśayet|
gāyatryā śeṣakumbhāṃstu pūracecchuddhavāribhiḥ|| 26.31 ||
gandhāmbho'ryamadeśe'tha puṣpatoyaṃ vivasvati|
tathaupamānikaṃ mitre sugandhāmalakaṃ dhare|| 26.32 ||
sāvitre'kṣatatoyaṃ tu nālikerakamindrake|
aikṣavaṃ rudradāse tu tathā''pe taṇḍulodakam|| 26.33 ||
aṣṭākṣareṇa sarvāṃstān daivikān pūrayet kramāt|
pūritān pūritān sadyaḥ pallavairupaśobhitān|| 26.34 ||
cakramantreṇa vinyasya cakrikāśca sataṇḍulāḥ|
vāsobirveṣṭayet sarvān navaiḥ keśādivarjitaiḥ|| 26.35 ||
darbhān nyasyākṣatāṃścaiva mūlena vikiret kramāt|
aṣṭākṣareṇa samprokṣya svamantraiḥ pūjayed guruḥ|| 26.36 ||
praṇavena punaḥ prokṣya gandhatoyena caiva hi|
dīkṣitairdikṣu homāṃśca kārayitvā'dhivāsayet|| 26.37 ||
śāntiṃ ca pañcabhirhutvā kārayet svastivācanam|
devasannidhaye cātra maṅgalāyāghaśāntaye|| 26.38 ||
āśiṣo vācayed vidvān karmāvikalamastviti|
śāntitoyamathānīya sampātājyasamāyutam|| 26.39 ||
prokṣayet kalaśān sarvān kūrcāgreṇa punarguruḥ|
śuddhā bhavantvime kumbhāḥ sakuśāḥ sapidhānakāḥ|| 26.40 ||
vātātapaparītāśca śubhalakṣaṇasaṃyutāḥ|
pañcaviṃśatidhā viṣṇostattvabhedena tiṣṭhataḥ|| 26.41 ||
dravyeyattāśrayāḥ sarvāḥ saṅgacchantāṃ vibhūtayaḥ|
śāstroditamidaṃ cāstu snapanaṃ devanirmitam|| 26.42 ||
anena snapanenāstu prasanno bhagavān hariḥ|
ityuktvā'nyaiḥ kṛtānujño mūrtipaiḥ sahito guruḥ|| 26.43 ||
soṣṇīṣaḥ sottarīyaśca kṛtanyāsaḥ svalaṅkṛtaḥ|
nīrājanādisaṃyukto gacched garbhagṛhaṃ tataḥ|| 26.44 ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā mūlamantraṃ puraḥsthitaḥ|
dvāraṃ ca vāyunoddhāṭya dattvā'rdhyaṃ vāgyataḥ śuciḥ|| 26.45 ||
viśvenācchādanaṃ hṛtvā prakṣālya praṇavena ca|
sṛṣṭikrameṇa tattvaistu yuktaṃ dhyātvā sureśvaram|| 26.46 ||
sakalīkṛtya pādyārdhye punardattvā jitādinā|
gāyatryā kautukaṃ chittvā dadyādācamanaṃ punaḥ|| 26.47 ||
mardhnyakṣatān sadūrvāgrān pādayoścomiti kṣipet|
nīrājayet trirastreṇa maṅgalāni hṛdoddiśet|| 26.48 ||
śubhe tu taijase pātre śālitaṇḍulapūrite|
astrairaśvatthapatrasthairnavagomayakalpitaiḥ|| 26.49 ||
varṇojjvalairyathāśobhaṃ vardhamānājyavartibhiḥ|
ujjvālābhiryutaṃ madhye kuryānnīrājanaṃ tvidam|| 26.50 ||
pūjādravyāṇi kalpyāni śobhanānyatra dīkṣitaiḥ|
śāliṣṭaṃ niśācūrṇaṃ hemaratnākṣatāni ca|| 26.51 ||
snānodakasya cāṅgāni saṃyojyāni samāsataḥ|
dūrvāsyāmākapadmāni viṣṇuparṇīti pādyakam|| 26.52 ||
siddhārthaṃ gandhapūṣpaṃ ca phalaṃ yavatilākṣatam|
kuśāgraṃ cārghyamaṣṭāṅgaṃ kalpayecca samāsataḥ|| 26.53 ||
takkolajātikarpūralavaṅkakusumānvitam|
caturaṅgaṃ tathācāmaṃ kalpayet tatra sādhakaḥ|| 26.54 ||
aśvatthodumbarabrahmavaṭabilvavikaṅkataiḥ|
śamībhiḥ khadiraiḥ kuryāt kaṣāyaṃ tvagbhireva ca|| 26.55 ||
sahā niśā sadābhadrā śīrīṣaṃ sūryavartinī|
kuśāgraṃ caṣaḍaṅgāni mārjane kathitāni tu|| 26.56 ||
kadalīnālikerāmradhātrīpanasadāḍimaiḥ|
sabhavyabilvairaṣṭāṅgaṃ phalatoyaṃ ca kalpayet|| 26.57 ||
vajramauktikavaiḍūryapadmarāgapravālakaiḥ|
pañcāṅgaṃ ratnatoyaṃ syāllohaṃ hemādipañcakam|| 26.58 ||
sevyārtyambujaṭāsrāyomuraśītaistu gandhakam|
jātistrīmallikākundanandyāvartābjacampakaiḥ|| 26.59 ||
yūthikābakulābhyāṃ tu kartavyaṃ kusumodakam|
śaṅkhāritārkṣyakūrmaiśca lohajairaupamānikam|| 26.60 ||
ādyāṅgamitarālābhe pādyādīnāṃ praśastate|
pitaraḥ śrīsarasvatyau dakṣaviṣṇūśanendavaḥ|| 26.61 ||
indrārkayamaviśvākhyāḥ kubero vāyurapyajaḥ|
vasavaścaiva sāvitrī pādyādidravyadevatāḥ|| 26.62 ||
gandharvo varumastārkṣyo bhūmirnirṛtireva ca|
śrīdharaśca nṛsiṃho'śvamukho madhye paraḥ pumān|| 26.63 ||
idaṃ viṣṇurhiraṇyavarṇā āpohiṣṭhā prajāpate|
tejo'si ca dadhikrāvṇa āpyāyasva tathā madhu|| 26.64 ||
athākṛṣṇena cāśvatthe bhadraṃ karṇebhireva ca|
gaṇānāṃ vāyavāyāhi brahmajajñānameva ca|| 26.65 ||
upatvāgne devasyātvā bahirmantrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ|
viṣṇerarāṭaṃ tattvā ca suvarmaṃ syonāpṛthivyapi|| 26.66 ||
śanno yādivyā yāsāṃ ca paromātreti daivike|
madhye'todevasūktaṃ ca sarvatrānte svapūraṇam|| 26.67 ||
pādyādimadhyaparyantaṃ snānamatra prakīrtitam|
pratidravyaṃ samabhyarcya dhūpayitvā nivedayet|| 26.68 ||
sakalīkṛtya mūlāṅgairmudrāḥ samyak pradarśya ca|
nīrājyārdhyādibhiḥ sāṅgaṃ yathānyāyaṃ prapūjayet|| 26.69 ||
ebhirevākhiladravyaiḥ snāpayedaṅgadevatāḥ|
caturvidhaṃ nivedyānnaṃ sopadaṃśaṃ ghṛtāplutam|| 26.70 ||
baliṃ ca sarvato dadyād bhūtebhyaḥ sārvakāmikam|
sārvavarṇikamannādyaṃ kuryāccaivānivāritam|| 26.71 ||
ācāryaṃ pūjayet kartā dakṣiṇābhiḥ samūrtipam|
homādhyayanakartṝṃśca sadasyān vaiṣṇavāṃstathā|| 26.72 ||
brāhmaṇān vaiṣṇavāñ śāntān sampūjya dvādaśāvarān|
śaktyā ca dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt kuryāt taiḥ svastivācanam|| 26.73 ||
vyatyastonātiriktādidoṣaśāntistato bhavet|
dravyairūnaśatārdhena saikāśītyā śatāṣṭabhiḥ|| 26.74 ||
ṣaḍadhyardhaśataiḥ saptadaśāḍhyadviśatena ca|
sadvipañcāśatā tena tribhirvā pañcabhiḥ śataiḥ|| 26.75 ||
kumbhottarasahasrāntaṃ snānamevaṃ prakalpayet|
iti samyak samākhyātaṃ viṣṇoḥ snapanamuttamam|| 26.76 ||
aihikāmutrikān kāmān sagyakkṛtvā yadāpnuyāt|| 26.77 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<27>
|| atha saptaviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt prokṣaṇādividhikramam|
navīkaraṇamuddiśya kāryaṃ sarvaṃ yathākramama|| 27.1 ||
sammṛjya sarvato gehaṃ kapilāgomayena ca|
tanmūtreṇa ca tenātra prokṣayecchamiti bruvan|| 27.2 ||
caturdikṣu tathā kṛtvā sagarbhagṛhamāditaḥ|
tūṣṇīṃ mantraiśca samprokṣya śuddhodairmantravāribhiḥ|| 27.3 ||
imā āpaḥ śivā gavyaiḥ pañcopaniṣadena ca|
sarvataścakrasajjaptairvikiret sarṣapākṣataiḥ|| 27.4 ||
saṃśodhya maṇḍapaṃ cāgre toraṇadhvajamaṇḍitam|
śayanaṃ kalpayet yamyak kusumotkarasaṃyutam|| 27.5 ||
tataḥ surakṣitaṃ kṛtvā puṇyāhaṃ tatra vācyet|
karmāryāṃ sthāpayitvā'dbhiḥ saratnābhiśca pañcabhiḥ|| 27.6 ||
tīrthodaiśca trayīsārasūktaiḥ snapanamācaret|
navābhyāṃ śuklavastrābhyāṃ veṣṭayitvā'kṣareṇa tu|| 27.7 ||
upavītamathādyena makuṭaṃ madhyamena tu|
uttarīyaṃ tṛtīyena puṃbījena ca bhūṣaṇam|| 27.8 ||
sarveṇa gandhapuṣpādyairiṣṭvā puṇyāhamācaret|
oṅkāraṃ bhagavan sarvavyāpin sarvapadaṃ punaḥ|| 27.9 ||
bhūtādhipata ityevaṃ sarvavedapadaṃ punaḥ|
sāramayetyataḥ sarvadevādhipata ityapi|| 27.10 ||
ehyehi puruṣetyenmahāpuruṣaśabdavat|
antādimadhyabhūteti parātparatareti ca|| 27.11 ||
vyaktāvyaktapadaṃ paścād sthūlasūkṣmamahāpadam|
yogin nama iti svāhaśabdāntena tamāhvayet|| 27.12 ||
sakalīkṛtya mūlena hṛdayādibhiromiti|
dadyāt pādyaṃ tathācāmamitigandhānulepnam|| 27.13 ||
imāḥ sumanasaḥ puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ caiva vanaspateḥ|
śubhaṃ jyotiḥ pradīpaṃ ca dadyādardhyaṃ daśāṅgavat|| 27.14 ||
āpaḥ siddhārthakā dūrvā akṣatāstitalaṇḍulāḥ|
kṣīraṃ yavāśca māṣāśca kuśāgrāṇīti tad viduḥ|| 27.15 ||
onnamaḥ padamādau tu tataḥ puruṣottamāya ca|
tataḥ samastalokādhipataye'rghyamanantaram|| 27.16 ||
nivedayāmisaṃyukto namaḥ svāhetyayaṃ maniḥ|
bhūyaścācamanaṃ dadyāt pañcabhiḥ snāpayet punaḥ|| 27.17 ||
pūrvavad vastrabhūṣādi pādyācamanamarcanam|
baddhvā'tha kautukaṃ dhenupradānaṃ svastivācanam|| 27.18 ||
kṛtvā'dhivāsayeddhomān pūrvavad dikṣu kārayet|
ṛgyajussāmātharvajñā juhuyurdikṣu dīkṣitāḥ|| 27.19 ||
kramācchābdikanairuktacchandaśśikṣāvido'nyataḥ|
jyotiḥkalpetihāsajñāḥ purāṇajñāḥ sadasyakāḥ|| 27.20 ||
mīmāṃsānyāyasāṅkhayajñā vedāntajñāśca sākṣiṇaḥ|
gāyatryā tu mathitvā'gnimakṣaraiḥ prokṣya cāṣṭabhiḥ|| 27.21 ||
tadyuktaistairniṣekādi kṛtvā pūrṇāṃ pradāya ca|
vihṛtyāgnimukhaṃ kṛtvā juhuyuḥ sarva eva te|| 27.22 ||
samidhaḥ kṣīrijā brāhmīratha pālāśya eva vā|
pṛthagaṣṭasahasrāṇi ghṛtāktā mantrasammitāḥ|| 27.23 ||
tataḥ prokṣya jalairbimbaṃ sadasyebhyo nivedayet|
suhutāḥ samidhaḥ pūrvamasmābhiḥ śāstradṛṣṭibhiḥ|| 27.24 ||
śaṃ bhavanto vadantvatra prīyatāṃ ca janārdanaḥ|
tata oṃ svasti bhavanto bruvantviti vidhānataḥ|| 27.25 ||
oṃ svasti śāntirityaṣṭau sadasyā brūyurādarāt|
oṃ svasti śāntiḥ suhutaṃ bhavatviti gururvadet|| 27.26 ||
sadasyānumatāḥ paścādājyena juhuyustathā|
ājyabhāgā hutāstāvadityuktvā śaṃ bruvantviti|| 27.27 ||
nivedayeyuraṣṭau te vadeyuśca śamastviti|
ācārya oṃ śaṃ suhutaṃ bhavatviti tadā vadet|| 27.28 ||
caruṃ hutvā nivedyaṃ tu caruhomaḥ kṛtastviti|
oṃ maṅgalaṃ bruvantvante te'pi maṅgalamastviti|| 27.29 ||
oṃ maṅgalaṃ ca suhutaṃ bhavatviti gururvadet|
punarājyaṃ tathā hutvā nivedyaṃ prāgvadeva tu|| 27.30 ||
śamityatra sadasyaiśca vaktavyaṃ guruṇā tathā|
sarvavyāhṛtibhirhomāḥ kartavyāstairvidānataḥ|| 27.31 ||
ādyantayoḥ parīṣekaḥ kāryaḥ pūrṇāṃ tathāntataḥ|
evaṃ hutvā tathā sarve juhuyuścāvasānikam|| 27.32 ||
indraviṣṇumarudbhyo'gnidharmanetṛbhya eva ca|
sādhyebhyaśca diśāpālaiḥ sarvadevagaṇaiḥ saha|| 27.33 ||
vyāhṛtyāgnivāyuśakravaruṇabrahmaviṣṇubhiḥ|
aśvibhyāṃ gopatiyamāgniviṣṇuvasubāskaraiḥ|| 27.34 ||
viśvabhūtadiśāpālanirṛtīraudrarūpakaiḥ|
viṣṇave ca punaḥ śūlapāṇaye ca yamāya ca|| 27.35 ||
sakāladharmo daṇḍī ca tryambako varuṇaḥ punaḥ|
viṣṇurmitro'tha parameṣṭhyādityabhūtasamudravān|| 27.36 ||
āpagāvāyuviṣṇubhyo marudbhyo jātavedase|
indrāya sarvalokebhyo brahmaṇe viśvakarmaṇe|| 27.37 ||
somāya somapārṣadbhyo grahanakṣatrasaṃyutaiḥ|
tārāgaṇairbhūtabhaumadivyanāgaiśca homayet|| 27.38 ||
sarvāśca devatā rudraviśvarūpākhyaviṣṇavaḥ|
brahmā rudrāḥ sarvadevāḥ sahavighnairgaṇādhipaiḥ|| 27.39 ||
ādāvante ca yoktavyāstathā vyāhṛtayaḥ pṛthak|
sasampātaistataḥ kumbhaṃ pūrayecchāntivāribhiḥ|| 27.40 ||
devasyottarato vidvān saṃsthāpya sakamaṇḍalum,
sarvatīrthāhṛtaiḥ sarvasamudrādāhṛtairapi|| 27.41 ||
prāksrotasībhyo vāpībhyaḥ pratyāhṛtairathāmbubhiḥ|
saratnakaiḥ sapavitraiḥ kalaśaṃ pūrayāmi ca|| 27.42 ||
sarvadvayaṃ tato vyāpin sarvalokādhisaṃyutam|
pataye namaḥ svāheti tārādiḥ pūraṇe manuḥ|| 27.43 ||
anena mantrarūpeṇa pūriyitvā kamaṇḍalum|
veṣṭayet kṣaumapaṭṭena sāpidhānamalaṅkṛtam|| 27.44 ||
śeṣaiḥ śāntyudakaiḥ pūrṇaṃ pūrayitvā'nyamāditaḥ|
sāpidhānamalaṅkṛtya kṣaumābhyāṃ pariveṣṭya ca|| 27.45 ||
gandhādibhirathābhyarcya kalaśaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum|
kalaśaṃ sarvakalyāṇaṃ gṛhītvā prokṣakaḥ śuciḥ|| 27.46 ||
sarvamaṅgalasaṃyukto navāmbaradharo hariḥ|
svayaṃ bhūtvā japanmantraṃ śanairgacchet pradakṣiṇam|| 27.47 ||
ādhāya sakuśe haste savye tūpari dakṣiṇam|
tathā kamaṇḍaluṃ cānyo gṛhītvā ca tadagrataḥ|| 27.48 ||
sakuśāṃ pātayed dhārāmavicchinnāmanutkaṭām|
śannodevyaṇoraṇīyānmantradvayamudāharet|| 27.49 ||
prāsādāntaśca saṃśodhya tathā kṛtvā vicakṣaṇaḥ|
kārayed bimbaśuddhiṃ ca yuktito mūrtipairguruḥ|| 27.50 ||
śāntiṃ kṛtvā tadagre tu kautukasnānamācaret|
pīṭhe niveśya tacchayyāmanugairapanāyayet|| 27.51 ||
kṣālitaṃ śuddhatoyena snāpayenmantravāribhiḥ|
śiṣṭaiḥ śāntyudakaiścaiva snāpayet praṇavena tu|| 27.52 ||
pādyācāmāmbaraṃ bhūṣāmupavītaṃ ca veṣṭanam|
pūrvavat sampradātavyaṃ dīpāntaṃ punareva ca|| 27.53 ||
brahmāṇamīśvaraṃ viprānanumānya yathāvidhi|
anujñādakṣiṇāṃ dattvā kuryācca svastivācanam|| 27.54 ||
śayane rudrasūktena pīṭhāccotthāpanaṃ bhavet|
sthāne puruṣasūktena brāhmeṇāsanakarmaṇi|| 27.55 ||
puruṣe'kṣararājaṃ vā madhyamaṃ śāṅkare tathā|
antyaṃ brāhme yathāyogaṃ yuktyā cotthāpanaṃ caret|| 27.56 ||
śāntikumbhaṃ puraskṛtya dhāraṃ caiva kamaṇḍaloḥ|
karmārcāmānayed vidvān vāgyato'ntaḥ smaran harim|| 27.57 ||
tattvajñena dhṛtaṃ kumbhaṃ samyagudvāhayet puraḥ|
śākunasvastisūktābhyāṃ jayaśabdādisaṃyutam|| 27.58 ||
sāgniṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya prāsādaṃ praviśet punaḥ|
dattvā'rghyaṃ kautukaṃ pīṭhe sthāpayed brāhmamuccaran|| 27.59 ||
śikhāsiromukhagrīvādvibāhuhṛdaye tathā|
nābhāvupastha ūrvośca karayoḥ pādayornyaset|| 27.60 ||
aindre tu pṛṣṭhataḥ kāryamevaṃ nyāsavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ|
tatastu prokṣayet vidvān taṃ samyag dviṣaḍakṣaraiḥ|| 27.61 ||
karmārcāgatamāvāhya gandhapuṣpodakena tu|
snāpayenmūlabimbaṃ tu tataḥ śāntyudakena ca|| 27.62 ||
sarvāntaiḥ kāsyahṛdguhyapādasthaiḥ kalpayet tanum|
hṛdayādīni cāṅgāni yathāsthānaṃ niveśayet|| 27.63 ||
pādyādi pūrvavat kāryaṃ puṇyāhaṃ vācayet tataḥ|
gandhapūṣpādibhiḥ samyak pūjayitvā ca bhaktitaḥ|| 27.64 ||
pāyasaṃ yāvakaṃ cānnaṃ gaulaṃ vā'tra nivedayet|
tad bhakṣyakṣīrasaṃyuktaṃ sājyaṃ svādūpadaṃśakam|| 27.65 ||
anuktamantrakārye'tra gāyatrīṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ vadet|
taccheṣeṇa tato dadyāt pārṣadebhyo baliṃ kramāt|| 27.66 ||
ācamyācamanīyaṃ ca dattvā cānnaṃ visṛjya tat|
dakṣiṇāṃ ca tato dadyādannādyaṃ cānivāritam|| 27.67 ||
cakrādīnāṃ tu mantrāḥ syuḥ svanāmapadalakṣitāḥ|
yaduktaṃ sthāpane karma tat sarvaṃ kārayet tataḥ|| 27.68 ||
sthāpanoktavidhānena snapanaṃ kārayet tataḥ|
utsavaṃ ca tathā yātrāṃ saptāhaṃ bhaktipūrvakam|| 27.69 ||
dinatrayaṃ tu vā kuryādekāhamathavā''padi|
tathā'vaśyaṃ tu kartavyā śāntyarthaṃ cotsavakriyā|| 27.70 ||
prokṣaṇaṃ sadya eveṣṭaṃ prāyaścitte viśeṣataḥ|
svarśaśca viṣṇusūktena sahasrāṣṭākṣarānvitaḥ|| 27.71 ||
śuddhau kṛtāyāmiṣṭo'yaṃ vidhiḥ pañcājyahomavān|
na tithirna ca nakṣatraṃ na kālasya parīkṣaṇam|| 27.72 ||
prāyaścitteṣu kartavyā sadya eva ca niṣkṛtiḥ|
catustridvyekavedāḥ syustantrajñāścādhikāriṇaḥ|| 27.73 ||
sadyaścedekavedo'pi bahuvedo(da)vilambane|
ācāryaḥ sarvatantrajñastadā syāccottamo vidhiḥ|| 27.74 ||
adhamo'pi vidhiḥ kāryaḥ sadyo'śaktairiti sthitiḥ|
padavākyapramāṇajñairvedayajñavratasthitaiḥ|| 27.75 ||
dakṣairjitendriyaiḥ śāntairmūrtipaiḥ sahito guruḥ|
evaṃ kuryād vidhānena jalasamprokṣaṇaṃ hareḥ|| 27.76 ||
āḍhakatrayasampūrṇamacchidraṃ kalaśaṃ śubham|
sakūrcaṃ vastrayugmana veṣṭitaṃ ratnasaṃyutam|| 27.77 ||
śālikūrcasthitaṃ pūrṇaṃ puruṣasūktena pūjayet|
mūlenāṅgacaturmūrtividhinā'rghyādibhiḥ kramāt|| 27.78 ||
tatra viṣṇuṃ mahāviṣṇuṃ sadāviṣṇuṃ ca vinyaset|
ullekhanādikaṃ kṛtvā saṃskṛtyāgniṃ ca homayet|| 27.79 ||
pauruṣaṃ viṣṇusūktaṃ ca cittisruksaptahotṛkam|
caturhotā ca ṣaḍḍhotā pañcopaniṣado jitam|| 27.80 ||
sāvitrī vyāhṛtiścaiva haṃsaḥśuciṣadityapi|
mūlatrayaṃ ca gāyatrīṃ juhuyāt susamāhitaḥ|| 27.81 ||
hutvā hutvā spṛśed bimbaṃ kalaśaṃ bhavanaṃ tathā|
mūlatrayeṇa homānte spṛśet prasṛtimudrayā|| 27.82 ||
sampātahutatoyena kalaśasthena sarvataḥ|
paribhramyāgrato dhārāṃ kṛtvā samprokṣayet kuśaiḥ|| 27.83 ||
vāstuhome kṛte'ntaśca vāstoṣpadaśapañcakaiḥ|
hunedaṣṭasahasraṃ tu pālāśaṃ khādiraṃ tu vā|| 27.84 ||
tathā pañcaśataṃ vā'pi kuryādaṣṭaśataṃ tu vā|
tāvadājyaṃ caruṃ lājāṃstilāñ śālīṃśca homayet|| 27.85 ||
pratyekamevamevaṃ tu mantre mantre ca homayet|
yathālābhaṃ tu hotavyaṃ śāstradṛṣṭena vartmanā|| 27.86 ||
vaidikānāṃ hunedājyaṃ śeṣaṃ vai mūlavidyayā|
evaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena kautukaṃ bandhayet tataḥ|| 27.87 ||
dvādaśākṣaramantreṇa padmasūtreṇa caiva hi|
rātrau mahotsavaṃ kuryācchaṅkhatūryaravaiḥ śubhaiḥ|| 27.88 ||
rātrau mahotsavaṃ kartavyo jalasamprokṣaṇaṃ divā|
prabhāte sumuhūrte ca prokṣaṇaṃ kartumārabhet|| 27.89 ||
āvāhya pūjayed devaṃ śaṅkhacakrādilakṣaṇam|
sāṅgaṃ dvādaśabhiścaiva daśabhirmurtibhiḥ saha|| 27.90 ||
ādityarudravasvādyaiḥ kinnarādyaiśca saṃvṛtam|
vāṅgayādiṃ sabinduṃ ca namaskārāntamuttamam|| 27.91 ||
praṇavādikamantreṇa snāpayet susamāhitaḥ|
śeṣodakena mantrajñaḥ snāpayed bhuvaneśvaram|| 27.92 ||
sūktābhyā

<28>
|| atha aṣṭāviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt karmaśeṣānahaṃ tava|
yadyogājjāyate sarvaṃ karmāvikalamuttamam|| 28.1 ||
lepādidūṣite bimbe śuddhiṃ kṛtvā yathoditām|
abhiṣekāt purā kāryāḥ śuddhayo'nyāśca yuktitaḥ|| 28.2 ||
vaiṣṇavānalanirdagdhabrahmatvagbhasmamiśritam|
tīrthatoyaṃ samāloḍya pañcapatrarasaṃ tathā|| 28.3 ||
gośṛṅgeṇābhiṣicyārcāṃ tatpiṣṭairlepayet punaḥ|
sahādūrvāsadābhadrāsiddhārtharajanīyutam|| 28.4 ||
doṣaghnaṃ kṣīriṇāṃ tvagbhirgharṣaṇaṃ lepanaṃ tathā|
dūrvāsiddhārthakalkena sagavyena ca lepanam|| 28.5 ||
candanāgarukarpūrakṣodairdhūpaḥ pureṇa ca|
parito dīpamālā ca kapilājyābjasūtrajā|| 28.6 ||
pippalatvaksahālakṣmīgandhanāgābjakesaraiḥ,
kṣīritvagrasasampiṣṭairlepanaṃ pañcabhistathā|| 28.7 ||
snānaprakāramālepaṃ dhūpanaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ|
sarvadoṣaharaṃ kuryād sthānavṛddhikaraṃ param|| 28.8 ||
pūraṇaṃ trividhaṃ proktamannapuṣpajalaiḥ śubhaiḥ|
puṣṭidaṃ jayadaṃ puṇyamapamṛtyuharaṃ tathā|| 28.9 ||
sarvadoṣaharaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ pāyasānnena pūraṇam|
kandājyaśarkarāpūpakadalīphalasaṃyutam|| 28.10 ||
śuddhyarthaṃ puṣṭidaṃ proktaṃ prāyaścittārthameva ca|
utsaveṣvapi kartavyamātmābhyudayakāṅkṣibhiḥ|| 28.11 ||
dhānyataṇḍulavastrasthān ghaṭān pañcāmbupūritān|
hemaratnādisaṃyuktān sakūrcān pallavāñcitān|| 28.12 ||
kamalādibhirācchādya kuśapuṣpādyalaṅkṛtām|
vāsobhirahataiśchannān gandhapuṣpaiśca pūjitān|| 28.13 ||
sakalena samālabhya pañcabhiḥ snāpayet kramāt|
tathā ca gandhairgavyaiśca payodadhyājyakādibhiḥ|| 28.14 ||
sampūjya vidhivad gandhairālipyācchādya vāsasā|
sugandhinā'hatenaiva sapīṭhaṃ kavacena tu|| 28.15 ||
śuddhapāyasahāridraistilaiḥ śuddhavinirmitaiḥ|
peyairlehyaiśca coṣyaiśca kandamūlaphalādibhiḥ|| 28.16 ||
surasairbahubhirdivyaiḥ sopadaṃśaiḥ samantataḥ|
dadhikṣīragulājyaiśca kālapakkaiśca pūrayet|| 28.17 ||
śītenānnena pūraḥ syādannasūktādibhiḥ śubhaiḥ|
sthāpayed ghaṭikāḥ sapta nādayet tūryanisvanaiḥ|| 28.18 ||
nivedayeccaturdikṣu pāyasādi yathākramam|
pañcabhirvrahmasūktena samidājyacarūn kramāt|| 28.19 ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā dikṣu bhūtabaliṃ haret|
tato'nujñāpya tat sarvaṃ visṛjyāpsu vinikṣipet|| 28.20 ||
tataścārādhayet samyagannādyaṃ cotsavaṃ punaḥ|
snapanaṃ kārayedevaṃ sarvasampad bhaviṣyati|| 28.21 ||
varṇasaṅkaravipraistu spṛṣṭe'ṣṭaśatamādiśet|
savarṇagāmibhiścaivaṃ brahmaghne'ṣṭasahasrakam|| 28.22 ||
jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ cātra gurutalpagate tathā|
suvarṇasteyasaṃsarge jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ tathā|| 28.23 ||
dvijānāṃ bhojanaṃ cātra snapanaṃ caiva kārayet|
surāpairapi saṃspṛṣṭe jalasaṃprokṣaṇādikam|| 28.24 ||
godānaṃ bahuviprāṇāmannādyaṃ snapanaṃ tathā|
brahmahatyādisaṃsṛṣṭairyādi spṛṣṭo janārdanaḥ|| 28.25 ||
jalasaṃprokṣaṇasnānahomādhyayanabhojanaiḥ|
bhūhemakapilādānapārāyaṇaśataṃ tathā|| 28.26 ||
ekasaṃparkavipreṇa pramādād yadi gamyate|
gehaṃ vā devabimbaṃ vā jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ tathā|| 28.27 ||
bahusaṃparkavipraistu spṛṣṭe bahuguṇaṃ tathā|
caṇḍālaśvapacaiḥ spṛṣṭe prāsāde bimba eva vā|| 28.28 ||
arcādravye ca saṃspṛṣṭe jalasaṃprokṣaṇādikam|
pañca ṣaṭ sapta vā'hāni mahāsnānaṃ dvijārcanam|| 28.29 ||
saptakārujanaiḥ spṛṣṭe bimbe prāsāda eva vā|
jalasaṃprokṣaṇasnānagodānānnādyavistaraḥ|| 28.30 ||
corarūpeṇa śūdrāstu saṃspṛṣṭā pratimā yadā|
jalasaṃprokṣaṇānnādyasnapanaiḥ suddhiriṣyate|| 28.31 ||
corarūpeṇa vaiśyaistu jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ kramāt|
snapanaṃ bhojanaṃ caiva kārayet susamāhitaḥ|| 28.32 ||
mohādathārthakāmād vā corarūpeṇa cennṛpaiḥ|
jalasaṃprokṣaṇenaiva śuddhiḥ syānnātra saṃśayaḥ|| 28.33 ||
varṇottameva cet spaṣṭaścorarūpeṇa keśavaḥ|
snapanaṃ kārayet tatra ṣoḍaśaiva ghaṭāḥ smṛtāḥ|| 28.34 ||
asnātvā yadi bhaktyā tu mantrasaṃskāravarjitam|
dvijottamena spṛśyeta pañcagavyena śodhayet|| 28.35 ||
nṛpeṇa yadi bhaktyā tu spṛṣṭo devo janārdanaḥ|
yathoktairnavabhiḥ kumbhaiḥ snapanenaiva śudhyati|| 28.36 ||
vaiśyena yadi saṃspṛṣṭo bhaktyā mantravivarjitam|
pañcāśatkalaśaireva snapena viśudhyati|| 28.37 ||
spṛṣṭaḥ śūdreṇa bhaktyā tu mantrasaṃskāravarjitam|
aṣṭottaraśatenaiva kartavyaṃ snapanaṃ punaḥ|| 28.38 ||
saptakārujanaiścāpi bhaktyā spṛṣṭe janārdane|
jalasaṃprokṣaṇenātra snapanena ca śudhyati|| 28.39 ||
eteṣāṃ cet striyā spṛṣṭo mohād vā yadi kāmataḥ|
pūrvoktā niṣkṛtiḥ kāryā śeṣaṃ brāhmaṇapūjanam|| 28.40 ||
godānaṃ ca punaḥ kuryād gavyasnānaṃ ca yuktitaḥ|
brāhmaṇyā coramārgeṇa spṛṣṭe tu madhusūdane|| 28.41 ||
snapanaṃ kārayecchaktyā brāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojanam|
rājanyavaiśyayoḥ sparśe mohād vā cauryakeṇa vā|| 28.42 ||
jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ snānaṃ viprabhojanasaṃyutam|
śūdrā yadi spṛśeccauryād garbhagehaṃ tu vā viśet|| 28.43 ||
jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ kuryānmahāsnapanasaṃyutam|
antyajānāṃ śunāṃ vā'pi garbhe kārusriyā'pi vā|| 28.44 ||
prasūte prokṣaṇaṃ kuryād dvādaśāhaṃ daśaiva vā|
mahāsnānaṃ ca kartavyamutsavānnādyasaṃyutam|| 28.45 ||
varṇajā yadi sūyet pramādād devamandire|
jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā mahāsnānena śudhyati|| 28.46 ||
kṣatriyā vā'tha vaiśyā vā yadyajñānāt prasūyate|
jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ kuryāt tridinaṃ vā caturdinam|| 28.47 ||
dviguṇaṃ sūtaśāvartusūtikādāhakaiḥ kramāt|
evaṃ mārgeṇa vai kuryājjalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ budhaḥ|| 28.48 ||
pārāyaṇaṃ cādhyayanaṃ godānaṃ bhojanaṃ tathā|
balibhramaṇvelāyāṃ tatpātraṃ yānameva vā|| 28.49 ||
pated yasmin diśābhāge tanmantreṇātra homayet|
ghṛtena haviṣā caiva pālāśāṣṭaśatena ca|| 28.50 ||
tathā nityabalisparśe kṛte śvānadvikādibhiḥ|
mūlamantradvayenaiva pañcopaniṣadā tathā|| 28.51 ||
viṣṇusūktena caivātra homaṃ kuryāt samāhitaḥ|
prāyaścitteṣu sarveṣu japahomeṣu śasyate|| 28.52 ||
aṣṭākṣaraṃ svasaṃkhyānaṃ dvādaśākṣarameva vā|
pātraṃ prakṣālya tu snātvā kṛtvā nyāsaṃ yathāvidhi|| 28.53 ||
samyagāvṛtya tāṃ bhūmiṃ gomayenopalipya ca|
pāyasaṃ śravayenmantrī ghṛtamiśraṃ suśobhanam|| 28.54 ||
vrihibhiḥ sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā tasmin vāso'tha taṇḍulān|
kuśapuṣpāṇi vinyasya prokṣyāstreṇa samāhitaḥ|| 28.55 ||
mūlena vinyaset pātraṃ praṇavena spaśet punaḥ|
tataścāstreṇa saṃprokṣya dakṣiṇe śāntimarcayet|| 28.56 ||
hutvā'nnaṃ mūlamantreṇa praṇavenājyameva ca|
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vā'pi sahasraṃ vā yathākramam|| 28.57 ||
haviḥśeṣaṃ tataḥ paścād balipātre vinikṣipet|
tat kṛtvā liṅgavat prokṣya mūlenāvāhya pūrvavat|| 28.58 ||
karmārcāṃ śodhayitvā vā tasyāmāvāhya pūjayet|
pādyārghyācamanaṃ dattvā gamdhapūṣpairatharcayet|| 28.59 ||
namaskṛtya tataḥ stutvā sottarīyaḥ samāhitaḥ|
uddhṛtya vyāhṛtīrjaptyā mūrdhri tāreṇa vinyaset|| 28.60 ||
śaṅkhatūryādisaṃyuktaṃ śanairgatvā pradakṣiṇam,
antaḥ praviśya tat pātramaiśānyāṃ sthāpayed budhaḥ|| 28.61 ||
udvāsya kṣālitaṃ tyaktvā pātramanyat samāharet|
anuyāge navaṃ bimbaṃ śodhayitvā'dhivāsayet|| 26.62 ||
vaiṣṇavāgnau tu juhuyāt prāyaścittaṃ viśeṣataḥ|
pañcopaniṣadairmantrairācāryaḥ susamāhitaḥ|| 28.63 ||
pūrṇāhutiṃ tato dadyād dvādaśākṣaravidyayā|
aṣṭākṣareṇa juhuyāt samidājyacarūn kramāt|| 28.64 ||
sahasraṃ vā śataṃ vā'pi pañcaviṃśatimeva vā|
lohakumbhaṃ tu saṃgṛhya suduḍhaṃ sūtraveṣṭitam|| 28.65 ||
pūrayitvā tu vidhanā saratnaṃ hemasaṃyutam|
bījaiḥ sarvaiḥ samāyuktaṃ sthāpayet tatra susthitam|| 28.66 ||
tatrāvāhya hariṃ paścāt pūjayecca vidhānataḥ|
pūrvoktenaiva mārgeṇa śeṣaṃ karma samācaret|| 28.67 ||
snapanaṃ cotsavaṃ kuryād vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā|
saṃvatsaraṃ naro bhaktyā samabhyarcya janārdanam|| 28.68 ||
yat phalaṃ samavāpnoti pavitrārohaṇena tat|
na karoti vidhānena pavitrārohaṇaṃ tu yaḥ|| 28.69 ||
tasya sāṃvatsarī pūjā niṣphalā kathitā budhaiḥ|
tasmāt kartavyamabde'bde pavitrārohaṇaṃ hareḥ|| 28.70 ||
śravaṇasya site pakṣe dvādaśyāṃ tu yathāvidhi|
siṃhasthe vā ravau kāryaṃ kanyāyāṃ tu sthite'thavā|| 28.71 ||
kanyayā kartitaṃ sūtraṃ kārpāsaṃ padmajaṃ tu vā|
kṣaumasūtraṃ tu vā kuryādalābhe darbhasambhavam|| 28.72 ||
nopayuktaṃ kriyāyāṃ syādanyabhaktādidūṣitam|
triguṇena ta kartavyaṃ hīnamadyottamaṃ tridhā|| 28.73 ||
tat saptaviṃśakaṃ kuryāccatuṣpañcāśakaṃ tathā|
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vā syād vanamālā sahasrikā|| 28.74 ||
phalaṃ ca mānuṣaṃ divyaṃ sālokyaṃ muktireva ca|
nābhyūrujānumātraṃ syāt paraṃ bimbapramāṇataḥ|| 28.75 ||
granthayo dvādaśa proktā dviguṇāsriguṇāstathā|
kartavyaṃ vanamālāyāṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ sadā|| 28.76 ||
adhivāsanasūtre tu granthayo dvādaśaiva tu|
mantro dvijoṣvidaṃviṣṇuranyeṣu dvādaśākṣaram|| 28.77 ||
tāvadāvartayenmantraṃ yāvanto granthayaḥ kṛtāḥ|
ekadhā ca dvidhā caiva tridhā cāpi pavitrake|| 28.78 ||
kuṅkumośīrakarpūracandanādivilepanaiḥ|
granthimadhye vilipyātha tattvanyāsaṃ tu yojayet|| 28.79 ||
adhivāsya pavitrāṇi caikādaśyāmupoṣitaḥ|
gandhapuṣpaiḥ sambhyarcya nyased devāgrato niśi|| 28.80 ||
tattvajñānaparaiḥ kāryaṃ pavitrārohaṇaṃ hareḥ|
ekādaśyāṃ tadabhyarcya mūlamantreṇa bhaktimān|| 28.81 ||
bhūṣādāmanivedyaiśca saṃpūjya garuḍadhvajam|
nṛttagītādisaṃyuktaṃ jāgaraṃ tatra kārayet|| 28.82 ||
sopavāsaḥ śucirbhūtvā kṛtajapyo jitendriyaḥ|
dadyāt dānaṃ dvijāgrebhyo bhaktyā'bhyarcya hariṃ smaran|| 28.83 ||
tatrādhivāsitaṃ prātastadādāya pavitrakam|
atodeveti sūktena viṣṇormūrdhni niveśayet|| 28.84 ||
śūdrastu mūlamantreṇa yena vā pūjayeddharim|
vedaghoṣairjayastotragītamaṅgalanisvanaiḥ|| 28.85 ||
pavitrārohaṇaṃ kuryād devaṃ vijñāpayet tataḥ|
maṇividrumamālābhirmandārakusumādibhiḥ|| 28.86 ||
iyaṃ sāṃvatsarī pūjā tavāstu garuḍadhvaja!|
mantrahīnaṃ kriyāhīnaṃ bhaktihīnaṃ janārdana!|| 28.87 ||
yat pūjitaṃ mayā deva! paripūrṇaṃ tadastute|
vanamālāṃ yathā deva! kaustubhaṃ satataṃ hṛdi|| 28.88 ||
tadvat pavitratantūṃśca pūjāṃ cemāṃ hṛdā vaha|
iti vijñāpya deveśaṃ bhaktyā''namya kṣamāpayet|| 28.89 ||
tataśca dadyād viprebhyo harimuddiśya dakṣiṇām|
tato'nupūjayed bhaktyā gurumanyāṃśca bhaktitaḥ|| 28.90 ||
mūlamantreṇa juhuyād vahnau saghṛtayāvakam|
tataśca pūjayet bhaktyā viprān dadyācca dakṣiṇām|| 28.91 ||
sarvāṃścaivārthinaḥ śaktyā pūjayedodanādibhiḥ|
yāvattattvayutā pūjā tāvatyo'ṅgulayo'tra vā|| 28.92 ||
yāvanto granthayaścoktāstattvaistairanumantrayet|
sāṃvat

<29>
|| athaikonatriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt samayācāralakṣaṇam|
dīkṣitānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ yadyogāt sarvasiddhayaḥ|| 29.1 ||
brāhmaṇādyāścaturvarṇāstrayastveṣāṃ ca dīkṣitāḥ|
bhaktā jitendriyāḥ śāntāḥ sarve samayinaḥ smṛtāḥ|| 29.2 ||
samayī dīkṣitaḥ paścāccakravartyabhiṣekavān|
guruścaiva tathācāryo bhagavān saptamaḥ smṛtaḥ|| 29.3 ||
yāgaḥ stomo mahāyāgaścādhvaro'tha savaḥ kratuḥ|
haristoma iti jñeyāḥ sapta yāgāḥ samāsataḥ|| 29.4 ||
vasante ca tathā grīṣme śaradvarṣāsu ca kramāt|
varṇināṃ vihitā dīkṣā sarveṣāṃ vottarāyaṇe|| 29.5 ||
sakṛd dvistriścaturdīkṣā saptakṛtvaśca varṇinām|
tathā dvādaśakṛtvaśca kāryā vā prativatsaram|| 29.6 ||
puṣpātavaśānnāma pūrvameva tu gṛhyate|
taccharmavarmaguptāntaṃ dāsāntaṃ ceha varṇinām|| 29.7 ||
kevalaṃ samayītyādi draṣṭavyaṃ keśāvādivat|
strīṇāṃ tu keśavādyākhyā devyantāstriṣu kīrtitāḥ|| 29.8 ||
śūdrāṇāṃ dāsadāsyantāḥ sarveṣāṃ yāgabhedataḥ|
sarve'pi dīkṣitāḥ samyak sadā guruparāyaṇāḥ|| 29.9 ||
varṇāśramagatān dharmān rakṣeyuḥ samayāṃstathā|
gurudevadvijāgnīnāṃ sarvadā paricaryayā|| 29.10 ||
vratopavāsaniyamairbhaktyā ceccheyurunnatim|
nāniṣṭvā devamaśnīyānnacādīkṣitaveśmani|| 29.11 ||
nirmālyaṃ na śpṛśed dadyāllaṅghayet vā'śanaṃ kutaḥ|
sarveṣāmeva devānāṃ nirmālyamaśuci smṛtam|| 29.12 ||
viṣṇubhuktaṃ tu puṣpādi śucītyādadate pare|
kāṃsyapātre na bhoktavyaṃ na vandyā cānyadevatā|| 29.13 ||
adīkṣitādyayogyeṣu siddhāntaṃ na prakāśayet|
nodakyayā'bhibhāṣeta na caṇḍālairnacāntyajaiḥ|| 29.14 ||
na kudeśe vasennityaṃ na ca pāṣaṇḍibhiḥ saha|
tiryak puṇḍraṃ na kurvīta no dviṭkāraṃ na saṅkaram|| 29.15 ||
śarṅgāṣṭhaṃ laśunaṃ śigru dhānyāmlaṃ cāvikaṃ payaḥ|
piṇyākaṃ koradūṣaṃ ca chatrākaṃ varjayet sadā|| 29.16 ||
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaṃ nātiṣṭheccakrarūpakam|
pratimāsannidhau nānyapratimāstutimācaret|| 29.17 ||
śṛṇuyānna parīvādaṃ naca kālaṃ vṛthā nayet|
cakrāṅko viṣṇunāmāṅkaputrabhṛtyapaśurbhavet|| 29.18 ||
catraiva tu śiraḥ kṛtvā svaped yatra ravirgataḥ|
nāgnigoguruviprārcādhānyeṣvaṅghrī prasārayet|| 29.19 ||
matsyakūrmavarāhāṇāṃ naca māṃsāni bhakṣayet|
viprāgnivaiṣṇavāśvatthān gāśca kuryāt pradakṣiṇam|| 29.20 ||
viṣṇorgṛhāṇi sarvāṇi dṛṣṭvā bhaktyā'bhivādayet|
na bāhubhyāṃ taret sindhuṃ na gavā nānyavāsasā|| 29.21 ||
na nagno vā viśet toyaṃ na dhāved varṣasaṃbhave|
ūrdhvapuṇḍrāstu ye martyāstān sarvācchakti'rcayet|| 29.22 ||
agrabhikṣāṃ sadā dadyād grāsamuṣṭiṃ gavāṃ tathā|
nādyāt paryuṣitaṃ cānnaṃ nāgataṃ paragehataḥ|| 29.23 ||
śiraspṛṣṭena tailena nāṅgaṃ kiñcidupaspṛśet|
nānantardhāya cāsīta nānyabhaktaṃ prapūjayet|| 29.24 ||
pratyakṣalavaṇaṃ nādyānniryāsāmedhyajāni vā|
vaiṣṇavairdīkṣitairviprairvivādaṃ na samācaret|| 29.25 ||
antyajaṃ vā śvapākaṃ vā nāvamanyeta vaiṣṇavam|
viṣṇvarthānyeva karmāṇi sarvāṇi manasā smaret|| 29.26 ||
api prāṇaparityāgaṃ kuryād viṣṇunimittataḥ|
kṛtena karmaṇā yena bhagavān bhuvaneśvaraḥ|| 29.27 ||
prasīdati hi tat kāryaṃ karmedaṃ vaiṣṇavaiḥ sadā|
satpathe manasi nyaste vāṇī tiṣṭhati satpathe|| 29.28 ||
indriyāṇi ca sarvāṇi tasmāt tacchikṣyamāditaḥ|
upakāraḥ paro dharmaḥ sarveṣāmiti niścayaḥ|| 29.29 ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena sarveṣāṃ hitamācaret|
dānāni kīrtibhūyāṃsi madhyamāni phaleṣvataḥ|| 29.30 ||
rahasyadānaṃ kartavyaṃ pātrakālādiyogataḥ|
pātre sarvāṇi deyāni dātavyāni manasvinā|| 29.31 ||
devameva samuddiśya yaśastatrānuṣaṅgikam|
jitendriyasya bhaktasya mānaso dharma uttamaḥ|| 29.32 ||
kāyikaḥ prākṛtasyokte madhyamasya tu vācikaḥ|
kriyārūpaḥ smṛto dharmo jñānarūpaṃ tu tatparam|| 29.33 ||
jñānena mokṣamāpnoti jñānārthā ceṣyate kriyā|
kevalaṃ tviha vijñānaṃ dharmasyānugrahādṛte|| 29.34 ||
nālaṃ ceto bhavāviṣṭaṃ prasādayitumañjasā|
tadiṣṭvā'nantarāyārthaṃ prāpya jñānamanugrahāt|| 29.35 ||
tataḥ sādhyo bhavenmokṣastadbhaktaireva nāparaiḥ|
prāsāde sthāpitaṃ devaṃ sarvadā na prakāśayet|| 29.36 ||
anyatrārcanavelāyāṃ pūjanānte prakāśayet|
aśicidravyasaṃsargaṃ janasammardameva ca|| 29.37 ||
prāsāde varjayennityaṃ lokopakaraṇāni ca|
vaidikā vaiṣṇavā mantrāḥ śāntikāḥ pauṣṭikāstathā|| 29.38 ||
yathāyogaṃ prayoktavyāstantramantrāstadantare|
ṛkṣu ye pāvanā mantrā yajuṣvapi ca sāmasu|| 29.39 ||
atharvaṇeṣu cādeyā devapūjādikarmasu|
ye mantrāḥ paramaṃ devaṃ sākṣāllakṣeṇa(kṣyeṇa) vā''śritāḥ|| 29.40 ||
sāṅgāḥ pṛthagupādeyāstān vicāryātra yojayet|
nahi tebhyaḥ paraṃ kiñcid vāṅmayaṃ bhuvi vidyate|| 29.41 ||
kāmadaṃ ca pavitraṃ ca ye mantrā vaidikāḥ smṛtāḥ|
taireva kṛtasaṃskāre vaṃśe jātaḥ svayaṃ punaḥ|| 29.42 ||
tānupekṣyāparān mantrān nānuvartitumarhati|
karmakāṇḍapradhānāstu vaidikā vidhayaḥ sthitāḥ|| 29.43 ||
kāmakāmairviyoktavyāstāṃ tāmāśritya devatām|
mantrāste vaidikāḥ siddhāḥ saṃbhūya pṛthageva vā|| 29.44 ||
karmasiddhau pravartante prāyo nānāphalāśrayāḥ|
te'nvīkṣyānvīkṣya saṃgrāhyā vaiṣṇavairvadavādibhiḥ|| 29.45 ||
tatpūjāyāmidaṃ tantraṃ bhavatyeva nibandhanam|
tajjñātvā vaidikān mantrānyānuddhṛtya ca svayam|| 29.46 ||
idaṃ ca tantramālambya pūjayet puruṣottamam|
yo japastutyupasthānaṃ taiḥ kṛtvā'nusmaran harim|| 29.47 ||
paṭhenmantrān yathānyāyaṃ sa mukhyo viṣṇuyājakaḥ|
japayajñaṃ viśeṣeṇa ye tu kurvanti vaidikaiḥ|| 29.48 ||
kāle pūjāṃ ca kurvanti teṣu saṃprīyate hariḥ|
dhyātvā'rkamaṇḍale viṣṇuṃ vedamantrairabhiṣṭutam|| 29.49 ||
dhāryamāṇe tataivāgnau juhuyāt kāryasiddhaye|
tatra yat kevalaṃ dhyānaṃ vedāntoktamanāśrayam|| 29.50 ||
na tatrendriyadaurbalyāt karmasthasyādhikāritā|
yathā giritaṭāgrasthavanaspatiphalecchayā|| 29.51 ||
upāye vartate'śrāntastathā'sau yatnamācaret|
sarvatra kramavān yatnaḥ kāryo necchaiva kevalā|| 29.52 ||
tat kāyavāṅmanoyogaiḥ kramādicchet parāṃ gatim|
nirākāre tu yā bhaktyā pūjeṣṭā dhyānameva vā|| 29.53 ||
ramaṇīyamivābhāti tadanarthasya kāraṇam|
sthūlabhāvaprasaṅgīni janmanā'syendriyāṇi hi|| 29.54 ||
sūkṣmārthaṃ na prapadyante cirācca kimutācirāt|
naca rūpaṃ vinā devo dhyātuṃ kenāpi śakyate|| 29.55 ||
sarvarūpanivṛttā hi buddhiḥ kutrāsya tiṣṭhati|
nivṛttā glāyate buddhirnidrayā vā parīyate|| 29.56 ||
tasmād vidvānupāsīta buddhyā sākārameva tam|
asti tasya parokṣaṃ taditi kiñcidanusmaret|| 29.57 ||
sarvathā''kāramuddiṣṭaṃ na parityajya paṇḍitaḥ|
paraṃ devamupāsīta muktaye vā phalāya vā|| 29.58 ||
mantrairāvartyamānaistu viṣṇupādasamāśrayaiḥ|
svarūpaṃ lakṣyate buddhau tatprasādena nānyathā|| 29.59 ||
bhaktyarthāḥ sarvamantrāḥ syuḥ stotrāṇi dhyānamarcanam|
sā yasya hṛdaye tīvrā sa bhakto nānya iṣyate|| 29.60 ||
priyāṇi devadattāni karmajānyapriyāṇi ca|
yaḥ paśyet satataṃ buddhyā sa bhakto netaro janaḥ|| 29.61 ||
tasya yogamayaṃ vidyādākāraṃ sārvavastukam|
nityaṃ bhaktānukampārthaṃ dhyeyo mantramayastu saḥ|| 29.62 ||
yadā nivṛttakarmāsau nirvāṇe ramate budhaḥ|
tadā sūkṣmaśarīraṃ taṃ pūjayet puruṣottamam|| 29.63 ||
śaktayo'syāyudhākārā vijñeyā bāhavo diśaḥ|
dyaurmūrdhā pṛthivī pādau dṛṣṭirarko manaḥ śaśī|| 29.64 ||
acchedyo'yamadāhyo'yamakledyo'śoṣya eva ca|
nityaḥ sarvagataḥ sthāṇuḥ pūjyo nityaṃ manīṣibhiḥ|| 29.65 ||
yadā yadā prasannā dhīrdhyāyet sūkṣmaṃ tadā tadā|
sīdanti saṃśayenaiva yatayo'pyatra viklavāḥ|| 29.66 ||
āgamārthaṃ dṛḍhaṃ kuryāt sarvavidyābhirātmavān|
vivekena ca śuddhena nāgamasyaiva saṃplavam|| 29.67 ||
svagṛhye dharmaśāstre ca yaduktaṃ tat sadācaret|
tantroktamaviruddhe ca kulavarṇāśramānugam|| 29.68 ||
srīśūdrāṇāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ naupanāyanikakriyā|
dīkṣopanayanaṃ teṣāṃ tāntrikaśca manāgvidhiḥ|| 29.69 ||
tāntrikāstantramantrāḥ syurvidhayaśca tadāśrayāḥ|
mūlamantraiśca gāyatryā vaidikaiśca tridhā kriyā|| 29.70 ||
viṭchūdrayornṛpe vipre sarvaṃ vā vipra ityate|
homādiṣu svamantrāḥ syuranulomāstu vā sadā|| 29.71 ||
caturvarṇodbhavāḥ sarve pratilomānulomajāḥ|
tatrānulomajāḥ śreṣṭhā varjyāstu pratilomajāḥ|| 29.72 ||
pratilomeṣu sarveṣu sūta ekastu gṛhyate|
nārhantyevetare dīkṣāṃ varṇāśramabahiṣkṛtāḥ|| 29.73 ||
brahmacārī gṛhasthaśca vānaprastho yatistathā|
caturdhā''śramiṇo bhinnāḥ punaścārabhedataḥ|| 29.74 ||
tatrādyo brahmacārī ca liṅgī śiṣya upāsakaḥ|
akṣāralavaṇāśī yo bhaikṣāhāro mitāśanaḥ|| 29.75 ||
sthaṇḍilājinaśāyī ca brahmacārī sa ucyate|
evaṃ karmāṇi yaḥ kuryāccakrādikṛtalakṣaṇaḥ|| 29.76 ||
devatārādhane saktaḥ sa liṅgīti prakīrtitaḥ|
bhaikṣamātreṇa jīvan yaḥ paṭhecchuśrūṣurarcakaḥ|| 29.77 ||
nānyakāryaparo nityaṃ sa śiṣyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
trisandhyaṃ pūjayan devaṃ nānyakāryaparaśca yaḥ|| 29.78 ||
triḥ snātvā'nucarennityaṃ guruṃ sa syādupāsakaḥ|
vratī gṛhastha evāḍhya ācāryo gṛhiṇastvime|| 29.79 ||
catvāra iha samproktāstathaivācārabhedataḥ|
vratopavāsī naktāśī niyatārco jitendriyaḥ|| 29.80 ||
ṛtugāmī mitāhāro vratī mūlaparaḥ sadā|
yajñādhyayanadevārcāśiṣyādibharaṇodyataḥ|| 29.81 ||
gṛhasya iti vijñeyaḥ svadāravratiko naraḥ|
sarvātithyaparo nityaṃ kāmabhojī sukhānvitaḥ|| 29.82 ||
devotsavaparo bhaktaḥ parvayājyāḍhya ucyate|
yājanādhyāpane yukto yāge vaidikatāntrike|| 29.83 ||
nityayājī jitakodha ācāryo'nugrahonmukhaḥ|
tathā vaikhānasastantrī gururniṣkala eva ca|| 29.84 ||
vānaprasthaścaturdhaiva tantre'smin parikīrtitaḥ|
akṣāralavaṇāśī yo vyākhyātā putradāravān|| 29.85 ||
sthaṇḍilājinaśāyī ca dhyāyan vaikhānasaḥ sadā|
vanyavṛttisrisandhyārcī japahomaparāyaṇaḥ|| 29.86 ||
valkalājinavastantrī darbhaśāyī japan sadā|
triḥ snāyī niyatāvāsaścīrī mūlaphalāśanaḥ|| 29.87 ||
tīrthasnāto'rcayan dhyāyan dviṣaṭkajapavān guruḥ|
yadṛcchālābhasantuṣṭo naktabhojī dṛḍhavrataḥ|| 29.88 ||
cīrī vimatsaro maunī niṣkalo'hnastrirarcayan|
haṃsaḥ paramahaṃsaśca bhagavān prabhurityapi|| 29.89 ||
caturvidhaḥ samākhyātastantre'smin dīkṣito yatiḥ|
śaṅkhacakradharo nityamekadaṇḍyekabhaikṣabhuk|| 29.90 ||
sadārcī karmavid dhyānī haṃso japaparaḥ sadā|
advaitanirato daṇḍī jñānadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ|| 29.91 ||
śānto'śikhopavītaśca bhaikṣāhāro vratasthitaḥ|
yatiḥ paramahaṃsaḥ syāt tantrakarmaṇi niṣṭhitaḥ|| 29.92 ||
śaṅkhacakrabṛsīśikyakamaṇḍalupavitravān|
tridaṇḍī pātravān bhikṣurbhagavān yogapaṭṭabhṛt|| 29.93 ||
etānyeva vahan nityaṃ chatraṃ pratikṛtiṃ tathā|
cakrayāgena deveśamarcayan niyatātmavān|| 29.94 ||
trisandhyārādhane yuktaḥ sattvastho japavān prabhuḥ|
viṣuvāyanayorjanmadvādaśīśravaṇeṣu ca|| 29.95 ||
aṣṭamyāṃ ca navamyāṃ ca pañcamyāṃ na striyaṃ vrajet|
mūlena pariṣicyānnaṃ spṛṣṭvā pītvā jalaṃ tathā|| 29.96 ||
dhyātvā'ntaḥ puruṣaṃ maunī dīptavaiśvānaraṃ tathā|
praṇavenāhutīḥ pañca kuryāt pañcātmane tataḥ|| 29.97 ||
vaktramātraṃ graset piṇḍaṃ na hasennodvijeta vā|
tyajet sarvatra cāpalyaṃ bhoktavyaṃ śabdavarjitam|| 29.98 ||
śvodakyāntyajapāṣaṇḍikākadevalukukkuṭān|
kārukān vā tadā paśyan snāyāt tyaktvā'nnamajjasā|| 29.99 ||
pañcopaniṣado japtvā prokṣayet praṇavena ca|
viṣṇuścātha mahāviṣṇuḥ sadāviṣṇurime trayaḥ|| 29.100 ||
savaneṣu kramāt pūjyā japadhyānādibhiḥ sadā|
prātastu viṣṇugāyatrī madhyāhne dvādaśākṣaram|| 29.101 ||
sāyamaṣṭākṣaraṃ nityaṃ mūlaṃ vā sarvadā japet|
utthāyānu garuṃ snātvā dhyātvā devaṃ prapūjayet|| 29.102 ||
nirgacchet savyapādena praviśeccālayaṃ punaḥ|
guroranujñayā bhaikṣaṃ caret pātreṇa śaṃ vadan|| 29.103 ||
gurave darśayitvā tu vinyaset tadanujñayā|
ācamya mūlamantreṇa hutvā'gnau samidhaḥ śuciḥ|| 29.104 ||
āsane sukhamāsīnaḥ pādau vinyasya bhūtale|
pātraṃ ca vāmahastena spṛśan prāṇāhutīścaret|| 29.105 ||
yadyuttiṣṭhedanācānto bhuktavānāsanād dvijaḥ|
snānaṃ sadyastu kartavyaṃ tasmāt tatraiva cācamet|| 29.106 ||
sandhyāmupāsya bhūyo'pi hutvā ca samidhastathā|
arcayitvā hariṃ bhikṣāṃ caret pūrvavadeva ca|| 29.107 ||
evaṃ prātaśca sāyaṃ ca gurorvacanamāsthitaḥ|
guroranujñayā paścād gṛhasthāśramamāśritaḥ|| 29.108 ||
snātvā tamarcayennityaṃ tataḥ karmāṇi cācaret|| 29.109 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāmekonatriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

<30>
|| atha triṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepād yogaṃ bhāgavataṃ param|
yadanyairaśrutaṃ pūrvaṃ hitānāṃ paramaṃ hitam|| 30.1 ||
yuktāhāravihārastu yuktaceṣṭaḥ samāhitaḥ|
yogaṃ bhāgavataṃ nityamabhyasenniyatātmavān|| 30.2 ||
pañcakālavibhāgajño mitabhāṣī mitāśanaḥ|
kāmakrodhādijid bhaktaḥ samaloṣṭāśmakāñcanaḥ|| 30.3 ||
samayācārasaṃyukto japadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ|
ekānte vijane sthāne nivāte śabdavarjite|| 30.4 ||
baddhvā yogāsanaṃ maunī yogaṃ yuñjīta yogavit|
pūjayitvā hariṃ pūjāṃ tatraiva pratipādayet|| 30.5 ||
kiṅkaratvena cātmānaṃ kṛtāñjaliradhomukhaḥ|
na yāceta phalaṃ kiñcit prasanno dāsyati svayam|| 30.6 ||
jānātyeva hi kālaṃ sa bhaktipūjāguṇāguṇān|
prasannastvanugṛhṇīyāt tvarayā na kadācana|| 30.7 ||
natveva nānugṛhṇīyājjanmāntaraśateṣvapi|
karmaṇāṃ pacyamānānāmapareṣāṃ parikṣaye|| 30.8 ||
prakāśayati bhaktānāṃ hariḥ kurvannanugraham|
tatastu saṃpadevāsya purastādupalakṣyate|| 30.9 ||
kṣīyante'syārayo nityaṃ vardhante saṃpadaḥ svayam|
phalaṃ tvayatnād bhūyaḥ syānnaśyanti vyādhayaḥ svayam|| 30.10 ||
na kvaciccāsya saṃkleśo vardhante paśavaḥ prajāḥ|
dīrghaṃ cāsya bhavedāyurjāyante subhagāḥ prajāḥ|| 30.11 ||
praśaṃsanti janāścainaṃ corayeyurna dasyavaḥ|
anupadravamaiśvaryaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhatyayantritam|| 30.12 ||
dharmādyabhimukhī dhīḥ syādadharmādiparāṅmukhī|
sukhenaivāparāntaśca puṇyakāle bhaviṣyati|| 30.13 ||
janmotkṛṣṭakule bhūyo bhaktiśca punaracyute|
utkṛṣṭatama eva syāt tathā janmani janmani|| 30.14 ||
akliṣṭamaṣṭadhaiśvaryaṃ tatprasādād bhaviṣyati|
viharedicchayā dīrghaṃ nirvāṇaṃ vā tathā'pnuyāt|| 30.15 ||
bhakteṣvasya prasādena bhavitavyaṃ tu sarvathā|
bhraṣṭo'pi vividhān kāmānāpnuyānna vimucyate|| 30.16 ||
kintu karmānubandhena saṃsāre kleśasambhavaḥ|
tasmāt tadbhaktirevāsya saṃpadāṃ kāraṇaṃ bhavet|| 30.17 ||
tajjñānena nṛṇāṃ bhaktirvardhanīyā prayatnataḥ|
vardhitā tu viśeṣeṇa sainaṃ nayati satpatham|| 30.18 ||
tasmāt kāryo viveko'tra guṇadoṣā yathā sthitāḥ|
cintayedakhilān doṣānekānte śuddhamānasaḥ|| 30.19 ||
kāmakopādibhirmuktaṃ manaḥ śuddhaṃ bhaviṣyati|
abhyāsena bhavecchuddhirmanaso yogavartmani|| 30.20 ||
śuddhe manasi tadbhaktirjāyate tasya niścalā|
tasyāṃ samupajātāyāṃ tattve ceto'vatiṣṭhate|| 30.21 ||
rāgadveṣaparītasya viphalaṃ tattvadarśanam|
tasmāt tau pūrvamutsāryau tattvaṃ samyag didṛkṣuṇā|| 30.22 ||
puṇyadeśeṣu dṛṣṭeṣu bhayeṣu maraṇeṣu ca|
tathā rogāvamāneṣu vairāgyamupajāyate|| 30.23 ||
maraṇādiṣu dṛṣṭeṣu svayameva samāhitaḥ|
buddhyā vivekamātiṣṭhennityaṃ vairāgyakāraṇam|| 30.24 ||
vivekājjāyate bhaktirvairāgyaṃ cāsya sarvadā|
tatastattvagatā buddhirbhaviṣyatyanapāyinī|| 30.25 ||
na bhogeṣu prasaktaḥ syānna kuryād durlabhe matim|
alpena parituṣṭaḥ syānnityaṃ bhāgavato budhaḥ|| 30.26 ||
vipākaṃ karmaṇāmeva paśyet paśyan priyāpriye|
idaṃ yuktamidaṃ neti nānuśocet kadācana|| 30.27 ||
prabhutvaṃ nātmanaḥ kiñciditi paśyed vivekavān|
ghoṣayennaiva kṛtyāni na kuryāt saṃbhramaṃ kvacit|| 30.28 ||
na cākāṅkṣed yaśaḥ kiñcid devadevasya pūjanāt|
yathā manaḥ priyeṣvevaṃ saktaṃ deve ca bhāvayet|| 30.29 ||
nityaṃ bhaktau tu jātāyāṃ jāyate tattvadarśanam|
tattvavid yatra tatrastho muktasaṅgaḥ parivrajet|| 30.30 ||
eka eva punastīrthaṃ kṣetraṃ cānuvrajet sadā|
bhaktyā devamanudhyāyet tanniṣṭhastatparāyaṇaḥ|| 30.31 ||
antakāle ca taṃ smṛtvā tatsāyujyamavāpnuyāt|
virakto'khilatattvāni vilāpya paramātmani|| 30.32 ||
kṛtvā nirviṣayaṃ cittamudāsīno vimucyate|
aiśvarye'pi sthito yogī na devaṃ jātu vismaret|| 30.33 ||
yataḥ kālakrameṇa syādupaghāto'nyathā dhruvam|
sāyujyaṃ pratipannāstu ye bhaktāḥ śuddhacetasaḥ|| 30.34 ||
teviṣṇoḥ kiṅkarā nityaṃ bhavanti nirupadravāḥ|
teṣāṃ bhaktparādhena saṃsāraḥ saṃbhavet punaḥ|| 30.35 ||
anyathā sthitireva syād vairāgyānmuktireva vā|
nivṛttāstu viśiṣyante sarvebhyastattvadarśinaḥ|| 30.36 ||
naiva teṣāṃ bhavo bhūyaḥ pramādo veha kaścana|
muktireva tu vijñeyā gatīnāmuttamā gatiḥ|| 30.37 ||
anyāstu gatayaḥ sarvāḥ sapramādāḥ syuradhruvāḥ|
tathā'pi mativaicitryādubhayaṃ saṃmataṃ nṛṇām|| 30.38 ||
aiśvaryamapavargo vā kasyacit kiñcideva tu|
tadgatā bhaktirevaiṣāmubhayasyāpi sādhanī|| 30.39 ||
samādhervardhamānasya vibhāgād bhedakāriṇaḥ|
sattvasthe yatnataścitte yogāṅgāni niṣevate|| 30.40 ||
teṣu sthitvā smared viṣṇumekāgreṇa tu cetasā|
tamaḥ kṣarati tenāsya rajaśca vaśinaḥ kramāt|| 30.41 ||
tato janmabhirukṛṣṭaiḥ siddhimevādhigacchati|
yadā tu sattvamātiṣṭhedaspṛṣṭo rajasā kvacit|| 30.42 ||
tato'sya muktireva syāt sāyujyaṃ vā mahātmanaḥ|
tasmād bhaktiṃ sadā'nvicched yā'sya sarvasya kāraṇam|| 30.43 ||
bhaktāvacyamānāyāṃ bhavecchreyo'sya nānyathā|
antarāyāstu ye nityaṃ yogābhyāsasya dūṣakāḥ|| 30.44 ||
teṣu prasajyamāneṣu tattvadarśanamāśrayet|
nātivelaṃ sadābhyasyenna śarīraṃ ca pīḍayet|| 30.45 ||
viṣayebhyo'pi yatnena nivarteta śanaiḥ śanaiḥ|
apūrvadarśane caiva lokavādāśraye tathā|| 30.46 ||
moghacintāprasaṅge ca yogamicchennaro budhaḥ|
nivṛtteṣveṣu sarveṣu bhaktiḥ puṃso vivardhane|| 30.47 ||
viṣayā dustyajāḥ sevyā dharmaśāstrāvirodhinaḥ|
bahvapāyatvayogena yatnād vecchāṃ nivartayet|| 30.48 ||
yebhyo nivartamānasya manaḥ śuddhaṃ bhavet sadā|
nivṛtterabhyupāyaśca śuddhireva parātmanaḥ|| 30.49 ||
śuddhāvādriyamāṇāyāṃ bībhatsurviṣayeṣvayam|
jāyate nāśuciṣveva saṃsargo'sya bhaviṣyati|| 30.50 ||
aśucīnāmasaṃsargāccittamasya prasīdati|
prasāde niḥspṛhasyāsya bhaktirmuktistato dhruvā|| 30.51 ||
śabdādiviṣayāñ jitvā ruddhā dvārāṇi sarvaśaḥ|
pravilāpyendriyāṇyantaścintayet paramaṃ padam|| 30.52 ||
saṃyamya mārutān pañca yogābhyāsena yogavit|
sthirīkṛtamanā nityaṃ dhyāyet tat paramaṃ padam|| 30.53 ||
varṇāśca pañcavāyūnāmantasthānāṃ vicintayet|
madhye madhye tu purveṣāṃ kramād dhyeyāḥ pare pare|| 30.54 ||
kāśavarṇo bhavet prāṇo rakto'pāno'sya madhyagaḥ|
vidyutsphuritabhā vyāna udānaḥ kundasannibhaḥ|| 30.55 ||
samānastu tato dhyeyaḥ śuddhasphaṭikasannibhaḥ|
evaṃ dhyātvā nirudhyāntaḥ kramaśaḥ pañca mārutān|| 30.56 ||
ṣaḍaṅgāni ca vijñāya prayuñjīta yathāvidhi|
prāṇāyāmo'tra pūrvaṃ tu pratyāhāro'tha dhāraṇā|| 30.57 ||
tatastarkaḥ samādhiśca dhyānaṃ cāṅgāni ṣaṭ kramāt|
ye proktā yogibhiḥ pūrvaṃ pūrarecakakumbhakāḥ|| 30.58 ||
prāṇāyāmāstu vijñeyā mātrābhiste pṛthak kramāt|
jānuṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya na drutaṃ na vilambitam|| 30.59 ||
kriyate yo'ṅgulisphoṭaḥ sā mātreha prakīrtitā|
yadā dvādaśamātrābhiḥ pūrakaḥ kriyate tadā|| 30.60 ||
recako dviguṇābhiḥ syāt triguṇābhiśca kumbhakaḥ|
prāṇānāṃ yastvihāyāmaḥ kramādevaṃ prakīrtitaḥ|| 30.61 ||
sarvaśāstreṣu śāstrajñaiḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate|
dhāraṇā tu bhaved yā sā prāṇāyāmaistribhiḥ smṛtā|| 30.62 ||
śyāmā pītā'ruṇā śuklā kramājjñeyā ca dhāraṇā|
nābhimadhyagatenādau bījena puruṣātmanā|| 30.63 ||
vāyavyā dhāraṇā kāryā sā śyāmā pūrakātmikā|
tato viśvātmabījena raktena hṛdi yā kṛtā|| 30.64 ||
āgneyī sā samuddiṣṭā dvitīyā recakā matā|
kaṇṭhe nivṛttibījena pītena kriyate tu yā|| 30.65 ||
māhendrī sā samuddiṣṭā dhāraṇā kumbhakātmikā|
mūrdhni sarvātmabījena śuklenaitaistribhistribhiḥ|| 30.66 ||
kriyate dhāraṇā yāntyā vāruṇī sā prakīrtitā|
sthiteṣu teṣu bījeṣu caitanyaṃ dhāryate yadā|| 30.67 ||
tadguṇānvitamityeva jagat sarvaṃ tadā smaret|
nābhyādiṣu yathoddiṣṭaṃ bījaṃ bhūtaguṇātmakam|| 30.68 ||
kriyābhirdhāryate yābhistatkriyā dhāraṇā matā|
anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ yasmād yadupalabhyate|| 30.69 ||
dhāraṇādiṣu kāleṣu sa tarkaḥ samprakīrtitaḥ|
āvaprayāsarūpeṇa yathākarma guṇātmakam|| 30.70 ||
samādhiśceha vijñeyo dhāraṇābhistribhistribhiḥ|
dhyātṛdhyeyasvarūpaṃ yat tatra rundhyāt pradarśanam|| 30.71 ||
samādhitriguṇaṃ yāvadevaṃ dhyānasya lakṣaṇam|
evaṃ sarvātmanā yasya jñeyaistulyaḥ kathañcana|| 30.72 ||
yogo'sau yogibhiḥ prokto vijñānakramayogataḥ|
guṇānāṃ yaḥ samāhāraḥ sahāyasya ca cetasaḥ|| 30.73 ||
sadbhāvabhāvato buddhyā sa yogaśceha kīrtitaḥ|
sa ca jñānakriyābhedād jñeyo yogo dvidhā budhaiḥ|| 30.74 ||
antaḥ karaṇavṛttyā yā teṣāmātmaikatā matā|
jñānayogaḥ sa uddiṣṭaḥ karmayogaḥ kriyātmakaḥ|| 30.75 ||
iṣṭvā''dau karmayogena jñānayogena taṃ yajet|
kāyikairvācikaiścaiva mānasaiśca punaḥ kramāt|| 30.76 ||
sthūle niveśitaṃ cittaṃ punaḥ sūkṣme niveśayet|
pararūpe yadā yuktastadā'sau mukta ucyate|| 30.77 ||
parātparataraṃ jyotiścidrūpamacalaṃ dhruvam|
yaḥ prapaśyati buddhyā'sau na punarbhuvi jāyate|| 30.78 ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena bhakto yogī bhavet sadā|
yukto devamupāsīta siddhirevaṃ bhaviṣyati|| 30.79 ||
evaṃ te sakalā''khyātā mayā'lpā viṣṇusaṃhitā|
sākṣādviṣṇumukhādeṣā mayā prāptā yugāntare|| 30.80 ||
jñānānāṃ paramaṃ jñānaṃ guhyānāṃ guhyamuttamam|
hitānāṃ ca hitaṃ nānyadato rakṣyamidaṃ tvayā|| 30.81 ||
yathā'haṃ śrutavān pūrvaṃ sākṣād bhagavato mukhāt|
tathedamakhilaṃ tantraṃ mayā tubhyaṃ niveditam|| 30.82||
nādīkṣitāya dātavyaṃ nāsaṃvatsaravāsine|
nābhaktāyāvinītāya nānyabhaktāya vā kvacit|| 30.83 ||
kṛtajñāya vinītāya śucaye brahmacāriṇe|
vaktavyaṃ sarvathā''cāryairityevaṃ viṣṇuśāsanam|| 30.84 ||
guptaṃ hi sthāpayet sarvamaguptaṃ nāśayennaraḥ|
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena gopyametat sadā budhaiḥ|| 30.85 ||
yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyānnityaṃ śrāvayed vā samāhitaḥ|
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇuloke mahīyate|| 30.86 ||
tasmād bhaktairidaṃ nityamadhyeyaṃ śrāvyameva ca|
pustake likhitaṃ nityaṃ pūjanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ|| 30.87 ||
yatredaṃ prayatairnityaṃ pūjyate pustakasthitam|
na tatra vyādhicorādibhayaṃ kiñcit prajāyate|| 30.88 ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena gṛhe satatamarcayet|
sthāpayed gopayeccātra vardhate śrīracañcalā|| 30.89 ||

|| iti viṣṇusaṃhitāyāṃ triṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
